# v1 wave 5: DERP server, region and probe Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: the skill the **Execution:** line names — dforge:subagent-driven-development for `subagent`, dforge:executing-plans for `inline`. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build wave 5 of the v1 completion spec: the `derp` module (the agent's `derp.server.install`, `derp.server.uninstall`, `derp.binary.update` with its recovery handler, `derp.cert.renew` and two agent reads; the controller's `derp.server.probe`, two internal reads, the `derp.servers` service with the `derp.server.list` read, and the composites `derp.server.deploy`, `derp.server.upgrade`, `derp.cert.rotate`, `derp.region.set`, `derp.region.remove` and `derp.server.remove`), the verified derper release artifact (`release/derper.version`, `releasetool derper`, `derper.json`, the agent's fetch), the `/derp` web page, the M5 DERP harness test, and register row 300 (the latency probe URL).

**Architecture:** The `derp` module follows the tailscale module's shape: a `module` value holding an internal struct `dp` whose methods construct one `core.ActionDef` per action, a `deps` struct that tests replace with fakes, a module-private test host, and one registration task that wires every constructor into `Manifest()` and commits the schema snapshots. Pure logic lives in two packages without I/O: `derp/server` (unit file, flags, certificate pin and port rules) and `derp/artifact` (the derper version and manifest, plus the verified fetch over `internal/update`). The agent half's long actions are task actions (`core.DefineTask`, spec 1.3c); the binary update is an agent-local transaction journaled in its own table with a module recovery handler (1.3b). The controller half reads agents through `env.Agents`, the tailnet through new exported reads of the tailnet module, and computes policy edits and fallback checks in leading Internal read steps of its composites, because a composite's `Steps` reads only its input and the controller database.

**Tech Stack:** Go 1.27 (`go.mod`: `go 1.27`, toolchain `go1.27.3`) (standard library, `internal/platform/tslocal`, `internal/platform/netstat`, the `internal/platform` exec allowlist with `useradd`/`userdel` for `darkpanel-derp`, `internal/update` with minisign), SQLite through `internal/core/store`, React 19 + TypeScript + TanStack Query + darkraise-ui (Vitest), the KVM harness in `test/harness` (GitHub Actions `harness.yml`), the release workflow `release.yml`.

**Spec:** docs/dforge/specs/2026-10-21-v1-completion-design.md

**Execution:** subagent — `claude --model sonnet --effort high` — the plan runs parallel waves, and each task's **Implementer:** line chooses its implementer

**Parallelism:** waves — 1-3 | 4-6 | 7-9 | 10-11 | 12-14 | 15 | 16 | 17-19 | 20-22 | 23 | 24 | 25-26 | 27-28 | 29 | 30

**Task check:** none — full suite every 3 tasks

**Integration check:** `mkdir -p /root/.cache/w5-tmp/integ && export TMPDIR=/root/.cache/w5-tmp/integ GOTOOLCHAIN=local && timeout 600 go build ./... && timeout 300 go vet ./... && timeout 300 go vet -tags harness ./test/... && timeout 1500 go test -count=1 ./... && timeout 900 golangci-lint run --allow-serial-runners ./... && timeout 600 golangci-lint run --allow-serial-runners --build-tags harness ./test/... && timeout 300 go mod tidy -diff && timeout 1200 make lint-web test-web`

**CI:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml .github/workflows/harness.yml .github/workflows/release.yml .github/workflows/runner-probe.yml` — platforms: ubuntu-24.04, self-hosted — local: `make test-adr`; `make lint-sh test-release`; `make lint-go test-go vuln`; `make lint-web test-web`; `make build verify-build` — uncovered: none

**Worktree setup:** `timeout 600 make web-deps`

**Wave check:** `mkdir -p /root/.cache/w5-tmp/wave && export TMPDIR=/root/.cache/w5-tmp/wave GOTOOLCHAIN=local && timeout 600 go build ./... && timeout 300 go vet ./... && timeout 900 go test -count=1 ./internal/modules/... ./internal/update/... ./release/... ./internal/core/... ./schemas/... && timeout 300 go vet -tags harness ./test/... && timeout 1200 make lint-web test-web`

**Plan review:** 2026-11-02 — dforge:judge-opus — executability 16 / coherence 16 / coverage 17 / assumptions 16 (round 1)

## Global Constraints

- Scope is spec revision 8 as amended on `main`: section 4.3 (R61–R64), the derp rows of section 5, the derp error codes of section 11, the M5 row of Verification for the DERP unit tests (`derp.cert.rotate` and its undo; upgrade and rotation refused without a fallback region) and for the DERP harness checks that need no tailnet, and the register rows the tasks' **Items:** lines name (v1-completion 25, 26, 27, 28 and 300). The live DERP demonstration (spec 12 item 2, register row 55) and everything of waves 6–10 are out of scope. Spec 5 allows the wave that builds a module to add read actions and resources: `derp.server.status`, `derp.node.probe`, `derp.region.edits`, `derp.fallback.check` and `derp.server.list` are such additions.
- Host safety (absolute). This host is live: it runs an installed `darkpanel-agent`, `darkpanel-controller` and a Docker daemon, and has no tailscaled. No test or command of this plan runs `useradd`, `userdel`, `systemctl`, `derper`, `tailscale` or `tailscaled` on this host, talks to a tailscaled socket, binds port 80, 443 or 3478, writes under `/usr/local/lib/darkpanel`, `/etc/systemd/system`, `/var/lib/darkpanel`, `/etc/darkpanel` or `/run/darkpanel`, or starts, stops, restarts or reloads any systemd unit. Every host effect in a unit test goes through the module's fakes (Contracts "DERP test host"); real derper runs only on harness VMs (Task 13). Only Task 1 (public sources and a derper build into `$TMPDIR`) and Task 8's build check (one `releasetool derper` run into `$TMPDIR`) reach the network; no unit test does: provider and release calls go to `httptest` servers on `127.0.0.1`.
- Every `go`, `golangci-lint`, `make` and `pnpm` command runs with `GOTOOLCHAIN=local`, an explicit `timeout`, and `TMPDIR=/root/.cache/w5-tmp/<task number>` (`mkdir -p` it first; remove it and run `go clean -testcache` when the task ends), also where a step's command omits one of them: `/tmp` is an 8 GiB tmpfs and Unix socket paths over 108 bytes fail. When `df -h /` passes 90 %, trim Go build cache entries older than a few hours. Web commands use `pnpm -C web exec vitest run <files>` for single files (`pnpm -C web vitest run` does not work here).
- Dependencies: no task changes `go.mod`, `go.sum`, `web/package.json` or `web/pnpm-lock.yaml`; only the standard library and modules already required are used, and after every task `GOTOOLCHAIN=local timeout 300 go mod tidy -diff` prints nothing. No package of this repository imports `tailscale.com`: `releasetool derper` builds derper in a temporary module of its own (Contracts "Derper release build").
- After every task all of these pass, with their real output recorded: `go build ./...`; `go vet ./...` (no output); `go test -count=1 ./...` (every line `ok` or `?`); `go test -race -count=1` on the task's packages; `golangci-lint run --allow-serial-runners ./...` (`0 issues.`); `go mod tidy -diff` (no output). A task that edits `test/harness/` also runs `go vet -tags harness ./test/...` and `go test -tags harness -count=1 -run 'Unit' ./test/harness/...`. A task that edits `web/` also runs `pnpm -C web lint`, `pnpm -C web typecheck` and `pnpm -C web test`. A task that adds or changes an action, resource or input type runs `make schemas` and commits the snapshots it changes, or `modules.TestSnapshotsCurrent` fails; only the tasks whose **Files:** name `schemas/` do this (Task 29).
- Contract-check gate: after wave 1-3 merges, the controller reads Task 1's report `docs/dforge/notes/v1-w5-derp-spike.md`. Any entry under its `## Divergences` heading other than `none` stops execution until the integrator amends Contracts and every task that states the old value (a header amendment), before wave 4-6 starts. In the same step the integrator copies the grant from the `**Visibility grant:**` line of Task 1's "Recorded contract (DERP, ...)" bullet in spec 4.3 into Contracts "DERP region edits" (the `visibilityGrant` line), replacing the provisional value when it differs, and commits it. Tasks after wave 1-3 implement their text as written.
- TDD: write the test, run it and see the expected RED (usually a build failure naming the first undefined identifier), implement, run GREEN. Quote the RED and GREEN output in the report, not a paraphrase.
- Frozen files (spec 1.12, and the earlier waves' modules this plan does not own), which no task edits: `internal/core/contract.go`, `units.go`, `define.go`, `registry.go`, `guard.go`, `internal/core/ext/**`, `internal/core/errcode/errcode.go`, `internal/core/lifecycle/**`, the core migrations, `internal/platform/exec.go`, `files.go`, `files_servicedir.go`, `systemd.go`, `ownership.go`, `internal/platform/exec_rules.go`, `internal/platform/tslocal/**`, `internal/platform/netstat/**`, `internal/modules/modules.go`, `internal/cli/modules.go`, `internal/controller/server/**`, `internal/controller/api/**`, `internal/controller/events/**`, `internal/controller/listen/**`, `cmd/darkpanel/**`, `go.mod`, `go.sum`, `web/package.json`, `web/pnpm-lock.yaml`, `web/src/modules/registry.ts`, `web/src/modules/types.ts`, `web/src/app.tsx`, `test/harness/pins.go`, `test/harness/images.json`, `internal/modules/firewall/**`, `internal/modules/docker/**`, `internal/modules/cloudflare/**`, `Makefile`; `internal/modules/tailscale/**` except Task 5's `latency.go` and `latency_test.go`; `internal/modules/tailnet/**` except Task 4's files; `internal/update/**` except Task 3's files. A task that finds a missing code, allowlist rule or extension point in a frozen file stops and reports it; it does not edit the file. Every program and argument vector this plan runs is already allowlisted (Assumptions).
- A task edits only the files of its own **Files:** block. Each task of a wave runs in its own worktree from the wave's base (`main` plus every merged earlier wave), so a package-wide command sees only merged work plus the task's own files. "The base" in a task's text means the work its **Depends on** line names.
- Test isolation: `t.TempDir()` for files and databases; TCP and UDP only on `127.0.0.1` port 0 (`httptest` servers, a STUN fake on `net.ListenPacket("udp", "127.0.0.1:0")`); `coretest.NewFakeClock` wherever a duration above one second is waited on (the 60 s health check, the 30 s certificate wait, the 60 s service period, the 30-day and 10-minute health thresholds); no fixed path outside a temporary directory; no global mutable state shared between tests of different tasks. Tasks of one wave therefore share no port, path or global state.
- Tasks that share a Go package declare no top-level identifier, test helpers included, that another task declares. Every new test identifier carries its task's prefix from Contracts "Test prefixes", except the shared helpers of Contracts "DERP test host", which only Task 12 declares.
- Code style: match the surrounding code; a doc comment on every exported identifier; comments only for a non-obvious why, in full sentences, never naming a plan, task, register row or review; `//nolint` names the rule and a reason; gofmt clean. Error strings start with the package name (`derp:`, `server:`, `artifact:`, `update:`, `tailnet:`, `tailscale:`, `releasetool:`, `harness:`). Refusals are `*errcode.Error` with client-safe details using only codes already in `internal/core/errcode/errcode.go` (`derp.region_taken`, `derp.port_in_use`, `derp.verify_failed`, `derp.not_ready`, `derp.no_fallback`, `tailscale.unavailable`, `update.not_found`, `update.signature_invalid`, `cloud.not_connected`, `plan.state_changed`, `request.invalid_input`, `not_found`); a refusal that committed nothing is wrapped with `core.Definite`. No detail, diff line, `StateDoc`, effect, log line, audit entry or error carries a secret (derper's node key file, a certificate private key, a tailnet credential).
- Lock keys: `derp.server.install`, `derp.server.uninstall`, `derp.binary.update` and `derp.cert.renew` lock `derp`; the reads take none; the composites take `composite:derp` (the engine's coordination lock); their children take their own locks.
- Values copied from the spec, used exactly: lock key `derp`; system user `darkpanel-derp`; binary `/usr/local/lib/darkpanel/derper` (with `derper.new` and `derper.prev` beside it, all kind `file`, mode 0755); unit `/etc/systemd/system/darkpanel-derper.service` (kind `unit`); service directory `/var/lib/darkpanel/derp/` (kind `service-dir`, user `darkpanel-derp`, subdirectory `certs`); key file `-c /var/lib/darkpanel/derp/derper.key`; flags `-a :443 -http-port 80 -stun -stun-port 3478`, `-verify-clients`, `-certmode letsencrypt` or `-certmode manual`, `-hostname <name or public IP>`, `-certdir /var/lib/darkpanel/derp/certs` (each flag and its value are separate argv tokens, in the order Contracts "DERP server unit" `Flags` gives); ports 80/tcp, 443/tcp, 3478/udp with firewall label `derp`; region ids 900–999; the DERP map node pin `CertName: "sha256-raw:<hex SHA-256 of the certificate DER>"`; the certificate files `<certdir>/<ip>.crt` and `.key`, renewed to `.prev` names with `RenameIn`; warnings 30 days before `NotAfter` (`degraded`) and `broken` after it; the binary health check up to 60 s; the typed acknowledgement `nodes will relay only through <region code>`; the artifact names `derper-<derper version>-linux-<arch>` and `derper.json` with members `darkpanelVersion`, `derperVersion`, `artifacts`; `CGO_ENABLED=0`; task unit names `darkpanel-task-<change id>-<op>-<attempt>.service`; the probe paths `GET /derp/probe` (TLS) and STUN on 3478.
- Commits use `<type>(<scope>): <subject>`, type one of feat|fix|docs|style|refactor|test|chore|perf, subject at most 50 characters, imperative, no trailing period, no attribution of any kind. English only.
- The plan ends with the gates in `docs/dforge/gates.md`. Tasks 3, 13 and 30 touch `internal/update/`, `test/harness/` and `.github/workflows/harness.yml`, so gate 7 (harness) applies to the branch once it is pushed, as well as on `main`; dispatch `harness.yml` on the branch per the owner's standing authorization. No task creates or pushes a tag, and no task runs `release.yml`.

### Derper artifact (from Tasks 2, 9)

Package `internal/modules/derp/artifact`. Task 2 writes `manifest.go` and creates `release/derper.version` holding the single line `v1.104.1`:

```go
// DerperVersion is the derper release this Darkpanel builds, publishes and installs; release/derper.version holds
// the same value (a test reads that file and compares).
const DerperVersion = "v1.104.1"

// ManifestName is the release asset listing the derper builds.
const ManifestName = "derper.json"

// Arches are the architectures a release builds derper for, in order.
var Arches = []string{"amd64", "arm64"}

// BinaryName is the release asset of derper version for arch: "derper-" + version + "-linux-" + arch
// (BinaryName("v1.104.1", "amd64") == "derper-v1.104.1-linux-amd64").
func BinaryName(version, arch string) string

// PinVersion is the tailscaled version a DERP host is pinned to: DerperVersion without its leading "v" ("1.104.1").
func PinVersion() string

// Manifest is derper.json.
type Manifest struct {
	DarkpanelVersion string            `json:"darkpanelVersion"` // a version update.ParseVersion accepts, without "v"
	DerperVersion    string            `json:"derperVersion"`    // ^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$
	Artifacts        map[string]string `json:"artifacts"`        // arch → 64 lower-case hex digits; exactly the keys of Arches
}

// ParseManifest decodes b strictly: unknown members, a missing member, a version that does not match its pattern,
// an arch outside Arches, a missing arch or a hash that is not 64 lower-case hex digits is an error naming it.
func ParseManifest(b []byte) (Manifest, error)

// Encode is m as two-space indented JSON with a trailing newline (map keys sorted, as encoding/json sorts them);
// it validates m as ParseManifest does first.
func (m Manifest) Encode() ([]byte, error)
```

Task 9 writes `fetch.go`:

```go
// Source is where Fetch reads a release.
type Source struct {
	Client   *http.Client
	BaseURL  string                                            // trustedkeys.BaseURL() in production
	Keys     []trustedkeys.Key                                 // trustedkeys.Keys() in production
	Releases func(ctx context.Context) ([]update.Release, error) // (*update.IndexSource).Releases in production
}

// Fetch returns derper version want for arch from the release of Darkpanel version dpVersion: it finds dpVersion in
// Releases (absent: update.not_found, "darkpanel <dpVersion> is not a published release; a DERP server needs a
// released darkpanel"), reads ManifestName with update.DownloadAsset (at most 64 KiB), checks the manifest's
// darkpanelVersion equals dpVersion (else update.signature_invalid) and its derperVersion equals want (else
// update.not_found, "release <dpVersion> carries derper <v>, not <want>"), reads BinaryName(want, arch) with
// update.DownloadAsset (at most 128 MiB) and checks its SHA-256 against the manifest's artifacts[arch] (else
// update.signature_invalid). sha is the 64 lower-case hex digits of the binary.
func Fetch(ctx context.Context, src Source, dpVersion, want, arch string) (bin []byte, sha string, err error)
```

### Release asset download (from Task 3)

Package `internal/update`, new file `asset.go`; `Download` keeps its signature and behaviour, and the checksums fetch and signature check it shares with `DownloadAsset` move into one unexported helper both call:

```go
// DownloadAsset fetches asset name of rel from <baseURL>/v<version>/: checksums.txt and its signatures, verified
// exactly as Download verifies them (VerifyChecksums against rel.Channel), then name, read up to max bytes, whose
// SHA-256 must match its checksums.txt line. name matches ^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,127}$ (else an error
// "update: <name> is not an asset name"). An unserved checksums.txt or asset is update.not_found; a hash mismatch is
// update.signature_invalid ("<name> does not match the signed checksums.txt"); an asset larger than max is an error
// naming max. Every URL is built here from baseURL.
func DownloadAsset(ctx context.Context, client *http.Client, baseURL string, keys []trustedkeys.Key, rel Release, name string, max int64) ([]byte, error)
```

### Tailnet reads for other modules (from Task 4)

Package `internal/modules/tailnet`, new file `export.go`. Unexported methods carry the logic so the package's tests can point them at the fake server; the exported functions call them on one package-level `tn` built by `productionTN()` (one token cache for every caller):

```go
func (t tn) readPolicy(ctx context.Context, env core.Env) (Policy, error)   // as tailnet.policy.get reads it
func (t tn) listDevices(ctx context.Context, env core.Env) ([]Device, error) // as tailnet.device.list lists them, same order
// ReadPolicy, ListDevices: for controller-side modules; env needs DB, Sealer and Clock. No account:
// cloud.not_connected, as the actions answer.
func ReadPolicy(ctx context.Context, env core.Env) (Policy, error)
func ListDevices(ctx context.Context, env core.Env) ([]Device, error)
// Connected reports whether the tailnet account row exists (loadAccount), without a provider call.
func Connected(ctx context.Context, db *store.DB) (bool, error)
```

Task 4 also pins two behaviours of `policy.Apply` and `tailnet.policy.patch` that later tasks rely on, with tests: a `set` at `/derpMap/Regions/900` (a member whose name is digits) and a `delete` of it work on an object member, and `tailnet.policy.patch` with an empty `edits` list plans an empty diff and applies as a no-op (Assumptions; Task 4 fixes `internal/modules/tailnet/policy/policy.go` or `policy_patch.go` only if a test shows otherwise).

### Latency probe URL (from Task 5)

Package `internal/modules/tailscale`, `latency.go`: `latencyProbeURLs` skips a node whose `HostName` holds `/`, `?`, `#`, `@`, `\`, whitespace, a control character, or a `:` that is not part of an IPv6 literal, and a node whose `DERPPort` is above 65535; it builds each URL with `url.URL{Scheme: "https", Host: ..., Path: latencyCheckPath}` (IPv6 in brackets through `net.JoinHostPort` when a port is added). Its signature and every other behaviour are unchanged.

### DERP server unit (from Task 7)

Package `internal/modules/derp/server`, pure (no I/O):

```go
const (
	ServiceUser    = "darkpanel-derp"
	BinaryPath     = "/usr/local/lib/darkpanel/derper"
	BinaryNewPath  = BinaryPath + ".new"
	BinaryPrevPath = BinaryPath + ".prev"
	UnitName       = "darkpanel-derper.service"
	UnitPath       = "/etc/systemd/system/" + UnitName
	StateDir       = "/var/lib/darkpanel/derp"
	CertSubdir     = "certs"
	CertDir        = StateDir + "/" + CertSubdir
	KeyPath        = StateDir + "/derper.key"
	HTTPPort       = 80
	HTTPSPort      = 443
	STUNPort       = 3478
	ModeLetsEncrypt = "letsencrypt"
	ModeIP          = "ip"
	RuleLabel       = "derp"
	PinPrefix       = "sha256-raw:"
)

// Config is one server's configuration.
type Config struct {
	Mode          string // ModeLetsEncrypt | ModeIP
	HostName      string // letsencrypt: a DNS name; ip: the public IP literal in canonical form
	VerifyClients bool
}

// CheckConfig refuses, as request.invalid_input with a field error: a Mode other than the two (/mode); for
// letsencrypt, a HostName that is not a lower-case DNS name of at most 253 bytes with at least two labels, or is an
// IP literal (/hostName); for ip, a HostName that is not a canonical IP literal, or is not global unicast, or is
// private, loopback, link-local, unspecified or a Tailscale address (100.64.0.0/10, fd7a:115c:a1e0::/48) (/hostName).
func CheckConfig(c Config) error

// Flags is derper's argument vector after the program path, in this order:
// -c KeyPath -a :443 -http-port 80 -stun -stun-port 3478 -hostname <HostName> -certmode <letsencrypt|manual>
// -certdir CertDir, then -verify-clients when VerifyClients.
func Flags(c Config) []string

// UnitFile is the unit text (Contracts "DERP unit text").
func UnitFile(c Config) []byte

// CertFiles is the ip mode's certificate and key file names inside CertDir: "<ip>.crt", "<ip>.key"; ok is false
// for letsencrypt (derper's own cache, never pinned or renamed).
func CertFiles(c Config) (crt, key string, ok bool)

// Pin is PinPrefix + the lower-case hex SHA-256 of der.
func Pin(der []byte) string

// ParseCert decodes the first CERTIFICATE block of pemBytes: its DER and NotAfter.
func ParseCert(pemBytes []byte) (der []byte, notAfter time.Time, err error)

// RuleID is the firewall rule id of one DERP port of host hostID: "0" + the first 25 characters of the Crockford
// base32 (alphabet 0123456789ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTVWXYZ, no padding) of SHA-256("darkpanel/derp/" + hostID + "/" + port),
// port one of "80/tcp", "443/tcp", "3478/udp". It matches the firewall's ULID id pattern.
func RuleID(hostID, port string) string

// PortRules are the three rules derp.server.deploy adds, in port order 80/tcp, 443/tcp, 3478/udp: each
// model.Rule{ID: RuleID(hostID, port), Action: allow, Direction: in, Proto: tcp|udp, From: "any", To: "any",
// ToPort: "80"|"443"|"3478", Family: both}, with no comment (firewall.rules.apply's label adds it).
func PortRules(hostID string) []model.Rule
```

### DERP unit text (from Task 7)

`UnitFile` returns exactly this text, `<flags>` being `Flags(c)` joined with single spaces:

```
[Unit]
Description=Darkpanel DERP server
After=network-online.target tailscaled.service
Wants=network-online.target

[Service]
User=darkpanel-derp
ExecStart=/usr/local/lib/darkpanel/derper <flags>
AmbientCapabilities=CAP_NET_BIND_SERVICE
CapabilityBoundingSet=CAP_NET_BIND_SERVICE
NoNewPrivileges=yes
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=5

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
```

If Task 1 records that `-verify-clients` does not work for a non-root user, the contract-check gate changes `User=` and the spec, and this entry with it.

### Derper release build (from Task 8)

`release/releasetool` gains the subcommand `releasetool derper <darkpanel version> <out dir> [-arch amd64|arm64]` in the new file `derper.go` (and the usage text in `main.go`): for each arch (the one given, else every `artifact.Arches` entry) it builds `tailscale.com/cmd/derper@artifact.DerperVersion` in a fresh temporary module (`go mod init derperbuild`, `go get tailscale.com/cmd/derper@<v>`, then `go build -trimpath -ldflags "-s -w" -o <out>/<artifact.BinaryName(v, arch)> tailscale.com/cmd/derper` with `CGO_ENABLED=0 GOOS=linux GOARCH=<arch>` and the caller's `GOTOOLCHAIN`), writes `<out>/derper.json` (`artifact.Manifest{<darkpanel version>, v, {arch: sha}}.Encode()`) only when every arch of `artifact.Arches` was built, and, when `<out>/checksums.txt` exists, rewrites it as `release/package.sh` writes it: one `<sha256>  <name>` line per regular file of `<out>` other than `checksums.txt` and `*.minisig`, sorted by name in byte order. The build step is a function value (`type derperBuilder func(ctx context.Context, version, arch, dst string) error`) so tests replace it; the darkpanel version is checked with `parseVersion`. `.github/workflows/release.yml` runs `go run ./release/releasetool derper "$VERSION" <the release directory make release wrote>` right after `make release`, and `release/README.md` documents the step and that bumping `release/derper.version` promptly after a Tailscale security release is part of the release routine (spec 4.3).

### DERP internals (from Task 12)

Package `internal/modules/derp`. Task 12 writes `deps.go`, `wire.go`, `store.go`, `derpmap.go`, `migrations/agent/0001_derp.sql`, `migrations/controller/0001_derp.sql`, and changes `derp.go` to hold `type module struct{ d dp }` and `Module() core.Module { return module{d: productionDP()} }` (its manifest still declares nothing until Task 29):

```go
const (
	lockKey         = "derp"
	healthWindow    = 60 * time.Second // binary.update's health check
	certWait        = 30 * time.Second // install and cert.renew wait for derper's certificate files
	availableWait   = 5 * time.Second
	servicePeriod   = 60 * time.Second // the derp.servers service
	staleAfter      = 10 * time.Minute // a server row older than this is degraded
	expiryWarning   = 30 * 24 * time.Hour
	maxProbeAgents  = 8
)
type dp struct{ d deps }
type deps struct {
	local          func() localAPI                                                          // production: tslocal client on tslocal.DefaultSocket
	fetch          func(ctx context.Context, dpVersion, want, arch string) ([]byte, string, error) // production: artifact.Fetch with the production Source (30 s index client, 5 min download client)
	version        string                                                                   // production: buildinfo.Version
	arch           string                                                                   // production: runtime.GOARCH
	readFile       func(name string) ([]byte, error)                                         // production: os.ReadFile (certificate files under CertDir)
	lookupUser     func(name string) (uid, gid int, ok bool, err error)                     // production: os/user.Lookup; an unknown user is ok false, nil error
	sockets        func() ([]netstat.Socket, error)                                          // production: netstat.Sockets
	linkViaTailnet func() (bool, error)                                                      // production: the saved controller address (daemon.LoadCredentials(paths.Production().AgentTLSDir)) is an IP literal inside a Tailscale range (link.IsTailscaleAddr); not enrolled: false
	tlsProbe       func(ctx context.Context, t probeTarget) (time.Duration, error)           // production: probeTLS
	stun           func(ctx context.Context, server netip.AddrPort) (time.Duration, error)  // production: stun.Probe (internal/modules/tailscale/stun)
	resolve        func(ctx context.Context, host string) ([]netip.Addr, error)              // production: net.DefaultResolver.LookupNetIP(ctx, "ip", host)
	policy         func(ctx context.Context, env core.Env) (tailnet.Policy, error)           // production: tailnet.ReadPolicy
	devices        func(ctx context.Context, env core.Env) ([]tailnet.Device, error)         // production: tailnet.ListDevices
	connected      func(ctx context.Context, db *store.DB) (bool, error)                     // production: tailnet.Connected
}
type localAPI interface {
	Status(ctx context.Context) (*tslocal.Status, error)
	WhoIs(ctx context.Context, addr netip.Addr) (*tslocal.WhoIs, error)
	DebugDERPRegion(ctx context.Context, region string) (*tslocal.DERPRegionReport, error)
}
// probeTarget is one DERP server as a prober reaches it.
type probeTarget struct {
	HostName string // the server's -hostname: a DNS name or, in ip mode, the IP literal
	Address  string // the address to connect to; "" resolves HostName
	Port     int    // 443
	CertName string // ip mode: the pin (PinPrefix...); "" verifies the chain against the system roots for HostName
}
func productionDP() dp
// probeTLS GETs https://<Address or HostName>:<Port>/derp/probe once with a 5 s timeout and no redirects; the TLS
// ServerName is HostName unless it is an IP literal; with a CertName it accepts exactly the leaf whose SHA-256
// matches the pin and whose NotBefore/NotAfter contain now; else it verifies the chain. Any answer but 200 is an error.
func probeTLS(ctx context.Context, t probeTarget) (time.Duration, error)
// available: nil when tailscaled answers Status within availableWait with BackendState "Running"; no answer:
// errcode tailscale.unavailable "tailscaled does not answer on its socket"; another state: errcode derp.not_ready
// "the host is not on the tailnet (tailscaled is <state>)".
func (d dp) available(ctx context.Context, env core.Env) error
func agentMigrations() fs.FS
func controllerMigrations() fs.FS
// ownedPaths: {ServiceUser, user}, {BinaryPath, file}, {BinaryNewPath, file}, {BinaryPrevPath, file},
// {UnitPath, unit}, {StateDir, service-dir, User ServiceUser, Subdirs [CertSubdir]} (the default name pattern).
func ownedPaths() []core.OwnedPath
```

`migrations/agent/0001_derp.sql`, `STRICT`, times as fixed-width RFC 3339 UTC text:

```sql
-- The DERP server installed on this host; at most one.
CREATE TABLE derp_server (
    id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),
    change_id       TEXT NOT NULL,
    mode            TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (mode IN ('letsencrypt', 'ip')),
    host_name       TEXT NOT NULL,
    verify_clients  INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (verify_clients IN (0, 1)),
    derper_version  TEXT NOT NULL,
    binary_sha256   TEXT NOT NULL,
    cert_name       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    cert_not_after  TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    installed_at    TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at      TEXT NOT NULL
) STRICT;
-- One row per derp.binary.update change: its agent-local transaction.
CREATE TABLE derp_updates (
    change_id     TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    from_version  TEXT NOT NULL,
    from_sha256   TEXT NOT NULL,
    to_version    TEXT NOT NULL,
    to_sha256     TEXT NOT NULL,
    phase         TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (phase IN ('staged', 'kept', 'swapped', 'restarted', 'done', 'rolling_back', 'rolled_back', 'failed')),
    result        TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    updated_at    TEXT NOT NULL
) STRICT;
```

`migrations/controller/0001_derp.sql`:

```sql
-- One row per host the derp.servers service found running a DERP server.
CREATE TABLE derp_servers (
    host_id         TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    mode            TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (mode IN ('letsencrypt', 'ip')),
    host_name       TEXT NOT NULL,
    region_id       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    region_code     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    derper_version  TEXT NOT NULL,
    cert_name       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    cert_not_after  TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    unit_active     INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (unit_active IN (0, 1)),
    checked_at      TEXT NOT NULL,
    error           TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
) STRICT;
```

`store.go` (with `type queryer interface { QueryRowContext(...) *sql.Row; QueryContext(...) (*sql.Rows, error) }`; a zero `time.Time` is stored as `''`):

```go
type localServer struct {
	ChangeID, Mode, HostName string
	VerifyClients            bool
	DerperVersion, BinarySHA256, CertName string
	CertNotAfter, InstalledAt, UpdatedAt  time.Time
}
func loadLocalServer(ctx context.Context, q queryer) (localServer, bool, error)
func insertLocalServer(ctx context.Context, db *store.DB, s localServer) error
func setLocalBinary(ctx context.Context, db *store.DB, version, sha string, at time.Time) error
func setLocalCert(ctx context.Context, db *store.DB, certName string, notAfter, at time.Time) error
func deleteLocalServer(ctx context.Context, db *store.DB) error // no row is not an error

type updateRow struct {
	ChangeID, FromVersion, FromSHA256, ToVersion, ToSHA256, Phase, Result string
	UpdatedAt                                                          time.Time
}
func insertUpdate(ctx context.Context, db *store.DB, u updateRow) error
func loadUpdate(ctx context.Context, q queryer, changeID string) (updateRow, bool, error)
func setUpdatePhase(ctx context.Context, db *store.DB, changeID, phase, result string, at time.Time) error

type serverRecord struct {
	HostID, Mode, HostName  string
	RegionID                int
	RegionCode, DerperVersion, CertName string
	CertNotAfter            time.Time
	UnitActive              bool
	CheckedAt               time.Time
	Error                   string
}
func loadServerRecords(ctx context.Context, q queryer) ([]serverRecord, error) // ordered by host_id
func loadServerRecord(ctx context.Context, q queryer, hostID string) (serverRecord, bool, error)
func upsertServerRecord(ctx context.Context, db *store.DB, r serverRecord) error
func deleteServerRecord(ctx context.Context, db *store.DB, hostID string) error
```

`derpmap.go`, pure:

```go
// derpMap is the policy file's "derpMap" member as the module reads it (Tailscale's JSON names: "OmitDefaultRegions",
// "Regions" keyed by the decimal region id, each region "RegionID", "RegionCode", "RegionName", "Nodes", each node
// "Name", "RegionID", "HostName", "CertName", "IPv4", "IPv6", "STUNPort", "DERPPort").
type derpMap struct {
	Present            bool // the policy has a derpMap member
	OmitDefaultRegions bool
	Regions            map[int]derpRegion
}
type derpRegion struct {
	RegionID               int
	RegionCode, RegionName string
	Nodes                  []derpNode
}
type derpNode struct {
	Name, HostName, CertName, IPv4, IPv6 string
	RegionID, STUNPort, DERPPort         int
}
// parseDERPMap reads text (HuJSON) through policy.Canonical; a Regions key that is not a decimal integer equal to
// its region's RegionID is an error naming it.
func parseDERPMap(text string) (derpMap, error)
// regionOf returns the custom region (id 900-999) one of whose nodes has HostName equal to hostName, ignoring case.
func (m derpMap) regionOf(hostName string) (derpRegion, bool)
// customRegions returns the ids 900-999 present, ascending.
func (m derpMap) customRegions() []int
```

### DERP wire shapes (Task 12; read by Tasks 6, 15–28)

Package `internal/modules/derp`, file `wire.go`, written by Task 12 verbatim with `json` tags in lower camel case as named (and `jsonschema_description` on every field, patterns as given); no other task declares any of these:

```go
// derp.server.install (agent)
type ServerInstallInput struct {
	Mode          string `json:"mode"`                    // enum letsencrypt, ip
	HostName      string `json:"hostName"`                // maxLength 253
	VerifyClients *bool  `json:"verifyClients,omitempty"` // nil means true
}
type ServerInstalled struct {
	DerperVersion string     `json:"derperVersion"`
	CertName      string     `json:"certName"`     // ip mode: the pin; "" for letsencrypt
	CertNotAfter  *time.Time `json:"certNotAfter"` // ip mode only
}
// derp.server.uninstall (agent)
type ServerUninstallInput struct{}
type ServerUninstalled struct {
	Removed []string `json:"removed"` // effect resources removed, in order; empty when nothing was installed
}
// derp.binary.update (agent)
type BinaryUpdateInput struct {
	Version string `json:"version"` // pattern ^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$
}
type BinaryUpdated struct {
	From string `json:"from"`
	To   string `json:"to"`
}
// derp.cert.renew (agent)
type CertRenewInput struct{}
type CertRenewed struct {
	CertName      string    `json:"certName"`
	CertNotAfter  time.Time `json:"certNotAfter"`
	PriorCertName string    `json:"priorCertName"`
}
// derp.server.status (agent read)
type ServerStatusInput struct {
	Whois []string `json:"whois,omitempty"` // maxItems 256; IP literals to look up on this host's tailscaled
}
type ServerStatus struct {
	Installed        bool         `json:"installed"`
	Mode             string       `json:"mode"`          // "" when not installed
	HostName         string       `json:"hostName"`
	VerifyClients    bool         `json:"verifyClients"`
	DerperVersion    string       `json:"derperVersion"`
	UnitActive       bool         `json:"unitActive"`
	CertName         string       `json:"certName"`
	CertNotAfter     *time.Time   `json:"certNotAfter"`
	AvailableVersion string       `json:"availableVersion"` // artifact.DerperVersion of this agent's build
	LinkViaTailnet   bool         `json:"linkViaTailnet"`
	OnTailnet        bool         `json:"onTailnet"`        // tailscaled answers with BackendState Running
	Tags             []string     `json:"tags"`             // the node's tags, sorted; empty when none
	Addresses        []string     `json:"addresses"`        // the node's Tailscale addresses, IPv4 first
	Visible          []Visibility `json:"visible"`          // one per Whois entry, in input order
}
type Visibility struct {
	Address string `json:"address"`
	Visible bool   `json:"visible"` // WhoIs answers a node
	Node    string `json:"node"`    // its MagicDNS name without the trailing dot; "" when not visible
}
// derp.node.probe (agent read): this agent probes one DERP server.
type NodeProbeInput struct {
	HostName string `json:"hostName"`
	Address  string `json:"address,omitempty"`
	CertName string `json:"certName,omitempty"`
	RegionID int    `json:"regionId,omitempty"` // 900-999; 0 skips the region diagnostics
}
type NodeProbe struct {
	TLS    ProbeResult        `json:"tls"`
	STUN   ProbeResult        `json:"stun"`
	Region *RegionDiagnostics `json:"region"` // nil when RegionID is 0 (nullable, as every output pointer)
}
type ProbeResult struct {
	OK        bool    `json:"ok"`
	LatencyMs float64 `json:"latencyMs"` // 0 when not OK
	Error     string  `json:"error"`     // "" when OK
}
// RegionDiagnostics is the local API's DERP-region debug report: connectivity diagnostics only (spec 4.3).
type RegionDiagnostics struct {
	Info     []string `json:"info"`
	Warnings []string `json:"warnings"`
	Errors   []string `json:"errors"`
}
// derp.server.probe (controller read)
type ServerProbeInput struct {
	HostID string   `json:"hostId"`
	Agents []string `json:"agents,omitempty"` // maxItems 8; empty: every core_hosts row other than HostID, by id, at most maxProbeAgents
}
type ServerProbe struct {
	HostID     string        `json:"hostId"`
	RegionID   int           `json:"regionId"`   // 0 when the region is not published
	RegionCode string        `json:"regionCode"`
	Server     ServerStatus  `json:"server"`
	Sources    []ProbeSource `json:"sources"`    // "controller" first, then the agents in input order
	Nodes      []NodeUse     `json:"nodes"`
	Ready      bool          `json:"ready"`      // probeReady
	Warnings   []string      `json:"warnings"`
}
type ProbeSource struct {
	Source string             `json:"source"` // "controller" or a host id
	Name   string             `json:"name"`   // "controller" or the host's name
	TLS    ProbeResult        `json:"tls"`
	STUN   ProbeResult        `json:"stun"`
	Region *RegionDiagnostics `json:"region"`
	Error  string             `json:"error"`  // the agent read failed; TLS and STUN then carry it too
}
type NodeUse struct {
	Name      string `json:"name"`      // device name, or the host name when the tailnet is not connected
	Address   string `json:"address"`
	HostID    string `json:"hostId"`    // "" when the node is no Darkpanel host
	Visible   bool   `json:"visible"`   // the DERP host's tailscaled knows it (whois)
	Preferred string `json:"preferred"` // the node's home region code (tailscale.status preferredDerp); "" when unknown
	Observed  bool   `json:"observed"`  // a peer of the node is currently relayed through this region
}
// derp.server.list (controller read): the derp_servers table with health
type ServerListInput struct{}
type Servers struct {
	Servers          []ServerView `json:"servers"` // by host id
	AvailableVersion string       `json:"availableVersion"`
}
type ServerView struct {
	HostID        string     `json:"hostId"`
	Host          string     `json:"host"`     // core_hosts name
	Mode          string     `json:"mode"`
	HostName      string     `json:"hostName"`
	RegionID      int        `json:"regionId"`
	RegionCode    string     `json:"regionCode"`
	DerperVersion string     `json:"derperVersion"`
	CertName      string     `json:"certName"`
	CertNotAfter  *time.Time `json:"certNotAfter"`
	UnitActive    bool       `json:"unitActive"`
	Health        string     `json:"health"`   // "ok" | "degraded" | "broken"
	Reasons       []string   `json:"reasons"`  // one sentence per reason; empty when ok
	CheckedAt     time.Time  `json:"checkedAt"`
	Error         string     `json:"error"`
}
// derp.region.edits (controller internal read)
type RegionEditsInput struct {
	Op                 string `json:"op"`                           // enum set, remove, rotate
	HostID             string `json:"hostId,omitempty"`             // set, rotate
	RegionID           int    `json:"regionId"`                     // 900-999
	RegionCode         string `json:"regionCode,omitempty"`         // set: ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,31}$
	RegionName         string `json:"regionName,omitempty"`         // set: maxLength 64
	OmitDefaultRegions bool   `json:"omitDefaultRegions,omitempty"` // set
	Acknowledge        string `json:"acknowledge,omitempty"`        // set: the typed acknowledgement
	CertName           string `json:"certName,omitempty"`           // rotate: the new pin
}
type RegionEdits struct {
	Edits    []policy.Edit `json:"edits"`    // empty (not null) when the policy already holds the result
	Warnings []string      `json:"warnings"`
}
// derp.fallback.check (controller internal read)
type FallbackCheckInput struct {
	HostID   string `json:"hostId"`
	RegionID int    `json:"regionId"`
}
type FallbackCheck struct {
	Path string `json:"path"` // "link" | "default-regions" | "region:<id>"
}
// composites (controller)
type DeployInput struct {
	HostID        string `json:"hostId"`
	Mode          string `json:"mode"`
	HostName      string `json:"hostName"`
	VerifyClients *bool  `json:"verifyClients,omitempty"`
}
type Deployed struct {
	DerperVersion string     `json:"derperVersion"`
	CertName      string     `json:"certName"`
	CertNotAfter  *time.Time `json:"certNotAfter"`
}
type UpgradeInput struct {
	HostID string `json:"hostId"`
}
type Upgraded struct {
	From string `json:"from"`
	To   string `json:"to"`
}
type CertRotateInput struct {
	HostID string `json:"hostId"`
}
type CertRotated struct {
	CertName     string    `json:"certName"`
	CertNotAfter time.Time `json:"certNotAfter"`
}
type RegionSetInput struct {
	HostID             string `json:"hostId"`
	RegionID           int    `json:"regionId"`
	RegionCode         string `json:"regionCode"`
	RegionName         string `json:"regionName"`
	OmitDefaultRegions bool   `json:"omitDefaultRegions,omitempty"`
	Acknowledge        string `json:"acknowledge,omitempty"`
}
type RegionRemoveInput struct {
	RegionID int `json:"regionId"`
}
type ServerRemoveInput struct {
	HostID string `json:"hostId"`
}
type RegionChanged struct {
	ETag string `json:"etag"` // the policy's ETag after the step; "" when nothing changed
}
type ServerRemoved struct {
	Removed []string `json:"removed"`
}
```

Integer region ids carry `jsonschema:"minimum=900,maximum=999"`; `HostID` fields match the host id pattern the core uses for `core.host.*` inputs.

### DERP actions (Tasks 15–28; registered by Task 29)

All `Version: 1`. One method on `dp` per action, resource or service, each in its task's file, each returning the declaration only (no socket, file, exec or provider call while constructing). Task 29 calls exactly these and declares no other:

| Action | Class / constructor | Side | Other | Method, file, task |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `derp.server.install` | compensable task, `DefineTask` with an `Undoer` | agent | lock `derp`; `LongRunning`; `ApplyTimeout: 15 * time.Minute`; `Available: d.available` | `serverInstall()`, `server_install.go`, 15 |
| `derp.binary.update` | compensable task, `DefineTask` with an `Undoer` and a `Recoverer` | agent | lock `derp`; `LongRunning`; `ApplyTimeout: 15 * time.Minute`; `Available: d.available` | `binaryUpdate()`, `binary_update.go` (Task 24 adds `binary_update_recover.go`), 16 |
| `derp.server.status` | read, `DefineRead` | agent | — | `serverStatus()`, `server_status.go`, 17 |
| `derp.node.probe` | read, `DefineRead` | agent | — | `nodeProbe()`, `node_probe.go`, 18 |
| `derp.server.uninstall` | compensable, `DefineMutating`, `CompensatedBy: "derp.server.install"` | agent | lock `derp`; `Converges: true`; `ApplyTimeout: 45 * time.Second` | `serverUninstall()`, `server_uninstall.go`, 19 |
| `derp.cert.renew` | compensable, `DefineMutating` with an `Undoer` | agent | lock `derp`; `ApplyTimeout: 45 * time.Second`; `Available: d.available` | `certRenew()`, `cert_renew.go`, 20 |
| `derp.server.deploy`, `derp.server.upgrade` | composite, `DefineComposite`, `Unwind: core.UnwindReverse` | controller | — | `serverDeploy()`, `serverUpgrade()`, `composite_deploy.go`, 21 |
| `derp.region.set`, `derp.region.remove`, `derp.cert.rotate` | composite, `DefineComposite`, `Unwind: core.UnwindReverse` | controller | — | `regionSet()`, `regionRemove()`, `certRotate()`, `composite_region.go`, 22 |
| `derp.server.remove` | composite, `DefineComposite`, `Unwind: core.UnwindForward` | controller | — | `serverRemove()`, `composite_remove.go`, 23 |
| `derp.server.probe` | read, `DefineRead` | controller | — | `serverProbe()`, `server_probe.go`, 25 |
| `derp.server.list`; service `derp.servers` | read, `DefineRead`; `ext.Service` through `module.Controller` | controller | service every `servicePeriod` | `serverList()`, `servers.go`, 26 (`func (m module) Controller(deps ext.Deps) ([]ext.Route, []ext.Service, error)` in the same file) |
| `derp.region.edits` | read, `DefineInternalRead`, `Internal: true` | controller | — | `regionEdits()`, `region_edits.go`, 27 |
| `derp.fallback.check` | read, `DefineInternalRead`, `Internal: true` | controller | — | `fallbackCheck()`, `fallback_check.go`, 28 |

### DERP shared helpers (from Tasks 17, 18, 25)

```go
// Task 17, server_status.go: the controller-side read of a host's derp.server.status/v1 through
// env.Agents.Agent(ctx, hostID).Read(ctx, env.Principal, core.ActionRef{Name: "derp.server.status", Version: 1}, ...).
func readStatus(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, hostID string, whois []string) (ServerStatus, error)
// Task 18, node_probe.go: the same for derp.node.probe/v1.
func readNodeProbe(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, hostID string, in NodeProbeInput) (NodeProbe, error)
// Task 18, node_probe.go: probeTarget and STUN address of a server; letsencrypt resolves HostName (first address,
// IPv4 preferred) through d.d.resolve; ip mode uses HostName. An unresolvable name is an error naming it.
func (d dp) target(ctx context.Context, s ServerStatus) (probeTarget, netip.AddrPort, error)
// Task 18, node_probe.go: one TLS and one STUN measurement against a target, as ProbeResults.
func (d dp) probeOnce(ctx context.Context, t probeTarget, stunAddr netip.AddrPort) (tls, stun ProbeResult)
// Task 25, server_probe.go: derp.server.probe's whole read, which Tasks 27 and 28 call.
func (d dp) probeServer(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in ServerProbeInput) (ServerProbe, error)
// Task 25, server_probe.go: ready when the controller's TLS and STUN are OK and at least one agent source other than
// the DERP host has TLS and STUN OK; with no agent source at all it is ready from the controller alone with the
// warning "no agent other than the DERP host probed <hostName>; only the controller's probe backs the region".
// reason names the first failing source when not ready.
func probeReady(p ServerProbe) (ready bool, reason string, warnings []string)
```

`derp.server.probe` reads `readStatus(HostID, whois: the nodes' addresses)`: a server that is not installed is `derp.not_ready` ("no DERP server is installed on <host>"); its nodes are the tailnet's devices (first address each) when `d.d.connected` is true, else the agents' `tailscale.status` selves; `Preferred` and `Observed` come from each agent's `tailscale.status` resource (`preferredDerp`; a peer with `connection` "derp" and `region` equal to the region code); `RegionID` and `RegionCode` come from `loadServerRecord`.

### DERP install, update and renewal effects (Tasks 15, 16, 19, 20, 24)

`derp.server.install` plans: `CheckConfig`; refuses an existing `derp_server` row (`request.invalid_input`, "a DERP server is already installed on this host"), a listening socket on 80/tcp, 443/tcp or 3478/udp (`derp.port_in_use`, naming each port and its process), and an existing user, binary, unit file or service directory that `env.Files.Owns` does not record as the module's (`request.invalid_input`, naming it); `VerifyClients` false adds the diff line `! client verification is off: any node that knows the region can relay through it`. Its task journals, each before its host change, create effects on `derp.user:darkpanel-derp` (`platform.NewOwnership(env.DB).Record` of `{ServiceUser, derp, user}` before `useradd --system --no-create-home --shell /usr/sbin/nologin darkpanel-derp`), `derp.file:/usr/local/lib/darkpanel/derper` (the fetched binary, `d.d.fetch(ctx, d.d.version, artifact.DerperVersion, d.d.arch)`, written mode 0755), `derp.file:/etc/systemd/system/darkpanel-derper.service` (mode 0644), `derp.dir:/var/lib/darkpanel/derp` (`EnsureServiceDir`), `derp.unit:darkpanel-derper.service` (`Reload`, `Enable`, `Start`; Post `{"enabled": true, "active": true}`) and `derp.server:this` (the `derp_server` row); in ip mode it waits up to `certWait` for the certificate files and stores `Pin` and `NotAfter`; the unit not active within `certWait` is `derp.verify_failed`. Undo runs in reverse journal order (delete the row; stop and disable the unit; `RemoveServiceDir`; remove the unit file and reload; remove the binary; `userdel` and `Forget`), each only when the matching effect was made.

`derp.server.uninstall` plans the same removals for what exists (an empty diff when nothing is installed) and applies them with delete effects, `Converges`; a stopped or absent unit, binary, directory or user counts as removed.

`derp.binary.update` journals one modify effect `{Resource: "derp.binary:derper", Local: true, InverseLocal: true, Before: {State: {"version": <from>, "sha256": <from sha>}}, After: {State: {"version": <to>, "sha256": <to sha>}}}` before phase `staged`; phases: `staged` (fetched and written to `BinaryNewPath`), `kept` (`Rename(BinaryPath, BinaryPrevPath)`), `swapped` (`Rename(BinaryNewPath, BinaryPath)`), `restarted` (`Restart(UnitName)`), `done` (health within `healthWindow` on `env.Clock`: the unit active, `tlsProbe` of `/derp/probe` at `127.0.0.1`, `stun` at `127.0.0.1:3478`, and tailscaled answering `Status`; then `setLocalBinary`, `Made`). It refuses a version other than the agent's own `artifact.DerperVersion` (`request.invalid_input`, field `/version`, "this agent's darkpanel release carries derper <v>; update the agent first") and a host without a server (`derp.not_ready`). A failed check runs `rollback`, then answers `derp.verify_failed`:

```go
// rollback restores derper.prev: phase rolling_back; when the phase reached kept, Rename(BinaryPrevPath, BinaryPath)
// (which replaces the new binary), Restart, and the unit active within healthWindow; then Remove(BinaryNewPath) when
// present; it ends in rolled_back (the effect Skipped), or failed with the first failing step's error, which it returns.
func (h binaryUpdateHandler) rollback(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, u updateRow) error
```

`binary_update.go` declares `type binaryUpdateHandler struct{ d dp }` (value receivers) holding `Plan`, `Task`, `Verify` and `rollback`. Task 24 writes `Observe` (`{"version": <derp_server's derper_version>, "sha256": <SHA-256 of BinaryPath>}`, `Matches` 1, `Exclusive` true), `Inverse` (loads the update by `e.ChangeID`; `done`: renames prev back exactly as `rollback` does), and `Recover` by phase: none or `staged` → remove `BinaryNewPath`, `RecoveryRolledBack`; `done` → `RecoveryCompleted` with the `BinaryUpdated` output; `rolled_back` → `RecoveryRolledBack`; `failed` → `RecoveryNeedsRepair` with its result; `kept`, `swapped`, `restarted`, `rolling_back` → `rollback`, then `RecoveryRolledBack`, or `RecoveryNeedsRepair` with its error.

`derp.cert.renew` (ip mode only; letsencrypt is `request.invalid_input`, "derper renews a Let's Encrypt certificate itself") journals a modify effect `{Resource: "derp.cert:<ip>", Local: true, InverseLocal: true, Before: {State: {"certName": <pin>, "notAfter": <time>}}}`, then `RenameIn(StateDir, "certs/<ip>.crt", "certs/<ip>.crt.prev")` and the same for `.key`, `Restart`, waits up to `certWait` for the new files, and `setLocalCert`; its undo renames the two `.prev` files back over the new ones, restarts and checks the pin equals `Before`.

### DERP composites (from Tasks 21–23)

Steps (`core.Step`; `Host` the DERP host for agent actions, `""` for controller ones; each step `After` the one before it):

- Every input-derived member reaches a step through a `${input.<member>}` reference, never spliced into the template (the engine reads any `${` as a reference). A composite plan carries no diff line of its own, so `derp.server.upgrade` and `derp.cert.rotate` state their window in their `Summary` ("Pin tailscaled, then replace derper; between the two steps the versions differ and the region may refuse clients." and "Renew the IP certificate, then publish its pin; between the two steps clients holding the old pin cannot use the region."), which the change dialog shows.
- `derp.server.deploy`: `tailscaled` (`tailscale.package.pin/v1` `{"version": artifact.PinVersion()}`), `install` (`derp.server.install/v1` `{mode, hostName, verifyClients}`), `ports` (`firewall.rules.apply/v1` `{"label": "derp", "add": server.PortRules(hostId)}`). Output from `install`.
- `derp.server.upgrade`: `fallback` (`derp.fallback.check/v1` `{hostId, regionId}`; only when `loadServerRecord`'s region id is not 0), `tailscaled` (pin `{"version": artifact.PinVersion()}`), `binary` (`derp.binary.update/v1` `{"version": artifact.DerperVersion}`). No `derp_servers` row: `derp.not_ready` ("no DERP server is known on <host>").
- `derp.cert.rotate`: `fallback` (only with a region), `cert` (`derp.cert.renew/v1` `{}`), `edits` (`derp.region.edits/v1` `{"op": "rotate", hostId, regionId, "certName": "${steps.cert.output.certName}"}`; only with a region), `region` (`tailnet.policy.patch/v1` `{"edits": "${steps.edits.output.edits}"}`; only with a region).
- `derp.region.set`: `edits` (`{"op": "set", hostId, regionId, regionCode, regionName, omitDefaultRegions, acknowledge}`), `region` (patch with `${steps.edits.output.edits}`).
- `derp.region.remove`: `edits` (`{"op": "remove", regionId}`), `region`.
- `derp.server.remove` (forward): `edits` and `region` (only when `tailnet.Connected` and the row's region id is not 0), `uninstall` (`derp.server.uninstall/v1` `{}`), `unpin` (`tailscale.package.unpin/v1` `{}`), `ports` (`firewall.rules.apply/v1` `{"delete": [the three server.RuleID values]}`).

### DERP region edits (from Task 27)

`derp.region.edits` computes, never writes:

- `set`: refuses `derp.not_ready` unless the newest `core_changes` row with action `derp.server.deploy` whose `input_json` has `hostId` equal to `HostID` is in state `completed`, and `probeReady(probeServer({hostId}))` is ready (its warnings become the output's); refuses `derp.region_taken` when the derpMap holds `RegionID` with a node whose `HostName` differs from the server's; refuses `request.invalid_input` (field `/acknowledge`) when `OmitDefaultRegions` is asked, no other custom region in the map passes `probeReady`, and `Acknowledge` is not exactly `nodes will relay only through <RegionCode>`; refuses `derp.not_ready` when the server's node has no tag ("the DERP host's node has no tag; the visibility grant needs one"). Edits: the region object `{"RegionID": id, "RegionCode": code, "RegionName": name, "Nodes": [{"Name": "<id>a", "RegionID": id, "HostName": <server hostName>, "DERPPort": 443, "STUNPort": 3478}]}` (ip mode adds `"IPv4"` or `"IPv6"` with the address and `"CertName"` with the pin) set at `/derpMap` (as `{"Regions": {"<id>": region}}`, plus `"OmitDefaultRegions": true` when asked) when the policy has no derpMap, at `/derpMap/Regions` (as `{"<id>": region}`) when the derpMap has no Regions, else at `/derpMap/Regions/<id>` (and `/derpMap/OmitDefaultRegions` set when asked and different); then the visibility grant added with `addItem` at `/grants` (or `set` of `/grants` to `[grant]` when absent) unless an equal item is present. `visibilityGrant` is `{"src": ["*"], "dst": [<the first of the server's tags, sorted>], "ip": ["tcp:443"]}` (provisional; the contract-check gate replaces it with Task 1's recorded grant).
- `remove`: `delete` of `/derpMap/Regions/<id>` when present; when no custom region remains and `OmitDefaultRegions` is set, also `set` of `/derpMap/OmitDefaultRegions` to `false`; when no custom region remains, the visibility grant removed with `removeItem`. Region absent: no edits.
- `rotate`: `set` of `/derpMap/Regions/<id>` with the policy's region object, the node whose `HostName` is the server's given `CertName`. Region absent: no edits.

### DERP fallback check (from Task 28)

`derp.fallback.check` answers the first path that holds, else refuses `derp.no_fallback` ("the DERP host's link depends on region <id> and no other region is reachable; add a second region or clear omitDefaultRegions"): `link` when `readStatus(HostID).LinkViaTailnet` is false; `default-regions` when the derpMap is absent or `OmitDefaultRegions` is false; `region:<id>` for the first other custom region (ascending) whose `derp_servers` row's host passes `probeReady(probeServer({hostId: that host}))`.

### DERP servers service and list (from Task 26)

Service `derp.servers` (actor `system:derp.servers`, principal `core.Principal{Actor: "system:derp.servers", Scope: core.ScopeAdmin}`): a pass at start, then every `servicePeriod` armed after each pass (the `cloudflare.hostnames` shape). A pass reads every `core_hosts` row; per host `readStatus(host, nil)`: installed → `upsertServerRecord` with its fields and `checked_at` now; not installed → `deleteServerRecord`; a read error → the existing row keeps its fields with `error` set (no row: nothing). When `d.d.connected` is true, the region of each row is `parseDERPMap(policy).regionOf(hostName)` (else id 0). The read `derp.server.list` (a controller read, because the dispatcher sends every resource read to an agent: `internal/controller/dispatch/dispatch.go:182-190`; the module declares no resource) answers `Servers` from `loadServerRecords` joined with `core_hosts` names; health: `broken` when the unit is inactive or the ip certificate's `NotAfter` has passed; else `degraded` when `NotAfter` is within `expiryWarning`, `checked_at` is older than `staleAfter`, or `error` is set; else `ok`; one reason sentence per condition.

### DERP test host (from Task 12)

`internal/modules/derp/testhost_test.go`, used by every module-root test task:

```go
type dpHost struct {
	t        *testing.T
	local    *dpLocal              // the fake local API
	units    *dpUnits              // records calls; State answers from a map
	files    map[string][]byte     // what Files wrote, by path (service-dir entries as dir + "/" + name)
	reads    map[string][]byte     // what deps.readFile answers before files; a missing path is fs.ErrNotExist
	ran      [][]string            // every exec vector run: path then args
	scripts  map[string][]dpRun    // queued answers per "path arg0 arg1 ...", consumed in order; unscripted runs answer exit 0
	db       *store.DB             // agent database with the core and derp agent migrations
	ctlDB    *store.DB             // controller database with the core and derp controller migrations
	journal  *dpJournal            // an in-memory core.EffectJournal
	clock    *coretest.FakeClock
	owned    map[string]bool       // paths Files and the ownership store recorded as the module's
	users    map[string][2]int     // lookupUser answers (uid, gid); useradd adds ServiceUser, userdel removes it
	sockets  []netstat.Socket
	link     bool                  // linkViaTailnet's answer
	fetchBin []byte                // fetch's answer (sha computed); fetchErr overrides
	fetchErr error
	fetches  []string              // "dpVersion want arch" per call
	tls      map[string]dpProbe    // tlsProbe answers keyed by Address, else HostName; a missing key errors
	stun     map[netip.AddrPort]dpProbe
	resolved map[string][]netip.Addr
	agents   map[string]*dpAgent   // controller side: host id -> fake agent
	policy   tailnet.Policy        // policyErr overrides
	policyErr error
	devices  []tailnet.Device
	tailnet  bool                  // connected's answer
}
type dpRun struct{ res core.CmdResult; err error }
type dpProbe struct{ d time.Duration; err error }
type dpLocal struct {
	Status  *tslocal.Status
	WhoIs   map[netip.Addr]*tslocal.WhoIs // a missing address is tslocal's not-found error
	Regions map[string]*tslocal.DERPRegionReport
	Errs    map[string]error // by method name: "Status", "WhoIs", "DebugDERPRegion"
}
type dpUnits struct {
	States map[string]core.UnitState
	Calls  []string // "reload", "enable <unit>", "disable <unit>", "start <unit>", "stop <unit>", "restart <unit>"
}
type dpAgent struct {
	Reads     map[string]func(input json.RawMessage) (any, error) // by "<name>/v<version>"
	Resources map[string]any                                      // by resource name
	Calls     []string                                            // "<name>/v<version> <input JSON>" per Read
}
func newDPHost(t *testing.T) *dpHost      // a logged-in tailscaled (BackendState Running, tags ["tag:derp"], 100.64.0.10) on amd64, darkpanel version "1.4.0"
func (h *dpHost) dp() dp                  // a dp whose deps all point at the host's fakes
func (h *dpHost) env(changeID string) core.Env    // agent side: HostID "h1", db, Exec, Files, Units, Clock, Effects (when changeID != "")
func (h *dpHost) ctlEnv(changeID string) core.Env // controller side: ctlDB, Agents (the dpAgents through coretest.FakeAgent), Clock, Sealer, Effects
func (h *dpHost) script(vector string, runs ...dpRun)
func (h *dpHost) agent(hostID string) *dpAgent    // creates the fake agent on first use
func (h *dpHost) addHost(id, name string, local bool) // a core_hosts row in ctlDB
func (h *dpHost) addChange(action, state string, input any) string // a core_plans and core_changes row in ctlDB; returns the change id
```

`dpHost` implements `core.Runner` (checking every vector against the production exec allowlist exactly as `internal/modules/tailscale/testhost_test.go` does) and `core.FileWriter` (refusing every path `ownedPaths` does not declare with that kind); `dpUnits` implements `core.Units`, refusing every unit other than `darkpanel-derper.service`. Go refuses a field and a method of one name, so `dpLocal` does not implement `localAPI` itself: Task 12 adds the adapter `thLocal{*dpLocal}`, which `h.dp()` hands to the deps; later tasks read and write the fields and never name the adapter.

### Web: derp (Tasks 6, 10, 11, 14)

`web/src/modules/derp/types.ts` (Task 6) exports `DERP_MODULE = "derp"`, `DERP_ACTION_VERSION = 1`, `derpKey(name: "servers" | "probe", hostId?: string): string[]`, the TypeScript mirrors `DerpServers`, `DerpServerView`, `ServerProbe`, `ProbeSource`, `ProbeResult`, `NodeUse`, `RegionDiagnostics`, `ServerStatus` of Contracts "DERP wire shapes" (same JSON names), and the guards `isDerpServers(v: unknown): v is DerpServers` and `isServerProbe(v: unknown): v is ServerProbe`; `fixtures.ts` exports `serversFixture` (two servers: an ip-mode server in region 900, health ok; a letsencrypt server without a region, health degraded with one reason) and `probeFixture` (controller and one agent OK, one node visible and observed, one not visible); `derp-change.tsx` exports `DerpChange({action, title, input, onClose})`, the tailnet module's `TailnetChange` shape with `DERP_*` constants. Task 10 writes `servers-table.tsx` (`ServersTable({servers, onAction})`, `onAction(action: "probe" | "region" | "upgrade" | "rotate" | "remove", server: DerpServerView)`, showing health with its reasons, the version against `availableVersion`, and the certificate expiry) and `probe-panel.tsx` (`ProbePanel({hostId})`, reading `derp.server.probe/v1` and rendering sources, region diagnostics labelled "connectivity diagnostics", nodes with visibility and preferred versus observed use). Task 11 writes `deploy-dialog.tsx` (`DeployDialog({onClose})`: host select from the hosts query, mode, host name or public IP, a "Verify clients" checkbox on by default whose clearing shows a warning; applies `derp.server.deploy` through `DerpChange`) and `region-dialog.tsx` (`RegionDialog({server, probe, onClose})`: region id 900–999, code, name, "Omit default regions" with the typed acknowledgement field, a warning when `probe.warnings` is not empty; applies `derp.region.set`). Task 14 writes `derp-page.tsx` (`DerpPage()`: the `derp.server.list/v1` read (host-less, as the tailnet pages read controller actions), `ServersTable`, the deploy button, and per action the probe panel, the region dialog, or `DerpChange` for `derp.server.upgrade`, `derp.cert.rotate`, `derp.server.remove`, `derp.region.remove`) and replaces `global.tsx` (default export rendering `DerpPage`). Reads and resources use the helpers of `web/src/api/client.ts` as `web/src/modules/tailnet/devices-page.tsx` uses them.

### Harness M5 DERP (from Tasks 13, 30)

`test/harness/m5_derp_test.go` (Task 13): `TestM5DERP`, behind the flag `-m5d` with its own work directory and the flag `-derper <path>` (default `bin/harness/` + `artifact.BinaryName(artifact.DerperVersion, "amd64")`), boots one enrolled agent VM through the wave 0 lab helpers (as `m5Lab` does), installs derper over the VM's SSH exactly as the module would (`useradd` vector, the binary, `server.UnitFile(server.Config{Mode: ip, HostName: <the VM's address>, VerifyClients: false})`, `systemctl daemon-reload`, `enable --now`), and checks: the unit active; derper's process running as `darkpanel-derp`; listeners on 80/tcp, 443/tcp and 3478/udp; `GET https://<VM>/derp/probe` from the runner answering 200 with the served leaf's `server.Pin` equal to the pin of `<certdir>/<ip>.crt` on the VM; a STUN binding through `stun.Probe` from the runner. `VerifyClients: false` because the harness tailscaled is logged out (Assumptions). Task 30 adds to `.github/workflows/harness.yml`: in the `build` job, an `actions/cache` step keyed on `hashFiles('release/derper.version')` for `bin/harness/derper-*` and `go run ./release/releasetool derper 0.0.0 bin/harness -arch amd64` when the cache missed; a new job `vm-m5d`, shaped as `vm-m5` (needs `build`, the same OS matrix, KVM and QEMU setup, the downloaded artifacts and the harness cache), with `timeout-minutes: 60` and one step running `go test -tags harness -timeout 40m -v -run '^TestM5DERP$' ./test/harness/ -args -m5d` with the same `-cache` argument; `vm-m5` is unchanged, since its 100-minute limit is already spent by the node and pin steps.

## Assumptions (evidence)

- The `derp` module is a registered skeleton: `internal/modules/derp/derp.go` declares only `ID`, `module`, `Module()` and an empty `Manifest()`, with `TestManifestIsASkeleton`; `internal/cli/derp.go` returns no command; `web/src/modules/derp/global.tsx` renders nothing; `registry.ts` already lists the DERP global page (read 2026-11-01). Every action and resource gets a generated CLI command, so no CLI file changes.
- The exec allowlist already permits `useradd --system --no-create-home --shell /usr/sbin/nologin darkpanel-derp` and `userdel darkpanel-derp` (`internal/platform/exec_rules.go:30,85-86,119-131`, read 2026-11-01); the file provider supports kind `service-dir` with `EnsureServiceDir`, `RenameIn` inside a declared subdirectory and the default name pattern `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,127}$`, which admits `<ip>.crt.prev` (`internal/platform/files_servicedir.go:24-34,107-111`); `Rename` replaces its target within one directory (`files.go:444`); the error codes `derp.region_taken`, `derp.port_in_use`, `derp.not_ready`, `derp.no_fallback`, `derp.verify_failed` exist (`internal/core/errcode/errcode.go:114-117,132`).
- `internal/update` verifies `checksums.txt` with `VerifyChecksums` and finds an asset's line with `checksumFor`; `Download` handles only the tarball (`internal/update/download.go:54-112,115`), so Task 3 adds `DownloadAsset` beside it. The agent's release base URL and keys are `trustedkeys.BaseURL()` and `trustedkeys.Keys()` (`cmd/darkpanel/agent.go:175`, `internal/update/trustedkeys/keys.go:22,30`); its version is `buildinfo.Version` ("dev" in a development build, `internal/buildinfo/buildinfo.go`).
- `release/package.sh` writes `checksums.txt` over every file of the release directory except itself and `*.minisig` (`release/package.sh:45-46`), and `make release` recreates that directory (`Makefile:123-128`), so `releasetool derper` runs after `make release` and rewrites `checksums.txt` before the `sign` job signs it (`.github/workflows/release.yml:99`). Whether the build attestation step covers the new files is unverified — Task 8 reads `release.yml` and extends the attested subject list if it names files explicitly.
- `tailnet.policy.get`'s and `tailnet.device.list`'s logic is reachable only through `tn` methods (`internal/modules/tailnet/policy.go:23`, `devices.go:91`, `account.go:42`), so Task 4 exports thin wrappers; `policy.Edit` is `{op, path, value}` with ops `set`, `delete`, `addItem`, `removeItem` (`internal/modules/tailnet/policy/policy.go:19-23`). Whether `policy.Apply` accepts `/derpMap/Regions/900` as an object member and whether `tailnet.policy.patch` with no edits plans an empty diff: unverified — Task 4 verifies both and fixes the policy files if not.
- Controller-side actions reach agents through `env.Agents.Agent(ctx, hostID)` (`core.HostDirectory`, `internal/core/contract.go:186`; `AgentClient.Read` and `Resource`, `contract.go:1020-1023`), and `coretest.FakeAgent` takes per-method functions (`internal/core/coretest/agent.go:20`), which the test host wraps.
- `firewall.rules.apply` takes `{label, add: []model.Rule, delete: []string}`, accepts rule ids matching `^[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26}$`, and declares `Converges` (`internal/modules/firewall/actions_rules.go:20-35,118`); `tailscale.package.pin` takes `{version}` and a pin over an existing pin moves the hold (`internal/modules/tailscale/wire.go:142`, `package_pin.go`); `tailscale.package.unpin` with no active pin plans an empty diff (wave 4 ruling).
- Ruling: `derp.server.install` records the `darkpanel-derp` user through `platform.NewOwnership(env.DB).Record` before `useradd` and its undo and `derp.server.uninstall` call `Forget` after `userdel`; no `FileWriter` method records a user (`internal/core/contract.go:674-692`; `internal/platform/ownership.go:21-40`), and the core uninstall removes every recorded user.
- Ruling: a composite's `Steps` reads only its input and the controller database (`internal/core/contract.go:303-311`), so readiness, a taken region, the omit-default check, the edits and the fallback run in leading Internal read steps (`DefineInternalRead`, `internal/core/define.go:222`), which the engine runs at apply; a whole-string `${steps.<id>.output.<field>}` reference yields any JSON value, a read step's output included. A step with a reference shows as "planned after" in the composite's preview, so a region diff appears at apply, not in the preview.
- Ruling: `derp.region.set`'s "deploy confirmed" is the newest `derp.server.deploy` change for the host in state `completed`: a composite whose last step is revertible waits in `awaiting_confirmation` and moves to `completed` once confirmed (`internal/core/lifecycle/composite_run.go:917,995`).
- Ruling: the controller records no agent address (`core_hosts` holds `id`, `name`, `is_local`, `created_at`; `internal/core/store/migrations/controller/0001_core.sql:9-14`), so the agent's link via the tailnet is answered by the agent itself (`ServerStatus.LinkViaTailnet`, from the saved controller address as `internal/modules/tailscale/pin_session.go` reads it).
- Ruling: the exec allowlist has no journal reader, so `derp.binary.update`'s health check replaces "derper's log shows that it reached tailscaled" (spec 4.3) with "tailscaled answers `Status`" next to the unit, TLS and STUN checks; Task 1 records this deviation in spec 4.3.
- Ruling: a plan's diff cannot carry a warning computed in a read step, so the "fleet with no other agent" warning of `derp.region.set` is shown by the web region dialog from `derp.server.probe`'s warnings, and the edits read step returns it in its output.
- Ruling: the derper build is a `releasetool` subcommand called from `release.yml`, because `Makefile` is not in wave 5's ownership row (spec "Parallel decomposition").
- Ruling: the agent takes derper only from its own Darkpanel version's release, found in the release index with its channel (`update.Release{Version, Channel}`); a version absent from the index, including a development build, cannot install DERP (`update.not_found`).
- Ruling: `derp.server.upgrade` upgrades to the controller's `artifact.DerperVersion`, which the agent refuses unless its own build carries the same version, so an upgrade follows the agents' Darkpanel update.
- The harness VMs run tailscaled 1.104.1 logged out (`test/harness/provision.go`, `images.json`), so `derp.server.install` (available only on the tailnet) cannot run there; `TestM5DERP` installs derper over SSH with `server.UnitFile` and `VerifyClients: false` and checks the user, ports, probe, STUN and pin. Whether derper with `-certmode manual` and an IP `-hostname` generates its certificate on a VM without a tailnet: unverified — Task 1 reads it from the source and Task 13's run proves it.
- `release/derper.version` is `v1.104.1`, the version the harness tailscaled runs, so the pin `1.104.1` matches the harness images (`test/harness/images.json`). Whether `tailscale.com/cmd/derper@v1.104.1` builds with `CGO_ENABLED=0` under this repository's Go 1.26 toolchain: unverified — Task 1 builds it into `$TMPDIR`.
- The visibility grant `{"src": ["*"], "dst": [<tag>], "ip": ["tcp:443"]}`, the region node fields and `-verify-clients` for a non-root user through tailscaled's socket are provisional: unverified — Task 1 settles each from Tailscale's documentation and source at v1.104.1 and records them in spec 4.3 (contract-check gate).
- `plan-lint` may warn of a placeholder in Task 1: a flagged line is Task 1's own check command, which greps the spec for `TBD` and `TODO`; it is not a placeholder.
- Task 7's six golden rule ids (`TestSvRuleID`, `TestSvPortRules`) were recomputed independently on 2026-11-02 with a scratch Go program (`crypto/sha256` over `"darkpanel/derp/" + hostID + "/" + port`, `encoding/base32` with the alphabet `0123456789ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTVWXYZ` and no padding, the first 25 characters after a `"0"`); all six match.
- Ruling: spec 4.3's "the harness builds them with test keys and caches the build" is met only in part. The harness cannot run `derp.server.install` (its tailscaled is logged out), so it caches an unsigned amd64 derper and `TestM5DERP` installs it over SSH (Tasks 13, 30). The signed path (`checksums.txt` and its signature, then `derper.json`, then the binary) is covered by Task 3's and Task 9's unit tests against a release signed with test keys on `httptest`.
- Scoring follows the programme's convention (plans w1-w4): the files axis counts kinds of file (Go source, Go test, SQL, TypeScript, shell, workflow YAML, generated schema), not instances, so a single-package task with many files scores files 1.
