- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Append to `release/releasetool/releasetool_test.go`. Add the imports `context`, `crypto/sha256`, `encoding/hex`, `errors`, `fmt`, `sort` and `github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/derp/artifact` to its import block (it already imports `bytes`, `os`, `path/filepath`, `strings` and `testing`); keep the block gofmt-sorted.

```go
// drCall is one call of the fake builder.
type drCall struct{ version, arch, dst string }

// drFake returns a builder that writes "derper <arch>" to dst, and the calls it received. An arch in failArch makes it
// fail after the other arch was built.
func drFake(failArch string) (derperBuilder, *[]drCall) {
	var calls []drCall
	build := func(_ context.Context, version, arch, dst string) error {
		calls = append(calls, drCall{version, arch, dst})
		if arch == failArch {
			return errors.New("go build failed")
		}
		return os.WriteFile(dst, []byte("derper "+arch), 0o755) //nolint:gosec // G306: a test binary
	}
	return build, &calls
}

func drSum(b []byte) string {
	s := sha256.Sum256(b)
	return hex.EncodeToString(s[:])
}

func drName(arch string) string { return artifact.BinaryName(artifact.DerperVersion, arch) }

func drRead(t *testing.T, path string) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	b, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // G304: a path under the test's temporary directory
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return b
}

func drMissing(t *testing.T, path string) {
	t.Helper()
	if _, err := os.Stat(path); !errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		t.Errorf("%s exists (stat error %v), want it absent", path, err)
	}
}

func TestDrBuildsEveryArchAndWritesTheManifest(t *testing.T) {
	out := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "out")
	build, calls := drFake("")
	var stdout bytes.Buffer
	if err := runDerper(context.Background(), []string{"1.4.0", out}, &stdout, build); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("runDerper: %v", err)
	}
	wantCalls := []drCall{
		{artifact.DerperVersion, "amd64", filepath.Join(out, drName("amd64"))},
		{artifact.DerperVersion, "arm64", filepath.Join(out, drName("arm64"))},
	}
	if len(*calls) != len(wantCalls) || (*calls)[0] != wantCalls[0] || (*calls)[1] != wantCalls[1] {
		t.Errorf("builder calls %+v, want %+v", *calls, wantCalls)
	}
	m := artifact.Manifest{
		DarkpanelVersion: "1.4.0",
		DerperVersion:    artifact.DerperVersion,
		Artifacts:        map[string]string{"amd64": drSum([]byte("derper amd64")), "arm64": drSum([]byte("derper arm64"))},
	}
	want, err := m.Encode()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got := drRead(t, filepath.Join(out, artifact.ManifestName))
	if !bytes.Equal(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("derper.json =\n%s\nwant\n%s", got, want)
	}
	if parsed, err := artifact.ParseManifest(got); err != nil || parsed.Artifacts["amd64"] != m.Artifacts["amd64"] {
		t.Errorf("ParseManifest = %+v, %v", parsed, err)
	}
	wantOut := fmt.Sprintf("%s  %s\n%s  %s\n", m.Artifacts["amd64"], drName("amd64"), m.Artifacts["arm64"], drName("arm64"))
	if stdout.String() != wantOut {
		t.Errorf("stdout = %q, want %q", stdout.String(), wantOut)
	}
	drMissing(t, filepath.Join(out, "checksums.txt"))
}

func TestDrOneArchWritesNoManifest(t *testing.T) {
	out := t.TempDir()
	build, calls := drFake("")
	var stdout bytes.Buffer
	if err := runDerper(context.Background(), []string{"1.4.0", out, "-arch", "arm64"}, &stdout, build); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("runDerper: %v", err)
	}
	if len(*calls) != 1 || (*calls)[0].arch != "arm64" {
		t.Errorf("builder calls %+v, want one call for arm64", *calls)
	}
	if string(drRead(t, filepath.Join(out, drName("arm64")))) != "derper arm64" {
		t.Error("the arm64 binary was not written to the output directory")
	}
	drMissing(t, filepath.Join(out, drName("amd64")))
	drMissing(t, filepath.Join(out, artifact.ManifestName))
	if want := drSum([]byte("derper arm64")) + "  " + drName("arm64") + "\n"; stdout.String() != want {
		t.Errorf("stdout = %q, want %q", stdout.String(), want)
	}
}

func TestDrRewritesChecksumsAsPackageShWritesThem(t *testing.T) {
	out := t.TempDir()
	files := map[string]string{
		"ZZ-first.txt":                           "upper case sorts before lower case in byte order",
		"darkpanel_1.4.0.cdx.json":               "{}",
		"darkpanel_1.4.0_linux_amd64.tar.gz":     "tarball",
		"install.sh":                             "#!/bin/sh\n",
		"checksums.txt":                          "stale\n",
		"checksums.txt.0123456789ABCDEF.minisig": "signature",
	}
	for name, body := range files {
		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(out, name), []byte(body), 0o644); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // G306: test files
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	if err := os.Mkdir(filepath.Join(out, "subdir"), 0o755); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // G301: a test directory
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(out, "subdir", "inner.txt"), []byte("inner"), 0o644); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // G306: test files
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	build, _ := drFake("")
	if err := runDerper(context.Background(), []string{"1.4.0", out}, &bytes.Buffer{}, build); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("runDerper: %v", err)
	}

	bodies := map[string][]byte{
		"ZZ-first.txt":                       []byte(files["ZZ-first.txt"]),
		"darkpanel_1.4.0.cdx.json":           []byte("{}"),
		"darkpanel_1.4.0_linux_amd64.tar.gz": []byte("tarball"),
		"install.sh":                         []byte("#!/bin/sh\n"),
		drName("amd64"):                      []byte("derper amd64"),
		drName("arm64"):                      []byte("derper arm64"),
		artifact.ManifestName:                drRead(t, filepath.Join(out, artifact.ManifestName)),
	}
	names := make([]string, 0, len(bodies))
	for n := range bodies {
		names = append(names, n)
	}
	sort.Strings(names)
	var want strings.Builder
	for _, n := range names {
		fmt.Fprintf(&want, "%s  %s\n", drSum(bodies[n]), n)
	}
	if got := string(drRead(t, filepath.Join(out, "checksums.txt"))); got != want.String() {
		t.Errorf("checksums.txt =\n%s\nwant\n%s", got, want.String())
	}
	if want := names[0]; want != "ZZ-first.txt" {
		t.Fatalf("test setup: first name is %s", want)
	}
}

func TestDrFailedBuildLeavesTheOutputUntouched(t *testing.T) {
	out := t.TempDir()
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(out, "checksums.txt"), []byte("old\n"), 0o644); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // G306: test files
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	build, _ := drFake("arm64")
	err := runDerper(context.Background(), []string{"1.4.0", out}, &bytes.Buffer{}, build)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "arm64") {
		t.Fatalf("runDerper = %v, want an error naming arm64", err)
	}
	drMissing(t, filepath.Join(out, artifact.ManifestName))
	if got := string(drRead(t, filepath.Join(out, "checksums.txt"))); got != "old\n" {
		t.Errorf("checksums.txt = %q after a failed build, want it unchanged", got)
	}
}

func TestDrRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	out := t.TempDir()
	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		args  []string
		usage bool
	}{
		{"a tag instead of a version", []string{"v1.4.0", out}, false},
		{"a development version", []string{"dev", out}, false},
		{"an unknown architecture", []string{"1.4.0", out, "-arch", "riscv64"}, false},
		{"no output directory", []string{"1.4.0"}, true},
		{"no arguments", nil, true},
		{"an architecture flag without a value", []string{"1.4.0", out, "-arch"}, true},
		{"an unknown flag", []string{"1.4.0", out, "-march", "amd64"}, true},
		{"a trailing word", []string{"1.4.0", out, "-arch", "amd64", "extra"}, true},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		build, calls := drFake("")
		err := runDerper(context.Background(), tt.args, &bytes.Buffer{}, build)
		if err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: runDerper = nil, want an error", tt.name)
			continue
		}
		if tt.usage && !strings.HasPrefix(err.Error(), "usage:") {
			t.Errorf("%s: error %q, want the usage text", tt.name, err)
		}
		if len(*calls) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("%s: the builder was called %d times, want none", tt.name, len(*calls))
		}
	}
	entries, err := os.ReadDir(out)
	if err != nil || len(entries) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("a refused run wrote %v (read error %v), want an empty directory", entries, err)
	}
}

func TestDrRunDispatchesTheSubcommand(t *testing.T) {
	for _, args := range [][]string{{"derper"}, {"derper", "1.4.0"}, {"derper", "1.4.0", t.TempDir(), "-arch"}} {
		if err := run(args, strings.NewReader(""), &bytes.Buffer{}); err == nil || !strings.HasPrefix(err.Error(), "usage:") {
			t.Errorf("run(%q) = %v, want the usage error", args, err)
		}
	}
	if !strings.Contains(usage, "releasetool derper <darkpanel version> <out dir> [-arch amd64|arm64]") {
		t.Errorf("the usage text lacks the derper subcommand:\n%s", usage)
	}
}
```

The test prefix is `fe` (Contracts "Test prefixes"). The tests run an `httptest` server on `127.0.0.1` that serves a signed release in the production layout (`v<darkpanel version>/checksums.txt`, its signature, `derper.json` and the two binaries); nothing reaches the network.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `internal/modules/derp/artifact/fetch_test.go`:

```go
package artifact

import (
	"bytes"
	"cmp"
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"

	"aead.dev/minisign"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/update"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/update/trustedkeys"
)

// feDarkpanel is the darkpanel version every test release belongs to.
const feDarkpanel = "1.4.0"

type feSigner struct {
	key  trustedkeys.Key
	priv minisign.PrivateKey
}

func feNewSigner(t *testing.T) feSigner {
	t.Helper()
	pub, priv, err := minisign.GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return feSigner{key: trustedkeys.Key{ID: fmt.Sprintf("%016X", pub.ID()), PublicKey: pub.String()}, priv: priv}
}

// sign signs data in hashed mode with the trusted comment of a release's checksums.txt.
func (s feSigner) sign(data []byte, version, channel string) []byte {
	r := minisign.NewReader(bytes.NewReader(data))
	_, _ = io.Copy(io.Discard, r)
	return r.SignWithComments(s.priv, fmt.Sprintf("version=%s channel=%s purpose=checksums", version, channel), "signature from darkpanel test key "+s.key.ID)
}

func feSum(b []byte) string {
	sum := sha256.Sum256(b)
	return hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])
}

// feServer serves release files under URL as v<version>/<file>, records every path asked for, and can answer one
// path with an endless stream of zero bytes.
type feServer struct {
	URL     string
	srv     *httptest.Server
	mu      sync.Mutex
	files   map[string][]byte
	endless map[string]bool
	asked   []string
	sent    map[string]int64
}

func newFeServer(t *testing.T) *feServer {
	t.Helper()
	s := &feServer{files: map[string][]byte{}, endless: map[string]bool{}, sent: map[string]int64{}}
	s.srv = httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		name := strings.TrimPrefix(r.URL.Path, "/")
		s.mu.Lock()
		s.asked = append(s.asked, name)
		body, ok := s.files[name]
		endless := s.endless[name]
		s.mu.Unlock()
		switch {
		case endless:
			chunk := make([]byte, 1<<20)
			for sent := int64(0); sent < 200<<20; sent += int64(len(chunk)) {
				if _, err := w.Write(chunk); err != nil {
					return
				}
				s.mu.Lock()
				s.sent[name] += int64(len(chunk))
				s.mu.Unlock()
			}
		case !ok || r.Method != http.MethodGet:
			http.NotFound(w, r)
		default:
			_, _ = w.Write(body)
		}
	}))
	s.URL = s.srv.URL
	t.Cleanup(s.srv.Close)
	return s
}

func (s *feServer) put(name string, b []byte) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.files[name] = bytes.Clone(b)
}

func (s *feServer) get(name string) []byte {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	return bytes.Clone(s.files[name])
}

func (s *feServer) remove(name string) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	delete(s.files, name)
}

func (s *feServer) paths() []string {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	return slices.Clone(s.asked)
}

// sign rebuilds checksums.txt of release dp from the files now served under v<dp>/ and signs it for channel, so a
// test that changed a file and signs again has a release whose signed list matches what is served.
func (s *feServer) sign(signer feSigner, dp, channel string) {
	prefix := "v" + dp + "/"
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	var names []string
	for name := range s.files {
		if rest, ok := strings.CutPrefix(name, prefix); ok && !strings.HasPrefix(rest, "checksums.txt") {
			names = append(names, rest)
		}
	}
	slices.Sort(names)
	var sums bytes.Buffer
	for _, n := range names {
		fmt.Fprintf(&sums, "%s  %s\n", feSum(s.files[prefix+n]), n)
	}
	s.files[prefix+"checksums.txt"] = bytes.Clone(sums.Bytes())
	s.files[prefix+"checksums.txt."+signer.key.ID+".minisig"] = signer.sign(sums.Bytes(), dp, channel)
}

// fePublish serves release dp (stable) with derper version derper for every arch of Arches and returns each build.
func fePublish(t *testing.T, s *feServer, signer feSigner, dp, derper string) map[string][]byte {
	t.Helper()
	builds := map[string][]byte{}
	m := Manifest{DarkpanelVersion: dp, DerperVersion: derper, Artifacts: map[string]string{}}
	for _, arch := range Arches {
		bin := []byte("derper " + derper + " " + arch)
		builds[arch] = bin
		s.put("v"+dp+"/"+BinaryName(derper, arch), bin)
		m.Artifacts[arch] = feSum(bin)
	}
	enc, err := m.Encode()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	s.put("v"+dp+"/"+ManifestName, enc)
	s.sign(signer, dp, "stable")
	return builds
}

// feManifest is the manifest of release dp for derper, listing the hashes of builds.
func feManifest(t *testing.T, dp, derper string, builds map[string][]byte) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	m := Manifest{DarkpanelVersion: dp, DerperVersion: derper, Artifacts: map[string]string{}}
	for arch, bin := range builds {
		m.Artifacts[arch] = feSum(bin)
	}
	enc, err := m.Encode()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return enc
}

// feSource reads the index of one stable release, feDarkpanel, and trusts keys.
func feSource(s *feServer, keys ...trustedkeys.Key) Source {
	return Source{
		Client:  s.srv.Client(),
		BaseURL: s.URL,
		Keys:    keys,
		Releases: func(context.Context) ([]update.Release, error) {
			return []update.Release{{Version: "1.3.0", Channel: "stable"}, {Version: feDarkpanel, Channel: "stable"}}, nil
		},
	}
}

func feBinaryAsked(paths []string) bool {
	for _, p := range paths {
		if strings.Contains(p, "-linux-") {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

func TestFeFetch(t *testing.T) {
	signer, other := feNewSigner(t), feNewSigner(t)
	srv := newFeServer(t)
	builds := fePublish(t, srv, signer, feDarkpanel, DerperVersion)
	if bytes.Equal(builds["amd64"], builds["arm64"]) {
		t.Fatal("the test builds must differ")
	}
	src := feSource(srv, other.key, signer.key)

	bin, sha, err := Fetch(context.Background(), src, feDarkpanel, DerperVersion, "amd64")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Fetch amd64: %v", err)
	}
	if !bytes.Equal(bin, builds["amd64"]) || sha != feSum(builds["amd64"]) {
		t.Errorf("Fetch amd64 = %q, %s; want the amd64 build and its SHA-256", bin, sha)
	}
	for _, p := range srv.paths() {
		if !strings.HasPrefix(p, "v"+feDarkpanel+"/") {
			t.Errorf("asked for %s; the agent takes derper only from its own release", p)
		}
		if strings.Contains(p, "linux-arm64") {
			t.Errorf("asked for %s while fetching amd64", p)
		}
	}

	bin, sha, err = Fetch(context.Background(), src, feDarkpanel, DerperVersion, "arm64")
	if err != nil || !bytes.Equal(bin, builds["arm64"]) || sha != feSum(builds["arm64"]) {
		t.Errorf("Fetch arm64 = %q, %s, %v; want the arm64 build", bin, sha, err)
	}
	if len(sha) != 64 || sha != strings.ToLower(sha) {
		t.Errorf("sha %q is not 64 lower-case hex digits", sha)
	}
}

func TestFeFetchRefuses(t *testing.T) {
	signer, stranger := feNewSigner(t), feNewSigner(t)
	manifestPath := "v" + feDarkpanel + "/" + ManifestName
	binaryPath := "v" + feDarkpanel + "/" + BinaryName(DerperVersion, "amd64")
	sigPath := "v" + feDarkpanel + "/checksums.txt." + signer.key.ID + ".minisig"
	resign := func(s *feServer) { s.sign(signer, feDarkpanel, "stable") }

	tests := []struct {
		name     string
		dp, want string // zero values mean feDarkpanel and DerperVersion
		arch     string // zero value means amd64
		tamper   func(t *testing.T, s *feServer)
		code     errcode.Code // "" means a plain error
		detail   string       // the exact detail of a coded error, else a substring of the message
		noBinary bool
	}{
		{name: "darkpanel version not in the index", dp: "1.5.0", code: errcode.UpdateNotFound,
			detail: "darkpanel 1.5.0 is not a published release; a DERP server needs a released darkpanel", noBinary: true},
		{name: "development build", dp: "dev", code: errcode.UpdateNotFound,
			detail: "darkpanel dev is not a published release; a DERP server needs a released darkpanel", noBinary: true},
		{name: "manifest names another derper", want: "v1.105.0", code: errcode.UpdateNotFound,
			detail: "release " + feDarkpanel + " carries derper " + DerperVersion + ", not v1.105.0", noBinary: true},
		{name: "manifest not published", tamper: func(_ *testing.T, s *feServer) { s.remove(manifestPath); resign(s) },
			code: errcode.UpdateNotFound, noBinary: true},
		{name: "manifest changed after signing", tamper: func(_ *testing.T, s *feServer) { s.put(manifestPath, append(s.get(manifestPath), ' ')) },
			code: errcode.UpdateSignatureInvalid, detail: "derper.json does not match the signed checksums.txt", noBinary: true},
		{name: "manifest names another darkpanel", tamper: func(t *testing.T, s *feServer) {
			s.put(manifestPath, feManifest(t, "1.3.0", DerperVersion, map[string][]byte{"amd64": s.get(binaryPath), "arm64": []byte("x")}))
			resign(s)
		}, code: errcode.UpdateSignatureInvalid, noBinary: true},
		{name: "manifest with an unknown member", tamper: func(t *testing.T, s *feServer) {
			doc := strings.TrimSuffix(strings.TrimSpace(string(feManifest(t, feDarkpanel, DerperVersion, map[string][]byte{"amd64": []byte("a"), "arm64": []byte("b")}))), "}")
			s.put(manifestPath, []byte(doc+`,"surprise":1}`))
			resign(s)
		}, detail: "surprise", noBinary: true},
		{name: "manifest over its limit", tamper: func(_ *testing.T, s *feServer) { s.put(manifestPath, bytes.Repeat([]byte(" "), 64<<10+1)); resign(s) },
			detail: "65536", noBinary: true},
		{name: "binary changed after signing", tamper: func(_ *testing.T, s *feServer) { s.put(binaryPath, []byte("evil")) },
			code: errcode.UpdateSignatureInvalid, detail: BinaryName(DerperVersion, "amd64") + " does not match the signed checksums.txt"},
		{name: "binary that the manifest does not list by hash", tamper: func(_ *testing.T, s *feServer) { s.put(binaryPath, []byte("evil")); resign(s) },
			code: errcode.UpdateSignatureInvalid},
		{name: "binary not listed in checksums.txt", tamper: func(_ *testing.T, s *feServer) { s.remove(binaryPath); resign(s) },
			code: errcode.UpdateNotFound},
		{name: "checksums.txt signed for the beta channel, listed as stable", tamper: func(_ *testing.T, s *feServer) { s.sign(signer, feDarkpanel, "beta") },
			code: errcode.UpdateSignatureInvalid, noBinary: true},
		{name: "signed by a key the agent does not trust", tamper: func(_ *testing.T, s *feServer) {
			s.remove(sigPath)
			s.sign(stranger, feDarkpanel, "stable")
		}, code: errcode.UpdateSignatureInvalid, noBinary: true},
		{name: "architecture without a build", arch: "riscv64", detail: "riscv64", noBinary: true},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			srv := newFeServer(t)
			fePublish(t, srv, signer, feDarkpanel, DerperVersion)
			if tt.tamper != nil {
				tt.tamper(t, srv)
			}
			dp, want, arch := cmp.Or(tt.dp, feDarkpanel), cmp.Or(tt.want, DerperVersion), cmp.Or(tt.arch, "amd64")
			bin, sha, err := Fetch(context.Background(), feSource(srv, signer.key), dp, want, arch)
			if err == nil {
				t.Fatalf("Fetch accepted the release and returned %d bytes", len(bin))
			}
			if bin != nil || sha != "" {
				t.Errorf("a refused fetch returned %d bytes and sha %q", len(bin), sha)
			}
			var ce *errcode.Error
			switch {
			case tt.code != "":
				if !errors.As(err, &ce) || ce.Code != tt.code {
					t.Fatalf("Fetch = %v, want %s", err, tt.code)
				}
				if tt.detail != "" && ce.Detail != tt.detail {
					t.Errorf("detail %q, want %q", ce.Detail, tt.detail)
				}
			case tt.detail != "" && !strings.Contains(err.Error(), tt.detail):
				t.Errorf("Fetch = %v, want an error mentioning %q", err, tt.detail)
			}
			if tt.noBinary && feBinaryAsked(srv.paths()) {
				t.Errorf("a binary was fetched before the refusal: %v", srv.paths())
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestFeFetchReadsTheIndexChannel(t *testing.T) {
	signer := feNewSigner(t)
	srv := newFeServer(t)
	builds := fePublish(t, srv, signer, feDarkpanel, DerperVersion)
	srv.sign(signer, feDarkpanel, "beta")
	src := feSource(srv, signer.key)
	src.Releases = func(context.Context) ([]update.Release, error) {
		return []update.Release{{Version: feDarkpanel, Channel: "beta"}}, nil
	}
	bin, _, err := Fetch(context.Background(), src, feDarkpanel, DerperVersion, "amd64")
	if err != nil || !bytes.Equal(bin, builds["amd64"]) {
		t.Fatalf("Fetch of a release the index lists as beta and the signature says beta = %q, %v", bin, err)
	}
}

func TestFeFetchIndexFailure(t *testing.T) {
	signer := feNewSigner(t)
	srv := newFeServer(t)
	fePublish(t, srv, signer, feDarkpanel, DerperVersion)
	boom := errors.New("index down")
	src := feSource(srv, signer.key)
	src.Releases = func(context.Context) ([]update.Release, error) { return nil, boom }
	if _, _, err := Fetch(context.Background(), src, feDarkpanel, DerperVersion, "amd64"); !errors.Is(err, boom) {
		t.Fatalf("Fetch = %v, want the index error", err)
	}
	if got := srv.paths(); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("the release server was asked for %v after the index failed", got)
	}
}

func TestFeFetchBoundsTheBinary(t *testing.T) {
	if testing.Short() {
		t.Skip("streams 128 MiB")
	}
	signer := feNewSigner(t)
	srv := newFeServer(t)
	fePublish(t, srv, signer, feDarkpanel, DerperVersion)
	name := "v" + feDarkpanel + "/" + BinaryName(DerperVersion, "amd64")
	srv.mu.Lock()
	srv.endless[name] = true
	srv.mu.Unlock()
	_, _, err := Fetch(context.Background(), feSource(srv, signer.key), feDarkpanel, DerperVersion, "amd64")
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "134217728") {
		t.Fatalf("Fetch of an endless binary = %v, want an error naming the 128 MiB limit", err)
	}
	srv.mu.Lock()
	sent := srv.sent[name]
	srv.mu.Unlock()
	if sent >= 200<<20 {
		t.Errorf("the server streamed %d bytes: the client did not stop at its limit", sent)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

All in `internal/modules/derp/artifact/fetch.go`, in the style of `manifest.go` beside it (a doc comment on every exported identifier, the `artifact:` prefix on errors that are not `errcode` errors). Declare the two limits as unexported constants: the manifest at `64 << 10` bytes and a binary at `128 << 20` bytes.

1. `Source` exactly as Contracts gives it.
2. `Fetch(ctx, src, dpVersion, want, arch)`, in this order, stopping at the first failure and returning a nil slice and an empty sha with it:
   1. `arch` must be an entry of `Arches`, else `fmt.Errorf("artifact: no derper build for architecture %q", arch)`, before any request.
   2. Read `src.Releases(ctx)`; an error is returned wrapped with `%w` as `artifact: read the release index: ...` and no request is made to the release server. Take the first entry whose `Version` equals `dpVersion` and use that `update.Release` (with its channel) for both downloads. Absent, including the version `dev`, is `errcode.New(errcode.UpdateNotFound, "darkpanel <dpVersion> is not a published release; a DERP server needs a released darkpanel")`.
   3. Read `ManifestName` with `update.DownloadAsset(ctx, src.Client, src.BaseURL, src.Keys, rel, ManifestName, <64 KiB>)` and return its error unchanged (the update package already makes an unserved or unlisted asset `update.not_found`, a mismatch `update.signature_invalid`, and names the limit).
   4. `ParseManifest` the bytes; its error is returned wrapped with `%w`.
   5. The manifest's `DarkpanelVersion` must equal `dpVersion`, else `errcode.New(errcode.UpdateSignatureInvalid, ...)` with a detail that names `derper.json` and both versions. Its `DerperVersion` must equal `want`, else `errcode.New(errcode.UpdateNotFound, "release <dpVersion> carries derper <manifest's derperVersion>, not <want>")`. The darkpanel check comes first, and no binary is requested before both pass.
   6. Read `BinaryName(want, arch)` with `update.DownloadAsset(..., <128 MiB>)`; its error is returned unchanged.
   7. Compute the SHA-256 of the bytes as 64 lower-case hex digits and compare it with `manifest.Artifacts[arch]`; a difference is `errcode.New(errcode.UpdateSignatureInvalid, "<binary name> does not match derper.json")`. Return the bytes and the digest.
3. The two checks are independent: the signed `checksums.txt` vouches for the manifest and for the binary (inside `DownloadAsset`), and the manifest must also agree with the binary, so neither alone is enough.
4. `fetch.go` imports no `net/http` beyond the `*http.Client` type in `Source`, reads no environment and starts no goroutine.

```tsx
test("shows each server's mode, region, version, certificate and health", () => {

  const headers = within(screen.getByRole("table", { name: "DERP servers" })).getAllByRole("columnheader")
  expect(headers.map((h) => h.textContent)).toEqual(["Host", "Mode", "Server name", "Region", "Version", "Certificate", "Health", "Actions"])

  const ip = within(stRow("derp-1"))
  expect(ip.getByText("IP certificate")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(ip.getByText("203.0.113.10")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(ip.getByText("fra-x (900)")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(ip.getByText("v1.104.1")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(ip.queryByText(/available/)).toBeNull()
  expect(ip.getByText(`Expires ${formatTime("2027-06-01T00:00:00Z")}`)).toBeTruthy()
  expect(ip.getByText("ok")).toBeTruthy()

  const le = within(stRow("derp-2"))
  expect(le.getByText("Let's Encrypt")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(le.getByText("derp.example.com")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(le.getByText("Not published")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(le.getByText("v1.103.0")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(le.getByText("v1.104.1 available")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(le.getByText("Renewed by derper")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(le.getByText("degraded")).toBeTruthy()
  const reasons = le.getByRole("list", { name: "Reasons for derp-2" })
  expect(within(reasons).getAllByRole("listitem").map((i) => i.textContent)).toEqual(["The server was last checked 12 minutes ago."])
  expect(ip.queryByRole("list")).toBeNull()
})

test("a broken server lists every reason and the failed check", () => {
  const broken: DerpServerView = {
    ...stIP,
    health: "broken",
    unitActive: false,
    reasons: ["The derper unit is not active.", "The certificate expired."],
    error: "agent unreachable",
  }
  renderWithProviders(<ServersTable servers={{ servers: [broken], availableVersion: "v1.104.1" }} onAction={() => {}} />)
  const row = within(stRow("derp-1"))
  expect(row.getByText("broken")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(within(row.getByRole("list", { name: "Reasons for derp-1" })).getAllByRole("listitem").map((i) => i.textContent)).toEqual([
    "The derper unit is not active.",
    "The certificate expired.",
  ])
  expect(row.getByText("Last check failed: agent unreachable")).toBeTruthy()
})

test("offers an upgrade only for an old version and a rotation only for an IP certificate", () => {
  renderWithProviders(<ServersTable servers={stServers} onAction={() => {}} />)
  const ip = within(stRow("derp-1"))
  expect(ip.getByRole("button", { name: "Probe derp-1" })).toBeTruthy()
  expect(ip.getByRole("button", { name: "Region of derp-1" })).toBeTruthy()
  expect(ip.getByRole("button", { name: "Rotate certificate of derp-1" })).toBeTruthy()
  expect(ip.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove derp-1" })).toBeTruthy()
  expect(ip.queryByRole("button", { name: "Upgrade derp-1" })).toBeNull()
  const le = within(stRow("derp-2"))

  expect(le.queryByRole("button", { name: "Rotate certificate of derp-2" })).toBeNull()

const stActions = [
  { button: "Probe derp-1", action: "probe", server: stIP },
  { button: "Region of derp-1", action: "region", server: stIP },
  { button: "Rotate certificate of derp-1", action: "rotate", server: stIP },

]

})
```

Create `web/src/modules/derp/probe-panel.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react"
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event"
import { expect, test } from "vitest"
import { axe } from "vitest-axe"
import { renderWithProviders, stubFetch } from "@/test/utils"
import { probeFixture } from "./fixtures"
import { ProbePanel } from "./probe-panel"
import type { ServerProbe } from "./types"

const PP_PROBE = "POST /api/v1/actions/derp.server.probe/v1/read"

const ppProbe: ServerProbe = {
  ...probeFixture,
  hostId: "h2",
  regionId: 900,
  regionCode: "fra-x",
  server: { ...probeFixture.server, hostName: "203.0.113.10", derperVersion: "v1.104.1", unitActive: true },
  sources: [
    {
      source: "controller",
      name: "controller",
      tls: { ok: true, latencyMs: 12.34, error: "" },
      stun: { ok: true, latencyMs: 8.5, error: "" },
      region: null,
      error: "",
    },
    {
      source: "h3",
      name: "web-1",
      tls: { ok: true, latencyMs: 40, error: "" },
      stun: { ok: false, latencyMs: 0, error: "timeout" },
      region: { info: ["DERP server answered"], warnings: ["no STUN reply"], errors: ["TLS name mismatch"] },
      error: "",
    },
  ],
  nodes: [
    { name: "web-1", address: "100.64.0.7", hostId: "h3", visible: true, preferred: "fra-x", observed: true },
    { name: "laptop", address: "100.64.0.8", hostId: "", visible: false, preferred: "", observed: false },
  ],
  ready: true,
  warnings: [],
}

function ppCells(row: HTMLElement | undefined): (string | null)[] {
  return within(row as HTMLElement).getAllByRole("cell").map((c) => c.textContent)
}

test("reads the probe of the host and says whether the region can be published", async () => {
  const calls = stubFetch({ [PP_PROBE]: { body: { output: ppProbe } } })
  renderWithProviders(<ProbePanel hostId="h2" />)
  expect(await screen.findByText("Ready to publish")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(calls.filter((c) => c.method === "POST").map((c) => c.body)).toEqual([{ input: { hostId: "h2" } }])
  expect(screen.getByText("Region: fra-x (900)")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(screen.getByText("203.0.113.10, derper v1.104.1, unit active")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(screen.queryByRole("list", { name: "Warnings" })).toBeNull()
})

test("lists the probe's warnings and says when the server is not ready", async () => {
  const warning = "no agent other than the DERP host probed 203.0.113.10; only the controller's probe backs the region"
  stubFetch({ [PP_PROBE]: { body: { output: { ...ppProbe, ready: false, regionId: 0, regionCode: "", warnings: [warning] } } } })
  renderWithProviders(<ProbePanel hostId="h2" />)
  expect(await screen.findByText("Not ready")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(screen.getByText("Region: not published")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(within(screen.getByRole("list", { name: "Warnings" })).getAllByRole("listitem").map((i) => i.textContent)).toEqual([warning])
  expect(screen.queryByText("Ready to publish")).toBeNull()
})

test("shows the TLS and STUN result of each source", async () => {
  stubFetch({ [PP_PROBE]: { body: { output: ppProbe } } })
  renderWithProviders(<ProbePanel hostId="h2" />)
  const table = await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Probe sources" })
  expect(within(table).getAllByRole("columnheader").map((h) => h.textContent)).toEqual(["Source", "TLS", "STUN"])
  const rows = within(table).getAllByRole("row")
  expect(ppCells(rows[1])).toEqual(["controller", "OK, 12.3 ms", "OK, 8.5 ms"])
  expect(ppCells(rows[2])).toEqual(["web-1", "OK, 40.0 ms", "Failed: timeout"])
})

test("labels the region report as connectivity diagnostics and shows it only for the sources that ran it", async () => {
  stubFetch({ [PP_PROBE]: { body: { output: ppProbe } } })
  renderWithProviders(<ProbePanel hostId="h2" />)
  const report = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Connectivity diagnostics from web-1" })
  expect(within(report).getByText("DERP server answered")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(within(report).getByText("no STUN reply")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(within(report).getByText("TLS name mismatch")).toBeTruthy()
  expect(screen.queryByRole("region", { name: "Connectivity diagnostics from controller" })).toBeNull()
  expect(screen.getByText(/do not prove that two nodes can relay through it/)).toBeTruthy()
})

test("shows whether each node is visible, its preferred region and whether it is relayed through this one", async () => {
  stubFetch({ [PP_PROBE]: { body: { output: ppProbe } } })
  renderWithProviders(<ProbePanel hostId="h2" />)
  const table = await screen.findByRole("table", { name: "Nodes" })
  expect(within(table).getAllByRole("columnheader").map((h) => h.textContent)).toEqual([
    "Node",
    "Address",
    "Visible",
    "Preferred region",
    "Relayed through this region",
  ])
  const rows = within(table).getAllByRole("row")
  expect(ppCells(rows[1])).toEqual(["web-1", "100.64.0.7", "Yes", "fra-x", "Yes"])
  expect(ppCells(rows[2])).toEqual(["laptop", "100.64.0.8", "No", "Unknown", "No"])
})

test("Probe again reads the probe once more", async () => {
  const calls = stubFetch({ [PP_PROBE]: { body: { output: ppProbe } } })
  renderWithProviders(<ProbePanel hostId="h2" />)
  await screen.findByText("Ready to publish")
  expect(calls.filter((c) => c.path.endsWith("/read"))).toHaveLength(1)
  await userEvent.setup().click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Probe again" }))
  await waitFor(() => expect(calls.filter((c) => c.path.endsWith("/read"))).toHaveLength(2))
})

test("a refused read and an answer that is not a probe are errors", async () => {
  stubFetch({
    [PP_PROBE]: {
      status: 409,
      body: { type: "about:blank", title: "Not ready", status: 409, detail: "no DERP server is installed on web-1", code: "derp.not_ready" },
    },
  })
  const { unmount } = renderWithProviders(<ProbePanel hostId="h2" />)
  expect((await screen.findByRole("alert")).textContent).toBe("no DERP server is installed on web-1")
  expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Probe again" })).toBeTruthy()
  unmount()

  stubFetch({ [PP_PROBE]: { body: { output: { sources: "none" } } } })
  renderWithProviders(<ProbePanel hostId="h2" />)
  expect((await screen.findByRole("alert")).textContent).toBe("The controller answered with an unexpected probe result.")
})

test("the panel has no accessibility violations", async () => {
  stubFetch({ [PP_PROBE]: { body: { output: ppProbe } } })
  const { container } = renderWithProviders(<ProbePanel hostId="h2" />)
  await screen.findByText("Ready to publish")
  expect(await axe(container)).toHaveNoViolations()
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Style: `web/src/modules/tailscale/latency-panel.tsx` (a read panel) and `web/src/modules/tailnet/devices-page.tsx` (a table with row actions). darkraise-ui components only, named exports, no default exports.

1. `servers-table.tsx` exports `type ServerAction = "probe" | "region" | "upgrade" | "rotate" | "remove"` and `ServersTable({ servers, onAction }: { servers: DerpServers; onAction: (action: ServerAction, server: DerpServerView) => void })`.
   - No server: `EmptyState` with title `No DERP servers` and a description telling the operator to deploy one to a host; no table.
   - Otherwise a `Table` with `aria-label="DERP servers"` and the column headers, in this order and with exactly this text: Host, Mode, Server name, Region, Version, Certificate, Health, then a last header holding `<span className="sr-only">Actions</span>` (the headers read back as `Actions`). A row per server, keyed by `hostId`:
     - Host: `server.host`, medium weight. Mode: `Let's Encrypt` for `letsencrypt`, `IP certificate` for `ip`. Server name: `server.hostName` in the mono font.
     - Region: `<regionCode> (<regionId>)` when `regionId` is not 0, else `Not published`.
     - Version: `server.derperVersion`; when it differs from `servers.availableVersion`, also a small secondary `Badge` reading `<availableVersion> available`.
     - Certificate: for `ip` mode, `Expires <formatTime(certNotAfter)>` (a null `certNotAfter` shows `—`); for `letsencrypt`, `Renewed by derper`.
     - Health: a `Badge` whose text is `server.health` (`ok` default variant, `degraded` secondary, `broken` destructive). When `reasons` is not empty, a `<ul aria-label="Reasons for <host>">` with one `<li>` per reason below the badge; no list when it is empty. When `server.error` is not empty, a line `Last check failed: <error>`.
     - Actions, in a wrapping flex row of outline small `Button`s, each with a visible short text and an `aria-label` naming the host: `Probe` (`Probe <host>`, action `probe`), `Region` (`Region of <host>`, `region`), `Upgrade` (`Upgrade <host>`, `upgrade`; only when `derperVersion` differs from `availableVersion`), `Rotate certificate` (`Rotate certificate of <host>`, `rotate`; only in `ip` mode), and a destructive `Remove` (`Remove <host>`, `remove`). A click calls `onAction(action, server)` once with the server object of that row.
   - The table fetches nothing and holds no state.
2. `probe-panel.tsx` exports `ProbePanel({ hostId }: { hostId: string })`.
   - Reads with `useQuery({ queryKey: derpKey("probe", hostId), queryFn, refetchOnWindowFocus: false })`; `queryFn` is `readAction<unknown>("derp.server.probe", DERP_ACTION_VERSION, { hostId })` followed by `isServerProbe`: an answer the guard refuses throws `new Error("The controller answered with an unexpected probe result.")`. Every read sends probes, so the panel never refetches on its own.
   - Pending: `Spinner` with `aria-label="Probing the server"`. Error: a destructive `Alert` with `role="alert"` whose text is `errorMessage(error)` for an `ApiError` and the error's message otherwise (as `LatencyPanel` does), and a `Probe again` outline button that refetches (disabled while fetching).
   - Success, top to bottom: a `Badge` reading `Ready to publish` when `probe.ready`, else `Not ready`; a paragraph `Region: <regionCode> (<regionId>)` or `Region: not published` (region id 0); a paragraph `<server.hostName>, derper <server.derperVersion>, unit active` (`unit inactive` when `unitActive` is false); the warnings as `<ul aria-label="Warnings">` (omitted when empty); the `Probe sources` table (`aria-label`; headers Source, TLS, STUN; one row per source with `source.name` and a cell per measurement); the region reports; the `Nodes` table (`aria-label`); a `Probe again` outline button.
   - A measurement cell reads `OK, <latencyMs to one decimal> ms` when `ok`, else `Failed: <error>` (`Failed` alone when the error is empty). Plain text cells.
   - Region reports: for every source whose `region` is not null, a `<section aria-label="Connectivity diagnostics from <source name>">` with a heading `Connectivity diagnostics from <name>` and the report's `info`, `warnings` and `errors` as lists of plain items (a list is omitted when empty). When at least one source has a report, one paragraph follows: `Connectivity diagnostics show that the region answers a throwaway node key. They do not prove that two nodes can relay through it.`
   - Nodes table headers, in order: Node, Address, Visible, Preferred region, Relayed through this region. Cells: `node.name`, `node.address` (mono), `Yes` or `No` for `visible`, `node.preferred` or `Unknown` when empty, `Yes` or `No` for `observed`. No nodes: the table is replaced by `EmptyState` with title `No nodes`.
3. Neither file imports from `tailnet` or `tailscale` modules; both only read `DERP_*` names from `./types`.

Create `web/src/modules/derp/deploy-dialog.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react"
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event"
import { expect, test, vi } from "vitest"
import { axe } from "vitest-axe"
import type { Plan } from "@/api/types"
import { renderWithProviders, stubFetch, type FetchCall, type StubResponse } from "@/test/utils"
import { DeployDialog } from "./deploy-dialog"

const DD_HOSTS = "POST /api/v1/actions/core.host.list/v1/read"
const DD_PLAN = "POST /api/v1/actions/derp.server.deploy/v1/plan"

const ddHosts: StubResponse = {
  body: {
    output: {
      hosts: [
        { id: "h1", name: "controller", isLocal: true, online: true, modules: [], ingestion: null },
        { id: "h2", name: "web-1", isLocal: false, online: true, modules: [], ingestion: null },
      ],
    },
  },
}

function ddPlan(over: Partial<Plan> = {}): StubResponse {
  return {
    body: {
      planId: "p1",
      hostId: null,
      action: "derp.server.deploy",
      version: 1,
      inputHash: "h",
      diff: [{ op: "~", text: "deploy a DERP server" }],
      guards: [],
      expiresAt: "2026-11-02T13:00:00Z",
      confirmation: null,
      ...over,
    },
  }
}

function ddPlans(calls: FetchCall[]): unknown[] {
  return calls.filter((c) => c.method === "POST" && c.path.endsWith("derp.server.deploy/v1/plan")).map((c) => c.body)
}

const DD_OFF = "Client verification is off: any node that knows the region can relay through it."

async function ddOpen() {
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Deploy a DERP server" })
  await within(dialog).findByRole("radio", { name: "web-1" })
  return { user, dialog }
}

test("offers the hosts, keeps Review off until a host and a name are given, and plans a Let's Encrypt deployment", async () => {
  const calls = stubFetch({ [DD_HOSTS]: ddHosts, [DD_PLAN]: ddPlan() })
  renderWithProviders(<DeployDialog onClose={() => {}} />)
  const { user, dialog } = await ddOpen()
  const review = within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Review" })
  expect(review.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
  expect(within(dialog).getByRole("radio", { name: "controller" })).toBeTruthy()
  expect(within(dialog).getByRole("radio", { name: "Let's Encrypt", checked: true })).toBeTruthy()

  await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("radio", { name: "web-1" }))
  expect(review.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)
  await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText("DNS name"), "  derp.example.com ")
  await waitFor(() => expect(review.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(false))
  await user.click(review)

  const change = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Deploy a DERP server on web-1" })
  expect((await within(change).findByLabelText("Planned changes")).textContent).toBe("~ deploy a DERP server")
  expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog", { name: "Deploy a DERP server" })).toBeNull()
  expect(ddPlans(calls)).toEqual([{ input: { hostId: "h2", mode: "letsencrypt", hostName: "derp.example.com" } }])
})

test("an IP certificate asks for the public address", async () => {
  const calls = stubFetch({ [DD_HOSTS]: ddHosts, [DD_PLAN]: ddPlan() })
  renderWithProviders(<DeployDialog onClose={() => {}} />)
  const { user, dialog } = await ddOpen()
  await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("radio", { name: "web-1" }))
  await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("radio", { name: "IP certificate" }))
  expect(within(dialog).queryByLabelText("DNS name")).toBeNull()
  await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText("Public IP address"), "203.0.113.10")
  await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Review" }))
  await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Deploy a DERP server on web-1" })
  expect(ddPlans(calls)).toEqual([{ input: { hostId: "h2", mode: "ip", hostName: "203.0.113.10" } }])
})

test("client verification is on by default, and clearing it warns and sends verifyClients false", async () => {
  const calls = stubFetch({ [DD_HOSTS]: ddHosts, [DD_PLAN]: ddPlan() })
  renderWithProviders(<DeployDialog onClose={() => {}} />)
  const { user, dialog } = await ddOpen()
  expect(within(dialog).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Verify clients", checked: true })).toBeTruthy()
  expect(screen.queryByText(DD_OFF)).toBeNull()

  await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Verify clients" }))
  expect(screen.getByText(DD_OFF)).toBeTruthy()
  await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Verify clients" }))
  expect(screen.queryByText(DD_OFF)).toBeNull()

  await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Verify clients" }))
  await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("radio", { name: "web-1" }))
  await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText("DNS name"), "derp.example.com")
  await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Review" }))
  await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Deploy a DERP server on web-1" })
  expect(ddPlans(calls)).toEqual([{ input: { hostId: "h2", mode: "letsencrypt", hostName: "derp.example.com", verifyClients: false } }])
})

test("closing the change dialog closes the whole flow", async () => {
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react"
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event"
import { expect, test, vi } from "vitest"
import { axe } from "vitest-axe"
import type { Plan } from "@/api/types"
import { renderWithProviders, stubFetch, type FetchCall, type StubResponse } from "@/test/utils"
import { probeFixture } from "./fixtures"
import { RegionDialog } from "./region-dialog"
import type { DerpServerView, ServerProbe } from "./types"

const RD_PLAN = "POST /api/v1/actions/derp.region.set/v1/plan"

const rdServer: DerpServerView = {
  hostId: "h2",
  host: "derp-1",
  mode: "ip",
  hostName: "203.0.113.10",
  regionId: 0,
  regionCode: "",
  derperVersion: "v1.104.1",
  certName: "sha256-raw:ab",
  certNotAfter: "2027-06-01T00:00:00Z",
  unitActive: true,
  health: "ok",
  reasons: [],
  checkedAt: "2026-11-02T11:59:00Z",
  error: "",
}

const rdProbe: ServerProbe = { ...probeFixture, hostId: "h2", ready: true, warnings: [] }

function rdPlan(): StubResponse {
  return {
    body: {
      planId: "p1",
      hostId: null,
      action: "derp.region.set",
      version: 1,
      inputHash: "h",
      diff: [{ op: "~", text: "publish the region" }],
      guards: [],
      expiresAt: "2026-11-02T13:00:00Z",
      confirmation: null,
    } satisfies Plan,
  }
}

function rdPlans(calls: FetchCall[]): unknown[] {
  return calls.filter((c) => c.method === "POST" && c.path.endsWith("derp.region.set/v1/plan")).map((c) => c.body)
}

function rdOpen(over: { server?: DerpServerView; probe?: ServerProbe; onClose?: () => void } = {}) {
  renderWithProviders(<RegionDialog server={over.server ?? rdServer} probe={over.probe ?? rdProbe} onClose={over.onClose ?? (() => {})} />)
  return screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Publish a DERP region on derp-1" })
}

async function rdFill(user: ReturnType<typeof userEvent.setup>, dialog: HTMLElement, code: string, name: string) {
  await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText("Region code"), code)
  await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText("Region name"), name)
}

test("starts at region 900, keeps Review off until the code and name are valid, and plans derp.region.set", async () => {
  const calls = stubFetch({ [RD_PLAN]: rdPlan() })
  const dialog = await rdOpen()
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  const review = within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Review" })
  expect((within(dialog).getByLabelText("Region id") as HTMLInputElement).value).toBe("900")
  expect(review.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true)

  await rdFill(user, dialog, "fra-x", "Frankfurt X")
  await waitFor(() => expect(review.hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(false))
  await user.click(review)

  const change = await screen.findByRole("dialog", { name: "Publish region fra-x" })
  expect((await within(change).findByLabelText("Planned changes")).textContent).toBe("~ publish the region")
  expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog", { name: "Publish a DERP region on derp-1" })).toBeNull()
  expect(rdPlans(calls)).toEqual([{ input: { hostId: "h2", regionId: 900, regionCode: "fra-x", regionName: "Frankfurt X" } }])
})

test("an existing region starts from the published id and code", async () => {
  stubFetch({ [RD_PLAN]: rdPlan() })
  const dialog = await rdOpen({ server: { ...rdServer, regionId: 905, regionCode: "ams-x" } })
  expect((within(dialog).getByLabelText("Region id") as HTMLInputElement).value).toBe("905")
  expect((within(dialog).getByLabelText("Region code") as HTMLInputElement).value).toBe("ams-x")
})

const rdInvalid = [
  { what: "an id below 900", field: "Region id", value: "899" },
  { what: "an id above 999", field: "Region id", value: "1000" },
  { what: "an id that is not a number", field: "Region id", value: "" },
  { what: "a code with a capital letter", field: "Region code", value: "Fra" },
  { what: "a code that starts with a digit", field: "Region code", value: "9fra" },
  { what: "a code over 32 characters", field: "Region code", value: "a".repeat(33) },
  { what: "a name over 64 characters", field: "Region name", value: "n".repeat(65) },
]

test.each(rdInvalid)("Review stays off for $what", async ({ field, value }) => {
  stubFetch({ [RD_PLAN]: rdPlan() })
  const dialog = await rdOpen()
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  await rdFill(user, dialog, "fra-x", "Frankfurt X")
  const input = within(dialog).getByLabelText(field)
  await user.clear(input)
  if (value !== "") await user.type(input, value)
  await waitFor(() => expect(within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Review" }).hasAttribute("disabled")).toBe(true))
})

test("omitting the default regions asks for the acknowledgement and sends what was typed", async () => {
  const calls = stubFetch({ [RD_PLAN]: rdPlan() })
  const dialog = await rdOpen()
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  await rdFill(user, dialog, "fra-x", "Frankfurt X")

  await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Omit default regions" }))
  const ack = within(dialog).getByLabelText("Acknowledgement")
  expect(within(dialog).getByText(/nodes will relay only through fra-x/)).toBeTruthy()
  await user.type(ack, "nodes will relay only through fra-x")
  await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole("button", { name: "Review" }))

  expect(rdPlans(calls)).toEqual([
    { input: { hostId: "h2", regionId: 900, regionCode: "fra-x", regionName: "Frankfurt X", omitDefaultRegions: true, acknowledge: "nodes will relay only through fra-x" } },
  ])
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Style: `web/src/pages/create-host-dialog.tsx` (a form that hands over to `ChangeDialog` in a second phase) and `web/src/pages/create-token-dialog.tsx` (radio groups). darkraise-ui components only.

1. `deploy-dialog.tsx` exports `DeployDialog({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void })`.
   - State: phase `form` or `plan`, the chosen host id, the mode (`letsencrypt` by default), the typed name, the `Verify clients` flag (true by default), and the planned input.
   - Hosts: `useQuery({ queryKey: HOSTS_QUERY_KEY, queryFn: listHosts })`. While pending the form shows a `Spinner` with `aria-label="Loading the hosts"`; on error a destructive `Alert` with `role="alert"` and `errorMessage(error)`, and Review stays disabled.
   - Form phase: a `Dialog` titled `Deploy a DERP server` with a description. Fields: a `RadioGroup` labelled `Host` with one `RadioGroupItem` per host, labelled with `host.name` (all hosts, in the order the query returns them, none preselected); a `RadioGroup` labelled `Certificate mode` with two items, `Let's Encrypt` and `IP certificate`; one `Input` whose label is `DNS name` in Let's Encrypt mode and `Public IP address` in IP mode (the text is kept when the mode changes); a `Checkbox` labelled `Verify clients`, checked. When it is cleared a warning `Alert` with `role="status"` shows exactly `Client verification is off: any node that knows the region can relay through it.` Footer: `Cancel` (outline; calls `onClose`) and `Review`.
   - Review is disabled until a host is chosen and the trimmed name is not empty. It switches to the plan phase with the input `{ hostId, mode, hostName: <trimmed> }` plus `verifyClients: false` only when the box is cleared (the field is absent when it is checked: the server reads absence as true). No other validation runs in the browser; the server's field errors reach the operator through the change dialog.
   - Plan phase: the form dialog is not rendered; `<DerpChange action="derp.server.deploy" title={`Deploy a DERP server on ${host name}`} input={input} onClose={onClose} />`. Closing the change dialog calls `onClose`; `DerpChange` itself refetches the DERP queries.
2. `region-dialog.tsx` exports `RegionDialog({ server, probe, onClose }: { server: DerpServerView; probe: ServerProbe; onClose: () => void })`.
   - State: the region id text (initially `String(server.regionId)` when it is not 0, else `900`), the code (initially `server.regionCode`), the name (initially empty), the omit flag (false), the acknowledgement text, the phase.
   - Form phase: a `Dialog` titled `Publish a DERP region on <server.host>`. Above the fields: when `probe.warnings` is not empty, a warning `Alert` with `role="status"` listing every warning as a list item; when `probe.ready` is false, a destructive-free (warning variant) `Alert` with the text `The probe has not passed yet; the server refuses to publish the region until it does.` A ready probe with no warnings shows neither (no element with `role="status"`).
   - Fields (labels exact): `Region id` (`Input type="number"`), `Region code`, `Region name`, a `Checkbox` `Omit default regions`, and, only while it is ticked, `Acknowledgement` with help text `To use only this region, type: nodes will relay only through <code>.` (`<region code>` in the placeholder position while the code is empty). Unticking hides the field and drops its text from the input.
   - None of the inputs sets a `maxLength` attribute (a test types 33 and 65 characters and expects Review to turn off, not the text to be cut). Valid means: the id text is an integer from 900 to 999; the code matches `^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,31}$`; the trimmed name is 1 to 64 characters. Review is disabled otherwise; an invalid code or id also shows its help text in the destructive colour with `aria-invalid` on the input (the rule in words: lower-case letters, digits and hyphens, starting with a letter, at most 32 characters; a number from 900 to 999).
   - Review switches to the plan phase with the input `{ hostId: server.hostId, regionId: <number>, regionCode, regionName: <trimmed> }`, plus `omitDefaultRegions: true` only when ticked, plus `acknowledge: <typed text>` only when ticked and the text is not empty.
   - Plan phase: the form is not rendered; `<DerpChange action="derp.region.set" title={`Publish region ${code}`} input={input} onClose={onClose} />`.
   - `Cancel` calls `onClose`.
3. Neither dialog reads a DERP resource itself; the page passes the probe in.
