- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `internal/modules/derp/derpmap_test.go`:

```go
package derp

import (
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

const sdPolicyWithRegions = `{
	// custom regions
	"acls": [],
	"derpMap": {
		"OmitDefaultRegions": true,
		"Regions": {
			"3": {"RegionID": 3, "RegionCode": "def", "RegionName": "Default", "Nodes": [
				{"Name": "3a", "RegionID": 3, "HostName": "default.example.net"},
			]},
			"900": {"RegionID": 900, "RegionCode": "fra-x", "RegionName": "Frankfurt X", "Nodes": [
				{"Name": "900a", "RegionID": 900, "HostName": "derp.Example.com", "DERPPort": 443, "STUNPort": 3478},
			]},
			"901": {"RegionID": 901, "RegionCode": "ams-x", "RegionName": "Amsterdam X", "Nodes": [
				{"Name": "901a", "RegionID": 901, "HostName": "203.0.113.10", "CertName": "sha256-raw:ab", "IPv4": "203.0.113.10", "DERPPort": 443, "STUNPort": 3478},
			]},
		},
	},
}`

func TestSdParseDERPMap(t *testing.T) {
	m, err := parseDERPMap(sdPolicyWithRegions)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !m.Present || !m.OmitDefaultRegions || len(m.Regions) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("parsed %+v", m)
	}
	fra := m.Regions[900]
	if fra.RegionID != 900 || fra.RegionCode != "fra-x" || fra.RegionName != "Frankfurt X" {
		t.Errorf("region 900 = %+v", fra)
	}
	wantFra := []derpNode{{Name: "900a", HostName: "derp.Example.com", RegionID: 900, STUNPort: 3478, DERPPort: 443}}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(fra.Nodes, wantFra) {
		t.Errorf("region 900 nodes %+v, want %+v", fra.Nodes, wantFra)
	}
	wantAms := derpNode{Name: "901a", HostName: "203.0.113.10", CertName: "sha256-raw:ab", IPv4: "203.0.113.10", RegionID: 901, STUNPort: 3478, DERPPort: 443}
	if got := m.Regions[901].Nodes; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []derpNode{wantAms}) {
		t.Errorf("region 901 nodes %+v, want %+v", got, wantAms)
	}
}

func TestSdParseDERPMapAbsentAndEmpty(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tt := range []struct {
		name, text string
		present    bool
	}{
		{"no derpMap member", `{"acls": []}`, false},
		{"a null derpMap", `{"derpMap": null}`, false},
		{"an empty derpMap", `{"derpMap": {}}`, true},
		{"a derpMap with only the omit flag", `{"derpMap": {"OmitDefaultRegions": true}}`, true},
	} {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			m, err := parseDERPMap(tt.text)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if m.Present != tt.present || len(m.Regions) != 0 || len(m.customRegions()) != 0 {
				t.Errorf("parsed %+v", m)
			}
			if _, ok := m.regionOf("anything"); ok {
				t.Error("regionOf found a region in a map without any")
			}
		})
	}
	if m, _ := parseDERPMap(`{"derpMap": {"OmitDefaultRegions": true}}`); !m.OmitDefaultRegions {
		t.Error("the omit flag was lost")
	}
}

func TestSdDERPMapCustomRegions(t *testing.T) {
	m, err := parseDERPMap(sdPolicyWithRegions)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := m.customRegions(); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []int{900, 901}) {
		t.Errorf("customRegions = %v, want [900 901] (region 3 is a default region)", got)
	}
}

func TestSdDERPMapRegionOf(t *testing.T) {
	m, err := parseDERPMap(sdPolicyWithRegions)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, tt := range []struct {
		host string
		want int
	}{
		{"derp.example.com", 900},
		{"DERP.EXAMPLE.COM", 900},
		{"203.0.113.10", 901},
		{"default.example.net", 0}, // a node of a default region never counts
		{"other.example.com", 0},
		{"", 0},
	} {
		r, ok := m.regionOf(tt.host)
		if (tt.want != 0) != ok || r.RegionID != tt.want {
			t.Errorf("regionOf(%q) = region %d, %v; want region %d", tt.host, r.RegionID, ok, tt.want)
		}
	}
}

func TestSdParseDERPMapRefuses(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tt := range []struct{ name, text, mention string }{
		{"a key that is not a number", `{"derpMap": {"Regions": {"abc": {"RegionID": 900}}}}`, "abc"},
		{"a key that differs from its region id", `{"derpMap": {"Regions": {"900": {"RegionID": 901}}}}`, "900"},
		{"a key with a sign", `{"derpMap": {"Regions": {"+900": {"RegionID": 900}}}}`, "+900"},
		{"text that is not a policy", `{"derpMap": `, ""},
		{"a derpMap that is not an object", `{"derpMap": 5}`, ""},
	} {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			_, err := parseDERPMap(tt.text)
			if err == nil {
				t.Fatal("parseDERPMap accepted the policy")
			}
			if !strings.Contains(err.Error(), tt.mention) {
				t.Errorf("error %q does not name %q", err, tt.mention)
			}
		})
	}
}
```

Create `internal/modules/derp/store_test.go`:

```go
package derp

import (
	"context"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

var sdNow = time.Date(2026, 11, 2, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

func sdLocalServer() localServer {
	return localServer{
		ChangeID: "c1", Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.10", VerifyClients: true, DerperVersion: "v1.104.1",
		BinarySHA256: strings.Repeat("a", 64), CertName: "sha256-raw:" + strings.Repeat("b", 64),
		CertNotAfter: sdNow.AddDate(1, 0, 0), InstalledAt: sdNow, UpdatedAt: sdNow,
	}
}

func sdText(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, query string) string {
	t.Helper()
	var s string
	if err := h.db.Read().QueryRow(query).Scan(&s); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return s
}

func TestSdLocalServer(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()

	if _, ok, err := loadLocalServer(ctx, h.db.Read()); err != nil || ok {
		t.Fatalf("loadLocalServer on an empty table = %v, %v; want none", ok, err)
	}
	want := sdLocalServer()
	if err := insertLocalServer(ctx, h.db, want); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got, ok, err := loadLocalServer(ctx, h.db.Read())
	if err != nil || !ok || !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("loadLocalServer = %+v, %v, %v; want %+v", got, ok, err, want)
	}
	if raw := sdText(t, h, `SELECT installed_at FROM derp_server`); raw != "2026-11-02T12:00:00.000000000Z" {
		t.Errorf("installed_at is stored as %q, want fixed-width RFC 3339 UTC text", raw)
	}
	if err := insertLocalServer(ctx, h.db, sdLocalServer()); err == nil {
		t.Error("a second server row was accepted: at most one server is installed")
	}
	bad := sdLocalServer()
	bad.Mode = "dns"
	if err := deleteLocalServer(ctx, h.db); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := insertLocalServer(ctx, h.db, bad); err == nil {
		t.Error("a mode outside letsencrypt and ip was accepted")
	}
}

func TestSdLocalServerUpdates(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	if err := setLocalBinary(ctx, h.db, "v1.105.0", "x", sdNow); err == nil {
		t.Error("setLocalBinary without a server row succeeded")
	}
	if err := setLocalCert(ctx, h.db, "p", sdNow, sdNow); err == nil {
		t.Error("setLocalCert without a server row succeeded")
	}
	if err := insertLocalServer(ctx, h.db, sdLocalServer()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	later := sdNow.Add(time.Hour)
	if err := setLocalBinary(ctx, h.db, "v1.105.0", strings.Repeat("c", 64), later); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got, _, _ := loadLocalServer(ctx, h.db.Read())
	want := sdLocalServer()
	want.DerperVersion, want.BinarySHA256, want.UpdatedAt = "v1.105.0", strings.Repeat("c", 64), later
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("after setLocalBinary: %+v, want %+v", got, want)
	}
	notAfter := sdNow.AddDate(2, 0, 0)
	if err := setLocalCert(ctx, h.db, "sha256-raw:new", notAfter, later.Add(time.Hour)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got, _, _ = loadLocalServer(ctx, h.db.Read())
	want.CertName, want.CertNotAfter, want.UpdatedAt = "sha256-raw:new", notAfter, later.Add(time.Hour)
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("after setLocalCert: %+v, want %+v", got, want)
	}
}

func TestSdLocalServerZeroTimeAndDelete(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	s := sdLocalServer()
	s.Mode, s.CertName, s.CertNotAfter = "letsencrypt", "", time.Time{}
	if err := insertLocalServer(ctx, h.db, s); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if raw := sdText(t, h, `SELECT cert_not_after FROM derp_server`); raw != "" {
		t.Errorf("a zero time is stored as %q, want an empty string", raw)
	}
	got, ok, err := loadLocalServer(ctx, h.db.Read())
	if err != nil || !ok || !got.CertNotAfter.IsZero() {
		t.Fatalf("loadLocalServer = %+v, %v, %v; want a zero CertNotAfter", got, ok, err)
	}
	for range 2 {
		if err := deleteLocalServer(ctx, h.db); err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("deleteLocalServer: %v (a missing row is not an error)", err)
		}
	}
	if _, ok, _ := loadLocalServer(ctx, h.db.Read()); ok {
		t.Error("the server row survived deleteLocalServer")
	}
}

func TestSdUpdates(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	if _, ok, err := loadUpdate(ctx, h.db.Read(), "c9"); err != nil || ok {
		t.Fatalf("loadUpdate of an unknown change = %v, %v", ok, err)
	}
	u := updateRow{
		ChangeID: "c9", FromVersion: "v1.103.0", FromSHA256: strings.Repeat("a", 64), ToVersion: "v1.104.1",
		ToSHA256: strings.Repeat("b", 64), Phase: "staged", UpdatedAt: sdNow,
	}
	if err := insertUpdate(ctx, h.db, u); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got, ok, err := loadUpdate(ctx, h.db.Read(), "c9")
	if err != nil || !ok || !reflect.DeepEqual(got, u) {
		t.Fatalf("loadUpdate = %+v, %v, %v; want %+v", got, ok, err, u)
	}
	if err := insertUpdate(ctx, h.db, u); err == nil {
		t.Error("a second update row for one change was accepted")
	}
	later := sdNow.Add(time.Minute)
	if err := setUpdatePhase(ctx, h.db, "c9", "failed", "derper did not start", later); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	u.Phase, u.Result, u.UpdatedAt = "failed", "derper did not start", later
	if got, _, _ := loadUpdate(ctx, h.db.Read(), "c9"); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, u) {
		t.Errorf("after setUpdatePhase: %+v, want %+v", got, u)
	}
	if err := setUpdatePhase(ctx, h.db, "c9", "teleported", "", later); err == nil {
		t.Error("a phase outside the table's list was accepted")
	}
	if got, _, _ := loadUpdate(ctx, h.db.Read(), "c9"); got.Phase != "failed" {
		t.Errorf("a refused phase changed the row to %q", got.Phase)
	}
	if err := setUpdatePhase(ctx, h.db, "nope", "done", "", later); err == nil {
		t.Error("setUpdatePhase of an unknown change succeeded")
	}
}

func sdRecord(host string) serverRecord {
	return serverRecord{
		HostID: host, Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.10", RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "fra-x", DerperVersion: "v1.104.1",
		CertName: "sha256-raw:ab", CertNotAfter: sdNow.AddDate(1, 0, 0), UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: sdNow,
	}
}

func TestSdServerRecords(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	q := h.ctlDB.Read()
	if recs, err := loadServerRecords(ctx, q); err != nil || len(recs) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("loadServerRecords on an empty table = %v, %v", recs, err)
	}
	if _, ok, err := loadServerRecord(ctx, q, "h2"); err != nil || ok {
		t.Fatalf("loadServerRecord of an unknown host = %v, %v", ok, err)
	}
	for _, id := range []string{"h3", "h2"} {
		if err := upsertServerRecord(ctx, h.ctlDB, sdRecord(id)); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	recs, err := loadServerRecords(ctx, q)
	if err != nil || len(recs) != 2 || recs[0].HostID != "h2" || recs[1].HostID != "h3" {
		t.Fatalf("loadServerRecords = %+v, %v; want h2 then h3", recs, err)
	}
	if got, ok, err := loadServerRecord(ctx, q, "h2"); err != nil || !ok || !reflect.DeepEqual(got, sdRecord("h2")) {
		t.Fatalf("loadServerRecord = %+v, %v, %v", got, ok, err)
	}

	changed := sdRecord("h2")
	changed.RegionID, changed.RegionCode, changed.UnitActive, changed.Error = 0, "", false, "agent unreachable"
	changed.CertNotAfter = time.Time{}
	changed.CheckedAt = sdNow.Add(time.Minute)
	if err := upsertServerRecord(ctx, h.ctlDB, changed); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got, _, _ := loadServerRecord(ctx, q, "h2"); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, changed) || !got.CertNotAfter.IsZero() {
		t.Errorf("after the second upsert: %+v, want %+v", got, changed)
	}
	if recs, _ := loadServerRecords(ctx, q); len(recs) != 2 {
		t.Errorf("an upsert of a known host added a row: %d rows", len(recs))
	}

	bad := sdRecord("h4")
	bad.Mode = "dns"
	if err := upsertServerRecord(ctx, h.ctlDB, bad); err == nil {
		t.Error("a mode outside letsencrypt and ip was accepted")
	}
	for range 2 {
		if err := deleteServerRecord(ctx, h.ctlDB, "h2"); err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("deleteServerRecord: %v (a missing row is not an error)", err)
		}
	}
	if _, ok, _ := loadServerRecord(ctx, q, "h2"); ok {
		t.Error("the record survived deleteServerRecord")
	}
}
```

Create `internal/modules/derp/wire_test.go`:

```go
package derp

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/schema"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/tailnet/policy"
)

func sdWireTypes() []any {
	return []any{
		ServerInstallInput{}, ServerInstalled{}, ServerUninstallInput{}, ServerUninstalled{}, BinaryUpdateInput{}, BinaryUpdated{},
		CertRenewInput{}, CertRenewed{}, ServerStatusInput{}, ServerStatus{}, Visibility{}, NodeProbeInput{}, NodeProbe{}, ProbeResult{},
		RegionDiagnostics{}, ServerProbeInput{}, ServerProbe{}, ProbeSource{}, NodeUse{}, Servers{}, ServerView{}, RegionEditsInput{},
		RegionEdits{}, FallbackCheckInput{}, FallbackCheck{}, DeployInput{}, Deployed{}, UpgradeInput{}, Upgraded{}, CertRotateInput{},
		CertRotated{}, RegionSetInput{}, RegionRemoveInput{}, ServerRemoveInput{}, RegionChanged{}, ServerRemoved{},
	}
}

func TestSdWireTypesExist(t *testing.T) {
	if n := len(sdWireTypes()); n != 36 {
		t.Errorf("%d wire types listed, want 36", n)
	}
}

const sdEmptyStatus = `{"installed":false,"mode":"","hostName":"","verifyClients":false,"derperVersion":"","unitActive":false,` +
	`"certName":"","certNotAfter":null,"availableVersion":"","linkViaTailnet":false,"onTailnet":false,"tags":[],"addresses":[],"visible":[]}`

func TestSdWireJSON(t *testing.T) {
	notAfter := time.Date(2027, 11, 2, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	checked := time.Date(2026, 11, 2, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	no := false
	emptyStatus := ServerStatus{Tags: []string{}, Addresses: []string{}, Visible: []Visibility{}}
	tests := []struct {
		name string
		v    any
		want string
	}{
		{"install input", ServerInstallInput{Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.10"}, `{"mode":"ip","hostName":"203.0.113.10"}`},
		{"install input without verification", ServerInstallInput{Mode: "letsencrypt", HostName: "derp.example.com", VerifyClients: &no},
			`{"mode":"letsencrypt","hostName":"derp.example.com","verifyClients":false}`},
		{"installed", ServerInstalled{DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", CertName: "sha256-raw:ab", CertNotAfter: &notAfter},
			`{"derperVersion":"v1.104.1","certName":"sha256-raw:ab","certNotAfter":"2027-11-02T12:00:00Z"}`},
		{"installed with a Let's Encrypt certificate", ServerInstalled{DerperVersion: "v1.104.1"}, `{"derperVersion":"v1.104.1","certName":"","certNotAfter":null}`},
		{"uninstall input", ServerUninstallInput{}, `{}`},
		{"uninstalled", ServerUninstalled{Removed: []string{"derp.unit:darkpanel-derper.service"}}, `{"removed":["derp.unit:darkpanel-derper.service"]}`},
		{"binary update input", BinaryUpdateInput{Version: "v1.104.1"}, `{"version":"v1.104.1"}`},
		{"binary updated", BinaryUpdated{From: "v1.103.0", To: "v1.104.1"}, `{"from":"v1.103.0","to":"v1.104.1"}`},
		{"renew input", CertRenewInput{}, `{}`},
		{"renewed", CertRenewed{CertName: "sha256-raw:new", CertNotAfter: notAfter, PriorCertName: "sha256-raw:old"},
			`{"certName":"sha256-raw:new","certNotAfter":"2027-11-02T12:00:00Z","priorCertName":"sha256-raw:old"}`},
		{"status input", ServerStatusInput{}, `{}`},
		{"status input with addresses", ServerStatusInput{Whois: []string{"100.64.0.7"}}, `{"whois":["100.64.0.7"]}`},
		{"status of an empty host", emptyStatus, sdEmptyStatus},
		{"status of a server", ServerStatus{Installed: true, Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.10", VerifyClients: true, DerperVersion: "v1.104.1",
			UnitActive: true, CertName: "sha256-raw:ab", CertNotAfter: &notAfter, AvailableVersion: "v1.104.1", LinkViaTailnet: true, OnTailnet: true,
			Tags: []string{"tag:derp"}, Addresses: []string{"100.64.0.10"}, Visible: []Visibility{{Address: "100.64.0.7", Visible: true, Node: "web.example.ts.net"}}},
			`{"installed":true,"mode":"ip","hostName":"203.0.113.10","verifyClients":true,"derperVersion":"v1.104.1","unitActive":true,` +
				`"certName":"sha256-raw:ab","certNotAfter":"2027-11-02T12:00:00Z","availableVersion":"v1.104.1","linkViaTailnet":true,"onTailnet":true,` +
				`"tags":["tag:derp"],"addresses":["100.64.0.10"],"visible":[{"address":"100.64.0.7","visible":true,"node":"web.example.ts.net"}]}`},
		{"node probe input", NodeProbeInput{HostName: "derp.example.com", Address: "203.0.113.10", CertName: "sha256-raw:ab", RegionID: 900},
			`{"hostName":"derp.example.com","address":"203.0.113.10","certName":"sha256-raw:ab","regionId":900}`},
		{"node probe input without options", NodeProbeInput{HostName: "derp.example.com"}, `{"hostName":"derp.example.com"}`},
		{"node probe", NodeProbe{TLS: ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: 12.5}, STUN: ProbeResult{Error: "timeout"}},
			`{"tls":{"ok":true,"latencyMs":12.5,"error":""},"stun":{"ok":false,"latencyMs":0,"error":"timeout"},"region":null}`},
		{"node probe with a region report", NodeProbe{Region: &RegionDiagnostics{Info: []string{"ok"}, Warnings: []string{}, Errors: []string{}}},
			`{"tls":{"ok":false,"latencyMs":0,"error":""},"stun":{"ok":false,"latencyMs":0,"error":""},"region":{"info":["ok"],"warnings":[],"errors":[]}}`},
		{"server probe input", ServerProbeInput{HostID: "h2"}, `{"hostId":"h2"}`},
		{"server probe input with agents", ServerProbeInput{HostID: "h2", Agents: []string{"h3"}}, `{"hostId":"h2","agents":["h3"]}`},
		{"server probe", ServerProbe{HostID: "h2", RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "fra-x", Server: emptyStatus,
			Sources: []ProbeSource{{Source: "controller", Name: "controller", TLS: ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: 1}, STUN: ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: 2}}},
			Nodes:   []NodeUse{{Name: "web", Address: "100.64.0.7", HostID: "h3", Visible: true, Preferred: "fra-x", Observed: true}}, Ready: true, Warnings: []string{}},
			`{"hostId":"h2","regionId":900,"regionCode":"fra-x","server":` + sdEmptyStatus + `,"sources":[{"source":"controller","name":"controller",` +
				`"tls":{"ok":true,"latencyMs":1,"error":""},"stun":{"ok":true,"latencyMs":2,"error":""},"region":null,"error":""}],` +
				`"nodes":[{"name":"web","address":"100.64.0.7","hostId":"h3","visible":true,"preferred":"fra-x","observed":true}],"ready":true,"warnings":[]}`},
		{"servers", Servers{Servers: []ServerView{{HostID: "h2", Host: "derp-1", Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.10", RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "fra-x",
			DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", CertName: "sha256-raw:ab", CertNotAfter: &notAfter, UnitActive: true, Health: "ok", Reasons: []string{}, CheckedAt: checked}},
			AvailableVersion: "v1.104.1"},
			`{"servers":[{"hostId":"h2","host":"derp-1","mode":"ip","hostName":"203.0.113.10","regionId":900,"regionCode":"fra-x","derperVersion":"v1.104.1",` +
				`"certName":"sha256-raw:ab","certNotAfter":"2027-11-02T12:00:00Z","unitActive":true,"health":"ok","reasons":[],"checkedAt":"2026-11-02T12:00:00Z",` +
				`"error":""}],"availableVersion":"v1.104.1"}`},
		{"region edits input to remove", RegionEditsInput{Op: "remove", RegionID: 900}, `{"op":"remove","regionId":900}`},
		{"region edits input to set", RegionEditsInput{Op: "set", HostID: "h2", RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "fra-x", RegionName: "Frankfurt X",
			OmitDefaultRegions: true, Acknowledge: "nodes will relay only through fra-x"},
			`{"op":"set","hostId":"h2","regionId":900,"regionCode":"fra-x","regionName":"Frankfurt X","omitDefaultRegions":true,"acknowledge":"nodes will relay only through fra-x"}`},
		{"region edits input to rotate", RegionEditsInput{Op: "rotate", HostID: "h2", RegionID: 900, CertName: "sha256-raw:new"},
			`{"op":"rotate","hostId":"h2","regionId":900,"certName":"sha256-raw:new"}`},
		{"region edits", RegionEdits{Edits: []policy.Edit{{Op: "delete", Path: "/derpMap/Regions/900"}}, Warnings: []string{}},
			`{"edits":[{"op":"delete","path":"/derpMap/Regions/900"}],"warnings":[]}`},
		{"fallback check input", FallbackCheckInput{HostID: "h2", RegionID: 900}, `{"hostId":"h2","regionId":900}`},
		{"fallback check", FallbackCheck{Path: "region:901"}, `{"path":"region:901"}`},
		{"deploy input", DeployInput{HostID: "h2", Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.10"}, `{"hostId":"h2","mode":"ip","hostName":"203.0.113.10"}`},
		{"deploy input without verification", DeployInput{HostID: "h2", Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.10", VerifyClients: &no},
			`{"hostId":"h2","mode":"ip","hostName":"203.0.113.10","verifyClients":false}`},
		{"deployed", Deployed{DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", CertName: "sha256-raw:ab", CertNotAfter: &notAfter},
			`{"derperVersion":"v1.104.1","certName":"sha256-raw:ab","certNotAfter":"2027-11-02T12:00:00Z"}`},
		{"upgrade input", UpgradeInput{HostID: "h2"}, `{"hostId":"h2"}`},
		{"upgraded", Upgraded{From: "v1.103.0", To: "v1.104.1"}, `{"from":"v1.103.0","to":"v1.104.1"}`},
		{"rotate input", CertRotateInput{HostID: "h2"}, `{"hostId":"h2"}`},
		{"rotated", CertRotated{CertName: "sha256-raw:new", CertNotAfter: notAfter}, `{"certName":"sha256-raw:new","certNotAfter":"2027-11-02T12:00:00Z"}`},
		{"region set input", RegionSetInput{HostID: "h2", RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "fra-x", RegionName: "Frankfurt X"},
			`{"hostId":"h2","regionId":900,"regionCode":"fra-x","regionName":"Frankfurt X"}`},
		{"region set input with omission", RegionSetInput{HostID: "h2", RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "fra-x", RegionName: "Frankfurt X", OmitDefaultRegions: true,
			Acknowledge: "nodes will relay only through fra-x"},
			`{"hostId":"h2","regionId":900,"regionCode":"fra-x","regionName":"Frankfurt X","omitDefaultRegions":true,"acknowledge":"nodes will relay only through fra-x"}`},
		{"region remove input", RegionRemoveInput{RegionID: 900}, `{"regionId":900}`},
		{"server remove input", ServerRemoveInput{HostID: "h2"}, `{"hostId":"h2"}`},
		{"region changed", RegionChanged{ETag: "e1"}, `{"etag":"e1"}`},
		{"server removed", ServerRemoved{Removed: []string{"a", "b"}}, `{"removed":["a","b"]}`},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			b, err := json.Marshal(tt.v)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if string(b) != tt.want {
				t.Errorf("got  %s\nwant %s", b, tt.want)
			}
			back := reflect.New(reflect.TypeOf(tt.v))
			if err := json.Unmarshal(b, back.Interface()); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if again, _ := json.Marshal(back.Elem().Interface()); string(again) != tt.want {
				t.Errorf("round trip changed the document: %s", again)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestSdWireDescriptions(t *testing.T) {
	for _, v := range sdWireTypes() {
		rt := reflect.TypeOf(v)
		for i := range rt.NumField() {
			f := rt.Field(i)
			if f.Tag.Get("json") == "" {
				t.Errorf("%s.%s has no json name", rt.Name(), f.Name)
			}
			if f.Tag.Get("jsonschema_description") == "" {
				t.Errorf("%s.%s has no jsonschema_description", rt.Name(), f.Name)
			}
		}
	}
}

func sdSchemaDef(t *testing.T, raw json.RawMessage) (def map[string]any) {
	t.Helper()
	var doc struct {
		Ref  string                    `json:"$ref"`
		Defs map[string]map[string]any `json:"$defs"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &doc); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("schema %s: %v", raw, err)
	}
	def = doc.Defs[strings.TrimPrefix(doc.Ref, "#/$defs/")]
	if def == nil {
		t.Fatalf("schema %s: no definition for %q", raw, doc.Ref)
	}
	return def
}

func sdProps(t *testing.T, v any) (props map[string]map[string]any, required []string) {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := schema.Input(reflect.TypeOf(v))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("input schema of %T: %v", v, err)
	}
	def := sdSchemaDef(t, raw)
	props = map[string]map[string]any{}
	members, _ := def["properties"].(map[string]any)
	for k, m := range members {
		props[k], _ = m.(map[string]any)
	}
	list, _ := def["required"].([]any)
	for _, r := range list {
		required = append(required, r.(string))
	}
	return props, required
}

func TestSdWireSchemas(t *testing.T) {
	for _, v := range []any{ServerInstallInput{}, ServerUninstallInput{}, BinaryUpdateInput{}, CertRenewInput{}, ServerStatusInput{}, NodeProbeInput{},
		ServerProbeInput{}, RegionEditsInput{}, FallbackCheckInput{}, DeployInput{}, UpgradeInput{}, CertRotateInput{}, RegionSetInput{}, RegionRemoveInput{},
		ServerRemoveInput{}} {
		if _, err := schema.Input(reflect.TypeOf(v)); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("input schema of %T: %v", v, err)
		}
	}
	for _, v := range []any{ServerInstalled{}, ServerUninstalled{}, BinaryUpdated{}, CertRenewed{}, ServerStatus{}, NodeProbe{}, ServerProbe{}, Servers{},
		RegionEdits{}, FallbackCheck{}, Deployed{}, Upgraded{}, CertRotated{}, RegionChanged{}, ServerRemoved{}} {
		if _, err := schema.Output(reflect.TypeOf(v)); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("output schema of %T: %v", v, err)
		}
	}

	props, required := sdProps(t, ServerInstallInput{})
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(props["mode"]["enum"], []any{"letsencrypt", "ip"}) || props["hostName"]["maxLength"] != float64(253) {
		t.Errorf("install input mode %v, hostName %v", props["mode"], props["hostName"])
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(required, []string{"mode", "hostName"}) && !reflect.DeepEqual(required, []string{"hostName", "mode"}) {
		t.Errorf("install input requires %v, want mode and hostName only (verifyClients is optional)", required)
	}

	props, _ = sdProps(t, BinaryUpdateInput{})
	if props["version"]["pattern"] != `^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$` {
		t.Errorf("update version pattern %v", props["version"]["pattern"])
	}
	props, _ = sdProps(t, ServerStatusInput{})
	if props["whois"]["maxItems"] != float64(256) {
		t.Errorf("whois %v", props["whois"])
	}
	props, _ = sdProps(t, ServerProbeInput{})
	if props["agents"]["maxItems"] != float64(8) || props["hostId"]["minLength"] != float64(1) {
		t.Errorf("probe input agents %v, hostId %v", props["agents"], props["hostId"])
	}
	props, _ = sdProps(t, RegionEditsInput{})
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(props["op"]["enum"], []any{"set", "remove", "rotate"}) || props["regionCode"]["pattern"] != `^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,31}$` ||
		props["regionName"]["maxLength"] != float64(64) {
		t.Errorf("region edits op %v, regionCode %v, regionName %v", props["op"], props["regionCode"], props["regionName"])
	}
	for _, v := range []any{RegionEditsInput{}, RegionSetInput{}, RegionRemoveInput{}, FallbackCheckInput{}} {
		props, _ := sdProps(t, v)
		if props["regionId"]["minimum"] != float64(900) || props["regionId"]["maximum"] != float64(999) {
			t.Errorf("%T regionId = %v, want 900 to 999", v, props["regionId"])
		}
	}
}

func TestSdProductionDP(t *testing.T) {
	d := productionDP().d
	for name, ok := range map[string]bool{
		"local": d.local != nil, "fetch": d.fetch != nil, "readFile": d.readFile != nil, "lookupUser": d.lookupUser != nil,
		"sockets": d.sockets != nil, "linkViaTailnet": d.linkViaTailnet != nil, "tlsProbe": d.tlsProbe != nil, "stun": d.stun != nil,
		"resolve": d.resolve != nil, "policy": d.policy != nil, "devices": d.devices != nil, "connected": d.connected != nil,
	} {
		if !ok {
			t.Errorf("productionDP leaves deps.%s unset", name)
		}
	}
	if d.arch != runtime.GOARCH || d.version != buildinfo.Version {
		t.Errorf("arch %q and version %q, want %q and %q", d.arch, d.version, runtime.GOARCH, buildinfo.Version)
	}
	_, err := d.local().Status(context.Background())
	if !errors.Is(err, tslocal.ErrUnavailable) || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "/nonexistent/darkpanel/") {
		t.Errorf("the local API of a test binary answered %v; it must dial a socket that never exists, never the host's tailscaled", err)
	}
}

func TestSdAvailable(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name   string
		set    func(h *dpHost, env *core.Env)
		code   errcode.Code
		detail string
	}{
		{"on the tailnet", func(*dpHost, *core.Env) {}, "", ""},
		{"no clock in the environment", func(_ *dpHost, env *core.Env) { env.Clock = nil }, "", ""},
		{"tailscaled does not answer", func(h *dpHost, _ *core.Env) {
			h.local.Errs = map[string]error{"Status": fmt.Errorf("dial: %w", tslocal.ErrUnavailable)}
		}, errcode.TailscaleUnavailable, "tailscaled does not answer on its socket"},
		{"logged out", func(h *dpHost, _ *core.Env) { h.local.Status.BackendState = "NeedsLogin" }, errcode.DERPNotReady,
			"the host is not on the tailnet (tailscaled is NeedsLogin)"},
		{"stopped", func(h *dpHost, _ *core.Env) { h.local.Status.BackendState = "Stopped" }, errcode.DERPNotReady,
			"the host is not on the tailnet (tailscaled is Stopped)"},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := newDPHost(t)
			env := h.env("")
			tt.set(h, &env)
			err := h.dp().available(context.Background(), env)
			if tt.code == "" {
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatalf("available = %v, want nil", err)
				}
				if env.Clock != nil && h.clock.Pending() != 0 {
					t.Errorf("available left %d timers armed", h.clock.Pending())
				}
				return
			}
			var ce *errcode.Error
			if !errors.As(err, &ce) || ce.Code != tt.code || ce.Detail != tt.detail {
				t.Errorf("available = %#v, want %s with %q", err, tt.code, tt.detail)
			}
		})
	}
}

// sdBlockedLocal never answers until its context ends.
type sdBlockedLocal struct{}

func (sdBlockedLocal) Status(ctx context.Context) (*tslocal.Status, error) {
	<-ctx.Done()
	return nil, ctx.Err()
}

func (sdBlockedLocal) WhoIs(context.Context, netip.Addr) (*tslocal.WhoIs, error) {
	return nil, errors.New("unused")
}

func (sdBlockedLocal) DebugDERPRegion(context.Context, string) (*tslocal.DERPRegionReport, error) {
	return nil, errors.New("unused")
}

func TestSdAvailableTimesOut(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	d := h.dp()
	d.d.local = func() localAPI { return sdBlockedLocal{} }
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { done <- d.available(context.Background(), h.env("")) }()
	for deadline := time.Now().Add(10 * time.Second); h.clock.Pending() == 0; {
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			t.Fatal("available never armed its timeout")
		}
		time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
	}
	h.clock.Advance(availableWait)
	select {
	case err := <-done:
		var ce *errcode.Error
		if !errors.As(err, &ce) || ce.Code != errcode.TailscaleUnavailable || ce.Detail != "tailscaled does not answer on its socket" {
			t.Errorf("available = %#v, want tailscale.unavailable", err)
		}
	case <-time.After(10 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("available did not end after availableWait")
	}
}

// sdCredentials saves an enrolled agent's credentials for a controller at address in dir.
func sdCredentials(t *testing.T, dir, address string) {
	t.Helper()
	caPub, caKey, err := ed25519.GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	caTmpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber: big.NewInt(1), Subject: pkix.Name{CommonName: "test agent root"},
		NotBefore: time.Now().Add(-time.Hour), NotAfter: time.Now().Add(24 * time.Hour),
		IsCA: true, BasicConstraintsValid: true, KeyUsage: x509.KeyUsageCertSign,
	}
	caDER, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, caTmpl, caTmpl, caPub, caKey)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ca, err := x509.ParseCertificate(caDER)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	pub, key, err := ed25519.GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	uri, err := url.Parse(transport.HostURI("h1"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tmpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber: big.NewInt(2), NotBefore: time.Now().Add(-time.Hour), NotAfter: time.Now().Add(12 * time.Hour),
		ExtKeyUsage: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageClientAuth}, URIs: []*url.URL{uri}, KeyUsage: x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature,
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, tmpl, ca, pub, caKey)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cert, err := x509.ParseCertificate(der)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	c := &daemon.Credentials{Key: key, Cert: cert, CA: ca, Controller: daemon.Endpoint{Address: address, SAN: transport.ControllerURI("C1")}}
	if _, err := daemon.SaveGeneration(dir, c, daemon.AnyCurrent); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func TestSdLinkViaTailnet(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name, address string // "" leaves the agent not enrolled
		want          bool
	}{
		{"not enrolled", "", false},
		{"controller over an IPv4 tailnet address", "100.64.0.9:7443", true},
		{"controller over an IPv6 tailnet address", "[fd7a:115c:a1e0::9]:7443", true},
		{"controller over a LAN address", "192.0.2.5:7443", false},
		{"controller by name", "controller.example.com:7443", false},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			dir := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "tls")
			if tt.address != "" {
				sdCredentials(t, dir, tt.address)
			}
			got, err := linkViaTailnetIn(dir)
			if err != nil || got != tt.want {
				t.Errorf("linkViaTailnetIn = %v, %v; want %v", got, err, tt.want)
			}
		})
	}
}

// sdCert makes a self-signed certificate for 127.0.0.1 valid between notBefore and notAfter, and its DER.
func sdCert(t *testing.T, notBefore, notAfter time.Time) (tls.Certificate, []byte) {
	t.Helper()
	key, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tmpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber: big.NewInt(7), Subject: pkix.Name{CommonName: "derp test"}, NotBefore: notBefore, NotAfter: notAfter,
		KeyUsage: x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature, ExtKeyUsage: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageServerAuth},
		IPAddresses: []net.IP{net.ParseIP("127.0.0.1")},
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, tmpl, tmpl, &key.PublicKey, key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return tls.Certificate{Certificate: [][]byte{der}, PrivateKey: key}, der
}

// sdHits records the requests a TLS test server received.
type sdHits struct {
	mu    sync.Mutex
	paths []string
}

func (h *sdHits) seen() []string {
	h.mu.Lock()
	defer h.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]string(nil), h.paths...)
}

// handler answers GET /derp/probe with status, or a redirect to /moved when status is 302.
func (h *sdHits) handler(status int) http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		h.mu.Lock()
		h.paths = append(h.paths, r.Method+" "+r.URL.Path)
		h.mu.Unlock()
		switch {
		case r.URL.Path == "/derp/probe" && status == http.StatusFound:
			http.Redirect(w, r, "/moved", http.StatusFound)
		case r.URL.Path == "/derp/probe":
			w.WriteHeader(status)
		default:
			w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
		}
	})
}

// sdTLSServer serves handler over TLS with cert on 127.0.0.1 and returns its port.
func sdTLSServer(t *testing.T, cert tls.Certificate, handler http.Handler) int {
	t.Helper()
	srv := httptest.NewUnstartedServer(handler)
	srv.TLS = &tls.Config{Certificates: []tls.Certificate{cert}, MinVersion: tls.VersionTLS12}
	srv.Config.ErrorLog = log.New(io.Discard, "", 0)
	srv.StartTLS()
	t.Cleanup(srv.Close)
	return srv.Listener.Addr().(*net.TCPAddr).Port
}

func TestSdProbeTLS(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Now()
	cert, der := sdCert(t, now.Add(-time.Hour), now.Add(time.Hour))
	hits := &sdHits{}
	port := sdTLSServer(t, cert, hits.handler(http.StatusOK))
	pin := server.Pin(der)
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		target  probeTarget
		wantErr bool
	}{
		{"the pinned leaf, reached by its IP literal", probeTarget{HostName: "127.0.0.1", Port: port, CertName: pin}, false},
		{"the pinned leaf, reached by address under another name", probeTarget{HostName: "derp.example.test", Address: "127.0.0.1", Port: port, CertName: pin}, false},
		{"another pin", probeTarget{HostName: "127.0.0.1", Port: port, CertName: server.Pin([]byte("another certificate"))}, true},
		{"no pin: the chain is verified and a self-signed leaf fails", probeTarget{HostName: "127.0.0.1", Port: port}, true},
	}
	succeeded := 0
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			d, err := probeTLS(context.Background(), tt.target)
			if (err != nil) != tt.wantErr {
				t.Fatalf("probeTLS = %v, %v; wantErr %v", d, err, tt.wantErr)
			}
			if err == nil {
				succeeded++
				if d <= 0 {
					t.Errorf("latency %v, want a positive duration", d)
				}
			}
		})
	}
	if got := hits.seen(); len(got) != succeeded || (len(got) > 0 && got[0] != "GET /derp/probe") {
		t.Errorf("the server saw %v; only the accepted probes may reach it, as GET /derp/probe", got)
	}
}

func TestSdProbeTLSChecksTheCertificateDates(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Now()
	for _, tt := range []struct {
		name                string
		notBefore, notAfter time.Time
		wantErr             bool
	}{
		{"valid", now.Add(-time.Hour), now.Add(time.Hour), false},
		{"expired", now.Add(-48 * time.Hour), now.Add(-time.Hour), true},
		{"not yet valid", now.Add(time.Hour), now.Add(48 * time.Hour), true},
	} {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			cert, der := sdCert(t, tt.notBefore, tt.notAfter)
			port := sdTLSServer(t, cert, (&sdHits{}).handler(http.StatusOK))
			_, err := probeTLS(context.Background(), probeTarget{HostName: "127.0.0.1", Port: port, CertName: server.Pin(der)})
			if (err != nil) != tt.wantErr {
				t.Errorf("probeTLS = %v, wantErr %v: a pinned leaf is accepted only inside its validity", err, tt.wantErr)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestSdProbeTLSNeedsA200AndFollowsNoRedirect(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Now()
	cert, der := sdCert(t, now.Add(-time.Hour), now.Add(time.Hour))
	for _, status := range []int{http.StatusNoContent, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, http.StatusNotFound, http.StatusFound} {
		t.Run(fmt.Sprint(status), func(t *testing.T) {
			hits := &sdHits{}
			port := sdTLSServer(t, cert, hits.handler(status))
			if _, err := probeTLS(context.Background(), probeTarget{HostName: "127.0.0.1", Port: port, CertName: server.Pin(der)}); err == nil {
				t.Errorf("probeTLS accepted an answer of %d", status)
			}
			for _, p := range hits.seen() {
				if p == "GET /moved" {
					t.Error("probeTLS followed a redirect")
				}
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestSdProbeTLSEndsWithItsContext(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Now()
	cert, der := sdCert(t, now.Add(-time.Hour), now.Add(time.Hour))
	port := sdTLSServer(t, cert, (&sdHits{}).handler(http.StatusOK))
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	cancel()
	if _, err := probeTLS(ctx, probeTarget{HostName: "127.0.0.1", Port: port, CertName: server.Pin(der)}); err == nil {
		t.Error("probeTLS answered under a cancelled context")
	}
}
```

```go
package derp

import (

	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"io/fs"
	"net/netip"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/derp/server"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/tailnet"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/platform/tslocal"

func sdTables(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, ctl bool, like string) []string {
	t.Helper()
	db := h.db
	if ctl {
		db = h.ctlDB
	}
	rows, err := db.Read().Query(`SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name LIKE ? ORDER BY name`, like)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = rows.Close() }()
	var out []string
	for rows.Next() {
		var n string
		if err := rows.Scan(&n); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		out = append(out, n)
	}
	return out
}

func TestSdHostDefaults(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()

	if e := h.env(""); e.Effects != nil || e.ChangeID != "" {
		t.Errorf("env(\"\") = change %q, journal %v, want neither", e.ChangeID, e.Effects)
	}
	env := h.env("c1")
	if env.HostID != "h1" || env.ChangeID != "c1" || env.DB != h.db || env.Exec == nil || env.Files == nil || env.Units == nil ||
		env.Effects == nil || env.Clock == nil || env.Log == nil || env.Principal.Scope != core.ScopeAdmin {
		t.Fatalf("env(c1) = %+v", env)
	}
	if got := env.Clock.Now(); !got.Equal(time.Date(2026, 11, 2, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)) {
		t.Errorf("clock reads %v", got)
	}
	ctl := h.ctlEnv("c2")
	if ctl.HostID != "" || ctl.ChangeID != "c2" || ctl.DB != h.ctlDB || ctl.Agents == nil || ctl.Effects == nil || ctl.Sealer == nil || ctl.Clock == nil || ctl.Units != nil {
		t.Fatalf("ctlEnv(c2) = %+v", ctl)
	}
	if e := h.ctlEnv(""); e.Effects != nil {
		t.Error("ctlEnv(\"\") has an effects journal")
	}

	if got := sdTables(t, h, false, "derp_%"); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"derp_server", "derp_updates"}) {
		t.Errorf("agent tables %v", got)
	}
	if got := sdTables(t, h, true, "derp_%"); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"derp_servers"}) {
		t.Errorf("controller tables %v", got)
	}

	d := h.dp().d
	st, err := d.local().Status(ctx)
	if err != nil || st.BackendState != "Running" || st.Version != "1.104.1" || st.Self == nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(st.Self.Tags, []string{"tag:derp"}) ||
		len(st.TailscaleIPs) != 1 || st.TailscaleIPs[0] != netip.MustParseAddr("100.64.0.10") {
		t.Errorf("Status = %+v, %v; want a running node tagged tag:derp at 100.64.0.10", st, err)
	}
	if d.version != "1.4.0" || d.arch != "amd64" {
		t.Errorf("version %q, arch %q", d.version, d.arch)
	}
	if _, err := d.readFile("/nonexistent"); !errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
		t.Errorf("readFile of a missing path = %v, want fs.ErrNotExist", err)
	}
	if _, _, ok, err := d.lookupUser(server.ServiceUser); err != nil || ok {
		t.Errorf("lookupUser before useradd = ok %v, %v", ok, err)
	}
	if v, err := d.linkViaTailnet(); err != nil || v {
		t.Errorf("linkViaTailnet = %v, %v", v, err)
	}
	if s, err := d.sockets(); err != nil || len(s) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("sockets = %v, %v", s, err)
	}
	if v, err := d.connected(ctx, h.ctlDB); err != nil || v {
		t.Errorf("connected = %v, %v", v, err)
	}
	if _, _, err := d.fetch(ctx, "1.4.0", "v1.104.1", "amd64"); err == nil {
		t.Error("fetch answered before a test set fetchBin")
	}
	if _, err := d.tlsProbe(ctx, probeTarget{HostName: "x.example.com"}); err == nil {
		t.Error("tlsProbe answered before a test set tls")
	}
	if _, err := d.stun(ctx, netip.MustParseAddrPort("127.0.0.1:3478")); err == nil {
		t.Error("stun answered before a test set stun")
	}
	if _, err := d.resolve(ctx, "derp.example.com"); err == nil {
		t.Error("resolve answered before a test set resolved")
	}
}

func TestSdHostDeps(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	d := h.dp().d
	boom := errors.New("boom")

	h.fetchBin = []byte("derper")
	bin, sha, err := d.fetch(ctx, "1.4.0", "v1.104.1", "amd64")
	sum := sha256.Sum256([]byte("derper"))
	if err != nil || string(bin) != "derper" || sha != hex.EncodeToString(sum[:]) {
		t.Errorf("fetch = %q, %q, %v", bin, sha, err)
	}
	h.fetchErr = boom
	if _, _, err := d.fetch(ctx, "1.4.0", "v1.104.1", "arm64"); !errors.Is(err, boom) {
		t.Errorf("fetch with fetchErr = %v", err)
	}
	if want := []string{"1.4.0 v1.104.1 amd64", "1.4.0 v1.104.1 arm64"}; !reflect.DeepEqual(h.fetches, want) {
		t.Errorf("fetches %v, want %v", h.fetches, want)
	}

	h.tls = map[string]dpProbe{"203.0.113.10": {d: 20 * time.Millisecond}, "derp.example.com": {err: boom}}
	if got, err := d.tlsProbe(ctx, probeTarget{HostName: "derp.example.com", Address: "203.0.113.10"}); err != nil || got != 20*time.Millisecond {
		t.Errorf("tlsProbe by address = %v, %v; the address key wins over the name", got, err)
	}
	if _, err := d.tlsProbe(ctx, probeTarget{HostName: "derp.example.com"}); !errors.Is(err, boom) {
		t.Errorf("tlsProbe by name = %v", err)
	}
	server1 := netip.MustParseAddrPort("203.0.113.10:3478")
	h.stun = map[netip.AddrPort]dpProbe{server1: {d: 5 * time.Millisecond}}
	if got, err := d.stun(ctx, server1); err != nil || got != 5*time.Millisecond {
		t.Errorf("stun = %v, %v", got, err)
	}
	if _, err := d.stun(ctx, netip.MustParseAddrPort("203.0.113.11:3478")); err == nil {
		t.Error("stun answered for an address nobody set")
	}
	h.resolved = map[string][]netip.Addr{"derp.example.com": {netip.MustParseAddr("203.0.113.10")}}
	if got, err := d.resolve(ctx, "derp.example.com"); err != nil || len(got) != 1 || got[0].String() != "203.0.113.10" {
		t.Errorf("resolve = %v, %v", got, err)
	}

	h.policy = tailnet.Policy{Text: "{}", ETag: "e1"}
	if p, err := d.policy(ctx, h.ctlEnv("")); err != nil || p.ETag != "e1" {
		t.Errorf("policy = %+v, %v", p, err)
	}
	h.policyErr = boom
	if _, err := d.policy(ctx, h.ctlEnv("")); !errors.Is(err, boom) {
		t.Errorf("policy with policyErr = %v", err)
	}
	h.devices = []tailnet.Device{{ID: "n1", Name: "web"}}
	if got, err := d.devices(ctx, h.ctlEnv("")); err != nil || len(got) != 1 || got[0].ID != "n1" {
		t.Errorf("devices = %v, %v", got, err)
	}
	h.tailnet, h.link = true, true
	h.sockets = []netstat.Socket{{Proto: netstat.ProtoTCP, State: netstat.StateListen}}
	h.users[server.ServiceUser] = [2]int{5, 6}
	if v, err := d.connected(ctx, h.ctlDB); err != nil || !v {
		t.Errorf("connected = %v, %v", v, err)
	}
	if v, err := d.linkViaTailnet(); err != nil || !v {
		t.Errorf("linkViaTailnet = %v, %v", v, err)
	}
	if s, err := d.sockets(); err != nil || len(s) != 1 {
		t.Errorf("sockets = %v, %v", s, err)
	}
	if uid, gid, ok, err := d.lookupUser(server.ServiceUser); err != nil || !ok || uid != 5 || gid != 6 {
		t.Errorf("lookupUser = %d, %d, %v, %v", uid, gid, ok, err)
	}
	h.reads[server.CertDir+"/203.0.113.10.crt"] = []byte("CERT")
	if b, err := d.readFile(server.CertDir + "/203.0.113.10.crt"); err != nil || string(b) != "CERT" {
		t.Errorf("readFile = %q, %v", b, err)
	}
}

func TestSdHostRun(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	useradd := core.Cmd{Path: "/usr/sbin/useradd", Args: []string{"--system", "--no-create-home", "--shell", "/usr/sbin/nologin", server.ServiceUser}}
	userdel := core.Cmd{Path: "/usr/sbin/userdel", Args: []string{server.ServiceUser}}

	if res, err := h.Run(ctx, useradd); err != nil || res.ExitCode != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("useradd = %+v, %v", res, err)
	}
	if ids := h.users[server.ServiceUser]; ids != [2]int{990, 990} {
		t.Errorf("useradd recorded %v, want uid and gid 990", ids)
	}
	if _, err := h.Run(ctx, userdel); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, ok := h.users[server.ServiceUser]; ok {
		t.Error("userdel left the user")
	}

	h.script("/usr/sbin/useradd --system --no-create-home --shell /usr/sbin/nologin "+server.ServiceUser,
		dpRun{res: core.CmdResult{ExitCode: 9, Stderr: []byte("user exists")}}, dpRun{err: errors.New("boom")})
	if res, err := h.Run(ctx, useradd); err != nil || res.ExitCode != 9 {
		t.Errorf("scripted useradd = %+v, %v", res, err)
	}
	if _, err := h.Run(ctx, useradd); err == nil || err.Error() != "boom" {
		t.Errorf("second scripted useradd error = %v", err)
	}
	if _, ok := h.users[server.ServiceUser]; ok {
		t.Error("a failed useradd still added the user")
	}

	for _, cmd := range []core.Cmd{
		{Path: "/usr/bin/tailscale", Args: []string{"up"}},
		{Path: "/usr/bin/systemctl", Args: []string{"restart", server.UnitName}},
		{Path: server.BinaryPath, Args: []string{"-h"}},
		{Path: "/usr/sbin/useradd", Args: []string{"--system", "--no-create-home", "--shell", "/usr/bin/bash", server.ServiceUser}},
		{Path: "/usr/sbin/userdel", Args: []string{"root"}},
	} {
		if _, err := h.Run(ctx, cmd); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "allowlist") {
			t.Errorf("%v: error %v, want a refusal by the production allowlist", cmd, err)
		}
	}
	if _, err := h.Run(ctx, core.Cmd{Path: "/usr/sbin/useradd", Args: []string{"--system", "--no-create-home", "--shell", "/usr/sbin/nologin", "darkpanel"}}); err == nil {
		t.Error("the fake added the user darkpanel; only the module's user may be added")
	}
	if len(h.ran) != 10 || h.ran[0][0] != "/usr/sbin/useradd" || h.ran[1][0] != "/usr/sbin/userdel" {
		t.Errorf("ran %v", h.ran)
	}
}

func TestSdHostFiles(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()

	if err := h.WriteFile(ctx, ID, server.BinaryPath, []byte("bin"), 0o755); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := h.WriteFile(ctx, ID, server.UnitPath, []byte("unit"), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for path, want := range map[string]string{server.BinaryPath: "bin", server.UnitPath: "unit"} {
		if string(h.files[path]) != want {
			t.Errorf("files[%s] = %q", path, h.files[path])
		}
		if b, err := h.dp().d.readFile(path); err != nil || string(b) != want {
			t.Errorf("readFile(%s) = %q, %v; deps.readFile must see what Files wrote", path, b, err)
		}
		if got, err := h.Owns(ctx, ID, path); err != nil || !got {
			t.Errorf("Owns(%s) = %v, %v", path, got, err)
		}
	}
	h.reads[server.BinaryPath] = []byte("seeded")
	if b, _ := h.dp().d.readFile(server.BinaryPath); string(b) != "seeded" {
		t.Errorf("readFile = %q; a seeded read wins over a written file", b)
	}
	if got, _ := h.Owns(ctx, ID, server.BinaryNewPath); got {
		t.Error("an unwritten path is owned")
	}
	if _, err := h.Owns(ctx, "docker", server.BinaryPath); err == nil {
		t.Error("Owns accepted another module")
	}

	refused := map[string]error{
		"another module":            h.WriteFile(ctx, "docker", server.BinaryPath, nil, 0o755),
		"an undeclared path":        h.WriteFile(ctx, ID, "/etc/passwd", nil, 0o644),
		"removing an undeclared unit": h.Remove(ctx, ID, "/etc/systemd/system/other.service"),
		"a service dir as a file":   h.WriteFile(ctx, ID, server.StateDir, nil, 0o644),
		"the user as a file":        h.WriteFile(ctx, ID, server.ServiceUser, nil, 0o644),
		"a managed file":            h.WriteManaged(ctx, ID, server.StateDir, "x", nil, 0o600, 0, 0),
		"a block":                   h.ReplaceBlock(ctx, ID, server.BinaryPath, "b", nil),
		"a restore":                 h.Restore(ctx, ID, core.RestoreEntry{Path: server.BinaryPath}),
	}
	for name, err := range refused {
		if err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s was accepted", name)
		}
	}

	if err := h.Remove(ctx, ID, server.UnitPath); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := h.Remove(ctx, ID, server.UnitPath); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("removing an absent unit = %v; a missing file is not an error", err)
	}
	if _, ok := h.files[server.UnitPath]; ok || h.owned[server.UnitPath] {
		t.Error("Remove left the unit file or its record")
	}
}

func TestSdHostRenames(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()

	h.files[server.BinaryPath] = []byte("old")
	h.files[server.BinaryNewPath] = []byte("new")
	if err := h.Rename(ctx, ID, server.BinaryPath, server.BinaryPrevPath); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := h.Rename(ctx, ID, server.BinaryNewPath, server.BinaryPath); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if string(h.files[server.BinaryPath]) != "new" || string(h.files[server.BinaryPrevPath]) != "old" || len(h.files[server.BinaryNewPath]) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("after the swap: %q, %q, %q", h.files[server.BinaryPath], h.files[server.BinaryPrevPath], h.files[server.BinaryNewPath])
	}
	if err := h.Rename(ctx, ID, server.BinaryPrevPath, server.BinaryPath); err != nil || string(h.files[server.BinaryPath]) != "old" {
		t.Errorf("renaming prev back over the binary = %v, %q; a rename replaces its target", err, h.files[server.BinaryPath])
	}
	if err := h.Rename(ctx, ID, server.BinaryNewPath, server.BinaryPath); !errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
		t.Errorf("renaming a missing file = %v, want fs.ErrNotExist", err)
	}
	h.files[server.UnitPath] = []byte("unit")
	if err := h.Rename(ctx, ID, server.UnitPath, server.BinaryPath); err == nil {
		t.Error("a unit path was renamed over a binary")
	}
	if err := h.Rename(ctx, ID, server.BinaryPath, "/usr/local/lib/darkpanel/other"); err == nil {
		t.Error("a rename to an undeclared path was accepted")
	}

	if err := h.EnsureServiceDir(ctx, ID, server.StateDir); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got, _ := h.Owns(ctx, ID, server.StateDir); !got {
		t.Error("EnsureServiceDir did not record the directory")
	}
	if err := h.EnsureServiceDir(ctx, ID, "/var/lib/darkpanel/other"); err == nil {
		t.Error("EnsureServiceDir accepted an undeclared directory")
	}

	crt := server.CertDir + "/203.0.113.10.crt"
	h.files[crt] = []byte("cert") // derper writes this, never Darkpanel
	if err := h.RenameIn(ctx, ID, server.StateDir, "certs/203.0.113.10.crt", "certs/203.0.113.10.crt.prev"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, ok := h.files[crt]; ok || string(h.files[crt+".prev"]) != "cert" {
		t.Errorf("after RenameIn: %v", h.files)
	}
	if _, err := h.dp().d.readFile(crt); !errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
		t.Errorf("readFile of the renamed certificate = %v, want fs.ErrNotExist", err)
	}
	for name, err := range map[string]error{
		"a missing source":     h.RenameIn(ctx, ID, server.StateDir, "certs/none.crt", "certs/none.crt.prev"),
		"a parent segment":     h.RenameIn(ctx, ID, server.StateDir, "certs/../derper.key", "certs/x"),
		"an undeclared subdir": h.RenameIn(ctx, ID, server.StateDir, "other/a.crt", "other/b.crt"),
		"another directory":    h.RenameIn(ctx, ID, "/var/lib/darkpanel/other", "a", "b"),
		"a name off pattern":   h.RenameIn(ctx, ID, server.StateDir, "certs/203.0.113.10.crt.prev", "certs/.hidden"),
	} {
		if err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s was accepted", name)
		}
	}
	if err := h.RemoveIn(ctx, ID, server.StateDir, "certs/203.0.113.10.crt.prev"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, ok := h.files[crt+".prev"]; ok {
		t.Error("RemoveIn left the file")
	}

	h.files[server.KeyPath] = []byte("key")
	if err := h.RemoveServiceDir(ctx, ID, server.StateDir); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, ok := h.files[server.KeyPath]; ok || h.owned[server.StateDir] {
		t.Errorf("RemoveServiceDir left %v and owned=%v", h.files, h.owned[server.StateDir])
	}
	if string(h.files[server.BinaryPath]) != "old" {
		t.Error("RemoveServiceDir removed a file outside the directory")
	}
}

func TestSdFakeLocal(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	api := h.dp().d.local()
	addr := netip.MustParseAddr("100.64.0.7")

	h.local.WhoIs = map[netip.Addr]*tslocal.WhoIs{addr: {Node: &tslocal.WhoIsNode{Name: "web.example.ts.net.", Tags: []string{"tag:server"}}}}
	if w, err := api.WhoIs(ctx, addr); err != nil || w.Node.Name != "web.example.ts.net." {
		t.Errorf("WhoIs = %+v, %v", w, err)
	}
	if _, err := api.WhoIs(ctx, netip.MustParseAddr("100.64.0.8")); !errors.Is(err, tslocal.ErrPeerNotFound) {
		t.Errorf("WhoIs of an unknown address = %v, want tslocal.ErrPeerNotFound", err)
	}
	h.local.Regions = map[string]*tslocal.DERPRegionReport{"fra-x": {Info: []string{"ok"}}}
	if r, err := api.DebugDERPRegion(ctx, "fra-x"); err != nil || len(r.Info) != 1 {
		t.Errorf("DebugDERPRegion = %+v, %v", r, err)
	}
	var apiErr *tslocal.APIError
	if _, err := api.DebugDERPRegion(ctx, "nope"); !errors.As(err, &apiErr) || apiErr.StatusCode != 404 {
		t.Errorf("DebugDERPRegion of an unknown region = %v, want an APIError 404", err)
	}

	boom := errors.New("boom")
	h.local.Errs = map[string]error{"Status": boom, "WhoIs": boom, "DebugDERPRegion": boom}
	_, e1 := api.Status(ctx)
	_, e2 := api.WhoIs(ctx, addr)
	_, e3 := api.DebugDERPRegion(ctx, "fra-x")
	for i, err := range []error{e1, e2, e3} {
		if !errors.Is(err, boom) {
			t.Errorf("method %d answered %v, want the queued error", i, err)
		}
	}
	h.local.update(func() {
		h.local.Errs = nil
		h.local.Status = nil
	})
	if _, err := api.Status(ctx); !errors.Is(err, tslocal.ErrUnavailable) {
		t.Errorf("Status with none set = %v, want tslocal.ErrUnavailable", err)
	}
}

func TestSdFakeUnits(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	u := h.env("").Units

	if st, err := u.State(ctx, server.UnitName); err != nil || st.LoadState != "not-found" {
		t.Errorf("State before install = %+v, %v", st, err)
	}
	h.units.States[server.UnitName] = core.UnitState{LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "active", SubState: "running"}
	if st, _ := u.State(ctx, server.UnitName); st.ActiveState != "active" {
		t.Errorf("State after a change = %+v", st)
	}
	for _, err := range []error{u.Reload(ctx), u.Enable(ctx, server.UnitName), u.Start(ctx, server.UnitName), u.Restart(ctx, server.UnitName),
		u.Stop(ctx, server.UnitName), u.Disable(ctx, server.UnitName)} {
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	want := []string{"reload", "enable " + server.UnitName, "start " + server.UnitName, "restart " + server.UnitName, "stop " + server.UnitName, "disable " + server.UnitName}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(h.units.Calls, want) {
		t.Errorf("Calls %v, want %v", h.units.Calls, want)
	}
	if st, _ := u.State(ctx, server.UnitName); st.ActiveState != "active" {
		t.Error("Stop changed the state: the fake keeps the state a test set")
	}
	for name, err := range map[string]error{
		"State of tailscaled":    func() error { _, err := u.State(ctx, "tailscaled.service"); return err }(),
		"Start of tailscaled":    u.Start(ctx, "tailscaled.service"),
		"Enable of another unit": u.Enable(ctx, "darkpanel-other.service"),
		"a transient service":    u.StartTransientService(ctx, "darkpanel-task-x-apply-1.service", core.TransientService{}),
		"Kill":                   u.Kill(ctx, server.UnitName, 15),
		"ResetFailed":            u.ResetFailed(ctx, server.UnitName),
	} {
		if err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s was allowed", name)
		}
	}
	if len(h.units.Calls) != len(want) {
		t.Errorf("a refused call was recorded: %v", h.units.Calls)
	}
}

func TestSdJournal(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	env := h.env("c1")
	seq, err := env.Effects.Intend(ctx, core.Effect{Kind: core.EffectCreate, Resource: "derp.user:" + server.ServiceUser}, []byte("secret"))
	if err != nil || seq != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("Intend = %d, %v", seq, err)
	}
	if err := env.Effects.Made(ctx, seq, "derp.user:"+server.ServiceUser, core.StateDoc{}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := env.Effects.Skipped(ctx, seq); err == nil {
		t.Error("an effect already made was skipped")
	}
	got := h.journal.effects()
	if len(got) != 1 || got[0].ChangeID != "c1" || got[0].State != core.EffectMade || got[0].Post == nil {
		t.Errorf("effects %+v", got)
	}
	if s, err := env.Effects.Secret(ctx, seq); err != nil || string(s) != "secret" {
		t.Errorf("Secret = %q, %v", s, err)
	}
}

func TestSdControllerSide(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ctx := context.Background()

	h.addHost("h1", "controller", true)
	h.addHost("h2", "web-1", false)
	rows, err := h.ctlDB.Read().Query(`SELECT id, name, is_local FROM core_hosts ORDER BY id`)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var hosts []string
	for rows.Next() {
		var id, name string
		var local int
		if err := rows.Scan(&id, &name, &local); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		hosts = append(hosts, id+":"+name+":"+string(rune('0'+local)))
	}
	_ = rows.Close()
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(hosts, []string{"h1:controller:1", "h2:web-1:0"}) {
		t.Errorf("core_hosts %v", hosts)
	}

	first := h.addChange("derp.server.deploy", "completed", map[string]any{"hostId": "h2"})
	h.clock.Advance(time.Minute)
	second := h.addChange("derp.server.deploy", "failed", map[string]any{"hostId": "h2"})
	if first == "" || first == second {
		t.Fatalf("change ids %q and %q must be distinct and not empty", first, second)
	}
	var action, state, input, created string
	if err := h.ctlDB.Read().QueryRow(`SELECT c.action, c.state, c.input_json, c.created_at FROM core_changes c JOIN core_plans p ON p.id = c.plan_id WHERE c.id = ?`, first).
		Scan(&action, &state, &input, &created); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if action != "derp.server.deploy" || state != "completed" || input != `{"hostId":"h2"}` || created != "2026-11-02T12:00:00.000000000Z" {
		t.Errorf("change %s = %q %q %q %q", first, action, state, input, created)
	}
	var newest string
	if err := h.ctlDB.Read().QueryRow(`SELECT id FROM core_changes ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1`).Scan(&newest); err != nil || newest != second {
		t.Errorf("newest change %q (%v), want %q", newest, err, second)
	}

	boom := errors.New("boom")
	ag := h.agent("h2")
	if h.agent("h2") != ag {
		t.Error("agent(h2) did not return the same fake twice")
	}
	ag.Reads["derp.server.status/v1"] = func(json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
		return ServerStatus{Installed: true, Mode: "ip", Tags: []string{}, Addresses: []string{}, Visible: []Visibility{}}, nil
	}
	ag.Reads["derp.node.probe/v1"] = func(json.RawMessage) (any, error) { return nil, boom }
	ag.Resources["tailscale.status"] = map[string]any{"preferredDerp": "fra-x"}

	env := h.ctlEnv("")
	client, err := env.Agents.Agent(ctx, "h2")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	raw, err := client.Read(ctx, env.Principal, core.ActionRef{Name: "derp.server.status", Version: 1}, json.RawMessage(`{"whois":["100.64.0.7"]}`))
	var st ServerStatus
	if err != nil || json.Unmarshal(raw, &st) != nil || !st.Installed || st.Mode != "ip" {
		t.Fatalf("Read = %s, %v", raw, err)
	}
	if _, err := client.Read(ctx, env.Principal, core.ActionRef{Name: "derp.node.probe", Version: 1}, json.RawMessage(`{}`)); !errors.Is(err, boom) {
		t.Errorf("a failing read = %v, want the read's own error", err)
	}
	if _, err := client.Read(ctx, env.Principal, core.ActionRef{Name: "derp.unknown", Version: 1}, json.RawMessage(`{}`)); err == nil {
		t.Error("a read nobody set was answered")
	}
	wantCalls := []string{`derp.server.status/v1 {"whois":["100.64.0.7"]}`, `derp.node.probe/v1 {}`, `derp.unknown/v1 {}`}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(ag.Calls, wantCalls) {
		t.Errorf("Calls %v, want %v", ag.Calls, wantCalls)
	}
	res, err := client.Resource(ctx, env.Principal, "tailscale.status")
	if err != nil || string(res) != `{"preferredDerp":"fra-x"}` {
		t.Errorf("Resource = %s, %v", res, err)
	}
	if _, err := client.Resource(ctx, env.Principal, "tailscale.latency"); err == nil {
		t.Error("a resource nobody set was answered")
	}
	var coded *errcode.Error
	if _, err := env.Agents.Agent(ctx, "h9"); !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != errcode.NotFound {
		t.Errorf("Agent of an unknown host = %v, want not_found", err)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Every file is in package `derp` and follows `internal/modules/tailscale/` (`deps.go`, `store.go`, `tailscale.go`, `wire.go`): a doc comment on every exported identifier, comments elsewhere only for a non-obvious why, errors prefixed `derp:`. Names and signatures are exactly those of Contracts "DERP internals", "DERP wire shapes" and "DERP test host".

1. `migrations/agent/0001_derp.sql` and `migrations/controller/0001_derp.sql`: the SQL of Contracts "DERP internals", character for character. `store.go` embeds each with `//go:embed migrations/agent/*.sql` and `//go:embed migrations/controller/*.sql` and returns `fs.Sub(..., "migrations/agent")` and `"migrations/controller"` from `agentMigrations()` and `controllerMigrations()` (a panic on the constant-path `fs.Sub` error, as the tailscale module does).
2. `store.go`: the time format `2006-01-02T15:04:05.000000000Z` (UTC), `storeTime` (a zero time is stored as `''`) and a parse that maps `''` back to the zero `time.Time`; `queryer`; the `localServer`, `updateRow` and `serverRecord` types and the functions of Contracts, with the signatures given there. Reads use the SQL columns in the table order; writes go through `db.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {...})`. `insertLocalServer` always writes `id = 1`, so a second insert fails on the primary key; an `UPDATE` that matches no row (`setLocalBinary`, `setLocalCert`, `setUpdatePhase`) is an error `derp: <what>: no such row`, built as the tailscale module's `storeUpdate` does; `deleteLocalServer` and `deleteServerRecord` ignore a missing row; `upsertServerRecord` is `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (host_id) DO UPDATE SET` every other column; `loadServerRecords` orders by `host_id`. `setLocalBinary` sets `derper_version`, `binary_sha256` and `updated_at` only; `setLocalCert` sets `cert_name`, `cert_not_after` and `updated_at` only. Database-side CHECK failures surface as errors wrapped with `derp:`.
3. `derpmap.go`: the three types and three functions of Contracts, pure. `parseDERPMap` runs `policy.Canonical([]byte(text))`, decodes the result into a private struct with Tailscale's member names (`derpMap`, `OmitDefaultRegions`, `Regions` as `map[string]` of a region with `RegionID`, `RegionCode`, `RegionName`, `Nodes` of `Name`, `RegionID`, `HostName`, `CertName`, `IPv4`, `IPv6`, `STUNPort`, `DERPPort`), and returns the exported shape. `Present` is true when `derpMap` is an object (absent or `null` is false; any other kind is an error). A `Regions` key refused unless `strconv.Atoi` accepts it, `strconv.Itoa` of the result equals the key and it equals the region's `RegionID`; the error names the key. `Regions` of a present map is a non-nil map. `regionOf` compares `strings.EqualFold` over the nodes of the custom regions (ids 900 to 999) only; `customRegions` returns those ids ascending.
4. `wire.go`: every type of Contracts "DERP wire shapes", in the order given, with the `json` names given and a `jsonschema_description` on every field (one short sentence, as `internal/modules/tailscale/wire.go` writes them). The schema tags: `ServerInstallInput.Mode` and `DeployInput.Mode` `enum=letsencrypt,enum=ip`; `HostName` fields `maxLength=253`; `VerifyClients *bool` `nullable` with `omitempty`; `BinaryUpdateInput.Version` `pattern=^v[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+$`; `ServerStatusInput.Whois` `maxItems=256`; `ServerProbeInput.Agents` `maxItems=8`; `RegionEditsInput.Op` `enum=set,enum=remove,enum=rotate`; `RegionEditsInput.RegionCode` and `RegionSetInput.RegionCode` `pattern=^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0\\,31}$` (commas inside a tag value are escaped `\\,`, as in `internal/modules/firewall/model/rule.go`); `RegionName` fields `maxLength=64`; `RegionEditsInput.RegionID`, `RegionSetInput.RegionID`, `RegionRemoveInput.RegionID` and `FallbackCheckInput.RegionID` `minimum=900,maximum=999`; `HostID` fields `minLength=1`, the pattern `core.host.*` inputs use (`internal/controller/coreactions/hosts.go`); every pointer field of an output type carries `nullable` — the `*time.Time` fields and `NodeProbe.Region` and `ProbeSource.Region` (`*RegionDiagnostics`) — because the registry's `TestPointerOutputsAreNullable` (`internal/modules/registry_test.go`) refuses an output pointer whose schema rejects null once Task 29 registers the actions. No tag may make `verifyClients`, `agents`, `whois`, `address`, `certName`, `regionId` (of `NodeProbeInput`), `hostId` (of `RegionEditsInput`), `regionCode`, `regionName`, `omitDefaultRegions`, `acknowledge` required: a member with `omitempty` is optional. `RegionEdits.Edits` is `[]policy.Edit` and imports `internal/modules/tailnet/policy`.
5. `deps.go`:
   - The constant block of Contracts "DERP internals" (`lockKey` and the others, with the doc comments the tailscale module's constants have), `type dp struct{ d deps }`, `deps`, `localAPI`, `probeTarget`, `productionDP`, `probeTLS`, `available`, `ownedPaths`.
   - `productionDP()`: the socket is `tslocal.DefaultSocket`, except that when `testing.Testing()` it is `/nonexistent/darkpanel/tailscaled.sock`, so a test binary that links the module never reaches the host's tailscaled (the tailscale module's `absentSocket`, same reason, same comment). `local` returns one shared `tslocal.New(socket)`; `fetch` builds once an `artifact.Source{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 5 * time.Minute}, BaseURL: trustedkeys.BaseURL(), Keys: trustedkeys.Keys(), Releases: update.NewIndexSource(trustedkeys.BaseURL(), &http.Client{Timeout: 30 * time.Second}, core.SystemClock{}).Releases}` and calls `artifact.Fetch`; `version` is `buildinfo.Version`; `arch` is `runtime.GOARCH`; `readFile` is `os.ReadFile`; `lookupUser` is a function that calls `os/user.Lookup`, parses the uid and gid, and maps `user.UnknownUserError` to `ok == false` with a nil error; `sockets` is `netstat.Sockets`; `linkViaTailnet` calls `linkViaTailnetIn(paths.Production().AgentTLSDir)`; `tlsProbe` is `probeTLS`; `stun` is `stun.Probe`; `resolve` is `net.DefaultResolver.LookupNetIP(ctx, "ip", host)`; `policy`, `devices` and `connected` are `tailnet.ReadPolicy`, `tailnet.ListDevices` and `tailnet.Connected`.
   - `linkViaTailnetIn(dir string) (bool, error)`: `daemon.LoadCredentials(dir)`; `daemon.ErrNotEnrolled` is `false, nil`; another error is wrapped with `derp:`; the controller address is split with `net.SplitHostPort`, its host parsed with `netip.ParseAddr` (a host name or an unparsable address is `false, nil`), and the answer is `link.IsTailscaleAddr(addr)`.
   - `probeTLS` as Contracts describes, with these decisions: an `http.Client` with a 5 s `Timeout` (the request context also ends it), `CheckRedirect` returning `http.ErrUseLastResponse`, and a `Transport` with its own `tls.Config` (`MinVersion: tls.VersionTLS12`). The URL is `https://` + `net.JoinHostPort(<Address, else HostName>, strconv.Itoa(Port))` + `/derp/probe`, a GET. The TLS `ServerName` is `HostName` unless `netip.ParseAddr(HostName)` succeeds (then it is empty). With a `CertName`, the config sets `InsecureSkipVerify: true` and a `VerifyPeerCertificate` that parses the leaf (`rawCerts[0]`), requires `server.Pin(leaf.Raw) == CertName` and `NotBefore <= time.Now() <= NotAfter`, and fails otherwise; `//nolint:gosec // G402: the leaf is checked against its pin in VerifyPeerCertificate`. Without a `CertName` the default chain verification runs against the system roots for `ServerName`. The latency is the time from just before the request to the response headers; the body is read through `io.LimitReader` (at most 64 KiB) and discarded; any status but 200 is an error naming the status. Every error is wrapped `derp: probe <host>: %w`.
   - `available` exactly as the tailscale module's (`internal/modules/tailscale/deps.go`, `available`): a `context.WithCancel` armed with `env.Clock.AfterFunc(availableWait, cancel)` (`core.SystemClock{}` when `env.Clock` is nil), the timer stopped on return; an error from `Status` is `errcode.Wrap(errcode.TailscaleUnavailable, err, "tailscaled does not answer on its socket")`; a `BackendState` other than `Running` is `errcode.New(errcode.DERPNotReady, "the host is not on the tailnet (tailscaled is <state>)")`.
   - `ownedPaths()` returns exactly the six entries of Contracts, in that order, with `core.OwnedUser`, `OwnedFile`, `OwnedUnit` and `OwnedServiceDir` kinds and an empty `Pattern` for the service directory.
6. `derp.go`: `type module struct{ d dp }`, `Module()` returns `module{d: productionDP()}`, the doc comment names what the module will do; `Manifest()` is unchanged. Task 29 adds the declarations; this task adds none.
7. `testhost_test.go` (test code, pinned by `testhost_check_test.go` above; modelled on `internal/modules/tailscale/testhost_test.go` and `fake_local_test.go`). Declare the compile-time assertions that `*dpHost` is a `core.Runner` and a `core.FileWriter`, `*dpUnits` a `core.Units`, `*dpJournal` a `core.EffectJournal`, and `thLocal{}` a `localAPI`. Types and fields are exactly Contracts "DERP test host"; additionally `dpHost` holds a `sync.Mutex`, and `dpLocal`, `dpUnits` and `dpAgent` hold their own. Behaviour:
   - `newDPHost(t)`: a temporary directory; an agent database (`store.Open`, `store.CoreMigrations(store.SideAgent)` plus `{Module: ID, FS: agentMigrations()}`, `store.Migrate` with `Side: store.SideAgent`, `BinaryVersion: "test"`, a backup root in the temporary directory, `Keep: 5`, `Now` the start time) and a controller database the same way with `store.SideController` and `controllerMigrations()`; a migration failure fails the test. The clock is a `coretest.NewFakeClock` at `2026-11-02 12:00:00 UTC`. The fake local API holds a `tslocal.Status{Version: "1.104.1", BackendState: "Running", TailscaleIPs: [100.64.0.10], Self: &PeerStatus{ID "n1", HostName "derp-host", DNSName "derp-host.example.ts.net.", OS "linux", TailscaleIPs, Tags ["tag:derp"], Online true}, MagicDNSSuffix "example.ts.net", CurrentTailnet}`. `units.States` holds only the derper unit, `{LoadState: "not-found"}`. Every map field is non-nil and empty; `link` and `tailnet` are false; `policy` is the zero `tailnet.Policy`.
   - `dp()`: the `deps` of Contracts pointing at the host. `local` returns `thLocal{h.local}`; `version` is `"1.4.0"` and `arch` `"amd64"`; `readFile` answers `reads`, then `files`, else `&fs.PathError{Op: "open", Path: name, Err: fs.ErrNotExist}`; `fetch` appends `"<dpVersion> <want> <arch>"` to `fetches` and answers `fetchErr` when set, else an error `dpHost: nothing is faked for fetch` when `fetchBin` is nil, else a copy of `fetchBin` and its lower-case hex SHA-256; `lookupUser` answers `users`; `sockets` a clone of `sockets`; `linkViaTailnet` answers `link`; `tlsProbe` looks up `tls[t.Address]` when `Address` is not empty and present, else `tls[t.HostName]`, and answers `dpProbe{d, err}` or an error `dpHost: nothing is faked for <key>`; `stun` looks up `stun[server]` the same way; `resolve` answers `resolved[host]` or an error `dpHost: no such host <host>`; `policy` answers `policyErr` when set, else `h.policy`; `devices` a clone of `devices`; `connected` answers `tailnet`.
   - `env(changeID)`: `core.Env{HostID: "h1", ChangeID, Principal: core.Principal{Actor: "user:u1", UserID: "u1", Scope: core.ScopeAdmin}, DB: h.db, Exec: h, Files: h, Sealer: coretest.Sealer{}, Clock: h.clock, Log: a logger to io.Discard, Units: h.units}`, with `Effects: h.journal` and the journal's change id set only when `changeID != ""`. `ctlEnv(changeID)`: the same principal, `HostID` empty, `DB: h.ctlDB`, `Sealer`, `Clock`, `Log`, `Units` nil, `Effects` as above, and `Agents` a fresh `coretest.MapHosts` built at each call with one `*coretest.FakeAgent` per `h.agents` entry: its `ReadFunc` records `"<name>/v<version> <input JSON>"` in the agent's `Calls`, answers the agent's `Reads["<name>/v<version>"]` function (json-marshalled result, or its error unchanged) and an error `dpHost: no read <name>/v<version>` when none is set; its `ResourceFunc` json-marshals `Resources[name]` or errors `dpHost: no resource <name>`. An unknown host id is `coretest.MapHosts`' own not_found.
   - `script(vector, runs...)`, `agent(hostID)` (creates once: non-nil `Reads` and `Resources`), `addHost(id, name, local)` (an `INSERT INTO core_hosts (id, name, is_local, created_at)` with the clock's time in the store format) and `addChange(action, state, input)` (inserts a `core_plans` row and a `core_changes` row; ids `p<n>` and `c<n>` from a counter on the host; the plan's `host_id` is `''` (the column is `NOT NULL` without a default, `internal/core/store/migrations/controller/0002_lifecycle.sql`) and the change's `plan_id` is the inserted plan's id (unique, a foreign key); the change has `host_id` empty, version 1, class `composite`, `compensated_by` empty, actor `user:u1`, the given `state`, `input_json` the JSON of `input`, `created_at` and `updated_at` the clock's time in the store format `2006-01-02T15:04:05.000000000Z`; the plan has the same action, version 1, actor, empty-string hashes, `[]` for diff and guards, empty confirmation, and `created_at`/`expires_at` an hour apart) returns the change id.
   - `Run`: record the vector in `ran`; refuse a vector the production allowlist does not admit with an error containing `is not in the production exec allowlist` (use `platform.ProductionRules()` as the tailscale host's `thAllowed` does); refuse any `useradd` or `userdel` whose user is not `server.ServiceUser`; answer the next scripted answer of the joined vector, else exit 0; when the answer is exit 0 with no error, a `useradd` adds `users[server.ServiceUser] = [2]int{990, 990}` and a `userdel` deletes it.
   - Files: `thOwned(module, path, kind)` refuses a module other than `ID` and a path/kind pair `ownedPaths()` does not declare. `WriteFile` accepts the three binary paths (kind file) and `UnitPath` (kind unit), stores a copy in `files` and sets `owned[path]`. `Remove` accepts the same paths, deletes the file and its record, and answers nil for an absent file. `Rename(from, to)` accepts two of the three binary paths, answers `fs.ErrNotExist` (wrapped in a `*fs.PathError`) for a missing source, and moves the bytes over the target. `EnsureServiceDir` accepts `StateDir` only and sets `owned[StateDir]`. `RenameIn(dir, from, to)` and `RemoveIn(dir, name)` accept `StateDir` only; a name is `<name>` or `certs/<name>` where `<name>` matches `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,127}$`; any other form (a parent segment, a second slash, another subdirectory) is refused; the files live at `StateDir + "/" + name`; `RenameIn` answers `fs.ErrNotExist` for a missing source. `RemoveServiceDir` accepts `StateDir` only, deletes every `files` key under `StateDir + "/"` and clears `owned[StateDir]`. `Owns(module, path)` refuses another module and answers `owned[path]` or, when that is false, `platform.NewOwnership(h.db).Owns(ctx, ID, path)`. `WriteManaged`, `RemoveManaged`, `ReplaceBlock`, `RemoveBlock`, `Restore`, `ReadSysctl` and `WriteSysctl` answer an `errTHUnused` error.
   - `dpUnits`: `State` accepts only `server.UnitName` and answers `States[unit]` (`{LoadState: "not-found"}` when absent); `Reload`, `Enable`, `Disable`, `Start`, `Stop` and `Restart` append `"reload"`, `"enable <unit>"` (`Enable` and `Disable` join several file names with `,`), `"disable <unit>"`, `"start <unit>"`, `"stop <unit>"`, `"restart <unit>"` to `Calls` and change no state; every other unit, and `Kill`, `ResetFailed`, `StartTransientService`, `StartTransientTimer` and `List`, is refused with an error and records nothing.
   - `dpLocal`: `thLocal` adapts it (a field and a method of one name cannot share a type). `Status` and `WhoIs` answer `Errs[<method>]` first; `Status` is a copy of the field, or an error wrapping `tslocal.ErrUnavailable` when nil; `WhoIs` answers `WhoIs[addr]` or `tslocal.ErrPeerNotFound`; `DebugDERPRegion` answers `Regions[region]` or `&tslocal.APIError{StatusCode: 404, Message: "no such region"}`. All three lock a mutex, and `update(f)` runs `f` under it.
   - `dpJournal`: an in-memory `core.EffectJournal` with the methods `Intend`, `Made`, `Skipped`, `List`, `Secret` and `effects()` of `tsJournal` (same state rules: only an intended effect can be made or skipped; `Intend` sets `Seq`, `State` and `ChangeID`).
   - Declare `thStart` (the clock's start) and the other constants and errors with the `th` prefix; declare nothing the Contracts name twice.

### Task 13: harness: TestM5DERP

**Files:**

- Create: `test/harness/m5_derp_helpers_test.go` (the helpers the steps call)
- Test: `test/harness/m5_derp_unit_test.go` (the `Unit` tests of the helpers; they need no VM)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Contracts "Harness M5 DERP" (this task's text), "DERP server unit" (`server.Config`, `ModeIP`, `CheckConfig`, `UnitFile`, `CertFiles`, `Pin`, `ParseCert`, and the constants `ServiceUser`, `BinaryPath`, `UnitName`, `UnitPath`, `StateDir`, `CertDir`, `KeyPath`, `HTTPPort`, `HTTPSPort`, `STUNPort`), "Derper artifact" (`artifact.DerperVersion`, `artifact.BinaryName`). Existing code: `stun.Request` and `stun.Parse` of `internal/modules/tailscale/stun`; of the harness, `fleet`, `harnessImages`, `NewLab`, `m5Configs`, `serveReleases`, `scenarioInstall`, `m3Run`, `m3dxShell`, `unitState`, `eventually`, `osFlag`, `m2Flag`, `agentIPv4` and `scannerIPv4`.
- Produces: `TestM5DERP` behind the flag `-m5d`, and the flag `-derper` (default `../../bin/harness/derper-v1.104.1-linux-amd64`, relative to the package directory as `-bin` is, which is `bin/harness/` + `artifact.BinaryName(artifact.DerperVersion, "amd64")` from the repository root). Task 30 builds that file and runs `TestM5DERP -m5d` in the workflow.
- Reading of Contracts "Harness M5 DERP": the runner reaches a VM only through its SSH connection, which carries TCP (`vm.VM.DialContext` dials from inside the VM) and no UDP, and the lab's internet link is not on the runner. "From the runner" therefore means: the runner's code makes the TLS request through the scanner VM's network (the scanner shares the lab's internet link with the agent), and the STUN request is a datagram that a `python3` one-liner on the scanner sends, whose answer the runner decodes with `stun.Parse`. The VM's UFW allows only 22/tcp and the agent port, so the scenario opens the three DERP ports with `ufw allow`; in production `firewall.rules.apply` does that (Task 21).
