Create `internal/modules/derp/binary_update_test.go`:

```go
package derp

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"maps"
	"net/netip"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/derp/artifact"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/derp/server"
)

const (
	buFrom   = "v1.99.0"
	buHostIP = "93.184.216.34"
	buPin    = "sha256-raw:0123456789abcdef"
)

var (
	buOld   = []byte("derper-old")
	buNew   = []byte("derper-new")
	buOther = []byte("derper-from-an-earlier-update")
)

func buSum(b []byte) string {
	s := sha256.Sum256(b)
	return hex.EncodeToString(s[:])
}

func buInput(version string) json.RawMessage {
	b, err := json.Marshal(BinaryUpdateInput{Version: version})
	if err != nil {
		panic(err)
	}
	return b
}

// buCall is one host change of the update and the phase of change c1's update row, then the state of its newest
// effect, when the change was made.
type buCall struct{ Call, State string }

type buTrace struct {
	h     *dpHost
	calls []buCall
}

func (tr *buTrace) note(ctx context.Context, call string) {
	state := "no row"
	if u, ok, _ := loadUpdate(ctx, tr.h.db.Read(), "c1"); ok {
		state = u.Phase
	}
	if effects, _ := tr.h.journal.List(ctx); len(effects) > 0 {
		state += "/" + string(effects[len(effects)-1].State)
	}
	tr.calls = append(tr.calls, buCall{call, state})
}

// buFiles traces WriteFile, Rename and Remove; a call named in fail is traced and then refused.
type buFiles struct {
	core.FileWriter
	tr   *buTrace
	fail map[string]error
}

func (f *buFiles) do(ctx context.Context, call string, run func() error) error {
	f.tr.note(ctx, call)
	if err := f.fail[call]; err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return run()
}

func (f *buFiles) WriteFile(ctx context.Context, module, path string, data []byte, mode fs.FileMode) error {
	return f.do(ctx, fmt.Sprintf("write %s %04o", path, uint32(mode.Perm())), func() error {
		return f.FileWriter.WriteFile(ctx, module, path, data, mode)
	})
}

func (f *buFiles) Rename(ctx context.Context, module, from, to string) error {
	return f.do(ctx, "rename "+from+" "+to, func() error { return f.FileWriter.Rename(ctx, module, from, to) })
}

func (f *buFiles) Remove(ctx context.Context, module, path string) error {
	return f.do(ctx, "remove "+path, func() error { return f.FileWriter.Remove(ctx, module, path) })
}

// buUnits traces Restart. The first failRestarts restarts are refused; onRestart, when set, runs after each
// successful one with the number of successful restarts so far.
type buUnits struct {
	core.Units
	tr           *buTrace
	failRestarts int
	restarts     int
	onRestart    func(n int)
}

func (u *buUnits) Restart(ctx context.Context, unit string) error {
	u.tr.note(ctx, "restart "+unit)
	if u.failRestarts > 0 {
		u.failRestarts--
		return errors.New("simulated failure of the restart")
	}
	if err := u.Units.Restart(ctx, unit); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	u.restarts++
	if u.onRestart != nil {
		u.onRestart(u.restarts)
	}
	return nil
}

// buSetup is a host whose derper is installed at buFrom, healthy, with change c1's environment; the fetch
// answers buNew.
type buSetup struct {
	h       *dpHost
	d       dp
	tr      *buTrace
	files   *buFiles
	units   *buUnits
	env     core.Env
	targets []probeTarget
	stuns   []netip.AddrPort
}

var buStunAddr = netip.MustParseAddrPort("127.0.0.1:3478")

func buNewSetup(t *testing.T) *buSetup {
	t.Helper()
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.fetchBin = buNew
	now := h.clock.Now()
	err := insertLocalServer(context.Background(), h.db, localServer{
		ChangeID: "c0", Mode: "ip", HostName: buHostIP, DerperVersion: buFrom, BinarySHA256: buSum(buOld), CertName: buPin,
		CertNotAfter: now.Add(300 * 24 * time.Hour), InstalledAt: now, UpdatedAt: now,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h.files[server.BinaryPath] = buOld
	h.owned[server.BinaryPath] = true
	h.units.States = map[string]core.UnitState{server.UnitName: {LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "active", SubState: "running", MainPID: 2000}}
	h.tls["127.0.0.1"] = dpProbe{d: time.Millisecond}
	h.stun[buStunAddr] = dpProbe{d: time.Millisecond}
	s := &buSetup{h: h, d: h.dp(), tr: &buTrace{h: h}}
	tlsProbe, stun := s.d.d.tlsProbe, s.d.d.stun
	s.d.d.tlsProbe = func(ctx context.Context, tg probeTarget) (time.Duration, error) {
		s.targets = append(s.targets, tg)
		return tlsProbe(ctx, tg)
	}
	s.d.d.stun = func(ctx context.Context, a netip.AddrPort) (time.Duration, error) {
		s.stuns = append(s.stuns, a)
		return stun(ctx, a)
	}
	s.env = h.env("c1")
	s.files = &buFiles{FileWriter: s.env.Files, tr: s.tr, fail: map[string]error{}}
	s.units = &buUnits{Units: s.env.Units, tr: s.tr}
	s.env.Files, s.env.Units = s.files, s.units
	return s
}

// buFilesAre reports whether the files the host holds are exactly want.
func buFilesAre(h *dpHost, want map[string][]byte) bool {
	return maps.EqualFunc(h.files, want, slices.Equal[[]byte])
}

func (s *buSetup) planEnv() core.Env { return s.h.env("") }

func (s *buSetup) effects(t *testing.T) []core.Effect {
	t.Helper()
	effects, err := s.h.journal.List(context.Background())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return effects
}

func (s *buSetup) update(t *testing.T) updateRow {
	t.Helper()
	u, ok, err := loadUpdate(context.Background(), s.h.db.Read(), "c1")
	if err != nil || !ok {
		t.Fatalf("update row of c1: found %v, error %v", ok, err)
	}
	return u
}

func (s *buSetup) server(t *testing.T) localServer {
	t.Helper()
	row, ok, err := loadLocalServer(context.Background(), s.h.db.Read())
	if err != nil || !ok {
		t.Fatalf("derp_server row: found %v, error %v", ok, err)
	}
	return row
}

// run calls the update's task while advancing the host's clock by one poll whenever it waits.
func (s *buSetup) run(t *testing.T) (json.RawMessage, error) {
	t.Helper()
	var out json.RawMessage
	var err error
	buDrive(t, s.h, func() {
		out, err = s.d.binaryUpdate().Handler.Task(context.Background(), s.env, buInput(artifact.DerperVersion))
	})
	return out, err
}

func buDrive(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, call func()) {
	t.Helper()
	done := make(chan struct{})
	go func() {
		defer close(done)
		call()
	}()
	for deadline := time.Now().Add(20 * time.Second); ; {
		select {
		case <-done:
			return
		default:
		}
		if h.clock.Pending() > 0 {
			h.clock.Advance(binaryUpdatePoll)
		} else {
			time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
		}
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			t.Fatal("the call did not finish")
		}
	}
}

func buCoded(t *testing.T, err error, code errcode.Code) *errcode.Error {
	t.Helper()
	var coded *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != code {
		t.Fatalf("error %v, want code %s", err, code)
	}
	return coded
}

func TestBuSpec(t *testing.T) {
	def := newDPHost(t).dp().binaryUpdate()
	sp, h := def.Spec, def.Handler
	if sp.Name != "derp.binary.update" || sp.Version != 1 || sp.Class != core.ClassCompensable || sp.Side != core.SideAgent ||
		!slices.Equal(sp.LockKeys, []string{"derp"}) || sp.ApplyTimeout != 15*time.Minute || !sp.Task || !sp.LongRunning ||
		sp.Available == nil || len(sp.Guards) != 0 || sp.Internal || sp.Converges {
		t.Errorf("spec %+v, want a long-running compensable agent task locking derp for 15 minutes, with an availability check", sp)
	}
	if def.InputType != reflect.TypeFor[BinaryUpdateInput]() || def.OutputType != reflect.TypeFor[BinaryUpdated]() {
		t.Errorf("input %v and output %v, want BinaryUpdateInput and BinaryUpdated", def.InputType, def.OutputType)
	}
	if h.Plan == nil || h.Task == nil || h.Verify == nil || h.Apply != nil {
		t.Errorf("handler has Plan %v Task %v Verify %v Apply %v, want a task handler", h.Plan != nil, h.Task != nil, h.Verify != nil, h.Apply != nil)
	}
}

func TestBuPlan(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	s := buNewSetup(t)
	h := s.d.binaryUpdate().Handler
	out, err := h.Plan(ctx, s.planEnv(), buInput(artifact.DerperVersion))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	line := func(op, text string) core.DiffLine { return core.DiffLine{Op: op, Text: text} }
	want := []core.DiffLine{
		line("-", "derper "+buFrom),
		line("+", "derper "+artifact.DerperVersion),
		line(" ", "the previous binary is kept as "+server.BinaryPrevPath),
		line(" ", fmt.Sprintf("roll back to derper %s if the unit, the TLS probe, STUN and tailscaled are not healthy within %d s", buFrom, int(healthWindow/time.Second))),
	}
	if !slices.Equal(out.Diff, want) || out.Confirmation != "" || len(out.Guards) != 0 || len(out.StateHash) != 64 {
		t.Errorf("plan %+v, want diff %+v, no confirmation, no guard and a 64-digit hash", out, want)
	}
	if len(s.h.ran) != 0 || len(s.h.fetches) != 0 || len(s.h.units.Calls) != 0 || len(s.effects(t)) != 0 || !buFilesAre(s.h, map[string][]byte{server.BinaryPath: buOld}) {
		t.Errorf("plan ran %v, fetched %v, called units %v, journaled %v and left files %v, want nothing", s.h.ran, s.h.fetches, s.h.units.Calls, s.effects(t), s.h.files)
	}
	again, err := h.Plan(ctx, s.planEnv(), buInput(artifact.DerperVersion))
	if err != nil || again.StateHash != out.StateHash {
		t.Errorf("a second plan hash %q (error %v), want %q", again.StateHash, err, out.StateHash)
	}
	if err := setLocalBinary(ctx, s.h.db, "v1.98.0", buSum(buOld), s.h.clock.Now()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if other, err := h.Plan(ctx, s.planEnv(), buInput(artifact.DerperVersion)); err != nil || other.StateHash == out.StateHash {
		t.Errorf("plan over another installed version: hash %q (error %v), want one different from %q", other.StateHash, err, out.StateHash)
	}
}

func TestBuRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		version string
		setup   func(t *testing.T, s *buSetup)
		code    errcode.Code
		field   string
		detail  string
	}{
		{"a version this agent does not carry", "v1.98.0", nil, errcode.InvalidInput, "/version",
			"this agent's darkpanel release carries derper " + artifact.DerperVersion + "; update the agent first"},
		{"something that is no version", "latest", nil, errcode.InvalidInput, "/version", "update the agent first"},
		{"the version already installed", artifact.DerperVersion, func(t *testing.T, s *buSetup) {
			if err := setLocalBinary(ctx, s.h.db, artifact.DerperVersion, buSum(buOld), s.h.clock.Now()); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
		}, errcode.InvalidInput, "/version", "already installed"},
		{"a host without a server", artifact.DerperVersion, func(t *testing.T, s *buSetup) {
			if err := deleteLocalServer(ctx, s.h.db); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
		}, errcode.DERPNotReady, "", "no DERP server"},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			s := buNewSetup(t)
			if tc.setup != nil {
				tc.setup(t, s)
			}
			h := s.d.binaryUpdate().Handler
			_, planErr := h.Plan(ctx, s.planEnv(), buInput(tc.version))
			_, taskErr := h.Task(ctx, s.env, buInput(tc.version))
			for what, err := range map[string]error{"plan": planErr, "task": taskErr} {
				if !core.IsDefinite(err) {
					t.Errorf("%s: error %v is not definite", what, err)
				}
				coded := buCoded(t, err, tc.code)
				if tc.field != "" && (len(coded.Fields) != 1 || coded.Fields[0].Path != tc.field) {
					t.Errorf("%s: fields %+v, want one at %s", what, coded.Fields, tc.field)
				}
				if !strings.Contains(coded.Detail, tc.detail) {
					t.Errorf("%s: detail %q lacks %q", what, coded.Detail, tc.detail)
				}
			}
			if _, ok, _ := loadUpdate(ctx, s.h.db.Read(), "c1"); ok || len(s.effects(t)) != 0 || len(s.h.fetches) != 0 || len(s.h.ran) != 0 ||
				len(s.h.units.Calls) != 0 || !buFilesAre(s.h, map[string][]byte{server.BinaryPath: buOld}) {
				t.Errorf("a refusal left an update row (%v), effects %v, fetches %v, commands %v, unit calls %v or files %v",
					ok, s.effects(t), s.h.fetches, s.h.ran, s.h.units.Calls, s.h.files)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestBuTaskUpdates(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	s := buNewSetup(t)
	raw, err := s.run(t)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var out BinaryUpdated
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &out); err != nil || out != (BinaryUpdated{From: buFrom, To: artifact.DerperVersion}) {
		t.Errorf("output %s (error %v), want the old and the new version", raw, err)
	}
	if want := []string{"1.4.0 " + artifact.DerperVersion + " amd64"}; !slices.Equal(s.h.fetches, want) {
		t.Errorf("fetches %v, want %v", s.h.fetches, want)
	}
	if !buFilesAre(s.h, map[string][]byte{server.BinaryPath: buNew, server.BinaryPrevPath: buOld}) {
		t.Errorf("files %v, want the new binary in place and the old one kept as derper.prev", s.h.files)
	}
	// Each phase is recorded, and the effect journaled, before the change it covers.
	wantCalls := []buCall{
		{"write " + server.BinaryNewPath + " 0755", "staged/intended"},
		{"rename " + server.BinaryPath + " " + server.BinaryPrevPath, "kept/intended"},
		{"rename " + server.BinaryNewPath + " " + server.BinaryPath, "swapped/intended"},
		{"restart " + server.UnitName, "restarted/intended"},
	}
	if !slices.Equal(s.tr.calls, wantCalls) {
		t.Errorf("host changes with the update's phase and effect:\n%+v\nwant\n%+v", s.tr.calls, wantCalls)
	}
	wantUpdate := updateRow{
		ChangeID: "c1", FromVersion: buFrom, FromSHA256: buSum(buOld), ToVersion: artifact.DerperVersion, ToSHA256: buSum(buNew), Phase: "done",
	}
	got := s.update(t)
	if !got.UpdatedAt.Equal(s.h.clock.Now()) {
		t.Errorf("updated_at %v, want %v", got.UpdatedAt, s.h.clock.Now())
	}
	got.UpdatedAt = time.Time{}
	if got != wantUpdate {
		t.Errorf("update row %+v, want %+v", got, wantUpdate)
	}
	row := s.server(t)
	if row.DerperVersion != artifact.DerperVersion || row.BinarySHA256 != buSum(buNew) || row.CertName != buPin || row.Mode != "ip" || row.HostName != buHostIP {
		t.Errorf("derp_server row %+v, want the new version and hash and the rest as before", row)
	}
	wantTargets := []probeTarget{{HostName: buHostIP, Address: "127.0.0.1", Port: 443, CertName: buPin}}
	if !slices.Equal(s.targets, wantTargets) || !slices.Equal(s.stuns, []netip.AddrPort{buStunAddr}) {
		t.Errorf("probed %+v and %v, want one TLS probe of %+v and one STUN probe of %v", s.targets, s.stuns, wantTargets, buStunAddr)
	}
	if !slices.Equal(s.h.units.Calls, []string{"restart " + server.UnitName}) {
		t.Errorf("unit calls %v, want one restart", s.h.units.Calls)
	}
	effects := s.effects(t)
	if len(effects) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d effects, want 1: %+v", len(effects), effects)
	}
	e := effects[0]
	doc := func(version string, bin []byte) string {
		return fmt.Sprintf(`{"version":%q,"sha256":%q}`, version, buSum(bin))
	}
	if e.Kind != core.EffectModify || e.Resource != "derp.binary:derper" || !e.Local || !e.InverseLocal || e.State != core.EffectMade ||
		string(e.Before.State) != doc(buFrom, buOld) || e.After == nil || string(e.After.State) != doc(artifact.DerperVersion, buNew) ||
		e.Post == nil || string(e.Post.State) != string(e.After.State) {
		t.Errorf("effect %+v, want a made, local modification of derp.binary:derper from %s to %s", e, doc(buFrom, buOld), doc(artifact.DerperVersion, buNew))
	}
	if err := s.d.binaryUpdate().Handler.Verify(ctx, s.env, buInput(artifact.DerperVersion), raw); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("verify after the update: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestBuTaskWaitsForTheHealthCheck(t *testing.T) {
	s := buNewSetup(t)
	tlsProbe := s.d.d.tlsProbe
	calls := 0
	s.d.d.tlsProbe = func(ctx context.Context, tg probeTarget) (time.Duration, error) {
		if calls++; calls <= 3 {
			return 0, errors.New("connection refused")
		}
		return tlsProbe(ctx, tg)
	}
	start := s.h.clock.Now()
	if _, err := s.run(t); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if waited := s.h.clock.Now().Sub(start); waited < 3*binaryUpdatePoll || waited >= healthWindow {
		t.Errorf("waited %v, want a few polls and less than %v", waited, healthWindow)
	}
	if s.update(t).Phase != "done" || !slices.Equal(s.h.units.Calls, []string{"restart " + server.UnitName}) {
		t.Errorf("phase %q and unit calls %v, want done after a single restart", s.update(t).Phase, s.h.units.Calls)
	}
}

func TestBuTaskFetchFailureChangesNothing(t *testing.T) {
	s := buNewSetup(t)
	s.h.fetchErr = errcode.New(errcode.UpdateNotFound, "darkpanel 1.4.0 is not a published release")
	_, err := s.run(t)
	_ = buCoded(t, err, errcode.UpdateNotFound)
	if !core.IsDefinite(err) {
		t.Errorf("error %v is not definite", err)
	}
	if _, ok, _ := loadUpdate(context.Background(), s.h.db.Read(), "c1"); ok || len(s.effects(t)) != 0 || len(s.h.units.Calls) != 0 ||
		!buFilesAre(s.h, map[string][]byte{server.BinaryPath: buOld}) {
		t.Errorf("a failed fetch left an update row (%v), effects %v, unit calls %v or files %v", ok, s.effects(t), s.h.units.Calls, s.h.files)
	}
}

// buRolledBack checks what every rolled-back update leaves: the old binary alone, the phase, the skipped effect and
// the server row as before.
func buRolledBack(t *testing.T, s *buSetup, err error, cause string) {
	t.Helper()
	coded := buCoded(t, err, errcode.DERPVerifyFailed)
	if core.IsDefinite(err) {
		t.Errorf("error %v is definite, but the update changed the host before it failed", err)
	}
	for _, want := range []string{cause, "derper is back at " + buFrom} {
		if !strings.Contains(coded.Detail, want) {
			t.Errorf("detail %q lacks %q", coded.Detail, want)
		}
	}
	if !buFilesAre(s.h, map[string][]byte{server.BinaryPath: buOld}) {
		t.Errorf("files %v, want only the old binary, back in place", s.h.files)
	}
	if u := s.update(t); u.Phase != "rolled_back" {
		t.Errorf("phase %q, want rolled_back", u.Phase)
	}
	if effects := s.effects(t); len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != core.EffectSkipped {
		t.Errorf("effects %+v, want the one effect skipped", effects)
	}
	if row := s.server(t); row.DerperVersion != buFrom || row.BinarySHA256 != buSum(buOld) {
		t.Errorf("derp_server row %+v, want the old version and hash", row)
	}
}

func TestBuTaskRollsBackAFailedStep(t *testing.T) {
	fail := func(call string) func(s *buSetup) {
		return func(s *buSetup) { s.files.fail[call] = errors.New("simulated failure at " + call) }
	}
	tests := []struct {
		name     string
		arm      func(s *buSetup)
		restarts int // the rollback's
	}{
		{"writing the new binary", fail(fmt.Sprintf("write %s 0755", server.BinaryNewPath)), 0},
		{"keeping the old binary", fail("rename " + server.BinaryPath + " " + server.BinaryPrevPath), 1},
		{"swapping the new binary in", fail("rename " + server.BinaryNewPath + " " + server.BinaryPath), 1},
		{"restarting derper", func(s *buSetup) { s.units.failRestarts = 1 }, 1},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			s := buNewSetup(t)
			tc.arm(s)
			_, err := s.run(t)
			buRolledBack(t, s, err, "simulated failure")
			if s.units.restarts != tc.restarts {
				t.Errorf("%d restarts succeeded, want %d, the rollback's", s.units.restarts, tc.restarts)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestBuTaskRollsBackAFailedHealthCheck(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		arm   func(s *buSetup)
		cause string
	}{
		{"the TLS probe", func(s *buSetup) { delete(s.h.tls, "127.0.0.1") }, "TLS probe"},
		{"the STUN probe", func(s *buSetup) { delete(s.h.stun, buStunAddr) }, "STUN probe"},
		{"tailscaled", func(s *buSetup) { s.h.local.Errs = map[string]error{"Status": errors.New("socket closed")} }, "tailscaled"},
		{"the unit", func(s *buSetup) {
			failed := core.UnitState{LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "failed", SubState: "failed"}
			active := s.h.units.States[server.UnitName]
			// The new binary leaves the unit failed; the restart of the rollback brings the old one up.
			s.units.onRestart = func(n int) {
				state := failed
				if n > 1 {
					state = active
				}
				s.h.units.States = map[string]core.UnitState{server.UnitName: state}
			}
		}, "not active"},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			s := buNewSetup(t)
			tc.arm(s)
			start := s.h.clock.Now()
			_, err := s.run(t)
			buRolledBack(t, s, err, tc.cause)
			if waited := s.h.clock.Now().Sub(start); waited < healthWindow {
				t.Errorf("gave up after %v, want the whole %v", waited, healthWindow)
			}
			if want := []string{"restart " + server.UnitName, "restart " + server.UnitName}; !slices.Equal(s.h.units.Calls, want) {
				t.Errorf("unit calls %v, want the restart and the rollback's restart", s.h.units.Calls)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestBuTaskReportsAFailedRollback(t *testing.T) {
	s := buNewSetup(t)
	delete(s.h.tls, "127.0.0.1")
	s.files.fail["rename "+server.BinaryPrevPath+" "+server.BinaryPath] = errors.New("simulated failure of the restore")
	_, err := s.run(t)
	coded := buCoded(t, err, errcode.DERPVerifyFailed)
	for _, want := range []string{"the rollback to " + buFrom + " failed", "derper needs repair"} {
		if !strings.Contains(coded.Detail, want) {
			t.Errorf("detail %q lacks %q", coded.Detail, want)
		}
	}
	if u := s.update(t); u.Phase != "failed" || !strings.Contains(u.Result, "simulated failure of the restore") {
		t.Errorf("update row %+v, want phase failed with the restore's error as the result", u)
	}
	if effects := s.effects(t); len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != core.EffectIntended {
		t.Errorf("effects %+v, want the one effect still intended, for the operator", effects)
	}
	if !buFilesAre(s.h, map[string][]byte{server.BinaryPath: buNew, server.BinaryPrevPath: buOld}) {
		t.Errorf("files %v, want the swap left as it was", s.h.files)
	}
}

func TestBuRollback(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	bin, prev, newer := server.BinaryPath, server.BinaryPrevPath, server.BinaryNewPath
	type files = map[string][]byte
	tests := []struct {
		name     string
		phase    string
		before   files
		after    files
		restarts int
		failure  string // "" when the rollback succeeds, else a substring of its error
	}{
		{"staged: nothing was moved", "staged", files{bin: buOld, newer: buNew}, files{bin: buOld}, 0, ""},
		{"staged, with a derper.prev left by an earlier update", "staged", files{bin: buOld, newer: buNew, prev: buOther}, files{bin: buOld, prev: buOther}, 0, ""},
		{"kept: the old binary was moved aside", "kept", files{prev: buOld, newer: buNew}, files{bin: buOld}, 1, ""},
		{"kept: the move never happened", "kept", files{bin: buOld, newer: buNew}, files{bin: buOld}, 1, ""},
		{"swapped", "swapped", files{bin: buNew, prev: buOld}, files{bin: buOld}, 1, ""},
		{"restarted", "restarted", files{bin: buNew, prev: buOld}, files{bin: buOld}, 1, ""},
		{"rolling_back after the restore", "rolling_back", files{bin: buOld}, files{bin: buOld}, 1, ""},
		{"rolling_back before the restore", "rolling_back", files{bin: buNew, prev: buOld}, files{bin: buOld}, 1, ""},
		{"derper.prev holds another binary", "swapped", files{bin: buNew, prev: buOther}, files{bin: buNew, prev: buOther}, 0, "derper.prev"},
		{"derper.prev is missing", "kept", files{newer: buNew}, files{newer: buNew}, 0, "derper.prev"},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			s := buNewSetup(t)
			clear(s.h.files)
			for p, b := range tc.before {
				s.h.files[p] = b
			}
			row := updateRow{
				ChangeID: "c1", FromVersion: buFrom, FromSHA256: buSum(buOld), ToVersion: artifact.DerperVersion, ToSHA256: buSum(buNew),
				Phase: tc.phase, UpdatedAt: s.h.clock.Now(),
			}
			if err := insertUpdate(ctx, s.h.db, row); err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			after := core.StateDoc{State: json.RawMessage(`{"version":"` + artifact.DerperVersion + `"}`)}
			_, err := s.env.Effects.Intend(ctx, core.Effect{
				Kind: core.EffectModify, Resource: "derp.binary:derper", Local: true, InverseLocal: true,
				Before: core.StateDoc{State: json.RawMessage(`{"version":"` + buFrom + `"}`)}, After: &after,
			}, nil)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			err = binaryUpdateHandler{d: s.d}.rollback(ctx, s.env, row)
			got := s.update(t)
			effects := s.effects(t)
			if tc.failure == "" {
				if err != nil || got.Phase != "rolled_back" || effects[0].State != core.EffectSkipped {
					t.Errorf("rollback = %v, phase %q, effect %s, want success, rolled_back and a skipped effect", err, got.Phase, effects[0].State)
				}
			} else {
				if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), tc.failure) || got.Phase != "failed" || !strings.Contains(got.Result, tc.failure) || effects[0].State != core.EffectIntended {
					t.Errorf("rollback = %v, phase %q, result %q, effect %s, want an error with %q, failed, the same result and the effect still intended",
						err, got.Phase, got.Result, effects[0].State, tc.failure)
				}
			}
			if !buFilesAre(s.h, tc.after) {
				t.Errorf("files %v, want %v", s.h.files, tc.after)
			}
			if s.units.restarts != tc.restarts {
				t.Errorf("%d restarts, want %d", s.units.restarts, tc.restarts)
			}
			if row := s.server(t); row.DerperVersion != buFrom || row.BinarySHA256 != buSum(buOld) {
				t.Errorf("derp_server row %+v, want the old version and hash", row)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestBuRollbackWaitsForTheUnit(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	s := buNewSetup(t)
	clear(s.h.files)
	s.h.files[server.BinaryPath], s.h.files[server.BinaryPrevPath] = buNew, buOld
	s.h.units.States = map[string]core.UnitState{server.UnitName: {LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "activating"}}
	row := updateRow{
		ChangeID: "c1", FromVersion: buFrom, FromSHA256: buSum(buOld), ToVersion: artifact.DerperVersion, ToSHA256: buSum(buNew),
		Phase: "restarted", UpdatedAt: s.h.clock.Now(),
	}
	if err := insertUpdate(ctx, s.h.db, row); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	start := s.h.clock.Now()
	var err error
	buDrive(t, s.h, func() { err = binaryUpdateHandler{d: s.d}.rollback(ctx, s.env, row) })
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "not active") {
		t.Fatalf("rollback = %v, want an error saying the unit is not active", err)
	}
	if waited := s.h.clock.Now().Sub(start); waited < healthWindow {
		t.Errorf("gave up after %v, want the whole %v", waited, healthWindow)
	}
	if u := s.update(t); u.Phase != "failed" || !strings.Contains(u.Result, "not active") {
		t.Errorf("update row %+v, want phase failed with the unit's state as the result", u)
	}
	if string(s.h.files[server.BinaryPath]) != string(buOld) {
		t.Errorf("binary %q, want the old one, restored before the wait", s.h.files[server.BinaryPath])
	}
}

func TestBuVerify(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	in := buInput(artifact.DerperVersion)
	updated := func(t *testing.T) *buSetup {
		t.Helper()
		s := buNewSetup(t)
		if _, err := s.run(t); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		return s
	}
	t.Run("an updated server whose unit is active", func(t *testing.T) {
		s := updated(t)
		if err := s.d.binaryUpdate().Handler.Verify(ctx, s.env, in, json.RawMessage(`{}`)); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("verify without the output, as recovery calls it: %v", err)
		}
	})
	t.Run("an inactive unit", func(t *testing.T) {
		s := updated(t)
		s.h.units.States = map[string]core.UnitState{server.UnitName: {LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "inactive"}}
		if err := s.d.binaryUpdate().Handler.Verify(ctx, s.env, in, json.RawMessage(`{}`)); err == nil {
			t.Error("verify accepted an inactive unit")
		}
	})
	t.Run("a server still at the old version", func(t *testing.T) {
		s := updated(t)
		if err := setLocalBinary(ctx, s.h.db, buFrom, buSum(buOld), s.h.clock.Now()); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if err := s.d.binaryUpdate().Handler.Verify(ctx, s.env, in, json.RawMessage(`{}`)); err == nil {
			t.Error("verify accepted a server recorded at the old version")
		}
	})
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the test to verify it fails**

Run: `mkdir -p /root/.cache/w5-tmp/16 && export TMPDIR=/root/.cache/w5-tmp/16 GOTOOLCHAIN=local && timeout 300 go vet ./internal/modules/derp/`
Expected: FAIL with `undefined: binaryUpdatePoll` and `undefined: binaryUpdateHandler` (and `dp` has no method `binaryUpdate`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `internal/modules/derp/binary_update.go`. Constructing the action does no I/O. The transaction follows the agent-local model of `internal/modules/tailscale/package_pin.go`: the update row of `derp_updates` names the last phase that may be half done, and each phase is written before the change it covers.

1. `func (d dp) binaryUpdate() core.ActionDef` returns `core.DefineTask[BinaryUpdateInput, BinaryUpdated]` with the spec of the "DERP actions" row (name `derp.binary.update`, `Version: 1`, class compensable, side agent, `LockKeys: []string{lockKey}`, `ApplyTimeout: 15 * time.Minute`, `Available: d.available`, a one-sentence `Summary`) and the handler `binaryUpdateHandler{d: d}`. The handler has `Plan`, `Task`, `Verify` and `rollback` here; it has no `Observe`, `Inverse` or `Recover` yet.
2. The precheck is shared by `Plan` and `Task`; every refusal is a `*errcode.Error` wrapped with `core.Definite`, and it returns the `localServer` row. In this order:
   - `in.Version` other than `artifact.DerperVersion`: `request.invalid_input` with one field error at `/version` and the detail `this agent's darkpanel release carries derper <artifact.DerperVersion>; update the agent first`.
   - No `derp_server` row: `derp.not_ready` (`errcode.DERPNotReady`), `no DERP server is installed on this host`.
   - The row's `DerperVersion` equals `in.Version`: `request.invalid_input`, field `/version`, `derper <version> is already installed`.
3. `Plan` runs the precheck, changes nothing and returns the lines `- derper <row version>`, `+ derper <in.Version>`, then two lines with `Op " "`: `the previous binary is kept as /usr/local/lib/darkpanel/derper.prev` and `roll back to derper <row version> if the unit, the TLS probe, STUN and tailscaled are not healthy within <healthWindow in whole seconds> s`. No guards, no confirmation. `StateHash` is the hex SHA-256 of the lines `from=` (row version), `sha=` (row `BinarySHA256`), `to=` and `arch=` with their values, joined by newlines.
4. `Task`:
   1. Runs the precheck, then `d.d.fetch(ctx, d.d.version, artifact.DerperVersion, d.d.arch)` before anything else, so a failed fetch leaves no row, no effect and no file; every error of the fetch is returned wrapped with `core.Definite`.
   2. Journals the one effect with `Effects.Intend`: `Kind: core.EffectModify`, `Resource: binaryEffectResource`, `Local: true`, `InverseLocal: true`, `Before` the state document `{"version":"<from>","sha256":"<from sha>"}` (member order as shown; `json.Marshal` of a struct) and `After` the same shape for the new version and the fetch's `sha`. `From` is the `derp_server` row's `DerperVersion` and `BinarySHA256`.
   3. Inserts the `derp_updates` row with `insertUpdate`: the change id, from and to versions and hashes, `Phase: "staged"`, `UpdatedAt: env.Clock.Now()`.
   4. Runs these steps in order; before the steps marked with a phase it writes that phase with `setUpdatePhase(ctx, env.DB, env.ChangeID, phase, "", env.Clock.Now())` (so the phase is on the row when the change runs): `writing the new binary` (`env.Files.WriteFile(ctx, ID, server.BinaryNewPath, bin, 0o755)`; the row is already `staged`); `keeping the old binary` (phase `kept`; `env.Files.Rename(ctx, ID, BinaryPath, BinaryPrevPath)`); `swapping the new binary in` (phase `swapped`; `Rename(ctx, ID, BinaryNewPath, BinaryPath)`); `restarting derper` (phase `restarted`; `env.Units.Restart(ctx, UnitName)`); `the health check`.
   5. The health check polls at once and then every `binaryUpdatePoll` on `env.Clock` (`env.Clock.AfterFunc` and a channel, giving up on `ctx.Done()`), until `healthWindow` has passed since the first poll, and succeeds on the first poll where all of these hold, checked in this order and failing with the first that does not: the unit's `ActiveState` is `active` (error `derp: darkpanel-derper.service is <state>, not active`); `d.d.tlsProbe` with `probeTarget{HostName: row.HostName, Address: "127.0.0.1", Port: 443, CertName: row.CertName}` succeeds (error `derp: the TLS probe of /derp/probe failed: <err>`); `d.d.stun` at `127.0.0.1:3478` succeeds (error `derp: the STUN probe failed: <err>`); `d.d.local().Status` answers (error `derp: tailscaled does not answer: <err>`). The error of the last poll is the cause.
   6. After the health check: `setLocalBinary(ctx, env.DB, in.Version, sha, env.Clock.Now())`, then phase `done`, then `Effects.Made(ctx, seq, binaryEffectResource, <the After document>)`. The output is `BinaryUpdated{From: <row version>, To: in.Version}`. (If `setLocalBinary` fails, treat it as a failed step named `recording the new binary`.)
   7. When any step of 4.4 or the setLocalBinary of 4.6 fails (a phase write that fails counts as that step failing), reload the update row with `loadUpdate`, call `rollback` with it and return an `*errcode.Error` with code `derp.verify_failed` (not Definite: the host changed). Its detail is `<step name> failed (<first line of the cause>); derper is back at <from version>` when `rollback` returned nil, and `<step name> failed (<first line of the cause>) and the rollback to <from version> failed (<first line of the rollback's error>); derper needs repair` when it did not. The cause is the wrapped error.
5. `func (h binaryUpdateHandler) rollback(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, u updateRow) error` restores the old binary from the row alone, with no controller. `u.Phase` is the phase when it was entered.
   1. Write phase `rolling_back` (empty result).
   2. Restore. When `u.Phase` is `kept`, `swapped`, `restarted` or `rolling_back` the swap may have happened. Take the SHA-256 of `BinaryPath` and of `BinaryPrevPath` with `d.d.readFile` (a missing file hashes as `""`; another read error fails the rollback). If the one of `BinaryPath` differs from `u.FromSHA256`: the one of `BinaryPrevPath` must equal `u.FromSHA256`, else the rollback fails with `derp: derper.prev does not hold the previous binary` (nothing is touched); then `env.Files.Rename(ctx, ID, BinaryPrevPath, BinaryPath)` (which replaces the new binary). If `BinaryPath` already holds the previous binary, no rename is made: this keeps a `derper.prev` left by an earlier update from replacing a good binary. Then `setLocalBinary(ctx, env.DB, u.FromVersion, u.FromSHA256, env.Clock.Now())`, then `env.Units.Restart(ctx, UnitName)`, then the same polling as the health check until the unit is `active` within `healthWindow` (error `derp: darkpanel-derper.service is <state>, not active`). In phase `staged` none of this runs. Last, whatever the phase, when `d.d.readFile(BinaryNewPath)` succeeds, `env.Files.Remove(ctx, ID, BinaryNewPath)`.
   3. On an error in step 2: write phase `failed` with the first line of the error, at most 200 runes, as the result, leave the effect intended, and return the error. On success: write phase `rolled_back`, then `Effects.List` and `Effects.Skipped` for the effect on `binaryEffectResource` that is still `intended` (none is fine), and return nil.
6. `Verify(ctx, env, in, out)` checks the host from the input alone: the `derp_server` row exists with `DerperVersion == in.Version`, and the unit is `active`; otherwise an error saying which.

```go
package derp

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"crypto/x509"
	"crypto/x509/pkix"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"encoding/pem"
	"errors"
	"math/big"
	"net"
	"net/netip"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/derp/artifact"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/derp/server"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/platform/tslocal"
)

var errSsBoom = errors.New("ss: boom")

const ssIP = "203.0.113.9"

var (
	ssStoredAfter = time.Date(2027, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 0, time.UTC)
	ssLiveAfter   = time.Date(2027, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 0, time.UTC)
	ssStoredPin   = server.PinPrefix + strings.Repeat("b", 64)
)

// ssCert returns a self-signed certificate for ip as PEM, and its pin computed here from the DER.
func ssCert(t *testing.T, ip string, notAfter time.Time) (certPEM []byte, pin string) {
	t.Helper()
	key, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tmpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber: big.NewInt(1),
		Subject:      pkix.Name{CommonName: ip},
		NotBefore:    notAfter.Add(-365 * 24 * time.Hour),
		NotAfter:     notAfter,
		IPAddresses:  []net.IP{net.ParseIP(ip)},
		KeyUsage:     x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature,
		ExtKeyUsage:  []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageServerAuth},
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, tmpl, tmpl, &key.PublicKey, key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	sum := sha256.Sum256(der)
	return pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE", Bytes: der}), "sha256-raw:" + hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])
}

// ssMaps makes the host maps a test writes into non-nil.
func ssMaps(h *dpHost) {
	if h.files == nil {
		h.files = map[string][]byte{}
	}
	if h.units.States == nil {
		h.units.States = map[string]core.UnitState{}
	}
}

// ssRow is the agent's server row for a server installed in mode at host name.
func ssRow(mode, hostName string) localServer {
	at := time.Date(2026, 11, 1, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	return localServer{
		ChangeID: "c0", Mode: mode, HostName: hostName, VerifyClients: true, DerperVersion: artifact.DerperVersion,
		BinarySHA256: strings.Repeat("a", 64), InstalledAt: at, UpdatedAt: at,
	}
}

func ssInstall(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, s localServer) {
	t.Helper()
	if err := insertLocalServer(context.Background(), h.db, s); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// ssTailnet makes tailscaled report backend, a self with tags and the addresses ips, in the order given.
func ssTailnet(h *dpHost, backend string, tags []string, ips ...string) {
	addrs := make([]netip.Addr, 0, len(ips))
	for _, ip := range ips {
		addrs = append(addrs, netip.MustParseAddr(ip))
	}
	h.local.Status = &tslocal.Status{
		BackendState: backend, TailscaleIPs: addrs,
		Self: &tslocal.PeerStatus{ID: "n1", HostName: "derp-1", DNSName: "derp-1.example.ts.net.", Tags: tags, TailscaleIPs: addrs, Online: true},
	}
}

// ssRead runs derp.server.status on d and decodes the answer twice: as a ServerStatus and as a generic map, which
// shows whether an empty list was encoded as [] and an absent time as null.
func ssRead(t *testing.T, d dp, env core.Env, input string) (ServerStatus, map[string]any, error) {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := d.serverStatus().Handler.Read(context.Background(), env, json.RawMessage(input))
	if err != nil {
		return ServerStatus{}, nil, err
	}
	var st ServerStatus
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &st); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the answer %s does not decode as a ServerStatus: %v", raw, err)
	}
	var generic map[string]any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &generic); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return st, generic, nil
}

func TestSsDefinition(t *testing.T) {
	def := newDPHost(t).dp().serverStatus()
	s := def.Spec
	if s.Name != "derp.server.status" || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassRead || s.Side != core.SideAgent ||
		len(s.LockKeys) != 0 || s.Available != nil || s.Internal || s.LongRunning || s.Task {
		t.Errorf("spec %+v, want a plain agent read derp.server.status/v1 without locks or an availability check", s)
	}
	if def.InputType != reflect.TypeFor[ServerStatusInput]() || def.OutputType != reflect.TypeFor[ServerStatus]() {
		t.Errorf("input %v and output %v, want ServerStatusInput and ServerStatus", def.InputType, def.OutputType)
	}
	if def.Handler.Read == nil {
		t.Error("the action has no Read handler")
	}
}

// TestSsNotInstalled: a host without a server still reports its tailnet identity; every list is an array, an
// absent certificate time is null, and the read runs no command and calls no unit method.
func TestSsNotInstalled(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	ssTailnet(h, "Running", []string{"tag:z", "tag:a"}, "fd7a:115c:a1e0::1", "100.64.0.10")
	got, generic, err := ssRead(t, h.dp(), h.env(""), `{}`)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := ServerStatus{
		AvailableVersion: artifact.DerperVersion, OnTailnet: true,
		Tags: []string{"tag:a", "tag:z"}, Addresses: []string{"100.64.0.10", "fd7a:115c:a1e0::1"}, Visible: []Visibility{},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("status\n got %+v\nwant %+v", got, want)
	}
	if v, ok := generic["certNotAfter"]; !ok || v != nil {
		t.Errorf("certNotAfter = %v (present %v), want null", v, ok)
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(generic["visible"], []any{}) {
		t.Errorf("visible = %#v, want []", generic["visible"])
	}
	if len(h.ran) != 0 || len(h.units.Calls) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("the read ran %v and called %v, want nothing", h.ran, h.units.Calls)
	}
}

// TestSsInstalledIPMode: the certificate is read from derper's file; the stored pin and time answer only when the
// file cannot be read as a certificate.
func TestSsInstalledIPMode(t *testing.T) {
	livePEM, livePin := ssCert(t, ssIP, ssLiveAfter)
	crt := server.CertDir + "/" + ssIP + ".crt"
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name      string
		file      []byte
		wantPin   string
		wantAfter time.Time
	}{
		{"the live certificate wins over the stored one", livePEM, livePin, ssLiveAfter},
		{"no certificate file falls back to the stored values", nil, ssStoredPin, ssStoredAfter},
		{"an unreadable certificate file falls back to the stored values", []byte("not a certificate"), ssStoredPin, ssStoredAfter},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := newDPHost(t)
			ssMaps(h)
			row := ssRow(server.ModeIP, ssIP)
			row.CertName, row.CertNotAfter = ssStoredPin, ssStoredAfter
			ssInstall(t, h, row)
			h.units.States[server.UnitName] = core.UnitState{LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "active", SubState: "running", MainPID: 700}
			if tc.file != nil {
				h.files[crt] = tc.file
			}
			got, _, err := ssRead(t, h.dp(), h.env(""), `{}`)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if !got.Installed || got.Mode != server.ModeIP || got.HostName != ssIP || !got.VerifyClients ||
				got.DerperVersion != artifact.DerperVersion || !got.UnitActive || got.AvailableVersion != artifact.DerperVersion {
				t.Errorf("status %+v, want an installed, active, verifying ip-mode server %s at %s", got, artifact.DerperVersion, ssIP)
			}
			if got.CertName != tc.wantPin || got.CertNotAfter == nil || !got.CertNotAfter.Equal(tc.wantAfter) {
				t.Errorf("certificate %q until %v, want %q until %v", got.CertName, got.CertNotAfter, tc.wantPin, tc.wantAfter)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestSsLetsEncrypt: a Let's Encrypt server has no pin and no certificate time, and its certificate file, which
// derper keeps in its own cache, is not read; the unit state and the client verification flag come through.
func TestSsLetsEncrypt(t *testing.T) {
	livePEM, _ := ssCert(t, ssIP, ssLiveAfter)
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name       string
		state      core.UnitState
		wantActive bool
	}{
		{"active", core.UnitState{LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "active", SubState: "running"}, true},
		{"inactive", core.UnitState{LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "inactive", SubState: "dead"}, false},
		{"failed", core.UnitState{LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "failed", SubState: "failed"}, false},
		{"activating", core.UnitState{LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "activating", SubState: "start"}, false},
		{"not found", core.UnitState{LoadState: "not-found"}, false},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := newDPHost(t)
			ssMaps(h)
			row := ssRow(server.ModeLetsEncrypt, "derp.example.com")
			row.VerifyClients = false
			ssInstall(t, h, row)
			h.units.States[server.UnitName] = tc.state
			h.files[server.CertDir+"/derp.example.com.crt"] = livePEM
			got, _, err := ssRead(t, h.dp(), h.env(""), `{}`)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if !got.Installed || got.Mode != server.ModeLetsEncrypt || got.HostName != "derp.example.com" || got.VerifyClients {
				t.Errorf("status %+v, want an installed Let's Encrypt server without client verification", got)
			}
			if got.UnitActive != tc.wantActive {
				t.Errorf("unitActive = %v, want %v for unit state %+v", got.UnitActive, tc.wantActive, tc.state)
			}
			if got.CertName != "" || got.CertNotAfter != nil {
				t.Errorf("certificate %q until %v, want none for a Let's Encrypt server", got.CertName, got.CertNotAfter)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestSsOffTailnet: a node that is not Running, or tailscaled not answering, is no error; the node's identity is
// withheld.
func TestSsOffTailnet(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name      string
		backend   string
		statusErr error
	}{
		{"logged out", "NeedsLogin", nil},
		{"stopped", "Stopped", nil},

			if tc.statusErr != nil {
				h.local.Errs = map[string]error{"Status": tc.statusErr}
			}
			got, generic, err := ssRead(t, h.dp(), h.env(""), `{}`)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}

				t.Errorf("status %+v, want OnTailnet false and no tags or addresses", got)

			}
		})
	}
}

func TestSsWhoisRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	entries := make([]string, 257)
	for i := range entries {
		entries[i] = "100.64.0.1"
	}
	tooMany, err := json.Marshal(ServerStatusInput{Whois: entries})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name, input, path string
	}{
		{"an entry that is not an address", `{"whois":["100.64.0.20","not-an-ip"]}`, "/whois/1"},
		{"a host name instead of an address", `{"whois":["web-1.example.ts.net"]}`, "/whois/0"},
		{"more than 256 entries", string(tooMany), "/whois"},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := newDPHost(t)
			_, _, err := ssRead(t, h.dp(), h.env(""), tc.input)
			coded := errcode.From(err)
			if err == nil || coded.Code != errcode.InvalidInput || len(coded.Fields) == 0 || coded.Fields[0].Path != tc.path {
				t.Errorf("err = %v (fields %+v), want request.invalid_input with a field error at %s", err, coded.Fields, tc.path)
			}
		})
	}
	t.Run("tailscaled failing is not read as not visible", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newDPHost(t)
		h.local.Errs = map[string]error{"WhoIs": errSsBoom}
		_, _, err := ssRead(t, h.dp(), h.env(""), `{"whois":["100.64.0.20"]}`)
		if !errors.Is(err, errSsBoom) || errcode.From(err).Code != errcode.TailscaleUnavailable {
			t.Errorf("err = %v, want tailscale.unavailable wrapping the failure", err)
		}
	})
}

func TestSsReadStatusOverAgents(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.addHost("h2", "web-2", false)
	want := ServerStatus{
		Installed: true, Mode: server.ModeIP, HostName: ssIP, VerifyClients: true, DerperVersion: artifact.DerperVersion,
		UnitActive: true, CertName: ssStoredPin, CertNotAfter: &ssStoredAfter, AvailableVersion: artifact.DerperVersion,
		OnTailnet: true, Tags: []string{"tag:derp"}, Addresses: []string{"100.64.0.10"},
		Visible: []Visibility{{Address: "100.64.0.9", Visible: true, Node: "web-1.example.ts.net"}},
	}
	ag := h.agent("h2")
	ag.Reads = map[string]func(json.RawMessage) (any, error){
		"derp.server.status/v1": func(json.RawMessage) (any, error) { return want, nil },
	}
	got, err := readStatus(ctx, h.ctlEnv(""), "h2", []string{"100.64.0.9"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("status\n got %+v\nwant %+v", got, want)
	}
	if _, err := readStatus(ctx, h.ctlEnv(""), "h2", nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	wantCalls := []string{`derp.server.status/v1 {"whois":["100.64.0.9"]}`, `derp.server.status/v1 {}`}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(ag.Calls, wantCalls) {
		t.Errorf("agent calls %q, want %q", ag.Calls, wantCalls)
	}

	ag.Reads["derp.server.status/v1"] = func(json.RawMessage) (any, error) { return nil, errSsBoom }
	if _, err := readStatus(ctx, h.ctlEnv(""), "h2", nil); !errors.Is(err, errSsBoom) || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "h2") {
		t.Errorf("err = %v, want the agent's failure, naming host h2", err)
	}
	ag.Reads["derp.server.status/v1"] = func(json.RawMessage) (any, error) { return "oops", nil }
	if _, err := readStatus(ctx, h.ctlEnv(""), "h2", nil); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "h2") {
		t.Errorf("err = %v, want a decode failure naming host h2", err)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

All of it goes in `server_status.go`; the file declares only identifiers beginning `serverStatus` besides `readStatus`.

1. `func (d dp) serverStatus() core.ActionDef` returns `core.DefineRead[ServerStatusInput, ServerStatus]` with `core.ActionSpec{Name: "derp.server.status", Summary: "Report the DERP server on this host and the host's place on the tailnet.", Version: 1, Side: core.SideAgent}` and the handler `serverStatusHandler{d: d}`. No `LockKeys` (the registry refuses locks on a read) and no `Available`: the read must answer on a host where tailscaled does not. Constructing the definition touches no socket, file or command.
2. `serverStatusHandler.Read(ctx, env, in)` runs these steps in order and runs no command and changes nothing:
   - Validate `in.Whois` first. More than 256 entries is `errcode.InvalidInput` with one `errcode.FieldError` at path `/whois`; an entry that `netip.ParseAddr` rejects, or that carries a zone, is `errcode.InvalidInput` with a field error at `/whois/<index>`.
   - Start from `ServerStatus{AvailableVersion: artifact.DerperVersion}` with `Tags`, `Addresses` and `Visible` set to empty, non-nil slices, so that they encode as `[]`, and `CertNotAfter` nil, so that it encodes as `null`.
   - `loadLocalServer(ctx, env.DB.Read())`. When a row exists: `Installed` true; `Mode`, `HostName`, `VerifyClients` and `DerperVersion` from the row; `UnitActive` is `env.Units.State(ctx, server.UnitName)` having `ActiveState == "active"` (only that value; `activating` and `failed` are false), and a failure of `State` is returned wrapped as `derp: read the state of darkpanel-derper.service: %w`.
   - Certificate, `ip` mode only: take the certificate file name from `server.CertFiles(server.Config{Mode: row.Mode, HostName: row.HostName})`, read `server.CertDir + "/" + name` through `d.d.readFile`, and decode it with `server.ParseCert`. On success `CertName` is `server.Pin(der)` and `CertNotAfter` points at the `NotAfter` in UTC. When the file is missing or does not parse, fall back to the row's `CertName` and, if non-zero, its `CertNotAfter` in UTC; with nothing stored `CertName` stays `""` and `CertNotAfter` nil. For `letsencrypt` the certificate is derper's own cache: nothing is read, `CertName` is `""` and `CertNotAfter` nil.
   - `LinkViaTailnet` is `d.d.linkViaTailnet()`. Its error is returned wrapped (`derp: read the controller address: %w`); it is never turned into `false`, because the fallback check reads this field and a guess would pass it.
   - Tailscaled: derive a context with `availableWait` as its timeout and call `d.d.local().Status` under it. When the call fails and the caller's own context is done, return that context's error; otherwise the failure is not an error of this read: `OnTailnet` stays false and `Tags` and `Addresses` stay empty. When `BackendState == "Running"` and `Self != nil`: `OnTailnet` true; `Tags` is a sorted copy of `Self.Tags`; `Addresses` are `Self.TailscaleIPs` (or the status's `TailscaleIPs` when `Self` has none) as strings, every IPv4 address first and the others after, each group in its listed order. A node that is not `Running` reports no tags and no addresses.
   - Whois, one entry per input entry, in input order, under the same `availableWait` bound: `d.d.local().WhoIs(ctx, addr)`. `tslocal.ErrPeerNotFound` (`errors.Is`) or an answer with a nil `Node` is `Visibility{Address: addr.String()}`. Any other failure is returned as `errcode.Wrap(errcode.TailscaleUnavailable, err, "tailscaled does not answer on its socket")`, or as the context's error when the caller's context is done: a tailscaled that cannot answer must not read as "not visible". An answer is `Visibility{Address: addr.String(), Visible: true, Node: <Node.Name without its trailing dot>}`.
3. `func readStatus(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, hostID string, whois []string) (ServerStatus, error)`: marshal `ServerStatusInput{Whois: whois}` (nil `whois` encodes as `{}`), get the client with `env.Agents.Agent(ctx, hostID)`, call `Read(ctx, env.Principal, core.ActionRef{Name: "derp.server.status", Version: 1}, input)`, and `json.Unmarshal` the answer into a `ServerStatus`. A failure of `Agent` or `Read` is returned wrapped with the host id (`derp: read the DERP status of host %s: %w`, so `errors.Is` still sees the cause), and so is a decode failure.
4. Do not edit `derp.go` or any frozen file; Task 29 registers the action.

Create `internal/modules/derp/node_probe_test.go` with exactly this content:

```go
package derp

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"net/netip"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/derp/server"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/platform/tslocal"
)

var errNpBoom = errors.New("np: boom")

var npPin = server.PinPrefix + strings.Repeat("c", 64)

func npAddrPort(s string) netip.AddrPort { return netip.MustParseAddrPort(s) }

// npRead runs derp.node.probe on d.
func npRead(t *testing.T, d dp, env core.Env, input string) (NodeProbe, error) {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := d.nodeProbe().Handler.Read(context.Background(), env, json.RawMessage(input))
	if err != nil {
		return NodeProbe{}, err
	}
	var out NodeProbe
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &out); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the answer %s does not decode as a NodeProbe: %v", raw, err)
	}
	return out, nil
}

// npNoProbe makes d fail the test when it probes or resolves anything.
func npNoProbe(t *testing.T, d dp) dp {
	d.d.tlsProbe = func(context.Context, probeTarget) (time.Duration, error) {
		t.Error("a TLS probe ran")
		return 0, errNpBoom
	}
	d.d.stun = func(context.Context, netip.AddrPort) (time.Duration, error) {
		t.Error("a STUN probe ran")
		return 0, errNpBoom
	}
	d.d.resolve = func(context.Context, string) ([]netip.Addr, error) {
		t.Error("a name was resolved")
		return nil, errNpBoom
	}
	return d
}

func TestNpDefinition(t *testing.T) {
	def := newDPHost(t).dp().nodeProbe()
	s := def.Spec
	if s.Name != "derp.node.probe" || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassRead || s.Side != core.SideAgent ||
		len(s.LockKeys) != 0 || s.Available != nil || s.Internal || s.LongRunning || s.Task {
		t.Errorf("spec %+v, want a plain agent read derp.node.probe/v1 without locks or an availability check", s)
	}
	if def.InputType != reflect.TypeFor[NodeProbeInput]() || def.OutputType != reflect.TypeFor[NodeProbe]() {
		t.Errorf("input %v and output %v, want NodeProbeInput and NodeProbe", def.InputType, def.OutputType)
	}
	if def.Handler.Read == nil {
		t.Error("the action has no Read handler")
	}
}

// TestNpTargetIPMode: an ip-mode server is reached by its address, pinned, with STUN on the same address.
func TestNpTargetIPMode(t *testing.T) {
	for _, ip := range []string{"203.0.113.9", "2001:db8::1"} {
		t.Run(ip, func(t *testing.T) {
			d := npNoProbe(t, newDPHost(t).dp())
			got, stunAddr, err := d.target(context.Background(), ServerStatus{Installed: true, Mode: server.ModeIP, HostName: ip, CertName: npPin})
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if want := (probeTarget{HostName: ip, Port: 443, CertName: npPin}); got != want {
				t.Errorf("target %+v, want %+v", got, want)
			}
			if want := netip.AddrPortFrom(netip.MustParseAddr(ip), server.STUNPort); stunAddr != want {
				t.Errorf("STUN address %v, want %v", stunAddr, want)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestNpTargetLetsEncrypt: a Let's Encrypt server is reached by name, verified against the system roots, at the
// first address its name resolves to, IPv4 preferred.
func TestNpTargetLetsEncrypt(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name     string
		resolved []string
		want     string
	}{
		{"both families", []string{"2001:db8::7", "203.0.113.7", "203.0.113.8"}, "203.0.113.7"},
		{"only IPv6", []string{"2001:db8::7", "2001:db8::8"}, "2001:db8::7"},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			d := npNoProbe(t, newDPHost(t).dp())
			var addrs []netip.Addr
			for _, a := range tc.resolved {
				addrs = append(addrs, netip.MustParseAddr(a))
			}
			var asked string
			d.d.resolve = func(_ context.Context, host string) ([]netip.Addr, error) {
				asked = host
				return addrs, nil
			}
			got, stunAddr, err := d.target(context.Background(), ServerStatus{Installed: true, Mode: server.ModeLetsEncrypt, HostName: "derp.example.com"})
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if want := (probeTarget{HostName: "derp.example.com", Address: tc.want, Port: 443}); got != want {
				t.Errorf("target %+v, want %+v", got, want)
			}
			if want := netip.AddrPortFrom(netip.MustParseAddr(tc.want), server.STUNPort); stunAddr != want {
				t.Errorf("STUN address %v, want %v", stunAddr, want)
			}
			if asked != "derp.example.com" {
				t.Errorf("resolved %q, want derp.example.com", asked)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestNpTargetRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	resolveWith := func(addrs []netip.Addr, err error) func(context.Context, string) ([]netip.Addr, error) {
		return func(context.Context, string) ([]netip.Addr, error) { return addrs, err }
	}
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name    string
		s       ServerStatus
		resolve func(context.Context, string) ([]netip.Addr, error)
		named   string
	}{
		{"no server installed", ServerStatus{}, nil, ""},
		{"an unknown mode", ServerStatus{Installed: true, Mode: "dns", HostName: "derp.example.com"}, nil, ""},
		{"a name in ip mode", ServerStatus{Installed: true, Mode: server.ModeIP, HostName: "derp.example.com"}, nil, "derp.example.com"},
		{"a name that does not resolve", ServerStatus{Installed: true, Mode: server.ModeLetsEncrypt, HostName: "derp.example.com"},
			resolveWith(nil, errNpBoom), "derp.example.com"},
		{"a name with no address", ServerStatus{Installed: true, Mode: server.ModeLetsEncrypt, HostName: "derp.example.com"},
			resolveWith([]netip.Addr{}, nil), "derp.example.com"},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			d := npNoProbe(t, newDPHost(t).dp())
			if tc.resolve != nil {
				d.d.resolve = tc.resolve
			}
			_, _, err := d.target(context.Background(), tc.s)
			if err == nil {
				t.Fatal("target succeeded, want an error")
			}
			if tc.named != "" && !strings.Contains(err.Error(), tc.named) {
				t.Errorf("err = %v, want it to name %s", err, tc.named)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestNpProbeOnce(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name     string
		target   probeTarget
		stunAddr netip.AddrPort
		tls      map[string]dpProbe
		stun     map[netip.AddrPort]dpProbe
		wantTLS  ProbeResult
		wantSTUN ProbeResult
	}{
		{
			"both answer", probeTarget{HostName: "203.0.113.9", Port: 443}, npAddrPort("203.0.113.9:3478"),
			map[string]dpProbe{"203.0.113.9": {d: 12 * time.Millisecond}},
			map[netip.AddrPort]dpProbe{npAddrPort("203.0.113.9:3478"): {d: 1500 * time.Microsecond}},
			ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: 12}, ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: 1.5},
		},
		{
			"the TLS probe fails, keyed by the address", probeTarget{HostName: "derp.example.com", Address: "198.51.100.4", Port: 443},
			npAddrPort("198.51.100.4:3478"),
			map[string]dpProbe{"198.51.100.4": {err: errNpBoom}},
			map[netip.AddrPort]dpProbe{npAddrPort("198.51.100.4:3478"): {d: 2 * time.Millisecond}},
			ProbeResult{Error: "np: boom"}, ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: 2},
		},
		{
			"the STUN probe fails", probeTarget{HostName: "203.0.113.9", Port: 443}, npAddrPort("203.0.113.9:3478"),
			map[string]dpProbe{"203.0.113.9": {d: 3 * time.Millisecond}},
			map[netip.AddrPort]dpProbe{npAddrPort("203.0.113.9:3478"): {err: errNpBoom}},
			ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: 3}, ProbeResult{Error: "np: boom"},
		},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := newDPHost(t)
			h.tls, h.stun = tc.tls, tc.stun
			gotTLS, gotSTUN := h.dp().probeOnce(context.Background(), tc.target, tc.stunAddr)
			if gotTLS != tc.wantTLS || gotSTUN != tc.wantSTUN {
				t.Errorf("tls %+v and stun %+v, want %+v and %+v", gotTLS, gotSTUN, tc.wantTLS, tc.wantSTUN)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestNpReadProbes: the read probes the target the input describes and reports both measurements; the pin and
// the address reach the TLS probe, and STUN goes to the address.
func TestNpReadProbes(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name         string
		input        string
		resolved     []netip.Addr
		wantTarget   probeTarget
		wantSTUN     string
		wantResolved string
	}{
		{
			"an ip-mode server with its pin", `{"hostName":"203.0.113.9","certName":"` + npPin + `"}`, nil,
			probeTarget{HostName: "203.0.113.9", Port: 443, CertName: npPin}, "203.0.113.9:3478", "",
		},
		{
			"a name with the address the controller resolved", `{"hostName":"derp.example.com","address":"198.51.100.4"}`, nil,
			probeTarget{HostName: "derp.example.com", Address: "198.51.100.4", Port: 443}, "198.51.100.4:3478", "",
		},
		{
			"a name this agent resolves for STUN", `{"hostName":"derp.example.com"}`,
			[]netip.Addr{netip.MustParseAddr("2001:db8::7"), netip.MustParseAddr("203.0.113.7")},
			probeTarget{HostName: "derp.example.com", Port: 443}, "203.0.113.7:3478", "derp.example.com",
		},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := newDPHost(t)
			d := h.dp()
			var gotTarget probeTarget
			var gotSTUN []netip.AddrPort
			var resolvedHost string
			d.d.tlsProbe = func(_ context.Context, target probeTarget) (time.Duration, error) {
				gotTarget = target
				return 12 * time.Millisecond, nil
			}
			d.d.stun = func(_ context.Context, addr netip.AddrPort) (time.Duration, error) {
				gotSTUN = append(gotSTUN, addr)
				return 1500 * time.Microsecond, nil
			}
			d.d.resolve = func(_ context.Context, host string) ([]netip.Addr, error) {
				resolvedHost = host
				return tc.resolved, nil
			}
			got, err := npRead(t, d, h.env(""), tc.input)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			want := NodeProbe{TLS: ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: 12}, STUN: ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: 1.5}}
			if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
				t.Errorf("probe\n got %+v\nwant %+v", got, want)
			}
			if gotTarget != tc.wantTarget {
				t.Errorf("TLS target %+v, want %+v", gotTarget, tc.wantTarget)
			}
			if !reflect.DeepEqual(gotSTUN, []netip.AddrPort{npAddrPort(tc.wantSTUN)}) {
				t.Errorf("STUN servers %v, want [%s]", gotSTUN, tc.wantSTUN)
			}
			if resolvedHost != tc.wantResolved {
				t.Errorf("resolved %q, want %q", resolvedHost, tc.wantResolved)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestNpReadProbeFailures: a failed measurement is a result, not a failed read; a name this agent cannot resolve
// costs only the STUN measurement, because the TLS probe resolves the name itself.
func TestNpReadProbeFailures(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.tls = map[string]dpProbe{"derp.example.com": {d: 5 * time.Millisecond}}
	d := h.dp()
	d.d.resolve = func(context.Context, string) ([]netip.Addr, error) { return nil, errNpBoom }
	got, err := npRead(t, d, h.env(""), `{"hostName":"derp.example.com"}`)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if want := (ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: 5}); got.TLS != want {
		t.Errorf("tls %+v, want %+v", got.TLS, want)
	}
	if got.STUN.OK || got.STUN.LatencyMs != 0 || !strings.Contains(got.STUN.Error, "derp.example.com") {
		t.Errorf("stun %+v, want a failure naming derp.example.com", got.STUN)
	}

	h = newDPHost(t)
	h.tls = map[string]dpProbe{"203.0.113.9": {err: errNpBoom}}
	h.stun = map[netip.AddrPort]dpProbe{npAddrPort("203.0.113.9:3478"): {err: errNpBoom}}
	got, err = npRead(t, h.dp(), h.env(""), `{"hostName":"203.0.113.9"}`)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := NodeProbe{TLS: ProbeResult{Error: "np: boom"}, STUN: ProbeResult{Error: "np: boom"}}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("probe\n got %+v\nwant %+v", got, want)
	}
}

func TestNpReadRegion(t *testing.T) {
	newHost := func(t *testing.T) *dpHost {
		h := newDPHost(t)
		h.tls = map[string]dpProbe{"203.0.113.9": {d: time.Millisecond}}
		h.stun = map[netip.AddrPort]dpProbe{npAddrPort("203.0.113.9:3478"): {d: time.Millisecond}}
		return h
	}
	t.Run("the report of the region", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newHost(t)
		h.local.Regions = map[string]*tslocal.DERPRegionReport{
			"900": {Info: []string{"derper answered"}, Errors: []string{"no STUN reply"}},
		}
		got, err := npRead(t, h.dp(), h.env(""), `{"hostName":"203.0.113.9","regionId":900}`)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		want := &RegionDiagnostics{Info: []string{"derper answered"}, Warnings: []string{}, Errors: []string{"no STUN reply"}}
		if !reflect.DeepEqual(got.Region, want) {
			t.Errorf("region %+v, want %+v", got.Region, want)
		}
	})
	t.Run("no region id skips the diagnosis", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newHost(t)
		h.local.Errs = map[string]error{"DebugDERPRegion": errNpBoom}
		got, err := npRead(t, h.dp(), h.env(""), `{"hostName":"203.0.113.9"}`)
		if err != nil || got.Region != nil {
			t.Errorf("region %+v, err %v, want no region and no error", got.Region, err)
		}
	})
	t.Run("a failed diagnosis is reported in the region", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newHost(t)
		h.local.Errs = map[string]error{"DebugDERPRegion": errNpBoom}
		got, err := npRead(t, h.dp(), h.env(""), `{"hostName":"203.0.113.9","regionId":900}`)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if !got.TLS.OK || !got.STUN.OK {
			t.Errorf("tls %+v and stun %+v, want both measured", got.TLS, got.STUN)
		}
		if got.Region == nil || len(got.Region.Errors) != 1 || !strings.Contains(got.Region.Errors[0], "np: boom") ||
			len(got.Region.Info) != 0 || len(got.Region.Warnings) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("region %+v, want one error carrying the failure and nothing else", got.Region)
		}
	})
}

// TestNpReadValidation: the input is checked before anything is probed, with a field error at the input member.
func TestNpReadValidation(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name, input, path string
	}{
		{"an empty host name", `{"hostName":""}`, "/hostName"},
		{"a host name with a slash", `{"hostName":"a/b.example.com"}`, "/hostName"},
		{"an upper-case host name", `{"hostName":"Derp.Example.com"}`, "/hostName"},
		{"a single-label host name", `{"hostName":"derp"}`, "/hostName"},
		{"a loopback host name", `{"hostName":"127.0.0.1"}`, "/hostName"},
		{"a link-local host name", `{"hostName":"169.254.169.254"}`, "/hostName"},
		{"an unspecified host name", `{"hostName":"::"}`, "/hostName"},
		{"an address that is a name", `{"hostName":"derp.example.com","address":"derp2.example.com"}`, "/address"},
		{"a loopback address", `{"hostName":"derp.example.com","address":"::1"}`, "/address"},
		{"a link-local address", `{"hostName":"derp.example.com","address":"169.254.169.254"}`, "/address"},
		{"a pin without the prefix", `{"hostName":"203.0.113.9","certName":"letsencrypt"}`, "/certName"},
		{"a short pin", `{"hostName":"203.0.113.9","certName":"sha256-raw:abcd"}`, "/certName"},
		{"an upper-case pin", `{"hostName":"203.0.113.9","certName":"sha256-raw:` + strings.Repeat("A", 64) + `"}`, "/certName"},
		{"a region below 900", `{"hostName":"203.0.113.9","regionId":5}`, "/regionId"},
		{"a region above 999", `{"hostName":"203.0.113.9","regionId":1000}`, "/regionId"},
	} {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := newDPHost(t)
			_, err := npRead(t, npNoProbe(t, h.dp()), h.env(""), tc.input)
			coded := errcode.From(err)
			if err == nil || coded.Code != errcode.InvalidInput || len(coded.Fields) == 0 || coded.Fields[0].Path != tc.path {
				t.Errorf("err = %v (fields %+v), want request.invalid_input with a field error at %s", err, coded.Fields, tc.path)
			}
		})
	}
}

		Region: &RegionDiagnostics{Info: []string{}, Warnings: []string{}, Errors: []string{"e"}},

		`derp.node.probe/v1 {"hostName":"203.0.113.9","regionId":900}`,
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

All of it goes in `node_probe.go`; besides the three helpers of Contracts the file declares only identifiers beginning `nodeProbe`.

1. `func (d dp) nodeProbe() core.ActionDef` returns `core.DefineRead[NodeProbeInput, NodeProbe]` with `core.ActionSpec{Name: "derp.node.probe", Summary: "Probe a DERP server over TLS and STUN from this host.", Version: 1, Side: core.SideAgent}` and a handler `nodeProbeHandler{d: d}`. No `LockKeys`, no `Available`.
2. `nodeProbeHandler.Read(ctx, env, in)` validates the whole input first, before it probes or resolves anything. Every refusal is `errcode.InvalidInput` carrying one `errcode.FieldError` at the given path:
   - `/hostName`: empty; or neither an IP literal (`netip.ParseAddr`, no zone) nor a DNS name (lower-case letters, digits and hyphens; each label 1 to 63 characters and not starting or ending with a hyphen; at least two labels; at most 253 bytes); or an IP literal that is loopback, unspecified, multicast or link-local.
   - `/address`: when set, anything but an IP literal outside the same four classes.
   - `/certName`: when set, anything but `server.PinPrefix` followed by exactly 64 lower-case hex digits.
   - `/regionId`: neither 0 nor in 900 to 999.
   The classes are refused because the input makes this host send a request to the named address, and the controller's own servers are never on them.
3. Then it measures, in this order, and a failed measurement is a result, never a failed read:
   - The target is `probeTarget{HostName: in.HostName, Address: in.Address, Port: server.HTTPSPort, CertName: in.CertName}`.
   - The STUN address is `in.Address` when set; else `in.HostName` when it is an IP literal; else the first address of `d.d.resolve(ctx, in.HostName)`, IPv4 preferred, with port `server.STUNPort`. When that resolution fails or returns nothing, the STUN result is `ProbeResult{Error: "derp: cannot resolve <host>: <cause>"}` (the text names the host name) and STUN is not attempted, but the TLS probe still runs, because `tlsProbe` resolves the name itself.
   - Split the two measurements into unexported helpers `nodeProbeTLS(ctx, t) ProbeResult` (calls `d.d.tlsProbe`) and `nodeProbeSTUN(ctx, addr) ProbeResult` (calls `d.d.stun`). A success is `ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: <duration in milliseconds as a float64>}`; a failure is `ProbeResult{Error: err.Error()}` with `LatencyMs` 0. `probeOnce` calls the two in that order (TLS, then STUN); the read uses them directly in the unresolvable case above.
   - When `in.RegionID != 0`: `d.d.local().DebugDERPRegion(ctx, strconv.Itoa(in.RegionID))` under a context bounded by `availableWait`; the report becomes `RegionDiagnostics` with `Info`, `Warnings` and `Errors` copied and never nil. A failure becomes `RegionDiagnostics{Info: []string{}, Warnings: []string{}, Errors: []string{"derp: the region diagnosis failed: <err>"}}` and does not fail the read; a done caller context is returned as that context's error. `NodeProbe.Region` is nil when `in.RegionID` is 0, and `DebugDERPRegion` is then not called.
4. `func (d dp) target(ctx, s ServerStatus) (probeTarget, netip.AddrPort, error)`:
   - `s.Mode == server.ModeIP`: `s.HostName` must parse as an IP literal (else an error naming it); the target is `probeTarget{HostName: s.HostName, Port: server.HTTPSPort, CertName: s.CertName}` (no `Address`: the name is the address) and the STUN address is that IP at `server.STUNPort`. Nothing is resolved.
   - `s.Mode == server.ModeLetsEncrypt`: resolve `s.HostName` through `d.d.resolve`; the address is the first IPv4 result, else the first result. The target is `probeTarget{HostName: s.HostName, Address: addr.String(), Port: server.HTTPSPort}` with an empty `CertName` (the chain is verified against the system roots) and the STUN address is `addr` at `server.STUNPort`. A resolution error, or an empty answer, is an error naming the host name.
   - Any other `s.Mode` (including `""` for a host with no server) is an error.
5. `func readNodeProbe(ctx, env, hostID, in NodeProbeInput) (NodeProbe, error)`: as Task 17's `readStatus`, for `derp.node.probe` version 1 with `in` marshalled as it is (the optional members are omitted when empty); the input and output travel as JSON, a failure of `Agent`, `Read` or the decode is returned wrapped with the host id (`derp: probe from host %s: %w`).
6. Do not edit `derp.go` or any frozen file.
