```go
package derp

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/derp/server"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/platform"
)

const (
	unIP          = "203.0.113.9"
	unUnitRes     = "derp.unit:darkpanel-derper.service"
	unUnitFileRes = "derp.file:/etc/systemd/system/darkpanel-derper.service"
	unBinaryRes   = "derp.file:/usr/local/lib/darkpanel/derper"
	unDirRes      = "derp.dir:/var/lib/darkpanel/derp"
	unUserRes     = "derp.user:darkpanel-derp"
	unServerRes   = "derp.server:this"
)

var (
	unResources = []string{unUnitRes, unUnitFileRes, unBinaryRes, unDirRes, unUserRes, unServerRes}
	unActive    = core.UnitState{LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "active", SubState: "running", MainPID: 700}
	unInactive  = core.UnitState{LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "inactive", SubState: "dead"}
	unGone      = core.UnitState{LoadState: "not-found"}
	unFullDiff  = []core.DiffLine{
		{Op: "-", Text: "unit darkpanel-derper.service: stop and disable"},
		{Op: "-", Text: "file /etc/systemd/system/darkpanel-derper.service"},
		{Op: "-", Text: "file /usr/local/lib/darkpanel/derper, with its .new and .prev copies"},
		{Op: "-", Text: "directory /var/lib/darkpanel/derp, with the node key and the certificates in it"},
		{Op: "-", Text: "user darkpanel-derp"},
		{Op: "-", Text: "the DERP server record of this host"},
	}
)

func unMaps(h *dpHost) {
	if h.files == nil {
		h.files = map[string][]byte{}
	}
	if h.owned == nil {
		h.owned = map[string]bool{}
	}
	if h.users == nil {
		h.users = map[string][2]int{}
	}
	if h.units.States == nil {
		h.units.States = map[string]core.UnitState{}
	}
}

// unOwnUser records the service user as the module's in the agent database, where the module records it.
func unOwnUser(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) {
	t.Helper()
	err := platform.NewOwnership(h.db).Record(context.Background(), core.OwnedEntry{
		Path: server.ServiceUser, Module: ID, Kind: core.OwnedUser, CreatedAt: h.clock.Now(),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func unUserOwned(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) bool {
	t.Helper()
	owned, err := platform.NewOwnership(h.db).Owns(context.Background(), ID, server.ServiceUser)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return owned
}

func unServerExists(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) bool {
	t.Helper()
	_, found, err := loadLocalServer(context.Background(), h.db.Read())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return found
}

// unInstalled puts the host in the state a finished install leaves: an active unit, the unit file, the binary and
// its previous copy, the service directory with the node key and a certificate, the service user, and the row.
func unInstalled(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) {
	t.Helper()
	unMaps(h)
	h.units.States[server.UnitName] = unActive
	for _, p := range []string{server.UnitPath, server.BinaryPath, server.BinaryPrevPath, server.StateDir} {
		h.owned[p] = true
	}
	h.files[server.UnitPath] = []byte("[Unit]\n")
	h.files[server.BinaryPath] = []byte("derper")
	h.files[server.BinaryPrevPath] = []byte("derper-prev")
	h.files[server.KeyPath] = []byte("node key")
	h.files[server.CertDir+"/"+unIP+".crt"] = []byte("certificate")
	h.users[server.ServiceUser] = [2]int{990, 990}
	unOwnUser(t, h)
	at := time.Date(2026, 11, 1, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	err := insertLocalServer(context.Background(), h.db, localServer{
		ChangeID: "c0", Mode: server.ModeIP, HostName: unIP, VerifyClients: true, DerperVersion: "v1.104.1",
		BinarySHA256: strings.Repeat("a", 64), InstalledAt: at, UpdatedAt: at,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// unDrive runs call while advancing the host's clock by one poll whenever it waits; tick, when set, runs once, at
// the first wait, before the clock moves.
func unDrive(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, tick func(), call func()) {
	t.Helper()
	done := make(chan struct{})
	go func() {
		defer close(done)
		call()
	}()
	ticked := false
	for deadline := time.Now().Add(10 * time.Second); ; {
		select {
		case <-done:
			return
		default:
		}
		if h.clock.Pending() > 0 {
			if tick != nil && !ticked {
				ticked = true
				tick()
			}
			h.clock.Advance(uninstallPoll)
		} else {
			time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
		}
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			t.Fatal("the call did not finish")
		}
	}
}

func unPlan(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) core.PlanOutcome {
	t.Helper()
	out, err := h.dp().serverUninstall().Handler.Plan(context.Background(), h.env(""), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return out
}

// unApply applies derp.server.uninstall as change c1.
func unApply(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, tick func()) (ServerUninstalled, error) {
	t.Helper()
	var raw json.RawMessage
	var err error
	unDrive(t, h, tick, func() {
		raw, err = h.dp().serverUninstall().Handler.Apply(context.Background(), h.env("c1"), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	})
	if err != nil {
		return ServerUninstalled{}, err
	}
	var out ServerUninstalled
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &out); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the answer %s does not decode as a ServerUninstalled: %v", raw, err)
	}
	return out, nil
}

// unStops makes the unit report inactive, as it does once systemd has stopped it.
func unStops(h *dpHost) func() {
	return func() { h.units.States[server.UnitName] = unInactive }
}

func unEffects(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) []core.Effect {
	t.Helper()
	effects, err := h.env("c1").Effects.List(context.Background())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return effects
}

func unSame(t *testing.T, got json.RawMessage, want string) bool {
	t.Helper()
	var a, b any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(got, &a); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("document %s: %v", got, err)
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(want), &b); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("document %s: %v", want, err)
	}
	return reflect.DeepEqual(a, b)
}

// unDoc is the state document of resource: the unit's is its active flag, every other one its presence.
func unDoc(resource string, on bool) string {
	key := "present"
	if resource == unUnitRes {
		key = "active"
	}
	if on {
		return `{"` + key + `":true}`
	}
	return `{"` + key + `":false}`
}

func TestUnDefinition(t *testing.T) {
	def := newDPHost(t).dp().serverUninstall()
	s := def.Spec
	if s.Name != "derp.server.uninstall" || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassCompensable || s.Side != core.SideAgent ||
		!reflect.DeepEqual(s.LockKeys, []string{"derp"}) || s.CompensatedBy != "derp.server.install" || !s.Converges ||
		s.ApplyTimeout != 45*time.Second || s.LongRunning || s.Task || s.Available != nil || s.ForwardOnly {
		t.Errorf("spec %+v, want a compensable agent action locking derp, compensated by derp.server.install, converging, 45 s, with no availability check", s)
	}
	if def.InputType != reflect.TypeFor[ServerUninstallInput]() || def.OutputType != reflect.TypeFor[ServerUninstalled]() {
		t.Errorf("input %v and output %v, want ServerUninstallInput and ServerUninstalled", def.InputType, def.OutputType)
	}
	if h := def.Handler; h.Plan == nil || h.Apply == nil || h.Verify == nil || h.Observe == nil || h.Inverse != nil || h.Task != nil {
		t.Error("the handler needs Plan, Apply, Verify and Observe, and no Inverse or Task")
	}
}

func TestUnPlan(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("nothing installed plans nothing", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newDPHost(t)
		unMaps(h)
		h.units.States[server.UnitName] = unGone
		out := unPlan(t, h)
		if len(out.Diff) != 0 || len(out.Guards) != 0 || out.Confirmation != "" {
			t.Errorf("plan %+v, want an empty diff, no guard and no confirmation", out)
		}
	})
	t.Run("an installed server lists every removal", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newDPHost(t)
		unInstalled(t, h)
		out := unPlan(t, h)
		if !reflect.DeepEqual(out.Diff, unFullDiff) {
			t.Errorf("diff\n got %+v\nwant %+v", out.Diff, unFullDiff)
		}
		if out.StateHash == "" || out.StateHash != unPlan(t, h).StateHash || out.Confirmation != "" {
			t.Errorf("state hash %q (stable %v) and confirmation %q, want a stable hash and no confirmation",
				out.StateHash, out.StateHash == unPlan(t, h).StateHash, out.Confirmation)
		}
	})
	t.Run("a loaded unit that is not running is only disabled, and the hash follows it", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newDPHost(t)
		unInstalled(t, h)
		running := unPlan(t, h)
		h.units.States[server.UnitName] = unInactive
		stopped := unPlan(t, h)
		want := append([]core.DiffLine{{Op: "-", Text: "unit darkpanel-derper.service: disable"}}, unFullDiff[1:]...)
		if !reflect.DeepEqual(stopped.Diff, want) {
			t.Errorf("diff\n got %+v\nwant %+v", stopped.Diff, want)
		}
		if stopped.StateHash == running.StateHash {
			t.Error("the state hash did not change when the unit stopped")
		}
	})
	t.Run("a unit systemd does not know has no line", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newDPHost(t)
		unInstalled(t, h)
		h.units.States[server.UnitName] = unGone
		if out := unPlan(t, h); !reflect.DeepEqual(out.Diff, unFullDiff[1:]) {
			t.Errorf("diff\n got %+v\nwant %+v", out.Diff, unFullDiff[1:])
		}
	})
}

// TestUnApplyRemovesEverything: the removals run in install's reverse order, each journaled as a delete effect,
// and the unit is stopped and awaited before its user is deleted.
func TestUnApplyRemovesEverything(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	unInstalled(t, h)
	out, err := unApply(t, h, unStops(h))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(out.Removed, unResources) {
		t.Errorf("removed %v, want %v", out.Removed, unResources)
	}
	wantCalls := []string{"stop " + server.UnitName, "disable " + server.UnitName, "reload"}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(h.units.Calls, wantCalls) {
		t.Errorf("unit calls %q, want %q", h.units.Calls, wantCalls)
	}
	if want := [][]string{{"/usr/sbin/userdel", server.ServiceUser}}; !reflect.DeepEqual(h.ran, want) {
		t.Errorf("commands run %q, want %q", h.ran, want)
	}
	for _, p := range []string{server.UnitPath, server.BinaryPath, server.BinaryNewPath, server.BinaryPrevPath} {
		if _, ok := h.files[p]; ok || h.owned[p] {
			t.Errorf("%s is still there (file %v, owned %v)", p, ok, h.owned[p])
		}
	}
	if h.owned[server.StateDir] {
		t.Error("the service directory is still recorded as the module's")
	}
	if unUserOwned(t, h) || unServerExists(t, h) {
		t.Error("the user record or the server row is still there")
	}

	effects := unEffects(t, h)
	if len(effects) != len(unResources) {
		t.Fatalf("%d effects journaled, want %d", len(effects), len(unResources))
	}
	for i, e := range effects {
		res := unResources[i]
		if e.Resource != res || e.Kind != core.EffectDelete || !e.Local || e.State != core.EffectMade {
			t.Errorf("effect %d = %s %s local %v %s, want a made local delete of %s", i+1, e.Kind, e.Resource, e.Local, e.State, res)
			continue
		}
		if !unSame(t, e.Before.State, unDoc(res, true)) || e.After == nil || !unSame(t, e.After.State, unDoc(res, false)) ||
			e.Post == nil || !unSame(t, e.Post.State, unDoc(res, false)) {
			t.Errorf("effect %d (%s): before %s, after %+v, post %+v, want %s then %s", i+1, res, e.Before.State, e.After, e.Post, unDoc(res, true), unDoc(res, false))
		}
	}
}

// TestUnApplyConverges: running the action again, or from a state an earlier run left half way, removes what is
// left and nothing more.
func TestUnApplyConverges(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("again after a complete run", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newDPHost(t)
		unInstalled(t, h)
		if _, err := unApply(t, h, unStops(h)); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		h.units.States[server.UnitName] = unGone
		calls, ran, effects := len(h.units.Calls), len(h.ran), len(unEffects(t, h))
		out, err := unApply(t, h, nil)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if out.Removed == nil || len(out.Removed) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("removed %#v, want an empty, non-nil list", out.Removed)
		}
		if len(h.units.Calls) != calls || len(h.ran) != ran || len(unEffects(t, h)) != effects {
			t.Errorf("the second run called %v, ran %v and journaled %d effects, want nothing new", h.units.Calls[calls:], h.ran[ran:], len(unEffects(t, h))-effects)
		}
	})
	t.Run("the unit file and the binary are already gone", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newDPHost(t)
		unInstalled(t, h)
		h.units.States[server.UnitName] = unGone
		delete(h.owned, server.UnitPath)
		delete(h.owned, server.BinaryPath)
		delete(h.owned, server.BinaryPrevPath)
		out, err := unApply(t, h, nil)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if want := []string{unDirRes, unUserRes, unServerRes}; !reflect.DeepEqual(out.Removed, want) {
			t.Errorf("removed %v, want %v", out.Removed, want)
		}
		if len(h.units.Calls) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("unit calls %q, want none", h.units.Calls)
		}
	})
	t.Run("a loaded unit that is not running is disabled without a stop", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newDPHost(t)
		unInstalled(t, h)
		h.units.States[server.UnitName] = unInactive
		if _, err := unApply(t, h, nil); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if want := []string{"disable " + server.UnitName, "reload"}; !reflect.DeepEqual(h.units.Calls, want) {
			t.Errorf("unit calls %q, want %q", h.units.Calls, want)
		}
	})
	t.Run("the user is recorded but already deleted", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newDPHost(t)
		unInstalled(t, h)
		h.units.States[server.UnitName] = unInactive
		delete(h.users, server.ServiceUser)
		out, err := unApply(t, h, nil)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if !reflect.DeepEqual(out.Removed, unResources) {
			t.Errorf("removed %v, want %v", out.Removed, unResources)
		}
		if len(h.ran) != 0 || unUserOwned(t, h) {
			t.Errorf("commands run %q, user still recorded %v, want no userdel and the record forgotten", h.ran, unUserOwned(t, h))
		}
	})
}

// TestUnLeavesWhatIsNotOurs: a unit file and a user the module did not record are not its to remove.
func TestUnLeavesWhatIsNotOurs(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	unMaps(h)
	h.units.States[server.UnitName] = unGone
	h.files[server.UnitPath] = []byte("[Unit]\nDescription=someone else's\n")
	h.users[server.ServiceUser] = [2]int{990, 990}
	if out := unPlan(t, h); len(out.Diff) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("diff %+v, want none", out.Diff)
	}
	out, err := unApply(t, h, nil)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(out.Removed) != 0 || len(h.ran) != 0 || len(h.units.Calls) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("removed %v, ran %q, unit calls %q, want nothing", out.Removed, h.ran, h.units.Calls)
	}
	if _, ok := h.files[server.UnitPath]; !ok {
		t.Error("the unit file that is not the module's was removed")
	}
}

// TestUnStopTimeout: a unit that does not stop within the wait stops the removal there, with the first effect
// still intended for the engine to settle.
func TestUnStopTimeout(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	unInstalled(t, h)
	start := h.clock.Now()
	_, err := unApply(t, h, nil)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "did not stop") {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v, want the unit did not stop", err)
	}
	if waited := h.clock.Now().Sub(start); waited < uninstallStopWait {
		t.Errorf("gave up after %s, want at least %s", waited, uninstallStopWait)
	}
	effects := unEffects(t, h)
	if len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].Resource != unUnitRes || effects[0].State != core.EffectIntended {
		t.Errorf("effects %+v, want the unit's alone, intended", effects)
	}
	if want := []string{"stop " + server.UnitName}; !reflect.DeepEqual(h.units.Calls, want) {
		t.Errorf("unit calls %q, want %q", h.units.Calls, want)
	}
	if len(h.ran) != 0 || !h.owned[server.UnitPath] || !unUserOwned(t, h) || !unServerExists(t, h) {
		t.Error("something was removed after the unit failed to stop")
	}
}

// TestUnUserdelFails: a refused userdel leaves its effect intended and the row in place, after the effects before
// it were made.
func TestUnUserdelFails(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	unInstalled(t, h)
	h.units.States[server.UnitName] = unInactive
	h.script("/usr/sbin/userdel darkpanel-derp", dpRun{res: core.CmdResult{
		ExitCode: 8, Stderr: []byte("userdel: user darkpanel-derp is currently used by process 123\n"),
	}})
	_, err := unApply(t, h, nil)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "userdel") || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "currently used by process 123") {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v, want the userdel failure with its last stderr line", err)
	}
	effects := unEffects(t, h)
	if len(effects) != 5 {
		t.Fatalf("%d effects, want 5 (the row's is not journaled yet)", len(effects))
	}
	for i, e := range effects {
		want := core.EffectMade
		if i == 4 {
			want = core.EffectIntended
		}
		if e.Resource != unResources[i] || e.State != want {
			t.Errorf("effect %d = %s %s, want %s %s", i+1, e.Resource, e.State, unResources[i], want)
		}
	}
	if !unServerExists(t, h) || !unUserOwned(t, h) {
		t.Error("the row or the user record was removed after userdel failed")
	}
}

// TestUnObserve: the observation of each effect's resource is the document its effect expects, present before the
// removal and absent after.
func TestUnObserve(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	unInstalled(t, h)
	observe := func(res string) core.Observation {
		t.Helper()
		obs, err := h.dp().serverUninstall().Handler.Observe(context.Background(), h.env(""), core.Effect{Resource: res, Kind: core.EffectDelete})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("observe %s: %v", res, err)
		}
		return obs
	}
	check := func(on bool) {
		t.Helper()
		for _, res := range unResources {
			obs := observe(res)
			if !unSame(t, obs.Current.State, unDoc(res, on)) || obs.Matches != 1 || !obs.Exclusive {
				t.Errorf("observe %s = %s (matches %d, exclusive %v), want %s", res, obs.Current.State, obs.Matches, obs.Exclusive, unDoc(res, on))
			}
		}
	}
	check(true)
	if _, err := unApply(t, h, unStops(h)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	check(false)
	if _, err := h.dp().serverUninstall().Handler.Observe(context.Background(), h.env(""), core.Effect{Resource: "derp.file:/etc/passwd"}); err == nil {
		t.Error("observe of a resource the action never journals succeeded")
	}
}

func TestUnVerify(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h := newDPHost(t)
	unInstalled(t, h)
	verify := func() error {
		return h.dp().serverUninstall().Handler.Verify(ctx, h.env("c1"), json.RawMessage(`{}`), json.RawMessage(`{"removed":[]}`))
	}
	if err := verify(); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("verify passed on a host that still has the server")
	}
	if _, err := unApply(t, h, unStops(h)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := verify(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("verify after the removal: %v", err)
	}
	h.owned[server.BinaryPath] = true
	if err := verify(); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), server.BinaryPath) {
		t.Errorf("err = %v, want the binary named", err)
	}
	delete(h.owned, server.BinaryPath)
	h.units.States[server.UnitName] = unActive
	if err := verify(); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), server.UnitName) {
		t.Errorf("err = %v, want the unit named", err)
	}
}
```

Expected: FAIL at build time, `undefined: uninstallPoll`, `undefined: uninstallStopWait` and `...serverUninstall undefined (type dp has no field or method serverUninstall)`. Quote the output in the report.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

All of it goes in `server_uninstall.go`; the file declares only identifiers beginning `serverUninstall` or `uninstall`.

1. `func (d dp) serverUninstall() core.ActionDef` returns `core.DefineMutating[ServerUninstallInput, ServerUninstalled]` with `core.ActionSpec{Name: "derp.server.uninstall", Summary: "Remove the DERP server from this host.", Version: 1, Class: core.ClassCompensable, Side: core.SideAgent, LockKeys: []string{lockKey}, CompensatedBy: "derp.server.install", Converges: true, ApplyTimeout: 45 * time.Second}` and the handler `serverUninstallHandler{d: d}`. No `Available` (a host whose tailscaled is down must still be cleaned), no `LongRunning`. The handler has `Plan`, `Apply`, `Verify` and `Observe`, and no `Inverse`: the compensation is `derp.server.install`.
2. What exists is decided from records, read the same way by Plan, Apply, Observe and Verify; the six removals, in this order, are also the order of the install undo of Contracts "DERP install, update and renewal effects":
   - `derp.unit:darkpanel-derper.service` exists when `env.Units.State(ctx, server.UnitName)` has a `LoadState` other than `not-found`. Its "active" flag is true when `ActiveState` is neither `inactive` nor `failed`. Removal: `Stop` when active, then wait until the state is `inactive` or `failed` with an empty `PendingJob`, then `Disable(ctx, server.UnitName)`.
   - `derp.file:/etc/systemd/system/darkpanel-derper.service` exists when `env.Files.Owns(ctx, ID, server.UnitPath)`. Removal: `env.Files.Remove(ctx, ID, server.UnitPath)`, then `env.Units.Reload(ctx)`.
   - `derp.file:/usr/local/lib/darkpanel/derper` exists when `env.Files.Owns` holds for any of `server.BinaryPath`, `server.BinaryNewPath` and `server.BinaryPrevPath`. Removal: `env.Files.Remove` of the `.new` path, the `.prev` path and the binary, in that order (the binary last, so that a run cut off in between still finds it).
   - `derp.dir:/var/lib/darkpanel/derp` exists when `env.Files.Owns(ctx, ID, server.StateDir)`. Removal: `env.Files.RemoveServiceDir(ctx, ID, server.StateDir)`.
   - `derp.user:darkpanel-derp` exists when `platform.NewOwnership(env.DB).Owns(ctx, ID, server.ServiceUser)` (the install records the user there; `env.Files.Owns` is not used for it). A user that exists on the host (`d.d.lookupUser`) but is not recorded is someone else's and is left alone. Removal: when `d.d.lookupUser(server.ServiceUser)` reports the user, run `env.Exec.Run(ctx, core.Cmd{Path: "/usr/sbin/userdel", Args: []string{server.ServiceUser}})`, treating a non-zero exit code as the error `derp: userdel darkpanel-derp exited <code>: <last non-empty stderr line>`; then `platform.NewOwnership(env.DB).Forget(ctx, ID, server.ServiceUser, "")`. A recorded user that is already gone from the host still gets the `Forget`, without a `userdel`.
   - `derp.server:this` exists when `loadLocalServer(ctx, env.DB.Read())` finds the row. Removal: `deleteLocalServer`.
3. `Plan` reads those facts (a failed read is returned as a `core.Definite` error) and shows one `core.DiffLine{Op: "-"}` per existing removal, in order, with exactly these texts: `unit darkpanel-derper.service: stop and disable` (`unit darkpanel-derper.service: disable` when the unit is loaded but not active), `file /etc/systemd/system/darkpanel-derper.service`, `file /usr/local/lib/darkpanel/derper, with its .new and .prev copies`, `directory /var/lib/darkpanel/derp, with the node key and the certificates in it`, `user darkpanel-derp`, `the DERP server record of this host`. Nothing installed is an empty diff, no guards and no confirmation. `StateHash` is the hex SHA-256 of the existing resources joined with the unit's active flag, so that it changes when the unit stops.
4. `Apply` re-reads the facts (it does not trust the plan) and walks the six removals in order. For each one that exists: `env.Effects.Intend` a `core.Effect{Kind: core.EffectDelete, Resource: <name above>, Local: true, Before: <the document of the fact as just read: the unit's active flag as read, present true for the other five>, After: &<the document with the flag false>}` before touching the host; make the change; then `env.Effects.Made(ctx, seq, resource, <the After document>)` and append the resource name to the output. State documents are JSON objects with one boolean: `{"active": <flag>}` for the unit and `{"present": <bool>}` for the other five. A failure returns the error and leaves that effect intended (the engine settles it through `Observe`); no later removal runs, and `Skipped` is never called. The output is `ServerUninstalled{Removed: <the names of the effects made, in order>}` with `Removed` an empty, non-nil slice when nothing was installed.
   - The unit wait: right after `Stop` read the state; while it is still active, wait `uninstallPoll` on `env.Clock.AfterFunc` (select on its channel and `ctx.Done()`, as `internal/modules/tailscale/node_login.go` does in its wait loop) and read again, until `uninstallStopWait` has passed on `env.Clock` since the `Stop`, then fail with `derp: darkpanel-derper.service did not stop within 20s`.
5. `Observe(ctx, env, e)` answers `core.Observation{Current: <doc>, Matches: 1, Exclusive: true}` for the six resource names, the doc being the current value of the flag the facts above define; any other resource is an error naming it.
6. `Verify(ctx, env, in, out)` passes when the unit is not active and the other five facts are all absent; otherwise it returns an error naming the first leftover (the unit name, or the path, or `derp.user`, or `derp.server`). It only reads.
7. Do not edit `derp.go` or any frozen file; Task 29 registers the action. Nothing in this task runs `userdel`, `systemctl` or writes outside the fakes of Contracts "DERP test host".

Create `internal/modules/derp/cert_renew_test.go` with exactly this content:

```go
package derp

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"crypto/x509"
	"crypto/x509/pkix"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"encoding/pem"
	"math/big"
	"net"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/derp/server"
)

const (
	crIP       = "203.0.113.9"
	crResource = "derp.cert:" + crIP
)

var (
	crCrt      = server.CertDir + "/" + crIP + ".crt"
	crKey      = server.CertDir + "/" + crIP + ".key"
	crOldAfter = time.Date(2027, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 0, time.UTC)
	crNewAfter = time.Date(2027, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 0, time.UTC)
)

// crCert returns a self-signed certificate for ip as PEM, and its pin computed here from the DER.
func crCert(t *testing.T, ip string, notAfter time.Time) (certPEM []byte, pin string) {
	t.Helper()
	key, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tmpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber: big.NewInt(1),
		Subject:      pkix.Name{CommonName: ip},
		NotBefore:    notAfter.Add(-365 * 24 * time.Hour),
		NotAfter:     notAfter,
		IPAddresses:  []net.IP{net.ParseIP(ip)},
		KeyUsage:     x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature,
		ExtKeyUsage:  []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageServerAuth},
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, tmpl, tmpl, &key.PublicKey, key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	sum := sha256.Sum256(der)
	return pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE", Bytes: der}), "sha256-raw:" + hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])
}

// crHost is a host with an installed ip-mode server and the certificate derper made when it first started.
type crHost struct {
	*dpHost
	oldPEM []byte
	oldPin string
	newPEM []byte
	newPin string
}

func crInstalled(t *testing.T) *crHost {
	t.Helper()
	h := &crHost{dpHost: newDPHost(t)}
	h.oldPEM, h.oldPin = crCert(t, crIP, crOldAfter)
	h.newPEM, h.newPin = crCert(t, crIP, crNewAfter)
	if h.files == nil {
		h.files = map[string][]byte{}
	}
	if h.owned == nil {
		h.owned = map[string]bool{}
	}
	if h.units.States == nil {
		h.units.States = map[string]core.UnitState{}
	}
	h.units.States[server.UnitName] = core.UnitState{LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "active", SubState: "running", MainPID: 700}
	h.owned[server.StateDir] = true
	h.files[crCrt] = h.oldPEM
	h.files[crKey] = []byte("old key")
	at := time.Date(2026, 11, 1, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	err := insertLocalServer(context.Background(), h.db, localServer{
		ChangeID: "c0", Mode: server.ModeIP, HostName: crIP, VerifyClients: true, DerperVersion: "v1.104.1",
		BinarySHA256: strings.Repeat("a", 64), CertName: h.oldPin, CertNotAfter: crOldAfter, InstalledAt: at, UpdatedAt: at,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return h
}

// derperRestarts is the tick that stands for derper: once restarted without its certificate files it writes a new
// pair.
func (h *crHost) derperRestarts() func() {
	return func() {
		h.files[crCrt] = h.newPEM
		h.files[crKey] = []byte("new key")
	}
}

// crDrive runs call while advancing the host's clock by one poll whenever it waits; tick, when set, runs once, at
// the first wait, before the clock moves.
func crDrive(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, tick func(), call func()) {
	t.Helper()
	done := make(chan struct{})
	go func() {
		defer close(done)
		call()
	}()
	ticked := false
	for deadline := time.Now().Add(10 * time.Second); ; {
		select {
		case <-done:
			return
		default:
		}
		if h.clock.Pending() > 0 {
			if tick != nil && !ticked {
				ticked = true
				tick()
			}
			h.clock.Advance(certRenewPoll)
		} else {
			time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
		}
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			t.Fatal("the call did not finish")
		}
	}
}

// crApply applies derp.cert.renew as change c1.
func crApply(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, tick func()) (CertRenewed, error) {
	t.Helper()
	var raw json.RawMessage
	var err error
	crDrive(t, h, tick, func() {
		raw, err = h.dp().certRenew().Handler.Apply(context.Background(), h.env("c1"), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	})
	if err != nil {
		return CertRenewed{}, err
	}
	var out CertRenewed
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &out); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the answer %s does not decode as a CertRenewed: %v", raw, err)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func crEffects(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) []core.Effect {
	t.Helper()
	effects, err := h.env("c1").Effects.List(context.Background())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return effects
}

func crSame(t *testing.T, got json.RawMessage, want string) bool {
	t.Helper()
	var a, b any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(got, &a); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("document %s: %v", got, err)
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(want), &b); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("document %s: %v", want, err)
	}
	return reflect.DeepEqual(a, b)
}

func crDoc(pin string, notAfter time.Time) string {
	if pin == "" {
		return `{"certName":"","notAfter":""}`
	}
	return `{"certName":"` + pin + `","notAfter":"` + notAfter.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339) + `"}`
}

func crRow(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) localServer {
	t.Helper()
	row, found, err := loadLocalServer(context.Background(), h.db.Read())
	if err != nil || !found {
		t.Fatalf("server row: found %v, err %v", found, err)
	}
	return row
}

// crApplied is a host on which the renewal ran: the old pair sits under .prev names, the new pair is in place,
// and the journal holds the renewal's one effect.
func crApplied(t *testing.T) (*crHost, core.Effect) {
	t.Helper()
	h := crInstalled(t)
	if _, err := crApply(t, h.dpHost, h.derperRestarts()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	effects := crEffects(t, h.dpHost)
	if len(effects) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d effects, want 1", len(effects))
	}
	return h, effects[0]
}

func TestCrDefinition(t *testing.T) {
	def := newDPHost(t).dp().certRenew()
	s := def.Spec
	if s.Name != "derp.cert.renew" || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassCompensable || s.Side != core.SideAgent ||
		!reflect.DeepEqual(s.LockKeys, []string{"derp"}) || s.CompensatedBy != "" || s.ForwardOnly || s.ApplyTimeout != 45*time.Second ||
		s.LongRunning || s.Task || s.Available == nil {
		t.Errorf("spec %+v, want a compensable agent action locking derp, with its own undo, 45 s and an availability check", s)
	}
	if def.InputType != reflect.TypeFor[CertRenewInput]() || def.OutputType != reflect.TypeFor[CertRenewed]() {
		t.Errorf("input %v and output %v, want CertRenewInput and CertRenewed", def.InputType, def.OutputType)
	}
	if h := def.Handler; h.Plan == nil || h.Apply == nil || h.Verify == nil || h.Observe == nil || h.Inverse == nil || h.Task != nil {
		t.Error("the handler needs Plan, Apply, Verify, Observe and Inverse, and no Task")
	}
}

// TestCrRefusals: a host without a server, a Let's Encrypt server and a server without a certificate are refused
// at plan and at apply, before anything is journaled or changed.
func TestCrRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name   string
		setup  func(t *testing.T) *dpHost
		code   errcode.Code
		detail string
	}{
		{
			"no server installed",
			func(t *testing.T) *dpHost { return newDPHost(t) },
			errcode.DERPNotReady, "",
		},
		{
			"a Let's Encrypt server",
			func(t *testing.T) *dpHost {
				h := newDPHost(t)
				at := time.Date(2026, 11, 1, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
				err := insertLocalServer(context.Background(), h.db, localServer{
					ChangeID: "c0", Mode: server.ModeLetsEncrypt, HostName: "derp.example.com", VerifyClients: true,
					DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", BinarySHA256: strings.Repeat("a", 64), InstalledAt: at, UpdatedAt: at,
				})
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
				return h
			},
			errcode.InvalidInput, "derper renews a Let's Encrypt certificate itself",
		},
		{
			"derper has not written its certificate",
			func(t *testing.T) *dpHost {
				h := crInstalled(t)
				delete(h.files, crCrt)
				return h.dpHost
			},
			errcode.DERPNotReady, "",
		},
	} {
		for _, phase := range []string{"plan", "apply"} {
			t.Run(tc.name+" at "+phase, func(t *testing.T) {
				h := tc.setup(t)
				def := h.dp().certRenew()
				var err error
				if phase == "plan" {
					_, err = def.Handler.Plan(context.Background(), h.env(""), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
				} else {
					_, err = def.Handler.Apply(context.Background(), h.env("c1"), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
				}
				coded := errcode.From(err)
				if err == nil || coded.Code != tc.code || !core.IsDefinite(err) {
					t.Fatalf("err = %v (definite %v), want a definite %s", err, core.IsDefinite(err), tc.code)
				}
				if tc.detail != "" && coded.Detail != tc.detail {
					t.Errorf("detail %q, want %q", coded.Detail, tc.detail)
				}
				if len(h.units.Calls) != 0 || len(h.ran) != 0 {
					t.Errorf("unit calls %q and commands %q, want none", h.units.Calls, h.ran)
				}
				if phase == "apply" && len(crEffects(t, h)) != 0 {
					t.Error("a refused apply journaled an effect")
				}
			})
		}
	}
}

func TestCrPlan(t *testing.T) {
	h := crInstalled(t)
	out, err := h.dp().certRenew().Handler.Plan(context.Background(), h.env(""), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := []core.DiffLine{
		{Op: "-", Text: "certificate " + h.oldPin + " (valid until 2027-01-02T03:04:05Z)"},
		{Op: "+", Text: "a new self-signed certificate that derper generates when it restarts"},
		{Op: " ", Text: "derper restarts; connections through it drop"},
		{Op: " ", Text: "clients that hold the old pin cannot use the region until the DERP map carries the new one"},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(out.Diff, want) || len(out.Guards) != 0 || out.Confirmation != "" {
		t.Errorf("plan %+v, want diff %+v and no guard or confirmation", out, want)
	}
	if out.StateHash == "" {
		t.Error("the plan has no state hash")
	}
	h.files[crCrt] = h.newPEM
	again, err := h.dp().certRenew().Handler.Plan(context.Background(), h.env(""), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	if err != nil || again.StateHash == out.StateHash {
		t.Errorf("state hash %q after the certificate changed (err %v), want one different from %q", again.StateHash, err, out.StateHash)
	}
}

// TestCrApply: the old pair moves to .prev names, derper restarts and writes a new pair, and the new pin is
// stored; the effect is journaled with the old pin before any of it.
func TestCrApply(t *testing.T) {
	h, effect := crApplied(t)
	if got := crRow(t, h.dpHost); got.CertName != h.newPin || !got.CertNotAfter.Equal(crNewAfter) {
		t.Errorf("stored certificate %q until %v, want %q until %v", got.CertName, got.CertNotAfter, h.newPin, crNewAfter)
	}
	if want := []string{"restart " + server.UnitName}; !reflect.DeepEqual(h.units.Calls, want) {
		t.Errorf("unit calls %q, want %q", h.units.Calls, want)
	}
	for name, want := range map[string][]byte{
		crCrt + ".prev": h.oldPEM, crKey + ".prev": []byte("old key"), crCrt: h.newPEM, crKey: []byte("new key"),
	} {
		if got := h.files[name]; string(got) != string(want) {
			t.Errorf("%s holds %q, want %q", name, got, want)
		}
	}
	if effect.Resource != crResource || effect.Kind != core.EffectModify || !effect.Local || !effect.InverseLocal || effect.State != core.EffectMade {
		t.Errorf("effect = %s %s local %v inverse-local %v %s, want a made, local modify of %s", effect.Kind, effect.Resource, effect.Local, effect.InverseLocal, effect.State, crResource)
	}
	if !crSame(t, effect.Before.State, crDoc(h.oldPin, crOldAfter)) {
		t.Errorf("before state %s, want %s", effect.Before.State, crDoc(h.oldPin, crOldAfter))
	}
	if effect.Post == nil || !crSame(t, effect.Post.State, crDoc(h.newPin, crNewAfter)) {
		t.Errorf("post state %+v, want %s", effect.Post, crDoc(h.newPin, crNewAfter))
	}
}

func TestCrApplyOutput(t *testing.T) {
	h := crInstalled(t)
	out, err := crApply(t, h.dpHost, h.derperRestarts())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := CertRenewed{CertName: h.newPin, CertNotAfter: crNewAfter, PriorCertName: h.oldPin}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(out, want) {
		t.Errorf("output %+v, want %+v", out, want)
	}
}

// TestCrApplyTimeout: derper that writes no new pair within the wait fails the renewal with derp.verify_failed,
// leaves the effect intended, and leaves the stored pin alone.
func TestCrApplyTimeout(t *testing.T) {
	h := crInstalled(t)
	start := h.clock.Now()
	_, err := crApply(t, h.dpHost, nil)
	if err == nil || errcode.From(err).Code != errcode.DERPVerifyFailed {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v, want derp.verify_failed", err)
	}
	if waited := h.clock.Now().Sub(start); waited < certWait {
		t.Errorf("gave up after %s, want at least %s", waited, certWait)
	}
	effects := crEffects(t, h.dpHost)
	if len(effects) != 1 || effects[0].State != core.EffectIntended || effects[0].Resource != crResource {
		t.Errorf("effects %+v, want the renewal's alone, intended", effects)
	}
	if got := crRow(t, h.dpHost); got.CertName != h.oldPin {
		t.Errorf("stored pin %q, want the old %q", got.CertName, h.oldPin)
	}
	if want := []string{"restart " + server.UnitName}; !reflect.DeepEqual(h.units.Calls, want) {
		t.Errorf("unit calls %q, want %q", h.units.Calls, want)
	}
}

func TestCrInverse(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	t.Run("the old pair comes back and the old pin is stored again", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, effect := crApplied(t)
		if _, err := h.dp().certRenew().Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("c1"), effect); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if got := h.files[crCrt]; string(got) != string(h.oldPEM) {
			t.Errorf("%s holds %q, want the old certificate", crCrt, got)
		}
		if got := h.files[crKey]; string(got) != "old key" {
			t.Errorf("%s holds %q, want the old key", crKey, got)
		}
		if want := []string{"restart " + server.UnitName, "restart " + server.UnitName}; !reflect.DeepEqual(h.units.Calls, want) {
			t.Errorf("unit calls %q, want %q", h.units.Calls, want)
		}
		if got := crRow(t, h.dpHost); got.CertName != h.oldPin || !got.CertNotAfter.Equal(crOldAfter) {
			t.Errorf("stored certificate %q until %v, want %q until %v", got.CertName, got.CertNotAfter, h.oldPin, crOldAfter)
		}
	})
	t.Run("a restored pair whose pin is not the old one is an error and the row is kept", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, effect := crApplied(t)
		other, _ := crCert(t, crIP, crOldAfter)
		h.files[crCrt+".prev"] = other
		_, err := h.dp().certRenew().Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("c1"), effect)
		if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "pin") {
			t.Fatalf("err = %v, want the pin mismatch", err)
		}
		if got := crRow(t, h.dpHost); got.CertName != h.newPin {
			t.Errorf("stored pin %q, want the new %q still", got.CertName, h.newPin)
		}
	})
	t.Run("a missing .prev file changes nothing and says so", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, effect := crApplied(t)
		delete(h.files, crCrt+".prev")
		_, err := h.dp().certRenew().Handler.Inverse(ctx, h.env("c1"), effect)
		if err == nil || !core.IsDefinite(err) {
			t.Fatalf("err = %v (definite %v), want a definite error", err, core.IsDefinite(err))
		}
		if len(h.units.Calls) != 1 {
			t.Errorf("unit calls %q, want only the renewal's restart", h.units.Calls)
		}
		if got := h.files[crCrt]; string(got) != string(h.newPEM) {
			t.Errorf("%s holds %q, want the new certificate untouched", crCrt, got)
		}
	})
}

// TestCrObserve: the observation is the document of the live certificate, empty when the file is absent, which is
// how the engine tells a renewal that did not start from one that did.
func TestCrObserve(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h := crInstalled(t)
	observe := func(resource string) (core.Observation, error) {
		return h.dp().certRenew().Handler.Observe(ctx, h.env(""), core.Effect{Resource: resource, Kind: core.EffectModify})
	}
	check := func(want string) {
		t.Helper()
		obs, err := observe(crResource)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if !crSame(t, obs.Current.State, want) || obs.Matches != 1 || !obs.Exclusive {
			t.Errorf("observation %s (matches %d, exclusive %v), want %s", obs.Current.State, obs.Matches, obs.Exclusive, want)
		}
	}
	check(crDoc(h.oldPin, crOldAfter))
	delete(h.files, crCrt)
	check(crDoc("", time.Time{}))
	h.files[crCrt] = h.newPEM
	check(crDoc(h.newPin, crNewAfter))
	for _, bad := range []string{"derp.cert:derp.example.com", "derp.file:" + crCrt, "derp.cert:"} {
		if _, err := observe(bad); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("observe %q succeeded, want an error", bad)
		}
	}
}

func TestCrVerify(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, _ := crApplied(t)
	verify := func() error {
		return h.dp().certRenew().Handler.Verify(ctx, h.env("c1"), json.RawMessage(`{}`), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	}
	if err := verify(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("verify after the renewal: %v", err)
	}
	if err := setLocalCert(ctx, h.db, h.oldPin, crOldAfter, h.clock.Now()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := verify(); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "pin") {
		t.Errorf("err = %v, want the stored pin to differ from the certificate's", err)
	}
	if err := setLocalCert(ctx, h.db, h.newPin, crNewAfter, h.clock.Now()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h.units.States[server.UnitName] = core.UnitState{LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "inactive", SubState: "dead"}
	if err := verify(); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), server.UnitName) {
		t.Errorf("err = %v, want the inactive unit named", err)
	}
}
```

Expected: FAIL at build time, `undefined: certRenewPoll` and `...certRenew undefined (type dp has no field or method certRenew)`. Quote the output in the report.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

All of it goes in `cert_renew.go`; the file declares only identifiers beginning `certRenew`.

1. `func (d dp) certRenew() core.ActionDef` returns `core.DefineMutating[CertRenewInput, CertRenewed]` with `core.ActionSpec{Name: "derp.cert.renew", Summary: "Replace the DERP server's self-signed certificate with a new one.", Version: 1, Class: core.ClassCompensable, Side: core.SideAgent, LockKeys: []string{lockKey}, ApplyTimeout: 45 * time.Second, Available: d.available}` and a handler `certRenewHandler{d: d}` that has `Plan`, `Apply`, `Verify`, `Observe` and `Inverse` (the Undoer: `core.DefineMutating` picks `Observe` and `Inverse` up from the handler, and the registry requires exactly one of an undo, `CompensatedBy` or `ForwardOnly`). Declare `const certRenewPoll = 500 * time.Millisecond`.
2. Preconditions, shared by `Plan` and `Apply` and returned as `core.Definite` errors: no `derp_server` row is `errcode.DERPNotReady` ("no DERP server is installed on this host"); a row in mode `letsencrypt` is `errcode.InvalidInput` with the detail exactly `derper renews a Let's Encrypt certificate itself`; a certificate file that `d.d.readFile` cannot read, or `server.ParseCert` cannot decode, is `errcode.DERPNotReady` (say that derper has not written a certificate for the address yet). The file names come from `server.CertFiles(server.Config{Mode: server.ModeIP, HostName: row.HostName})` (`<ip>.crt`, `<ip>.key`), read under `server.CertDir`. Each precondition runs before anything is journaled or changed.
3. `Plan` shows four lines, with these exact texts: `-` `certificate <pin> (valid until <NotAfter, RFC 3339 UTC>)`; `+` `a new self-signed certificate that derper generates when it restarts`; ` ` `derper restarts; connections through it drop`; ` ` `clients that hold the old pin cannot use the region until the DERP map carries the new one`. `<pin>` is `server.Pin(der)` of the live certificate. `StateHash` is the hex SHA-256 of `<pin>|<NotAfter RFC 3339 UTC>`. No guards, no confirmation.
4. `Apply`, after the preconditions:
   - `env.Effects.Intend` a modify effect `core.Effect{Kind: core.EffectModify, Resource: "derp.cert:<ip>", Local: true, InverseLocal: true, Before: <doc of the live certificate>}` with `After` nil (the new pin cannot be known). The state document is the JSON object `{"certName": "<pin>", "notAfter": "<RFC 3339 UTC>"}`; when the file is absent the document is `{"certName": "", "notAfter": ""}`. It holds no key material.
   - `env.Files.RenameIn(ctx, ID, server.StateDir, server.CertSubdir+"/"+<crt>, server.CertSubdir+"/"+<crt>+".prev")`, then the same for the `.key` file; then `env.Units.Restart(ctx, server.UnitName)`.
   - Wait for derper's new pair: poll every `certRenewPoll` on `env.Clock.AfterFunc` (select on its channel and `ctx.Done()`, as `internal/modules/tailscale/node_login.go` does in its wait loop) until the certificate file reads back, decodes, and has a pin different from the old one, and the key file reads back (its bytes are discarded at once and never logged or stored; only its presence matters). Give up when `certWait` has passed on `env.Clock` since the restart, with `errcode.New(errcode.DERPVerifyFailed, "derper did not write a new certificate within 30s")`.
   - `setLocalCert(ctx, env.DB, newPin, newNotAfter, env.Clock.Now())`, then `env.Effects.Made(ctx, seq, resource, <doc of the new certificate>)`. The output is `CertRenewed{CertName: newPin, CertNotAfter: newNotAfter, PriorCertName: oldPin}`.
   - A failure after `Intend` returns the error and leaves the effect intended (the engine settles it through `Observe`); `Skipped` is never called and nothing is rolled back by hand.
5. `Observe(ctx, env, e)`: the resource must be `derp.cert:` followed by an IP literal (anything else is an error naming it). Read the live certificate file: absent (`errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist)`) is the empty document, a certificate is its document, any other read or decode failure is an error. Return `core.Observation{Current: <doc>, Matches: 1, Exclusive: true}`.
6. `Inverse(ctx, env, e)`: take the address from `e.Resource` as `Observe` does, and decode `e.Before.State`; an empty `certName` is an error. Then, in this order: `RenameIn` the `.crt.prev` file back over the `.crt` file (a failure here changed nothing and is returned as `core.Definite`); `RenameIn` the `.key.prev` file back over the `.key` file; `Restart` the unit; read the certificate file and compare its pin to `certName`, failing with `derp: the restored certificate of <ip> has pin <got>, want <want>` when they differ; only then `setLocalCert` with the before pin and the before `notAfter` parsed from RFC 3339. Return `core.InverseResult{}`.
7. `Verify(ctx, env, in, out)` reads only and ignores `out`, because recovery calls it with the zero value: the row must be in `ip` mode, the unit's `ActiveState` must be `active` (else an error naming `darkpanel-derper.service`), and the live certificate's pin must equal the row's `CertName` (else an error saying the stored pin differs from the certificate's).
8. Do not edit `derp.go` or any frozen file; Task 29 registers the action. No test or command here restarts a unit or touches `/var/lib/darkpanel`.

Create `internal/modules/derp/composite_deploy_test.go`:

```go
package derp

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"reflect"
	"regexp"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/schema"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/derp/artifact"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/derp/server"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/firewall"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/firewall/model"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/tailscale"
)

// cdHost is the DERP host of these tests; the agent-side test host's own id ("h1") is deliberately another.
const cdHost = "h7"

func cdBool(b bool) *bool { return &b }

// cdSteps asks def for its steps the way the engine does, through the erased handler, on the controller's database.
func cdSteps(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, def core.ActionDef, in any) ([]core.Step, error) {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := json.Marshal(in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return def.Handler.Steps(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), raw)
}

func cdJSON(t *testing.T, raw json.RawMessage) any {
	t.Helper()
	var v any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &v); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("%s: %v", raw, err)
	}
	return v
}

// cdInputRef is a template string that is exactly one reference to a member of the composite's input.
var cdInputRef = regexp.MustCompile(`^\$\{input\.([A-Za-z0-9]+)\}$`)

// cdResolve resolves the ${input.<member>} references of a step template against in as the engine does at plan time:
// a string that is exactly one reference becomes the member's value with its JSON type. A reference to a member the
// input does not hold fails the test, as it fails the plan.
func cdResolve(t *testing.T, tmpl json.RawMessage, in any) json.RawMessage {
	t.Helper()
	rawIn, err := json.Marshal(in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var input map[string]any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawIn, &input); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var walk func(v any) any
	walk = func(v any) any {
		switch x := v.(type) {
		case string:
			if m := cdInputRef.FindStringSubmatch(x); m != nil {
				val, ok := input[m[1]]
				if !ok {
					t.Errorf("template %s references input.%s, which the input does not hold", tmpl, m[1])
				}
				return val
			}
		case map[string]any:
			for k, e := range x {
				x[k] = walk(e)
			}
		case []any:
			for i, e := range x {
				x[i] = walk(e)
			}
		}
		return v
	}
	var tv any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(tmpl, &tv); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("template %s: %v", tmpl, err)
	}
	out, err := json.Marshal(walk(tv))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return out
}

// cdStrict decodes raw into v and refuses a member v does not have.
func cdStrict(t *testing.T, raw json.RawMessage, v any) {
	t.Helper()
	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(raw))
	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
	if err := dec.Decode(v); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("input %s: %v", raw, err)
	}
}

// cdAction returns the declaration of action name, version 1, of module m.
func cdAction(t *testing.T, m core.Module, name string) core.ActionDef {
	t.Helper()
	for _, a := range m.Manifest().Actions {
		if a.Spec.Name == name && a.Spec.Version == 1 {
			return a
		}
	}
	t.Fatalf("%s is not declared", name)
	return core.ActionDef{}
}

// cdSeed records, in the controller's database, a server on cdHost whose region is regionID (0: not published).
func cdSeed(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, regionID int) {
	t.Helper()
	err := upsertServerRecord(context.Background(), h.ctlDB, serverRecord{
		HostID: cdHost, Mode: server.ModeIP, HostName: "203.0.113.9", RegionID: regionID, RegionCode: "dp-eu",
		DerperVersion: "v1.103.0", UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: h.clock.Now(),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// cdWantStep fails the test unless s is the step id on host running action after the steps after.
func cdWantStep(t *testing.T, s core.Step, id, host, action string, after []string) {
	t.Helper()
	if s.ID != id || s.Host != host || s.Action.String() != action || !slices.Equal(s.After, after) {
		t.Errorf("step %+v, want %s: %s on host %q after %v", s, id, action, host, after)
	}
}

// TestCDDeclaration: both are controller-side composites that unwind in reverse and take the module's coordination lock;
// the upgrade's summary states the window between its steps, because a composite plan has no diff line of its own.
func TestCDDeclaration(t *testing.T) {
	d := newDPHost(t).dp()
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		def     core.ActionDef
		summary string // "" accepts any non-empty summary
	}{
		{"derp.server.deploy", d.serverDeploy(), ""},
		{"derp.server.upgrade", d.serverUpgrade(), "Pin tailscaled, then replace derper; between the two steps the versions differ and the region may refuse clients."},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		s := tc.def.Spec
		if s.Name != tc.name || s.Version != 1 || s.Summary == "" || s.Class != core.ClassComposite || s.Side != core.SideController ||
			s.Unwind != core.UnwindReverse || !slices.Equal(s.LockKeys, []string{"composite:derp"}) {
			t.Errorf("%s: spec %+v, want version 1, a summary, a controller-side composite, unwind reverse, lock composite:derp", tc.name, s)
		}
		if tc.summary != "" && s.Summary != tc.summary {
			t.Errorf("%s: summary %q, want %q", tc.name, s.Summary, tc.summary)
		}
		if tc.def.Handler.Steps == nil || tc.def.Handler.Output == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: the handler lacks Steps or Output", tc.name)
		}
	}
}

func TestCDDeploySteps(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		in      DeployInput
		install map[string]any
	}{
		{"letsencrypt, verification left to the default", DeployInput{HostID: cdHost, Mode: "letsencrypt", HostName: "derp.example.com"},
			map[string]any{"mode": "letsencrypt", "hostName": "derp.example.com"}},
		{"ip, verification off", DeployInput{HostID: cdHost, Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.9", VerifyClients: cdBool(false)},
			map[string]any{"mode": "ip", "hostName": "203.0.113.9", "verifyClients": false}},
		{"ip, verification asked for", DeployInput{HostID: cdHost, Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.9", VerifyClients: cdBool(true)},
			map[string]any{"mode": "ip", "hostName": "203.0.113.9", "verifyClients": true}},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := newDPHost(t)
			steps, err := cdSteps(t, h, h.dp().serverDeploy(), tc.in)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if len(steps) != 3 {
				t.Fatalf("%d steps, want tailscaled, install and ports", len(steps))
			}
			cdWantStep(t, steps[0], "tailscaled", cdHost, "tailscale.package.pin/v1", nil)
			cdWantStep(t, steps[1], "install", cdHost, "derp.server.install/v1", []string{"tailscaled"})
			cdWantStep(t, steps[2], "ports", cdHost, "firewall.rules.apply/v1", []string{"install"})
			if got, want := cdJSON(t, steps[0].Input), map[string]any{"version": artifact.PinVersion()}; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
				t.Errorf("pin input %s, want %v: tailscaled held at the derper version without its leading v", steps[0].Input, want)
			}
			if got := cdJSON(t, cdResolve(t, steps[1].Input, tc.in)); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, tc.install) {
				t.Errorf("install input %s, want %v", steps[1].Input, tc.install)
			}
			var ports struct {
				Label string       `json:"label"`
				Add   []model.Rule `json:"add"`
			}
			cdStrict(t, steps[2].Input, &ports)
			if ports.Label != "derp" || !reflect.DeepEqual(ports.Add, server.PortRules(cdHost)) {
				t.Errorf("ports input %s, want label derp and the three rules of host %s, 80/tcp, 443/tcp and 3478/udp", steps[2].Input, cdHost)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestCDDeployKeepsInputOutOfTheTemplate: the engine reads a "${" in a step template as a reference, so a value the
// operator typed reaches the install step only through a reference to the input, never spliced into the template.
func TestCDDeployKeepsInputOutOfTheTemplate(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	in := DeployInput{HostID: cdHost, Mode: "letsencrypt", HostName: "${steps.tailscaled.output.version}"}
	steps, err := cdSteps(t, h, h.dp().serverDeploy(), in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if strings.Contains(string(steps[1].Input), "steps.tailscaled") {
		t.Errorf("install input %s holds the typed host name, want a reference to the input", steps[1].Input)
	}
	var got ServerInstallInput
	cdStrict(t, cdResolve(t, steps[1].Input, in), &got)
	if got.HostName != in.HostName {
		t.Errorf("resolved host name %q, want %q", got.HostName, in.HostName)
	}
}

func TestCDUpgradeRefusesAnUnknownServer(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	_, err := cdSteps(t, h, h.dp().serverUpgrade(), UpgradeInput{HostID: cdHost})
	var coded *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != errcode.DERPNotReady || !strings.Contains(coded.Detail, cdHost) {
		t.Errorf("error %v, want %s naming host %s", err, errcode.DERPNotReady, cdHost)
	}
}

func TestCDUpgradeSteps(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name     string
		region   int
		ids      []string
		pinAfter []string
	}{
		{"a published region is checked for a fallback first", 900, []string{"fallback", "tailscaled", "binary"}, []string{"fallback"}},
		{"no region needs no fallback", 0, []string{"tailscaled", "binary"}, nil},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := newDPHost(t)
			cdSeed(t, h, tc.region)
			steps, err := cdSteps(t, h, h.dp().serverUpgrade(), UpgradeInput{HostID: cdHost})
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			var ids []string
			for _, s := range steps {
				ids = append(ids, s.ID)
			}
			if !slices.Equal(ids, tc.ids) {
				t.Fatalf("steps %v, want %v", ids, tc.ids)
			}
			if tc.region != 0 {
				cdWantStep(t, steps[0], "fallback", "", "derp.fallback.check/v1", nil)
				var fb FallbackCheckInput
				cdStrict(t, cdResolve(t, steps[0].Input, UpgradeInput{HostID: cdHost}), &fb)
				if fb != (FallbackCheckInput{HostID: cdHost, RegionID: tc.region}) {
					t.Errorf("fallback input %+v, want host %s and region %d", fb, cdHost, tc.region)
				}
				steps = steps[1:]
			}
			cdWantStep(t, steps[0], "tailscaled", cdHost, "tailscale.package.pin/v1", tc.pinAfter)
			cdWantStep(t, steps[1], "binary", cdHost, "derp.binary.update/v1", []string{"tailscaled"})
			if got, want := cdJSON(t, steps[0].Input), map[string]any{"version": artifact.PinVersion()}; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
				t.Errorf("pin input %s, want %v", steps[0].Input, want)
			}
			var bin BinaryUpdateInput
			cdStrict(t, steps[1].Input, &bin)
			if bin.Version != artifact.DerperVersion {
				t.Errorf("binary input %s, want version %s", steps[1].Input, artifact.DerperVersion)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestCDStepsMatchTheActions: the steps obey the rules the engine checks at plan time. A step that runs another
// module's action is an agent action on a host, has an undo handler when it is compensable (the composites unwind in
// reverse), the one revertible step is the last, and its input, with the input references resolved, is valid for the
// action's schema; a step that runs one of this module's actions has an input that decodes into the action's type.
func TestCDStepsMatchTheActions(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	cdSeed(t, h, 900)
	d := h.dp()
	deployIn := DeployInput{HostID: cdHost, Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.9", VerifyClients: cdBool(false)}
	deploy, err := cdSteps(t, h, d.serverDeploy(), deployIn)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	upgradeIn := UpgradeInput{HostID: cdHost}
	upgrade, err := cdSteps(t, h, d.serverUpgrade(), upgradeIn)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	foreign := map[string]core.ActionDef{
		"tailscale.package.pin": cdAction(t, tailscale.Module(), "tailscale.package.pin"),
		"firewall.rules.apply":  cdAction(t, firewall.Module(), "firewall.rules.apply"),
	}
	validator := schema.NewValidator()
	check := func(s core.Step, input json.RawMessage) {
		if def, ok := foreign[s.Action.Name]; ok {
			switch {
			case def.Spec.Side != core.SideAgent || s.Host == "":
				t.Errorf("step %s: %s is %s-side and the step names host %q, want an agent action on a host", s.ID, s.Action, def.Spec.Side, s.Host)
			case def.Spec.Class == core.ClassCompensable && def.Handler.Inverse == nil:
				t.Errorf("step %s: %s has no undo handler, which a reverse composite needs", s.ID, s.Action)
			case def.Spec.Class != core.ClassCompensable && def.Spec.Class != core.ClassRevertible:
				t.Errorf("step %s: %s is %s, which a step of these composites never is", s.ID, s.Action, def.Spec.Class)
			}
			if err := validator.ValidateInput(def, input); err != nil {
				t.Errorf("step %s: input %s is invalid for %s: %v", s.ID, input, s.Action, err)
			}
			return
		}
		switch s.Action.Name {
		case "derp.server.install":
			cdStrict(t, input, new(ServerInstallInput))
		case "derp.fallback.check":
			cdStrict(t, input, new(FallbackCheckInput))
		case "derp.binary.update":
			var in BinaryUpdateInput
			cdStrict(t, input, &in)
			if !regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$`).MatchString(in.Version) {
				t.Errorf("binary version %q does not match the action's pattern", in.Version)
			}
		default:
			t.Errorf("step %s runs %s, which the composites do not declare", s.ID, s.Action)
		}
	}
	for _, s := range deploy {
		check(s, cdResolve(t, s.Input, deployIn))
	}
	for _, s := range upgrade {
		check(s, cdResolve(t, s.Input, upgradeIn))
	}
	if last := deploy[len(deploy)-1]; last.Action.Name != "firewall.rules.apply" || foreign["firewall.rules.apply"].Spec.Class != core.ClassRevertible {
		t.Errorf("deploy ends with %s, want the revertible firewall.rules.apply, the one step that may be revertible, last", last.Action)
	}
}

func TestCDDeployOutput(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	def := h.dp().serverDeploy()
	in := json.RawMessage(`{"hostId":"h7","mode":"ip","hostName":"203.0.113.9"}`)
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		install string
		want    map[string]any
	}{
		{"ip", `{"derperVersion":"v1.104.1","certName":"sha256-raw:ab","certNotAfter":"2027-11-01T12:00:00Z"}`,
			map[string]any{"derperVersion": "v1.104.1", "certName": "sha256-raw:ab", "certNotAfter": "2027-11-01T12:00:00Z"}},
		{"letsencrypt", `{"derperVersion":"v1.104.1","certName":"","certNotAfter":null}`,
			map[string]any{"derperVersion": "v1.104.1", "certName": "", "certNotAfter": nil}},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			steps := map[string]json.RawMessage{"tailscaled": json.RawMessage(`{"version":"1.104.1"}`), "install": json.RawMessage(tc.install)}
			out, err := def.Handler.Output(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), in, steps)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if got := cdJSON(t, out); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, tc.want) {
				t.Errorf("output %s, want %v", out, tc.want)
			}
		})
	}
	steps := map[string]json.RawMessage{"ports": json.RawMessage(`{}`)}
	if _, err := def.Handler.Output(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), in, steps); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "install") {
		t.Errorf("output without the install step: %v, want an error naming install", err)
	}
}

func TestCDUpgradeOutput(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	def := h.dp().serverUpgrade()
	in := json.RawMessage(`{"hostId":"h7"}`)
	steps := map[string]json.RawMessage{"tailscaled": json.RawMessage(`{"version":"1.104.1"}`), "binary": json.RawMessage(`{"from":"v1.103.0","to":"v1.104.1"}`)}
	out, err := def.Handler.Output(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), in, steps)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got, want := cdJSON(t, out), map[string]any{"from": "v1.103.0", "to": "v1.104.1"}; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("output %s, want %v", out, want)
	}
	if _, err := def.Handler.Output(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), in, map[string]json.RawMessage{"tailscaled": steps["tailscaled"]}); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "binary") {
		t.Errorf("output without the binary step: %v, want an error naming binary", err)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `internal/modules/derp/composite_deploy.go`, in the shape of `tailnet`'s `nodeJoinComposer`. Every unexported identifier it declares starts with `serverDeploy` or `serverUpgrade`, so it cannot meet a name another task declares in this package. Both methods only build the declaration: no database read, socket or provider call happens while constructing.

1. `func (d dp) serverDeploy() core.ActionDef` returns `core.DefineComposite[DeployInput, Deployed]` with `core.ActionSpec{Name: ID + ".server.deploy", Version: 1, Unwind: core.UnwindReverse}` and a one-sentence `Summary`; `DefineComposite` itself sets the class, the controller side and the lock `composite:derp`. The handler is an empty struct type implementing `core.Composer[DeployInput, Deployed]`. `func (d dp) serverUpgrade() core.ActionDef` is the same for `UpgradeInput`, `Upgraded` and the name `derp.server.upgrade`. Its `Summary` is exactly `Pin tailscaled, then replace derper; between the two steps the versions differ and the region may refuse clients.` (Contracts "DERP composites": a composite plan carries no diff line, so the change dialog shows the window through the summary).
2. Reference rule for both composites (the engine reads every `${` in a step template as a reference, and a malformed one is an internal error): every member of a step's input that comes from the composite's input is written as a string that is exactly one `${input.<member>}` reference, using the member's JSON name; the engine replaces it at plan time with the member's value and its JSON type (a bool stays a bool) and escapes any `${` the value holds. A member the input does not hold is not written at all, because a reference to a missing member fails the plan. A value read from the controller database (the record's region id) is written into the template as a JSON number. The host id goes into `core.Step.Host`, which is not a template.
3. Deploy `Steps` returns exactly three steps, each `After` the one before it, all on `in.HostID`:
   - `tailscaled`: `tailscale.package.pin/v1`, input the JSON object `{"version": artifact.PinVersion()}` encoded with `encoding/json`, no `After`.
   - `install`: `derp.server.install/v1`, `After` `["tailscaled"]`, input an object with the members `mode` and `hostName` as references, plus `verifyClients` as a reference only when `in.VerifyClients` is not nil (nil means the install's default, on).
   - `ports`: `firewall.rules.apply/v1`, `After` `["install"]`, input the JSON encoding of a struct with `label` (`server.RuleLabel`) and `add` (`server.PortRules(in.HostID)`, a `[]model.Rule` from `internal/modules/firewall/model`); this package does not import the `firewall` package.
4. Deploy `Output` decodes the `install` step's output as `ServerInstalled` and returns `Deployed` with the same three members. A missing `install` entry is an error `derp: the deploy has no output of its install step`; a malformed one wraps the decode error with the `derp:` prefix.
5. Upgrade `Steps` loads `loadServerRecord(ctx, env.DB.Read(), in.HostID)`; no row is `errcode.New(errcode.DERPNotReady, "no DERP server is known on "+in.HostID)`. It returns, each `After` the one before it:
   - `fallback`, only when the record's `RegionID` is not 0: no host, `derp.fallback.check/v1`, input `{"hostId": <reference to hostId>, "regionId": <the record's RegionID as a JSON number>}`, no `After`.
   - `tailscaled`: on `in.HostID`, `tailscale.package.pin/v1`, the same pin input as the deploy, `After` `["fallback"]` when that step exists, else no `After`.
   - `binary`: on `in.HostID`, `derp.binary.update/v1`, input `{"version": artifact.DerperVersion}`, `After` `["tailscaled"]`.
6. Upgrade `Output` decodes the `binary` step's output as `BinaryUpdated` and returns `Upgraded{From, To}`; a missing `binary` entry is an error naming `binary`.
7. Nothing else changes: `derp.go` keeps its empty `Manifest()` (Task 29 registers the actions), and no schema snapshot is regenerated.

Create `internal/modules/derp/composite_region_test.go`:

```go
package derp

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"reflect"
	"regexp"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/schema"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/tailnet"
)

// cgHost is the DERP host of these tests; the agent-side test host's own id ("h1") is deliberately another.
const cgHost = "h7"

// cgPatch is the input of the region step: the edits the edits step computed, as one reference.
const cgPatch = `{"edits":"${steps.edits.output.edits}"}`

// cgSteps asks def for its steps the way the engine does, through the erased handler, on the controller's database.
func cgSteps(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, def core.ActionDef, in any) ([]core.Step, error) {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := json.Marshal(in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return def.Handler.Steps(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), raw)
}

func cgJSON(t *testing.T, raw json.RawMessage) any {
	t.Helper()
	var v any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &v); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("%s: %v", raw, err)
	}
	return v
}

// cgStrict decodes raw into v and refuses a member v does not have.
func cgStrict(t *testing.T, raw json.RawMessage, v any) {
	t.Helper()
	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(raw))
	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
	if err := dec.Decode(v); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("input %s: %v", raw, err)
	}
}

// cgInputRef is a template string that is exactly one reference to a member of the composite's input.
var cgInputRef = regexp.MustCompile(`^\$\{input\.([A-Za-z0-9]+)\}$`)

// cgResolve resolves the ${input.<member>} references of a step template against in as the engine does at plan time:
// a string that is exactly one reference becomes the member's value with its JSON type. A reference to a member the
// input does not hold fails the test, as it fails the plan. ${steps...} references stay.
func cgResolve(t *testing.T, tmpl json.RawMessage, in any) json.RawMessage {
	t.Helper()
	rawIn, err := json.Marshal(in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var input map[string]any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawIn, &input); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var walk func(v any) any
	walk = func(v any) any {
		switch x := v.(type) {
		case string:
			if m := cgInputRef.FindStringSubmatch(x); m != nil {
				val, ok := input[m[1]]
				if !ok {
					t.Errorf("template %s references input.%s, which the input does not hold", tmpl, m[1])
				}
				return val
			}
		case map[string]any:
			for k, e := range x {
				x[k] = walk(e)
			}
		case []any:
			for i, e := range x {
				x[i] = walk(e)
			}
		}
		return v
	}
	var tv any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(tmpl, &tv); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("template %s: %v", tmpl, err)
	}
	out, err := json.Marshal(walk(tv))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return out
}

// cgSeed records, in the controller's database, a server on cgHost whose region is regionID (0: not published).
func cgSeed(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, regionID int) {
	t.Helper()
	err := upsertServerRecord(context.Background(), h.ctlDB, serverRecord{
		HostID: cgHost, Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.9", RegionID: regionID, RegionCode: "dp-eu",
		DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: h.clock.Now(),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// cgWantStep fails the test unless s is the step id on host running action after the steps after.
func cgWantStep(t *testing.T, s core.Step, id, host, action string, after []string) {
	t.Helper()
	if s.ID != id || s.Host != host || s.Action.String() != action || !slices.Equal(s.After, after) {
		t.Errorf("step %+v, want %s: %s on host %q after %v", s, id, action, host, after)
	}
}

// cgWantPatch fails the test unless s is the region step: tailnet.policy.patch applying the edits step's edits.
func cgWantPatch(t *testing.T, s core.Step, after []string) {
	t.Helper()
	cgWantStep(t, s, "region", "", "tailnet.policy.patch/v1", after)
	if got, want := cgJSON(t, s.Input), cgJSON(t, json.RawMessage(cgPatch)); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("region input %s, want %s", s.Input, cgPatch)
	}
}

// TestCGDeclaration: the three are controller-side composites that unwind in reverse and take the module's
// coordination lock; the rotation's summary states the window between its steps.
func TestCGDeclaration(t *testing.T) {
	d := newDPHost(t).dp()
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		def     core.ActionDef
		summary string // "" accepts any non-empty summary
	}{
		{"derp.region.set", d.regionSet(), ""},
		{"derp.region.remove", d.regionRemove(), ""},
		{"derp.cert.rotate", d.certRotate(), "Renew the IP certificate, then publish its pin; between the two steps clients holding the old pin cannot use the region."},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		s := tc.def.Spec
		if s.Name != tc.name || s.Version != 1 || s.Summary == "" || s.Class != core.ClassComposite || s.Side != core.SideController ||
			s.Unwind != core.UnwindReverse || !slices.Equal(s.LockKeys, []string{"composite:derp"}) {
			t.Errorf("%s: spec %+v, want version 1, a summary, a controller-side composite, unwind reverse, lock composite:derp", tc.name, s)
		}
		if tc.summary != "" && s.Summary != tc.summary {
			t.Errorf("%s: summary %q, want %q", tc.name, s.Summary, tc.summary)
		}
		if tc.def.Handler.Steps == nil || tc.def.Handler.Output == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: the handler lacks Steps or Output", tc.name)
		}
	}
}

func TestCGRegionSetSteps(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name string
		in   RegionSetInput
		want RegionEditsInput
	}{
		{"a region added to the default ones",
			RegionSetInput{HostID: cgHost, RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "dp-eu", RegionName: "Frankfurt"},
			RegionEditsInput{Op: "set", HostID: cgHost, RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "dp-eu", RegionName: "Frankfurt"}},
		{"the default regions omitted with the acknowledgement",
			RegionSetInput{HostID: cgHost, RegionID: 941, RegionCode: "dp-us", RegionName: "Ohio", OmitDefaultRegions: true, Acknowledge: "nodes will relay only through dp-us"},
			RegionEditsInput{Op: "set", HostID: cgHost, RegionID: 941, RegionCode: "dp-us", RegionName: "Ohio", OmitDefaultRegions: true, Acknowledge: "nodes will relay only through dp-us"}},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := newDPHost(t)
			steps, err := cgSteps(t, h, h.dp().regionSet(), tc.in)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if len(steps) != 2 {
				t.Fatalf("%d steps, want edits and region", len(steps))
			}
			cgWantStep(t, steps[0], "edits", "", "derp.region.edits/v1", nil)
			cgWantPatch(t, steps[1], []string{"edits"})
			var got RegionEditsInput
			cgStrict(t, cgResolve(t, steps[0].Input, tc.in), &got)
			if got != tc.want {
				t.Errorf("edits input %+v, want %+v", got, tc.want)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestCGRegionSetKeepsInputOutOfTheTemplate: the engine reads a "${" in a step template as a reference, so a name the
// operator typed reaches the edits step only through a reference to the input, never spliced into the template.
func TestCGRegionSetKeepsInputOutOfTheTemplate(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	in := RegionSetInput{HostID: cgHost, RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "dp-eu", RegionName: "${steps.edits.output.edits}"}
	steps, err := cgSteps(t, h, h.dp().regionSet(), in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if strings.Contains(string(steps[0].Input), "steps.edits") {
		t.Errorf("edits input %s holds the typed name, want a reference to the input", steps[0].Input)
	}
	var got RegionEditsInput
	cgStrict(t, cgResolve(t, steps[0].Input, in), &got)
	if got.RegionName != in.RegionName {
		t.Errorf("resolved region name %q, want %q", got.RegionName, in.RegionName)
	}
}

func TestCGCertRotateRefusesAnUnknownServer(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	_, err := cgSteps(t, h, h.dp().certRotate(), CertRotateInput{HostID: cgHost})
	var coded *errcode.Error
	if !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != errcode.DERPNotReady || !strings.Contains(coded.Detail, cgHost) {
```
