func TestCGCertRotateSteps(t *testing.T) {
	in := CertRotateInput{HostID: cgHost}
	t.Run("a published region", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newDPHost(t)
		cgSeed(t, h, 905)
		steps, err := cgSteps(t, h, h.dp().certRotate(), in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		var ids []string
		for _, s := range steps {
			ids = append(ids, s.ID)
		}
		if !slices.Equal(ids, []string{"fallback", "cert", "edits", "region"}) {
			t.Fatalf("steps %v, want fallback, cert, edits, region", ids)
		}
		cgWantStep(t, steps[0], "fallback", "", "derp.fallback.check/v1", nil)
		cgWantStep(t, steps[1], "cert", cgHost, "derp.cert.renew/v1", []string{"fallback"})
		cgWantStep(t, steps[2], "edits", "", "derp.region.edits/v1", []string{"cert"})
		cgWantPatch(t, steps[3], []string{"edits"})
		var fb FallbackCheckInput
		cgStrict(t, cgResolve(t, steps[0].Input, in), &fb)
		if fb != (FallbackCheckInput{HostID: cgHost, RegionID: 905}) {
			t.Errorf("fallback input %+v, want host %s and the record's region 905", fb, cgHost)
		}
		if got := cgJSON(t, steps[1].Input); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, map[string]any{}) {
			t.Errorf("cert input %s, want {}", steps[1].Input)
		}
		var edits RegionEditsInput
		cgStrict(t, cgResolve(t, steps[2].Input, in), &edits)
		if want := (RegionEditsInput{Op: "rotate", HostID: cgHost, RegionID: 905, CertName: "${steps.cert.output.certName}"}); edits != want {
			t.Errorf("edits input %+v, want %+v: the new pin as a reference to the cert step's output", edits, want)
		}
	})
	t.Run("no region", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newDPHost(t)
		cgSeed(t, h, 0)
		steps, err := cgSteps(t, h, h.dp().certRotate(), in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if len(steps) != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("%d steps, want only cert: nothing is published to check or edit", len(steps))
		}
		cgWantStep(t, steps[0], "cert", cgHost, "derp.cert.renew/v1", nil)
	})
}

// TestCGStepsMatchTheActions: tailnet.policy.patch is a controller-side compensable action with an undo handler, as a
// reverse composite needs, and takes the edits the edits step yields; this module's steps decode into its types.
func TestCGStepsMatchTheActions(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	cgSeed(t, h, 905)
	d := h.dp()
	patch := func() core.ActionDef {
		for _, a := range tailnet.Module().Manifest().Actions {
			if a.Spec.Name == "tailnet.policy.patch" && a.Spec.Version == 1 {
				return a
			}
		}
		t.Fatal("tailnet.policy.patch is not declared")
		return core.ActionDef{}
	}()
	if patch.Spec.Side != core.SideController || patch.Spec.Class != core.ClassCompensable || patch.Handler.Inverse == nil {
		t.Fatalf("tailnet.policy.patch is %s-side, %s, undo handler %v; want a controller-side compensable action with an undo handler",
			patch.Spec.Side, patch.Spec.Class, patch.Handler.Inverse != nil)
	}
	validator := schema.NewValidator()
	composites := []struct {
		def core.ActionDef
		in  any
	}{
		{d.regionSet(), RegionSetInput{HostID: cgHost, RegionID: 905, RegionCode: "dp-eu", RegionName: "Frankfurt"}},
		{d.regionRemove(), RegionRemoveInput{RegionID: 905}},
		{d.certRotate(), CertRotateInput{HostID: cgHost}},
	}
	for _, c := range composites {
		steps, err := cgSteps(t, h, c.def, c.in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		for _, s := range steps {
			input := cgResolve(t, s.Input, c.in)
			switch s.Action.Name {
			case "tailnet.policy.patch":
				filled := json.RawMessage(strings.ReplaceAll(string(input), `"${steps.edits.output.edits}"`, `[]`))
				if err := validator.ValidateInput(patch, filled); err != nil {
					t.Errorf("%s step %s: input %s is invalid for %s: %v", c.def.Spec.Name, s.ID, filled, s.Action, err)
				}
			case "derp.region.edits":
				cgStrict(t, input, new(RegionEditsInput))
			case "derp.fallback.check":
				cgStrict(t, input, new(FallbackCheckInput))
			case "derp.cert.renew":
				cgStrict(t, input, new(struct{}))
			default:
				t.Errorf("%s step %s runs %s, which the composites do not declare", c.def.Spec.Name, s.ID, s.Action)
			}
		}
	}
}

func TestCGRegionOutput(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	steps := map[string]json.RawMessage{"region": json.RawMessage(`{"etag":"\"abc123\""}`)}
	tests := []struct {
		name string
		def  core.ActionDef
		in   string
	}{
		{"set", h.dp().regionSet(), `{"hostId":"h7","regionId":900,"regionCode":"dp-eu","regionName":"Frankfurt"}`},
		{"remove", h.dp().regionRemove(), `{"regionId":900}`},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			out, err := tc.def.Handler.Output(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), json.RawMessage(tc.in), steps)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if got, want := cgJSON(t, out), map[string]any{"etag": `"abc123"`}; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
				t.Errorf("output %s, want %v", out, want)
			}
			_, err = tc.def.Handler.Output(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), json.RawMessage(tc.in), map[string]json.RawMessage{})
			if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "region") {
				t.Errorf("output without the region step: %v, want an error naming region", err)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestCGCertRotateOutput(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	def := h.dp().certRotate()
	in := json.RawMessage(`{"hostId":"h7"}`)
	cert := json.RawMessage(`{"certName":"sha256-raw:aa","certNotAfter":"2027-11-01T12:00:00Z","priorCertName":"sha256-raw:bb"}`)
	out, err := def.Handler.Output(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), in, map[string]json.RawMessage{"cert": cert, "region": json.RawMessage(`{"etag":"x"}`)})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := map[string]any{"certName": "sha256-raw:aa", "certNotAfter": "2027-11-01T12:00:00Z"}
	if got := cgJSON(t, out); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Errorf("output %s, want %v: the new pin and expiry, not the prior pin", out, want)
	}
	if _, err := def.Handler.Output(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), in, map[string]json.RawMessage{"region": json.RawMessage(`{"etag":"x"}`)}); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "cert") {
		t.Errorf("output without the cert step: %v, want an error naming cert", err)
	}
}

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `internal/modules/derp/composite_region.go`, in the shape of `tailnet`'s `nodeJoinComposer`. Every unexported identifier it declares starts with `regionSet`, `regionRemove`, `certRotate` or `regionComposite` (for what the three share), so it cannot meet a name another task declares in this package. The methods only build declarations: no database read, socket or provider call happens while constructing.

1. `func (d dp) regionSet() core.ActionDef` returns `core.DefineComposite[RegionSetInput, RegionChanged]` with `core.ActionSpec{Name: ID + ".region.set", Version: 1, Unwind: core.UnwindReverse}` and a one-sentence `Summary`; `DefineComposite` itself sets the class, the controller side and the lock `composite:derp`. `regionRemove()` is the same for `RegionRemoveInput`, `RegionChanged` and `derp.region.remove`; `certRotate()` for `CertRotateInput`, `CertRotated` and `derp.cert.rotate`. `certRotate`'s `Summary` is exactly `Renew the IP certificate, then publish its pin; between the two steps clients holding the old pin cannot use the region.` (Contracts "DERP composites"). Each handler is an empty struct type implementing `core.Composer`.
2. Reference rule (the engine reads every `${` in a step template as a reference, and a malformed one is an internal error): every member of a step's input that comes from the composite's input is written as a string that is exactly one `${input.<member>}` reference, using the member's JSON name; the engine replaces it at plan time with the member's value and its JSON type (a number stays a number, a bool a bool) and escapes any `${` the value holds. A member the input does not hold is not written at all, because a reference to a missing member fails the plan. A value read from the controller database (the record's region id) is written into the template as a JSON number. The host id goes into `core.Step.Host`, which is not a template.
3. Every composite here has a step `region`: no host, `tailnet.policy.patch/v1`, `After` `["edits"]`, input exactly the fixed text below (a whole-string reference to the edits step's output, which keeps its JSON type, an array). One unexported constant and one helper build it for all three composites.

   ```
   {"edits":"${steps.edits.output.edits}"}
   ```
4. `regionSet` `Steps` returns `edits` then `region`. `edits`: no host, `derp.region.edits/v1`, no `After`, input an object with the member `op` set to the literal `"set"` and the members `hostId`, `regionId`, `regionCode` and `regionName` as references, plus `omitDefaultRegions` as a reference only when `in.OmitDefaultRegions` is true and `acknowledge` as a reference only when `in.Acknowledge` is not empty. `Output` decodes the `region` step's output (`{"etag": ...}`, the tailnet module's `PolicyWritten`) as `RegionChanged` and returns it as the patch reported it; a missing `region` entry is an error naming `region`.
5. `regionRemove` `Steps` returns `edits` then `region`. `edits`: no host, `derp.region.edits/v1`, input `op` the literal `"remove"` and `regionId` as a reference. Its `Output` is the same as `regionSet`'s.
6. `certRotate` `Steps` loads `loadServerRecord(ctx, env.DB.Read(), in.HostID)`; no row is `errcode.New(errcode.DERPNotReady, "no DERP server is known on "+in.HostID)`. With the record's `RegionID` not 0 it returns four steps, each `After` the one before it: `fallback` (no host, `derp.fallback.check/v1`, input `{"hostId": <reference to hostId>, "regionId": <the record's RegionID as a JSON number>}`), `cert` (on `in.HostID`, `derp.cert.renew/v1`, input `{}`, `After` `["fallback"]`), `edits` (no host, `derp.region.edits/v1`, `After` `["cert"]`, input `op` the literal `"rotate"`, `hostId` as a reference, `regionId` the record's RegionID as a JSON number, and `certName` the whole-string reference `${steps.cert.output.certName}`) and `region` (the shared patch step). With `RegionID` 0 it returns only `cert`, with no `After`. `Output` decodes the `cert` step's output as `CertRenewed` and returns `CertRotated{CertName, CertNotAfter}`, leaving out the prior pin; a missing `cert` entry is an error naming `cert`.
7. Error strings start with `derp:`. The composites add no diff line of their own; a composite plan is the children's. `derp.go` keeps its empty `Manifest()` (Task 29 registers the actions), and no schema snapshot is regenerated.

```go
package derp

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/schema"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/derp/server"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/firewall"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/tailnet"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/tailscale"
)

// cmHost is the DERP host of these tests; the agent-side test host's own id ("h1") is deliberately another.
const cmHost = "h7"

// cmSteps asks the composite for its steps the way the engine does, through the erased handler, on the controller's
// database.
func cmSteps(t *testing.T, d dp, h *dpHost) ([]core.Step, error) {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := json.Marshal(ServerRemoveInput{HostID: cmHost})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return d.serverRemove().Handler.Steps(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), raw)
}

func cmJSON(t *testing.T, raw json.RawMessage) any {
	t.Helper()
	var v any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &v); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("%s: %v", raw, err)
	}
	return v
}

// cmSeed records, in the controller's database, a server on cmHost whose region is regionID (0: not published).
func cmSeed(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, regionID int) {
	t.Helper()
	err := upsertServerRecord(context.Background(), h.ctlDB, serverRecord{
		HostID: cmHost, Mode: server.ModeIP, HostName: "203.0.113.9", RegionID: regionID, RegionCode: "dp-eu",
		DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: h.clock.Now(),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// cmWantStep fails the test unless s is the step id on host running action after the steps after.
func cmWantStep(t *testing.T, s core.Step, id, host, action string, after []string) {
	t.Helper()
	if s.ID != id || s.Host != host || s.Action.String() != action || !slices.Equal(s.After, after) {
		t.Errorf("step %+v, want %s: %s on host %q after %v", s, id, action, host, after)
	}
}

// cmAction returns the declaration of action name, version 1, of module m.
func cmAction(t *testing.T, m core.Module, name string) core.ActionDef {
	t.Helper()
	for _, a := range m.Manifest().Actions {
		if a.Spec.Name == name && a.Spec.Version == 1 {
			return a
		}
	}
	t.Fatalf("%s is not declared", name)
	return core.ActionDef{}
}

		s.Unwind != core.UnwindForward || !slices.Equal(s.LockKeys, []string{"composite:derp"}) {
		t.Errorf("spec %+v, want version 1, a summary, a controller-side composite, unwind forward, lock composite:derp", s)
	}
}

func TestCMSteps(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name      string
		seed      bool
		regionID  int
		connected bool
		ids       []string
	}{
		{"a published region on a connected tailnet is removed first", true, 905, true, []string{"edits", "region", "uninstall", "unpin", "ports"}},
		{"no region", true, 0, true, []string{"uninstall", "unpin", "ports"}},
		{"a tailnet that is not connected has no policy to edit", true, 905, false, []string{"uninstall", "unpin", "ports"}},
		{"a server the controller does not know is still removed", false, 0, true, []string{"uninstall", "unpin", "ports"}},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := newDPHost(t)
			h.tailnet = tc.connected
			if tc.seed {
				cmSeed(t, h, tc.regionID)
			}
			steps, err := cmSteps(t, h.dp(), h)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			var ids []string
			for _, s := range steps {
				ids = append(ids, s.ID)
			}
			if !slices.Equal(ids, tc.ids) {
				t.Fatalf("steps %v, want %v", ids, tc.ids)
			}
			var after []string
			if len(steps) == 5 {
				cmWantStep(t, steps[0], "edits", "", "derp.region.edits/v1", nil)
				cmWantStep(t, steps[1], "region", "", "tailnet.policy.patch/v1", []string{"edits"})
				if got, want := cmJSON(t, steps[0].Input), (map[string]any{"op": "remove", "regionId": float64(tc.regionID)}); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
					t.Errorf("edits input %s, want %v: the record's region", steps[0].Input, want)
				}
				if got, want := cmJSON(t, steps[1].Input), (map[string]any{"edits": "${steps.edits.output.edits}"}); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
					t.Errorf("region input %s, want %v", steps[1].Input, want)
				}
				after, steps = []string{"region"}, steps[2:]
			}
			cmWantStep(t, steps[0], "uninstall", cmHost, "derp.server.uninstall/v1", after)
			cmWantStep(t, steps[1], "unpin", cmHost, "tailscale.package.unpin/v1", []string{"uninstall"})
			cmWantStep(t, steps[2], "ports", cmHost, "firewall.rules.apply/v1", []string{"unpin"})
			for _, s := range steps[:2] {
				if got := cmJSON(t, s.Input); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, map[string]any{}) {
					t.Errorf("step %s input %s, want {}", s.ID, s.Input)
				}
			}
			want := map[string]any{"delete": []any{server.RuleID(cmHost, "80/tcp"), server.RuleID(cmHost, "443/tcp"), server.RuleID(cmHost, "3478/udp")}}
			if got := cmJSON(t, steps[2].Input); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
				t.Errorf("ports input %s, want %v: only the three rules of host %s", steps[2].Input, want, cmHost)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestCMConnectedIsAskedOnlyForARegion: a server with no region never needs the tailnet account, and an error while
// asking whether it is connected fails the steps rather than skipping the region.
func TestCMConnectedIsAskedOnlyForARegion(t *testing.T) {
	boom := errors.New("boom")
	asked := 0
	h := newDPHost(t)
	d := h.dp()
	d.d.connected = func(context.Context, *store.DB) (bool, error) { asked++; return false, boom }
	cmSeed(t, h, 0)
	if _, err := cmSteps(t, d, h); err != nil || asked != 0 {
		t.Errorf("no region: error %v after %d questions, want none and none", err, asked)
	}
	cmSeed(t, h, 905)
	if _, err := cmSteps(t, d, h); !errors.Is(err, boom) || asked != 1 {
		t.Errorf("a region: error %v after %d questions, want the connected error after one", err, asked)
	}
}

// TestCMStepsMatchTheActions: the composite is forward, so every non-read step's action converges, the one revertible
// step is the last, and an agent action has a host; each input is valid for its action's schema.
func TestCMStepsMatchTheActions(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.tailnet = true
	cmSeed(t, h, 905)
	steps, err := cmSteps(t, h.dp(), h)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	foreign := map[string]core.ActionDef{
		"tailnet.policy.patch":    cmAction(t, tailnet.Module(), "tailnet.policy.patch"),
		"tailscale.package.unpin": cmAction(t, tailscale.Module(), "tailscale.package.unpin"),
		"firewall.rules.apply":    cmAction(t, firewall.Module(), "firewall.rules.apply"),
	}
	validator := schema.NewValidator()
	for _, s := range steps {
		def, ok := foreign[s.Action.Name]
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		switch {
		case !def.Spec.Converges:
			t.Errorf("step %s: %s does not converge, which a forward composite's steps must", s.ID, s.Action)
		case def.Spec.Side == core.SideController && s.Host != "", def.Spec.Side == core.SideAgent && s.Host == "":
			t.Errorf("step %s: %s is %s-side but the step names host %q", s.ID, s.Action, def.Spec.Side, s.Host)
		}
		input := json.RawMessage(strings.ReplaceAll(string(s.Input), `"${steps.edits.output.edits}"`, `[]`))
		if err := validator.ValidateInput(def, input); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("step %s: input %s is invalid for %s: %v", s.ID, input, s.Action, err)
		}
	}
	if last := steps[len(steps)-1]; last.Action.Name != "firewall.rules.apply" || foreign["firewall.rules.apply"].Spec.Class != core.ClassRevertible {
		t.Errorf("the last step is %s, want the revertible firewall.rules.apply", last.Action)
	}
	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(steps[0].Input))
	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
	if err := dec.Decode(new(RegionEditsInput)); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("edits input %s: %v", steps[0].Input, err)
	}
}

func TestCMOutput(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	def := h.dp().serverRemove()
	in := json.RawMessage(`{"hostId":"h7"}`)
	tests := []struct {
		name      string
		uninstall string
		want      string
	}{
		{"what was removed", `{"removed":["derp.unit:darkpanel-derper.service","derp.user:darkpanel-derp"]}`, `{"removed":["derp.unit:darkpanel-derper.service","derp.user:darkpanel-derp"]}`},
		{"nothing was installed", `{"removed":[]}`, `{"removed":[]}`},
		{"no list at all is still a list", `{}`, `{"removed":[]}`},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			steps := map[string]json.RawMessage{"uninstall": json.RawMessage(tc.uninstall), "unpin": json.RawMessage(`{"version":"1.104.1","held":false}`), "ports": json.RawMessage(`{}`)}
			out, err := def.Handler.Output(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), in, steps)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if got, want := cmJSON(t, out), cmJSON(t, json.RawMessage(tc.want)); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
				t.Errorf("output %s, want %s", out, tc.want)
			}
		})
	}
	if _, err := def.Handler.Output(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), in, map[string]json.RawMessage{"ports": json.RawMessage(`{}`)}); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "uninstall") {
		t.Errorf("output without the uninstall step: %v, want an error naming uninstall", err)
	}
}
```

Expected: FAIL, a build failure `d.serverRemove undefined (type dp has no field or method serverRemove)`. Quote it.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `internal/modules/derp/composite_remove.go`. Every unexported identifier it declares starts with `serverRemove`, so it cannot meet a name another task declares in this package. The method only builds the declaration: no database read, socket or provider call happens while constructing.

1. `func (d dp) serverRemove() core.ActionDef` returns `core.DefineComposite[ServerRemoveInput, ServerRemoved]` with `core.ActionSpec{Name: ID + ".server.remove", Version: 1, Unwind: core.UnwindForward}` and a one-sentence `Summary`; `DefineComposite` itself sets the class, the controller side and the lock `composite:derp`. The handler is a struct holding the `dp` (it needs `d.d.connected`) and implements `core.Composer[ServerRemoveInput, ServerRemoved]`. The composite is forward: the engine repeats its steps on repair, which is why every step's action converges.
2. `Steps` loads `loadServerRecord(ctx, env.DB.Read(), in.HostID)`; a missing row is not an error (the controller may not have read the server yet, and the uninstall converges), it is a record with region 0. Only when the record's `RegionID` is not 0 does it ask `d.d.connected(ctx, env.DB)`, and it returns that call's error unchanged; when the answer is true it returns, first, `edits` (no host, `derp.region.edits/v1`, no `After`, input `{"op":"remove","regionId":<the record's RegionID as a JSON number>}`) and `region` (no host, `tailnet.policy.patch/v1`, `After` `["edits"]`, input exactly the fixed text `{"edits":"${steps.edits.output.edits}"}`, a whole-string reference to the edits step's output).
3. Then, always, three steps on `in.HostID`: `uninstall` (`derp.server.uninstall/v1`, input `{}`, `After` `["region"]` when the region steps exist, else no `After`), `unpin` (`tailscale.package.unpin/v1`, input `{}`, `After` `["uninstall"]`) and `ports` (`firewall.rules.apply/v1`, `After` `["unpin"]`, input the JSON encoding of a struct with the single member `delete`: the three ids of `server.PortRules(in.HostID)` in that order, which are `server.RuleID(host, "80/tcp")`, `"443/tcp"` and `"3478/udp"`). The ports step is the one revertible step and stays last. This package does not import the `firewall` package.
4. `Output` decodes the `uninstall` step's output as `ServerUninstalled` and returns `ServerRemoved{Removed}`; a `Removed` that is nil becomes an empty slice, so the output encodes `"removed":[]`, never `null`. A missing `uninstall` entry is an error naming `uninstall`, with the `derp:` prefix.
5. `derp.go` keeps its empty `Manifest()` (Task 29 registers the action), and no schema snapshot is regenerated.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `internal/modules/derp/binary_update_recover_test.go`. The tests seed no effect in the journal and assert nothing about it: `rollback` (Task 16) owns what it writes there.

```go
package derp

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"maps"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/derp/server"
)

const (
	rcResource = "derp.binary:derper"
	rcFrom     = "v1.103.0"
	rcTo       = "v1.104.1"
	rcRestart  = "restart " + server.UnitName
)

var (
	// rcOld and rcNew are the contents of the binary before and after the update.
	rcOld = []byte("derper v1.103.0")
	rcNew = []byte("derper v1.104.1")

	rcActive   = core.UnitState{LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "active", SubState: "running"}
	rcInactive = core.UnitState{LoadState: "loaded", ActiveState: "inactive", SubState: "dead"}
)

func rcSum(b []byte) string {
	s := sha256.Sum256(b)
	return hex.EncodeToString(s[:])
}

// rcSetup is a host with a DERP server installed at rcFrom, whose unit is active.
func rcSetup(t *testing.T) (*dpHost, dp) {
	t.Helper()
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.units.States[server.UnitName] = rcActive
	err := insertLocalServer(context.Background(), h.db, localServer{
		ChangeID: "c0", Mode: server.ModeIP, HostName: "203.0.113.9", DerperVersion: rcFrom, BinarySHA256: rcSum(rcOld),
		InstalledAt: h.clock.Now(), UpdatedAt: h.clock.Now(),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return h, h.dp()
}

// rcDef is the update's handler; it fails the test when the undo, the observer or the recovery handler is missing.
func rcDef(t *testing.T, d dp) core.ErasedHandler {
	t.Helper()
	h := d.binaryUpdate().Handler
	if h.Observe == nil || h.Inverse == nil || h.Recover == nil {
		t.Fatalf("handler has Observe %v, Inverse %v, Recover %v; want all three", h.Observe != nil, h.Inverse != nil, h.Recover != nil)
	}
	return h
}

// rcAdd records the update change id in phase.
func rcAdd(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, id, phase, result string) {
	t.Helper()
	err := insertUpdate(context.Background(), h.db, updateRow{
		ChangeID: id, FromVersion: rcFrom, FromSHA256: rcSum(rcOld), ToVersion: rcTo, ToSHA256: rcSum(rcNew),
		Phase: phase, Result: result, UpdatedAt: h.clock.Now(),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// rcLayout puts the three binary files where an update that reached phase leaves them.
func rcLayout(h *dpHost, phase string) {
	delete(h.files, server.BinaryPath)
	delete(h.files, server.BinaryPrevPath)
	delete(h.files, server.BinaryNewPath)
	switch phase {
	case "staged":
		h.files[server.BinaryPath], h.files[server.BinaryNewPath] = rcOld, rcNew
	case "kept":
		h.files[server.BinaryPrevPath], h.files[server.BinaryNewPath] = rcOld, rcNew
	case "swapped", "restarted", "rolling_back", "done":
		h.files[server.BinaryPath], h.files[server.BinaryPrevPath] = rcNew, rcOld
	default:
		h.files[server.BinaryPath] = rcOld
	}
}

// rcDrive runs call, advancing the clock by a second whenever it waits on it.
func rcDrive(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, call func()) {
	t.Helper()
	done := make(chan struct{})
	go func() {
		defer close(done)
		call()
	}()
	for deadline := time.Now().Add(20 * time.Second); ; {
		select {
		case <-done:
			return
		default:
		}
		if h.clock.Pending() > 0 {
			h.clock.Advance(time.Second)
		} else {
			time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
		}
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			t.Fatal("the call did not finish")
		}
	}
}

func rcSame(t *testing.T, a, b []byte) bool {
	t.Helper()
	var va, vb any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(a, &va); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("document %s: %v", a, err)
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(b, &vb); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("document %s: %v", b, err)
	}
	return reflect.DeepEqual(va, vb)
}

// rcRestored fails the test unless the host runs the old binary again: it is at BinaryPath, neither derper.prev nor
// derper.new is left, and the unit was restarted.
func rcRestored(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) {
	t.Helper()
	if !bytes.Equal(h.files[server.BinaryPath], rcOld) {
		t.Errorf("binary %q, want the old one %q", h.files[server.BinaryPath], rcOld)
	}
	for _, p := range []string{server.BinaryPrevPath, server.BinaryNewPath} {
		if _, ok := h.files[p]; ok {
			t.Errorf("%s is still there after the rollback", p)
		}
	}
	if !slices.Contains(h.units.Calls, rcRestart) {
		t.Errorf("unit calls %v, want %q", h.units.Calls, rcRestart)
	}
}

// rcPhase is the phase of update id.
func rcPhase(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, id string) string {
	t.Helper()
	u, ok, err := loadUpdate(context.Background(), h.db.Read(), id)
	if err != nil || !ok {
		t.Fatalf("update %s: %v, %v", id, ok, err)
	}
	return u.Phase
}

func TestRCDefinition(t *testing.T) {
	_, d := rcSetup(t)
	h := rcDef(t, d)
	if h.Plan == nil || h.Task == nil || h.Verify == nil || h.Apply != nil {
		t.Error("handler lacks Plan, Task or Verify, or has an Apply")
	}
}

func TestRCObserve(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	e := core.Effect{ChangeID: "c1", Resource: rcResource}
	t.Run("the version of the record and the hash of the file", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, d := rcSetup(t)
		h.files[server.BinaryPath] = rcNew
		if err := setLocalBinary(ctx, h.db, rcTo, rcSum(rcNew), h.clock.Now()); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		obs, err := rcDef(t, d).Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
		want := `{"version":"` + rcTo + `","sha256":"` + rcSum(rcNew) + `"}`
		if err != nil || !rcSame(t, obs.Current.State, []byte(want)) || obs.Matches != 1 || !obs.Exclusive {
			t.Errorf("observation %+v (%v), want %s, one exclusive match", obs, err, want)
		}
	})
	t.Run("the hash is the file's, not the record's", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, d := rcSetup(t)
		h.files[server.BinaryPath] = rcNew
		obs, err := rcDef(t, d).Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
		want := `{"version":"` + rcFrom + `","sha256":"` + rcSum(rcNew) + `"}`
		if err != nil || !rcSame(t, obs.Current.State, []byte(want)) {
			t.Errorf("observation %+v (%v), want %s", obs, err, want)
		}
	})
	t.Run("no record", func(t *testing.T) {
		h := newDPHost(t)
		h.files[server.BinaryPath] = rcNew
		obs, err := rcDef(t, h.dp()).Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
		if err != nil || string(obs.Current.State) != "null" || obs.Matches != 1 || !obs.Exclusive {
			t.Errorf("observation %+v (%v), want the state null, one exclusive match", obs, err)
		}
	})
	t.Run("no binary", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, d := rcSetup(t)
		obs, err := rcDef(t, d).Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e)
		if err != nil || string(obs.Current.State) != "null" {
			t.Errorf("observation %+v (%v), want the state null", obs, err)
		}
	})
	t.Run("a binary that cannot be read", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, d := rcSetup(t)
		d.d.readFile = func(string) ([]byte, error) { return nil, errors.New("input/output error") }
		if _, err := rcDef(t, d).Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), e); err == nil {
			t.Error("observed with an unreadable binary, want an error")
		}
	})
	t.Run("another resource", func(t *testing.T) {
		h, d := rcSetup(t)
		if _, err := rcDef(t, d).Observe(ctx, h.env("c1"), core.Effect{Resource: "derp.cert:203.0.113.9"}); err == nil {
			t.Error("observed another resource, want an error")
		}
	})
}

func TestRCRecoverWithoutARollback(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name   string
		phase  string // "" for no update row
		result string
		layout string
		want   core.RecoveryOutcome
	}{
		{"no update row: only a staged binary can be left", "", "", "staged", core.RecoveryOutcome{State: core.RecoveryRolledBack}},
		{"staged: derper.new is removed", "staged", "", "staged", core.RecoveryOutcome{State: core.RecoveryRolledBack}},
		{"staged with nothing written yet", "staged", "", "", core.RecoveryOutcome{State: core.RecoveryRolledBack}},
		{"already rolled back", "rolled_back", "", "", core.RecoveryOutcome{State: core.RecoveryRolledBack}},
		{"done", "done", "", "done", core.RecoveryOutcome{State: core.RecoveryCompleted, Output: json.RawMessage(`{"from":"` + rcFrom + `","to":"` + rcTo + `"}`)}},
		{"failed", "failed", "the unit is not active after the rollback", "swapped", core.RecoveryOutcome{State: core.RecoveryNeedsRepair}},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			ctx := context.Background()
			h, d := rcSetup(t)
			rcLayout(h, tc.layout)
			before := maps.Clone(h.files)
			if tc.phase != "" {
				rcAdd(t, h, "c1", tc.phase, tc.result)
			}
			out, err := rcDef(t, d).Recover(ctx, h.env("c1"))
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if out.State != tc.want.State || (tc.want.Output != nil && !rcSame(t, out.Output, tc.want.Output)) {
				t.Errorf("outcome %+v, want %+v", out, tc.want)
			}
			if tc.want.State == core.RecoveryNeedsRepair && (!strings.Contains(out.Reason, rcTo) || !strings.Contains(out.Reason, tc.result)) {
				t.Errorf("reason %q, want the target version and the update's result", out.Reason)
			}
			if len(h.units.Calls) != 0 || len(h.ran) != 0 {
				t.Errorf("recover restarted %v and ran %v, want nothing", h.units.Calls, h.ran)
			}
			wantFiles := before
			if tc.layout == "staged" && (tc.phase == "" || tc.phase == "staged") {
				wantFiles = map[string][]byte{server.BinaryPath: rcOld}
			}
			if !reflect.DeepEqual(h.files, wantFiles) {
				t.Errorf("files %v, want %v", slices.Sorted(maps.Keys(h.files)), slices.Sorted(maps.Keys(wantFiles)))
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestRCRecoverRollsAnInterruptedUpdateBack(t *testing.T) {
	for _, phase := range []string{"kept", "swapped", "restarted", "rolling_back"} {
		t.Run(phase, func(t *testing.T) {
			ctx := context.Background()
			h, d := rcSetup(t)
			rcLayout(h, phase)
			rcAdd(t, h, "c1", phase, "")
			var out core.RecoveryOutcome
			var err error
			rcDrive(t, h, func() { out, err = rcDef(t, d).Recover(ctx, h.env("c1")) })
			if err != nil || out.State != core.RecoveryRolledBack {
				t.Fatalf("outcome %+v, %v; want %s", out, err, core.RecoveryRolledBack)
			}
			rcRestored(t, h)
			if got := rcPhase(t, h, "c1"); got != "rolled_back" {
				t.Errorf("update phase %q, want rolled_back", got)
			}
			if row, _, err := loadLocalServer(ctx, h.db.Read()); err != nil || row.DerperVersion != rcFrom {
				t.Errorf("record %+v, %v; want the version %s it still holds", row, err, rcFrom)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestRCRecoverNeedsRepairWhenTheRollbackFails(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, d := rcSetup(t)
	h.units.States[server.UnitName] = rcInactive
	rcLayout(h, "swapped")
	rcAdd(t, h, "c1", "swapped", "")
	var out core.RecoveryOutcome
	var err error
	rcDrive(t, h, func() { out, err = rcDef(t, d).Recover(ctx, h.env("c1")) })
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out.State != core.RecoveryNeedsRepair || !strings.HasPrefix(out.Reason, "the rollback of the update to "+rcTo+" failed: ") {
		t.Errorf("outcome %+v, want %s with the rollback's error as the reason", out, core.RecoveryNeedsRepair)
	}
	if got := rcPhase(t, h, "c1"); got != "failed" {
		t.Errorf("update phase %q, want failed", got)
	}
}

func TestRCInverse(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, d := rcSetup(t)
	rcLayout(h, "done")
	rcAdd(t, h, "c1", "done", "")
	if err := setLocalBinary(ctx, h.db, rcTo, rcSum(rcNew), h.clock.Now()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var res core.InverseResult
	var err error
	rcDrive(t, h, func() {
		res, err = rcDef(t, d).Inverse(ctx, h.env("c1"), core.Effect{ChangeID: "c1", Resource: rcResource, State: core.EffectUndoing})
	})
	if err != nil || res != (core.InverseResult{}) {
		t.Fatalf("inverse = %+v, %v; want an empty result", res, err)
	}
	rcRestored(t, h)
	if got := rcPhase(t, h, "c1"); got != "rolled_back" {
		t.Errorf("update phase %q, want rolled_back", got)
	}
	if row, _, err := loadLocalServer(ctx, h.db.Read()); err != nil || row.DerperVersion != rcFrom || row.BinarySHA256 != rcSum(rcOld) {
		t.Errorf("record %+v, %v; want the old version %s and its hash back", row, err, rcFrom)
	}
}

func TestRCInverseOfARolledBackUpdateChangesNothing(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, d := rcSetup(t)
	rcLayout(h, "")
	rcAdd(t, h, "c1", "rolled_back", "")
	if _, err := rcDef(t, d).Inverse(ctx, h.env("c1"), core.Effect{ChangeID: "c1", Resource: rcResource, State: core.EffectUndoing}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(h.units.Calls) != 0 || len(h.ran) != 0 || !reflect.DeepEqual(h.files, map[string][]byte{server.BinaryPath: rcOld}) {
		t.Errorf("inverse restarted %v, ran %v and left files %v, want nothing changed", h.units.Calls, h.ran, slices.Sorted(maps.Keys(h.files)))
	}
}

func TestRCInverseFailures(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	h, d := rcSetup(t)
	def := rcDef(t, d)
	rcLayout(h, "done")
	if err := setLocalBinary(ctx, h.db, rcTo, rcSum(rcNew), h.clock.Now()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := def.Inverse(ctx, h.env("c5"), core.Effect{ChangeID: "c5", Resource: rcResource}); err == nil {
		t.Error("inverse of a change with no update row succeeded, want an error")
	}
	rcAdd(t, h, "c1", "done", "")
	if _, err := def.Inverse(ctx, h.env("c1"), core.Effect{ChangeID: "c1", Resource: "derp.cert:203.0.113.9"}); err == nil {
		t.Error("inverse of another resource succeeded, want an error")
	}
	rcAdd(t, h, "c2", "staged", "")
	if _, err := def.Inverse(ctx, h.env("c2"), core.Effect{ChangeID: "c2", Resource: rcResource}); err == nil {
		t.Error("inverse of an update that only staged a binary succeeded, want an error")
	}
	if len(h.units.Calls) != 0 || len(h.ran) != 0 || !bytes.Equal(h.files[server.BinaryPath], rcNew) {
		t.Errorf("the refused inverses restarted %v, ran %v and left binary %q, want nothing changed", h.units.Calls, h.ran, h.files[server.BinaryPath])
	}
	h.units.States[server.UnitName] = rcInactive
	var err error
	rcDrive(t, h, func() {
		_, err = def.Inverse(ctx, h.env("c1"), core.Effect{ChangeID: "c1", Resource: rcResource, State: core.EffectUndoing})
	})
	if err == nil {
		t.Error("inverse with a unit that stays inactive succeeded, want the rollback's error")
	}
	if got := rcPhase(t, h, "c1"); got != "failed" {
		t.Errorf("update phase %q after a failed rollback, want failed", got)
	}
	if row, _, err := loadLocalServer(ctx, h.db.Read()); err != nil || row.DerperVersion != rcTo {
		t.Errorf("record %+v, %v; the failed inverse must leave the record at %s", row, err, rcTo)
	}
}
```

Expected: FAIL, every `TestRC` test stopping at `handler has Observe false, Inverse false, Recover false; want all three` (the package builds, because `binaryUpdate()` exists). Quote it.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `internal/modules/derp/binary_update_recover.go`: three methods on `binaryUpdateHandler`, in the shape of `packagePinHandler`'s in `internal/modules/tailscale/package_pin_recover.go`. Every unexported identifier it declares starts with `binaryUpdateRecover`, so it cannot meet a name Task 16 declares. None of the three writes to `env.Effects`: the engine settles the update's effect through `Observe` after `Recover` returns and marks it undone after `Inverse` returns, and `rollback` does its own journal handling. Errors start with `derp:`.

1. A helper refuses an effect whose `Resource` is not `derp.binary:derper` (an error naming the resource). `Observe` and `Inverse` call it first.
2. `Observe(ctx, env, e)`: the current state is the JSON document `{"version": <derper_version of the derp_server row>, "sha256": <lower-case hex SHA-256 of the file at server.BinaryPath>}` in `core.StateDoc.State`, `Matches` 1, `Exclusive` true. The row comes from `loadLocalServer(ctx, env.DB.Read())`, the file from the handler's `dp` `deps.readFile`, never from the row's `binary_sha256`. No `derp_server` row, or a binary that is absent (`errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist)`), is the state `null` (the resource does not exist) with `Matches` 1 and `Exclusive` true; any other read error is returned.
3. `Inverse(ctx, env, e)`: load the update by `e.ChangeID` with `loadUpdate(ctx, env.DB.Read(), ...)`; no row is an error naming the change. Phase `rolled_back` returns `core.InverseResult{}` and touches nothing (an undo repeated after a crash). Phases `done` and `rolling_back` call `h.rollback(ctx, env, u)`, return its error unchanged when it fails and leave the `derp_server` row alone; on success they call `setLocalBinary(ctx, env.DB, u.FromVersion, u.FromSHA256, env.Clock.Now())` so the record names the binary that runs again, and return `core.InverseResult{}`. Any other phase is an error naming the phase and the change, run before anything on the host changes.
4. `Recover(ctx, env)`: load the update by `env.ChangeID`. It decides by the row's phase alone, because the engine calls it only once the apply unit has ended:
   - no row, or `staged`: remove `server.BinaryNewPath` through `env.Files.Remove(ctx, ID, server.BinaryNewPath)` when `deps.readFile` finds it (`fs.ErrNotExist` means absent, another read error is returned), then `RecoveryOutcome{State: core.RecoveryRolledBack}`; the row and the host are otherwise left as they are.
   - `rolled_back`: `RecoveryRolledBack`, nothing touched.
   - `done`: `RecoveryCompleted` with `Output` the JSON of `BinaryUpdated{From: u.FromVersion, To: u.ToVersion}`.
   - `failed`: `RecoveryNeedsRepair` with `Reason` `"the update of derper to <to version> failed: <the row's result>"`.
   - `kept`, `swapped`, `restarted`, `rolling_back`: call `h.rollback(ctx, env, u)`. On success `RecoveryRolledBack`; on error `RecoveryNeedsRepair` with `Reason` `"the rollback of the update to <to version> failed: "` followed by the error's text, and a nil error from `Recover`.
   - any other phase: an error naming it (the table's CHECK constraint makes it unreachable in practice).
5. `rollback`, `binaryUpdateHandler` and `binary_update.go` are Task 16's: do not edit them. If a test here fails because `rollback` behaves differently from Contracts "DERP install, update and renewal effects", stop and report it.

```go
package derp

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"net/netip"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/tailnet"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/tailscale"
)

const pbIP = "203.0.113.10"

var pbPin = "sha256-raw:" + strings.Repeat("ab", 32)

// pbStatus is derp.server.status of an installed ip-mode server.
func pbStatus() ServerStatus {
	return ServerStatus{
		Installed: true, Mode: "ip", HostName: pbIP, VerifyClients: true, DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", UnitActive: true,
		CertName: pbPin, AvailableVersion: "v1.104.1", OnTailnet: true, Tags: []string{"tag:derp"}, Addresses: []string{"100.64.0.10"},
	}
}

// pbAgent is the fake agent of host id with its answer maps ready to fill.
func pbAgent(h *dpHost, id string) *dpAgent {
	a := h.agent(id)
	if a.Reads == nil {
		a.Reads = map[string]func(json.RawMessage) (any, error){}
	}
	if a.Resources == nil {
		a.Resources = map[string]any{}
	}
	return a
}

// pbServe makes host id answer derp.server.status with s, marking each address of the request's whois list that
// visible names (address to node name) as visible.
func pbServe(h *dpHost, id string, s ServerStatus, visible map[string]string) {
	pbAgent(h, id).Reads["derp.server.status/v1"] = func(raw json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
		var in ServerStatusInput
		if len(raw) > 0 {
			if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &in); err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
		}
		out := s
		out.Visible = nil
		for _, a := range in.Whois {
			node, ok := visible[a]
			out.Visible = append(out.Visible, Visibility{Address: a, Visible: ok, Node: node})
		}
		return out, nil
	}
}

// pbNodeProbe makes host id answer derp.node.probe with both probes OK.
func pbNodeProbe(h *dpHost, id string, tlsMs, stunMs float64) {
	pbAgent(h, id).Reads["derp.node.probe/v1"] = func(json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
		return NodeProbe{
			TLS: ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: tlsMs}, STUN: ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: stunMs},
			Region: &RegionDiagnostics{Info: []string{"region reachable from " + id}, Warnings: []string{}, Errors: []string{}},
		}, nil
	}
}

// pbFailProbe makes host id answer derp.node.probe with err.
func pbFailProbe(h *dpHost, id string, err error) {
	pbAgent(h, id).Reads["derp.node.probe/v1"] = func(json.RawMessage) (any, error) { return nil, err }
}

// pbCalls decodes, in order, the inputs of the reads named read ("derp.node.probe/v1") that agent a received.
func pbCalls[T any](t *testing.T, a *dpAgent, read string) []T {
	t.Helper()
	var out []T
	for _, c := range a.Calls {
		body, ok := strings.CutPrefix(c, read+" ")
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		var v T
		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(body), &v); err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("call %q: %v", c, err)
		}
		out = append(out, v)
	}
	return out
}

// pbWorld is a controller with the DERP host h1 (alpha, a row in region 900), the probe hosts h2 (bravo) and h3
// (charlie), a connected tailnet without devices, and every probe answering OK.
func pbWorld(t *testing.T) *dpHost {
	t.Helper()
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.addHost("h1", "alpha", false)
	h.addHost("h2", "bravo", false)
	h.addHost("h3", "charlie", false)
	pbServe(h, "h1", pbStatus(), nil)
	pbNodeProbe(h, "h2", 20, 30)
	pbNodeProbe(h, "h3", 25, 35)
	h.tls = map[string]dpProbe{pbIP: {d: 10 * time.Millisecond}}
	h.stun = map[netip.AddrPort]dpProbe{netip.MustParseAddrPort(pbIP + ":3478"): {d: 5 * time.Millisecond}}
	h.tailnet = true
	err := upsertServerRecord(context.Background(), h.ctlDB, serverRecord{
		HostID: "h1", Mode: "ip", HostName: pbIP, RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "eu-derp", DerperVersion: "v1.104.1",
		CertName: pbPin, UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: h.clock.Now(),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return h
}

func pbRun(h *dpHost, in ServerProbeInput) (ServerProbe, error) {
	return h.dp().probeServer(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), in)
}

// pbNames lists the sources as "<source>=<name>".
func pbNames(p ServerProbe) []string {
	var out []string
	for _, s := range p.Sources {
		out = append(out, s.Source+"="+s.Name)
	}
	return out
}

func pbNear(got, want float64) bool { return math.Abs(got-want) < 0.001 }

// pbNodeWorld gives h1 and h2 and h3 a tailscale.status and h1 a whois view in which only two addresses are visible.
func pbNodeWorld(h *dpHost) {
	pbServe(h, "h1", pbStatus(), map[string]string{"100.64.0.10": "alpha.example.ts.net", "100.64.0.20": "bravo.example.ts.net"})
	pbAgent(h, "h1").Resources["tailscale.status"] = tailscale.Status{
		BackendState: "Running", PreferredDERP: "eu-derp",
		Self: &tailscale.Node{Addresses: []string{"100.64.0.10", "fd7a:115c:a1e0::10"}},
	}
	pbAgent(h, "h2").Resources["tailscale.status"] = tailscale.Status{
		BackendState: "Running", PreferredDERP: "nyc", Self: &tailscale.Node{Addresses: []string{"100.64.0.20"}},
		Peers: []tailscale.Peer{{Connection: "direct"}, {Connection: "derp", Region: "eu-derp"}},
	}
	pbAgent(h, "h3").Resources["tailscale.status"] = tailscale.Status{
		BackendState: "Running", PreferredDERP: "nyc", Self: &tailscale.Node{Addresses: []string{"100.64.0.30"}},
		Peers: []tailscale.Peer{{Connection: "derp", Region: "nyc"}},
	}
}

func TestPbDeclaration(t *testing.T) {
	def := newDPHost(t).dp().serverProbe()
	s := def.Spec
	if s.Name != "derp.server.probe" || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassRead || s.Side != core.SideController ||
		s.Internal || s.Reveal || s.MinScope != "" || len(s.LockKeys) != 0 || len(s.Guards) != 0 || s.Available != nil || s.Summary == "" {
		t.Errorf("spec %+v, want the controller read derp.server.probe v1 with no lock, guard, minimum scope or availability check", s)
	}
	if def.InputType != reflect.TypeFor[ServerProbeInput]() || def.OutputType != reflect.TypeFor[ServerProbe]() {
		t.Errorf("types %v and %v, want ServerProbeInput and ServerProbe", def.InputType, def.OutputType)
	}
	if def.Handler.Read == nil || def.Handler.Reveal != nil || def.Handler.Plan != nil || def.Handler.Apply != nil {
		t.Errorf("handler %+v, want the read handler only", def.Handler)
	}
}

// TestPbProbeReportsEverySource: the controller and every other host are probed in that order; an agent that
// cannot be read becomes a failed source carrying its client-safe detail; the region comes from the stored row
// and the DERP host's probe input carries the server's name, pin and region.
func TestPbProbeReportsEverySource(t *testing.T) {
	h := pbWorld(t)
	const failed = "tailscaled does not answer on its socket"
	pbFailProbe(h, "h3", errcode.New(errcode.TailscaleUnavailable, failed))
	got, err := pbRun(h, ServerProbeInput{HostID: "h1"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got.HostID != "h1" || got.RegionID != 900 || got.RegionCode != "eu-derp" || !got.Server.Installed || got.Server.HostName != pbIP || got.Server.CertName != pbPin {
		t.Errorf("probe %+v, want host h1, region 900 eu-derp and the server's status", got)
	}
	if want := []string{"controller=controller", "h2=bravo", "h3=charlie"}; !slices.Equal(pbNames(got), want) {
		t.Fatalf("sources %v, want %v", pbNames(got), want)
	}
	ctl, bravo, charlie := got.Sources[0], got.Sources[1], got.Sources[2]
	if !ctl.TLS.OK || !pbNear(ctl.TLS.LatencyMs, 10) || !ctl.STUN.OK || !pbNear(ctl.STUN.LatencyMs, 5) || ctl.Error != "" || ctl.Region != nil {
		t.Errorf("controller source %+v, want TLS 10 ms and STUN 5 ms, no error and no region diagnostics", ctl)
	}
	if !bravo.TLS.OK || !pbNear(bravo.TLS.LatencyMs, 20) || !bravo.STUN.OK || !pbNear(bravo.STUN.LatencyMs, 30) || bravo.Error != "" ||
		bravo.Region == nil || !slices.Equal(bravo.Region.Info, []string{"region reachable from h2"}) {
		t.Errorf("bravo source %+v, want TLS 20 ms, STUN 30 ms and its region diagnostics", bravo)
	}
	if charlie.Error != failed || charlie.TLS.OK || charlie.TLS.Error != failed || charlie.STUN.OK || charlie.STUN.Error != failed {
		t.Errorf("charlie source %+v, want the failure %q on the source, its TLS and its STUN", charlie, failed)
	}
	if !got.Ready || got.Warnings == nil || len(got.Warnings) != 0 || got.Nodes == nil {
		t.Errorf("ready %v, warnings %#v, nodes %#v; want ready, empty warnings and an empty node list, none null", got.Ready, got.Warnings, got.Nodes)
	}
	in := pbCalls[NodeProbeInput](t, h.agent("h2"), "derp.node.probe/v1")
	if want := (NodeProbeInput{HostName: pbIP, CertName: pbPin, RegionID: 900}); len(in) != 1 || in[0] != want {
		t.Errorf("bravo was asked %+v, want exactly %+v", in, want)
	}
}

// TestPbProbeAgentsInInputOrder: a given agent list is probed in its order, the DERP host included, no other host
// is asked, and a plain failure is reported as "the agent did not answer".
func TestPbProbeAgentsInInputOrder(t *testing.T) {
	h := pbWorld(t)
	h.addHost("h4", "delta", false)
	pbNodeProbe(h, "h1", 1, 1)
	pbNodeProbe(h, "h4", 1, 1)
	pbFailProbe(h, "h2", errors.New("connection refused"))
	got, err := pbRun(h, ServerProbeInput{HostID: "h1", Agents: []string{"h3", "h1", "h2"}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if want := []string{"controller=controller", "h3=charlie", "h1=alpha", "h2=bravo"}; !slices.Equal(pbNames(got), want) {
		t.Fatalf("sources %v, want %v", pbNames(got), want)
	}
	if e := got.Sources[3].Error; e != "the agent did not answer" || got.Sources[3].TLS.Error != e || got.Sources[3].STUN.Error != e {
		t.Errorf("bravo source %+v, want the plain failure reported as %q", got.Sources[3], "the agent did not answer")
	}
	if !got.Ready {
		t.Error("not ready, want ready: charlie, an agent other than the DERP host, passes")
	}
	if n := len(pbCalls[NodeProbeInput](t, h.agent("h4"), "derp.node.probe/v1")); n != 0 {
		t.Errorf("delta, not in the list, was probed %d times", n)
	}
}

func TestPbProbeRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	nine := []string{"h2", "h3", "h4", "h5", "h6", "h7", "h8", "h9", "h10"}
	tests := []struct {
		name   string
		setup  func(h *dpHost)
		in     ServerProbeInput
		code   errcode.Code
		detail string
		field  string
	}{
		{"a host without a server", func(h *dpHost) { pbServe(h, "h1", ServerStatus{}, nil) }, ServerProbeInput{HostID: "h1"},
			errcode.DERPNotReady, "no DERP server is installed on alpha", ""},
		{"an unknown host", nil, ServerProbeInput{HostID: "h9"}, errcode.NotFound, "", ""},
		{"an unknown agent", nil, ServerProbeInput{HostID: "h1", Agents: []string{"h2", "h9"}}, errcode.InvalidInput, "", "/agents/1"},
		{"more agents than the module probes", nil, ServerProbeInput{HostID: "h1", Agents: nine}, errcode.InvalidInput, "", "/agents"},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := pbWorld(t)
			if tt.setup != nil {
				tt.setup(h)
			}
			_, err := pbRun(h, tt.in)
			coded := errcode.From(err)
			if err == nil || coded.Code != tt.code || !core.IsDefinite(err) {
				t.Fatalf("err = %#v, want a definite %s refusal", err, tt.code)
			}
			if tt.detail != "" && coded.Detail != tt.detail {
				t.Errorf("detail %q, want %q", coded.Detail, tt.detail)
			}
			if tt.field != "" && (len(coded.Fields) != 1 || coded.Fields[0].Path != tt.field) {
				t.Errorf("fields %+v, want one at %s", coded.Fields, tt.field)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestPbProbeNodes: with a connected tailnet the nodes are its devices (first address each, one without an
// address skipped); the whois request carries exactly those addresses; a node that is a Darkpanel host takes its
// host id and its preferred region from that host's tailscale.status; observed use is a peer relayed through
// this server's region code and nothing else.
func TestPbProbeNodes(t *testing.T) {
	h := pbWorld(t)
	h.devices = []tailnet.Device{
		{ID: "d1", Name: "alpha-node", Addresses: []string{"100.64.0.10", "fd7a:115c:a1e0::10"}},
		{ID: "d2", Name: "bravo-node", Addresses: []string{"100.64.0.20"}},
		{ID: "d3", Name: "charlie-node", Addresses: []string{"100.64.0.30"}},
		{ID: "d4", Name: "phone", Addresses: []string{"100.64.0.40"}},
		{ID: "d5", Name: "no-address"},
	}
	pbNodeWorld(h)
	got, err := pbRun(h, ServerProbeInput{HostID: "h1"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := []NodeUse{
		{Name: "alpha-node", Address: "100.64.0.10", HostID: "h1", Visible: true, Preferred: "eu-derp"},
		{Name: "bravo-node", Address: "100.64.0.20", HostID: "h2", Visible: true, Preferred: "nyc", Observed: true},
		{Name: "charlie-node", Address: "100.64.0.30", HostID: "h3", Preferred: "nyc"},
		{Name: "phone", Address: "100.64.0.40"},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got.Nodes, want) {
		t.Errorf("nodes %+v, want %+v", got.Nodes, want)
	}
	calls := pbCalls[ServerStatusInput](t, h.agent("h1"), "derp.server.status/v1")
	if len(calls) != 1 || !slices.Equal(calls[0].Whois, []string{"100.64.0.10", "100.64.0.20", "100.64.0.30", "100.64.0.40"}) {
		t.Errorf("status requests %+v, want one whois list of the four first addresses", calls)
	}
}

// TestPbProbeNodesWithoutATailnet: without a tailnet account the nodes are the hosts' own nodes, named after the
// host and ordered by host id; a host whose tailscale.status cannot be read is not a node.
func TestPbProbeNodesWithoutATailnet(t *testing.T) {
	h := pbWorld(t)
	h.tailnet = false
	h.devices = []tailnet.Device{{ID: "d1", Name: "ignored", Addresses: []string{"100.64.0.99"}}}
	pbNodeWorld(h)
	delete(pbAgent(h, "h3").Resources, "tailscale.status")
	got, err := pbRun(h, ServerProbeInput{HostID: "h1"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := []NodeUse{
		{Name: "alpha", Address: "100.64.0.10", HostID: "h1", Visible: true, Preferred: "eu-derp"},
		{Name: "bravo", Address: "100.64.0.20", HostID: "h2", Visible: true, Preferred: "nyc", Observed: true},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got.Nodes, want) {
		t.Errorf("nodes %+v, want %+v", got.Nodes, want)
	}
}

// TestPbProbeCapsTheNodesItChecks: the status read takes at most 256 whois addresses, so a larger tailnet is cut
// to its first 256 devices and the probe says so.
func TestPbProbeCapsTheNodesItChecks(t *testing.T) {
	h := pbWorld(t)
	for i := range 300 {
		h.devices = append(h.devices, tailnet.Device{
			ID: fmt.Sprintf("d%d", i), Name: fmt.Sprintf("n%03d", i), Addresses: []string{fmt.Sprintf("100.64.%d.%d", i/200, i%200+1)},
		})
	}
	got, err := pbRun(h, ServerProbeInput{HostID: "h1"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	calls := pbCalls[ServerStatusInput](t, h.agent("h1"), "derp.server.status/v1")
	if len(calls) != 1 || len(calls[0].Whois) != 256 || len(got.Nodes) != 256 || got.Nodes[255].Name != "n255" {
		t.Fatalf("whois %d, nodes %d, want 256 of each ending at n255", len(calls[0].Whois), len(got.Nodes))
	}
	if len(got.Warnings) != 1 || !strings.Contains(got.Warnings[0], "300") || !strings.Contains(got.Warnings[0], "256") {
		t.Errorf("warnings %q, want one naming 300 devices and the 256 checked", got.Warnings)
	}
}

// TestPbProbeControllerFailures: a failing controller probe is a failed result in the controller source, not an
// error of the read, and the probe is then not ready whatever the agents report.
func TestPbProbeControllerFailures(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		setup   func(h *dpHost)
		tlsFail bool
		stunBad bool
		srcErr  string
	}{
		{"TLS does not answer", func(h *dpHost) { h.tls = map[string]dpProbe{} }, true, false, ""},
		{"STUN does not answer", func(h *dpHost) { h.stun = map[netip.AddrPort]dpProbe{} }, false, true, ""},
		{"the name does not resolve", func(h *dpHost) {
			pbServe(h, "h1", ServerStatus{Installed: true, Mode: "letsencrypt", HostName: "derp.example.com", UnitActive: true}, nil)
			h.resolved = map[string][]netip.Addr{}
		}, true, true, "derp.example.com"},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := pbWorld(t)
			tt.setup(h)
			got, err := pbRun(h, ServerProbeInput{HostID: "h1"})
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			ctl := got.Sources[0]
			if ctl.Source != "controller" || ctl.TLS.OK == tt.tlsFail || ctl.STUN.OK == tt.stunBad {
				t.Errorf("controller source %+v, want TLS failing %v and STUN failing %v", ctl, tt.tlsFail, tt.stunBad)
			}
			if tt.tlsFail && ctl.TLS.Error == "" || tt.stunBad && ctl.STUN.Error == "" {
				t.Errorf("controller source %+v, want an error text on each failing probe", ctl)
			}
			if tt.srcErr == "" && ctl.Error != "" || tt.srcErr != "" && (!strings.Contains(ctl.Error, tt.srcErr) || ctl.TLS.Error != ctl.Error || ctl.STUN.Error != ctl.Error) {
				t.Errorf("controller source error %q, want one naming %q carried by both results (empty when the probes themselves failed)", ctl.Error, tt.srcErr)
			}
			if got.Ready {
				t.Error("ready, want not ready with the controller failing")
			}
		})
	}
}

func pbSource(source, name string, tlsOK, stunOK bool) ProbeSource {
	s := ProbeSource{Source: source, Name: name, TLS: ProbeResult{OK: tlsOK}, STUN: ProbeResult{OK: stunOK}}
	if !tlsOK {
		s.TLS.Error = "tls failed"
	}
	if !stunOK {
		s.STUN.Error = "stun failed"
	}
	return s
}

func TestPbProbeReady(t *testing.T) {
	ctl := pbSource("controller", "controller", true, true)
	h1 := pbSource("h1", "alpha", true, true)
	h2, h3 := pbSource("h2", "bravo", true, true), pbSource("h3", "charlie", true, true)
	h2TLS, h3STUN := pbSource("h2", "bravo", false, true), pbSource("h3", "charlie", true, false)
	alone := []string{"no agent other than the DERP host probed 203.0.113.10; only the controller's probe backs the region"}
	none := []string{}
	tests := []struct {
		name     string
		sources  []ProbeSource
		ready    bool
		reason   string
		warnings []string
	}{
		{"an agent other than the DERP host passes", []ProbeSource{ctl, h2}, true, "", none},
		{"the controller alone", []ProbeSource{ctl}, true, "", alone},
		{"the DERP host's own probe does not count", []ProbeSource{ctl, h1}, true, "", alone},
		{"the controller's TLS fails", []ProbeSource{pbSource("controller", "controller", false, true), h2}, false, "controller: tls: tls failed", none},
		{"the controller's STUN fails", []ProbeSource{pbSource("controller", "controller", true, false), h2}, false, "controller: stun: stun failed", none},
		{"no agent passes", []ProbeSource{ctl, h2TLS, h3STUN}, false, "bravo: tls: tls failed", none},
		{"one agent passes", []ProbeSource{ctl, h2TLS, h3}, true, "", none},
		{"the DERP host passes but the other agent fails", []ProbeSource{ctl, h1, h2TLS}, false, "bravo: tls: tls failed", none},
		{"no controller source", []ProbeSource{h2}, false, "controller: not probed", none},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		p := ServerProbe{HostID: "h1", Server: ServerStatus{HostName: "203.0.113.10"}, Sources: tt.sources}
		ready, reason, warnings := probeReady(p)
		if ready != tt.ready || reason != tt.reason || !reflect.DeepEqual(warnings, tt.warnings) {
			t.Errorf("%s: ready %v, reason %q, warnings %#v; want %v, %q, %#v", tt.name, ready, reason, warnings, tt.ready, tt.reason, tt.warnings)
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

All of it goes in `internal/modules/derp/server_probe.go`. Only this file is new; `ServerProbe` and the other wire types already exist (Task 12) and are not redeclared.

1. `serverProbe()` returns `core.DefineRead[ServerProbeInput, ServerProbe]` with `core.ActionSpec{Name: ID + ".server.probe", Summary: "Probe a DERP server's TLS and STUN reachability from the controller and chosen agents, and show which nodes use its region.", Version: 1, Side: core.SideController}` and a handler type `probeReader{d dp}` whose `Read` calls `probeServer`. No lock key, guard, `Available` or `MinScope`. Constructing it touches no socket, file or database.
2. `probeReady(p)` is pure and never returns a nil warnings slice (an empty `[]string{}` when there is none). The controller source is the one whose `Source` is `"controller"`; none present is not ready with reason `controller: not probed`. A source passes when its `TLS.OK` and `STUN.OK` are both true. The failing check of a source gives `<Name>: tls: <TLS.Error>` when TLS failed, else `<Name>: stun: <STUN.Error>`. Rules, in order: a failing controller is not ready with its reason; the agent sources are those whose `Source` is neither `"controller"` nor `p.HostID` (the DERP host's probe of itself proves nothing); with none, ready with the single warning `no agent other than the DERP host probed <p.Server.HostName>; only the controller's probe backs the region`; with some, ready when at least one passes, otherwise not ready with the reason of the first agent source in `p.Sources` order.
3. `probeServer(ctx, env, in)` runs these steps in order and returns an error unchanged unless a step says otherwise (a cancelled `ctx` is returned as `ctx.Err()` wherever a step would otherwise treat a failure as data):
   1. Read the DERP host's name from `core_hosts` on `env.DB.Read()`. No row is `core.Definite(errcode.New(errcode.NotFound, "host <id> not found"))`.
   2. Choose the agents. A given list longer than `maxProbeAgents` is a definite `request.invalid_input` with one field error at `/agents`; each entry must be a `core_hosts` id, else a definite `request.invalid_input` with one field error at `/agents/<index>` and message `unknown host`. An empty list means every `core_hosts` row other than `in.HostID`, by id, at most `maxProbeAgents`. Names come from `core_hosts`.
   3. Read `tailscale.status` from every `core_hosts` row, by id and one after another, through `env.Agents.Agent(ctx, id)` and `AgentClient.Resource(ctx, env.Principal, "tailscale.status")`, decoding `tailscale.Status`. A host whose agent, read or decode fails, or whose `Self` is nil or has no address, contributes nothing and is not an error.
   4. Build the nodes (their `Visible`, `Preferred` and `Observed` come after step 6). When `d.d.connected(ctx, env.DB)` is true they are `d.d.devices(ctx, env)` in its order, each with its first address (a device with none is skipped), named after the device; `HostID` is the host whose `Self.Addresses` holds that address, else empty. When it is false they are the hosts of step 3 in id order, named after the `core_hosts` name, with `Self.Addresses[0]`. A connected-check or device-read error is returned. More than 256 nodes are cut to the first 256 and the probe's warnings gain `the tailnet has <n> devices; only the first 256 were checked for visibility`.
   5. `readStatus(ctx, env, in.HostID, <the nodes' addresses>)`. `Installed` false is `core.Definite(errcode.New(errcode.DERPNotReady, "no DERP server is installed on <host name>"))`. `loadServerRecord(ctx, env.DB.Read(), in.HostID)` gives `RegionID` and `RegionCode` (0 and "" without a row).
   6. Fill each node: `Visible` is the `Visibility` entry of its address in the status answer (false when absent); `Preferred` is `PreferredDERP` of its host's status ("" for a non-host); `Observed` is true when its host's status has a peer with `Connection == "derp"` and `Region` equal to the non-empty region code.
   7. The controller source: `d.target(ctx, status)`; an error gives `ProbeSource{Source: "controller", Name: "controller", Error: <err.Error()>}` whose `TLS` and `STUN` are `ProbeResult{Error: <that text>}`; otherwise `d.probeOnce(ctx, target, stunAddr)` fills `TLS` and `STUN`, and `Region` stays nil.
   8. One agent source per chosen agent, in order and one after another: `readNodeProbe(ctx, env, id, NodeProbeInput{HostName: status.HostName, CertName: status.CertName, RegionID: record.RegionID})` (no `Address`: each agent resolves the name itself). An error gives a failed source whose `Error`, `TLS.Error` and `STUN.Error` are the error's `errcode` `Detail` when it has a code other than `internal` and a detail, else `the agent did not answer`. Success copies `TLS`, `STUN` and `Region`.
   9. `Ready` and `Warnings` come from `probeReady` (its warnings first, then the cap warning). `Sources`, `Nodes` and `Warnings` are never nil.
4. Declare no other exported name and no identifier that Contracts gives (`probeTarget`, `probeTLS`, `probeOnce`, `readStatus`, `readNodeProbe`, `target`).

### Task 26: derp: servers service and list read

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/modules/derp/servers.go`
- Test: `internal/modules/derp/servers_test.go` (create)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Contracts "DERP servers service and list" (the whole behaviour) and "DERP actions" (the `derp.server.list` row); "DERP internals" (`dp`, `module`, `deps` fields `policy` and `connected`, `servicePeriod`, `staleAfter`, `expiryWarning`, `serverRecord`, `loadServerRecords`, `upsertServerRecord`, `deleteServerRecord`, `parseDERPMap` and `derpMap.regionOf`); "DERP wire shapes" (`ServerListInput`, `Servers`, `ServerView`, `ServerStatus`); "DERP shared helpers" (`readStatus`, Task 17); "Derper artifact" (`artifact.DerperVersion`); "DERP test host"; "Tailnet reads for other modules" (`tailnet.Policy`, reached through `d.d.policy`). The service follows the shape of `cloudflare.hostnames` (`internal/modules/cloudflare/hostnames_service.go`: `Controller`, `runHostnames` with `deps.Clock.AfterFunc`, `checkHostnames`), `ext.Service` and `ext.Deps` of `internal/core/ext/ext.go`, and the read the shape of `cloudflare.hostname.list` (`internal/modules/cloudflare/hostname_list.go`: `core.DefineRead` on the controller side).
- Produces: `func (d dp) serverList() core.ActionDef` and `func (m module) Controller(deps ext.Deps) ([]ext.Route, []ext.Service, error)` as Contracts "DERP actions" names them (Task 29 registers `serverList()`; the server finds `Controller` by type assertion on the module). Private: `type serverListReader struct{}`, `func serverHealth(r serverRecord, now time.Time) (health string, reasons []string)`, `func (d dp) runServers(ctx context.Context, deps ext.Deps) error` and `func (d dp) checkServers(ctx context.Context, deps ext.Deps, now time.Time) error`. The module declares no resource: the dispatcher sends every resource read to an agent (`internal/controller/dispatch/dispatch.go:182-190`), so the controller's list is an action.
