```go
package derp

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/coretest"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/ext"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/store"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/derp/artifact"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/tailnet"
)

const srIP = "203.0.113.10"

var srPin = "sha256-raw:" + strings.Repeat("ab", 32)

// srServe makes host id answer derp.server.status with s.
func srServe(h *dpHost, id string, s ServerStatus) {
	h.agent(id).Reads["derp.server.status/v1"] = func(json.RawMessage) (any, error) { return s, nil }
}

// srFail makes host id's derp.server.status answer err.
func srFail(h *dpHost, id string, err error) {
	h.agent(id).Reads["derp.server.status/v1"] = func(json.RawMessage) (any, error) { return nil, err }
}

// srNotAfter is the certificate expiry of the world's ip server: far enough for health ok.
func srNotAfter(h *dpHost) time.Time { return h.clock.Now().Add(200 * 24 * time.Hour) }

// srWorld is a controller with three hosts: h1 (alpha) runs an ip-mode server with a certificate, h2 (bravo) a
// letsencrypt server whose unit is stopped, h3 (charlie) none. No tailnet account is connected.
func srWorld(t *testing.T) *dpHost {
	t.Helper()
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.addHost("h1", "alpha", false)
	h.addHost("h2", "bravo", false)
	h.addHost("h3", "charlie", false)
	notAfter := srNotAfter(h)
	srServe(h, "h1", ServerStatus{Installed: true, Mode: "ip", HostName: srIP, DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", UnitActive: true, CertName: srPin, CertNotAfter: &notAfter})
	srServe(h, "h2", ServerStatus{Installed: true, Mode: "letsencrypt", HostName: "derp.example.com", DerperVersion: "v1.104.1"})
	srServe(h, "h3", ServerStatus{})
	return h
}

func srDeps(h *dpHost) ext.Deps {
	env := h.ctlEnv("")
	return ext.Deps{DB: env.DB, Sealer: env.Sealer, Clock: env.Clock, Log: slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler), Hosts: env.Agents}
}

func srPass(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) {
	t.Helper()
	if err := h.dp().checkServers(context.Background(), srDeps(h), h.clock.Now()); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("checkServers: %v", err)
	}
}

func srPut(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, r serverRecord) {
	t.Helper()
	if err := upsertServerRecord(context.Background(), h.ctlDB, r); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func srLoad(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, id string) (serverRecord, bool) {
	t.Helper()
	r, ok, err := loadServerRecord(context.Background(), h.ctlDB.Read(), id)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return r, ok
}

func srSame(a, b serverRecord) bool {
	return a.HostID == b.HostID && a.Mode == b.Mode && a.HostName == b.HostName && a.RegionID == b.RegionID && a.RegionCode == b.RegionCode &&
		a.DerperVersion == b.DerperVersion && a.CertName == b.CertName && a.CertNotAfter.Equal(b.CertNotAfter) && a.UnitActive == b.UnitActive &&
		a.CheckedAt.Equal(b.CheckedAt) && a.Error == b.Error
}

// srList runs derp.server.list as the controller's read handler does and decodes its answer.
func srList(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) (Servers, string) {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := h.dp().serverList().Handler.Read(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), json.RawMessage(`{}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var out Servers
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &out); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("value %s: %v", raw, err)
	}
	return out, string(raw)
}

func srWaitUntil(t *testing.T, what string, cond func() bool) {
	t.Helper()
	deadline := time.Now().Add(10 * time.Second)
	for !cond() {
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			t.Fatalf("timed out waiting for %s", what)
		}
		time.Sleep(5 * time.Millisecond)
	}
}

func TestSrDeclarations(t *testing.T) {
	d := newDPHost(t).dp()
	def := d.serverList()
	if def.Spec.Name != "derp.server.list" || def.Spec.Version != 1 || def.Spec.Class != core.ClassRead || def.Spec.Side != core.SideController ||
		def.Spec.Summary == "" || len(def.Spec.LockKeys) != 0 || def.Spec.Internal || def.Spec.Available != nil || def.Handler.Read == nil {
		t.Errorf("serverList spec = %+v, want a version 1 controller read with a summary, no lock, not internal and no availability check", def.Spec)
	}
	if def.InputType != reflect.TypeFor[ServerListInput]() || def.OutputType != reflect.TypeFor[Servers]() {
		t.Errorf("serverList types = %v and %v, want ServerListInput and Servers", def.InputType, def.OutputType)
	}
	routes, services, err := (module{d: d}).Controller(ext.Deps{})
	if err != nil || len(routes) != 0 || len(services) != 1 || services[0].Name != "derp.servers" || services[0].Run == nil {
		t.Errorf("Controller = %d routes, %+v services, %v; want no route and the service derp.servers", len(routes), services, err)
	}
	if _, ok := Module().(ext.Controller); !ok {
		t.Error("Module() does not implement ext.Controller, so the server would never start the service")
	}
}

func TestSrServerHealth(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 11, 2, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	base := serverRecord{HostID: "h1", Mode: "ip", HostName: srIP, UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: now}
	with := func(f func(r *serverRecord)) serverRecord {
		r := base
		f(&r)
		return r
	}
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		rec     serverRecord
		health  string
		reasons []string
	}{
		{"nothing wrong", base, "ok", []string{}},
		{"the unit is stopped", with(func(r *serverRecord) { r.UnitActive = false }), "broken", []string{"derper is not running"}},
		{"the certificate expired a second ago", with(func(r *serverRecord) { r.CertNotAfter = now.Add(-time.Second) }), "broken",
			[]string{"the certificate expired on 2026-11-02"}},
		{"the certificate expires now", with(func(r *serverRecord) { r.CertNotAfter = now }), "degraded", []string{"the certificate expires on 2026-11-02"}},
		{"the certificate expires in 30 days", with(func(r *serverRecord) { r.CertNotAfter = now.Add(30 * 24 * time.Hour) }), "degraded",
			[]string{"the certificate expires on 2026-12-02"}},
		{"the certificate expires in 30 days and a second", with(func(r *serverRecord) { r.CertNotAfter = now.Add(30*24*time.Hour + time.Second) }), "ok", []string{}},
		{"checked ten minutes ago", with(func(r *serverRecord) { r.CheckedAt = now.Add(-10 * time.Minute) }), "ok", []string{}},
		{"checked ten minutes and a second ago", with(func(r *serverRecord) { r.CheckedAt = now.Add(-10*time.Minute - time.Second) }), "degraded",
			[]string{"the last check was at 2026-11-02T11:49:59Z"}},
		{"the last read failed", with(func(r *serverRecord) { r.Error = "tailscaled does not answer on its socket" }), "degraded",
			[]string{"the host could not be read: tailscaled does not answer on its socket"}},
		{"every condition at once", with(func(r *serverRecord) {
			r.UnitActive, r.CertNotAfter, r.CheckedAt, r.Error = false, now.Add(24*time.Hour), now.Add(-11*time.Minute), "tailscaled does not answer on its socket"
		}), "broken", []string{
			"derper is not running",
			"the certificate expires on 2026-11-03",
			"the last check was at 2026-11-02T11:49:00Z",
			"the host could not be read: tailscaled does not answer on its socket",
		}},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		health, reasons := serverHealth(tt.rec, now)
		if health != tt.health || !reflect.DeepEqual(reasons, tt.reasons) {
			t.Errorf("%s: health %q, reasons %#v; want %q, %#v", tt.name, health, reasons, tt.health, tt.reasons)
		}
	}
}

// TestSrPassStoresInstalledServers: an installed server is stored with its status fields and the pass time, a host
// without a server loses its row, and so does a row whose host no longer exists; the status is read without a
// whois list.
func TestSrPassStoresInstalledServers(t *testing.T) {
	h := srWorld(t)
	now := h.clock.Now()
	for _, id := range []string{"h3", "h9"} {
		srPut(t, h, serverRecord{HostID: id, Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.99", DerperVersion: "v1.100.0", UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: now.Add(-time.Hour)})
	}
	srPass(t, h)
	want := map[string]serverRecord{
		"h1": {HostID: "h1", Mode: "ip", HostName: srIP, DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", CertName: srPin, CertNotAfter: srNotAfter(h), UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: now},
		"h2": {HostID: "h2", Mode: "letsencrypt", HostName: "derp.example.com", DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", CheckedAt: now},
	}
	for id, w := range want {
		if got, ok := srLoad(t, h, id); !ok || !srSame(got, w) {
			t.Errorf("record of %s = %+v (found %v), want %+v", id, got, ok, w)
		}
	}
	for _, id := range []string{"h3", "h9"} {
		if _, ok := srLoad(t, h, id); ok {
			t.Errorf("the record of %s survived, want it deleted", id)
		}
	}
	for _, id := range []string{"h1", "h2", "h3"} {
		if calls := h.agent(id).Calls; !reflect.DeepEqual(calls, []string{"derp.server.status/v1 {}"}) {
			t.Errorf("calls to %s = %q, want one derp.server.status without a whois list", id, calls)
		}
	}
}

// TestSrPassRegion: with a connected tailnet a server's region is the policy's custom region holding a node of its
// host name (ignoring case); without one it is 0; a policy that cannot be read or parsed leaves the stored region.
func TestSrPassRegion(t *testing.T) {
	const withRegion = `{"derpMap": {"Regions": {"900": {"RegionID": 900, "RegionCode": "eu-derp", "RegionName": "EU",
		"Nodes": [{"Name": "900a", "RegionID": 900, "HostName": "DERP.Example.com"}]}}}}`
	tests := []struct {
		name      string
		connected bool
		policy    tailnet.Policy
		policyErr error
		id        int
		code      string
	}{
		{"a connected tailnet publishes the region", true, tailnet.Policy{Text: withRegion}, nil, 900, "eu-derp"},
		{"no tailnet means no region", false, tailnet.Policy{Text: withRegion}, nil, 0, ""},
		{"an unreadable policy keeps the stored region", true, tailnet.Policy{}, errors.New("provider down"), 901, "old"},
		{"an unparsable policy keeps the stored region", true, tailnet.Policy{Text: "{"}, nil, 901, "old"},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := srWorld(t)
			h.tailnet, h.policy, h.policyErr = tt.connected, tt.policy, tt.policyErr
			srPut(t, h, serverRecord{HostID: "h2", Mode: "letsencrypt", HostName: "derp.example.com", RegionID: 901, RegionCode: "old",
				DerperVersion: "v1.100.0", UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: h.clock.Now().Add(-time.Minute)})
			srPass(t, h)
			got, ok := srLoad(t, h, "h2")
			if !ok || got.RegionID != tt.id || got.RegionCode != tt.code || got.DerperVersion != "v1.104.1" {
				t.Errorf("record of h2 = %+v (found %v), want region %d %q and the fresh version", got, ok, tt.id, tt.code)
			}
			if one, _ := srLoad(t, h, "h1"); one.RegionID != 0 || one.RegionCode != "" {
				t.Errorf("record of h1 has region %d %q, want none", one.RegionID, one.RegionCode)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestSrPassKeepsTheRegionWhenTheTailnetCheckFails: a failed connected check is no pass error and no reason to
// forget the stored region.
func TestSrPassKeepsTheRegionWhenTheTailnetCheckFails(t *testing.T) {
	h := srWorld(t)
	srPut(t, h, serverRecord{HostID: "h2", Mode: "letsencrypt", HostName: "derp.example.com", RegionID: 901, RegionCode: "old",
		DerperVersion: "v1.100.0", UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: h.clock.Now().Add(-time.Minute)})
	d := h.dp()
	d.d.connected = func(context.Context, *store.DB) (bool, error) { return false, errors.New("database busy") }
	if err := d.checkServers(context.Background(), srDeps(h), h.clock.Now()); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("checkServers: %v", err)
	}
	if got, ok := srLoad(t, h, "h2"); !ok || got.RegionID != 901 || got.RegionCode != "old" || got.DerperVersion != "v1.104.1" {
		t.Errorf("record of h2 = %+v (found %v), want the stored region 901 old and the fresh version", got, ok)
	}
}

// TestSrPassReadsThePolicyAtMostOnce: one policy read serves every server of a pass, and a pass that found no
// server reads none, so a tailnet without DERP costs the provider no call.
func TestSrPassReadsThePolicyAtMostOnce(t *testing.T) {
	const text = `{"derpMap": {"Regions": {"900": {"RegionID": 900, "RegionCode": "eu-derp",
		"Nodes": [{"Name": "900a", "RegionID": 900, "HostName": "derp.example.com"}]}}}}`
	for _, tt := range []struct {
		name  string
		setup func(h *dpHost)
		reads int
	}{
		{"two servers", func(*dpHost) {}, 1},
		{"no server", func(h *dpHost) {
			srServe(h, "h1", ServerStatus{})
			srServe(h, "h2", ServerStatus{})
		}, 0},
	} {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := srWorld(t)
			h.tailnet = true
			tt.setup(h)
			reads := 0
			d := h.dp()
			d.d.policy = func(context.Context, core.Env) (tailnet.Policy, error) {
				reads++
				return tailnet.Policy{Text: text}, nil
			}
			if err := d.checkServers(context.Background(), srDeps(h), h.clock.Now()); err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("checkServers: %v", err)
			}
			if reads != tt.reads {
				t.Errorf("%d policy reads, want %d", reads, tt.reads)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestSrPassKeepsAServerItCannotRead: a failed read keeps the row's fields and its check time and only sets the
// client-safe error; a host with no row stays without one; the next good read clears the error.
func TestSrPassKeepsAServerItCannotRead(t *testing.T) {
	h := srWorld(t)
	old := h.clock.Now().Add(-3 * time.Minute)
	stored := serverRecord{HostID: "h1", Mode: "ip", HostName: srIP, RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "eu-derp", DerperVersion: "v1.100.0",
		CertName: srPin, CertNotAfter: srNotAfter(h), UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: old}
	srPut(t, h, stored)
	const detail = "tailscaled does not answer on its socket"
	srFail(h, "h1", errcode.New(errcode.TailscaleUnavailable, detail))
	srFail(h, "h2", errors.New("connection refused"))
	srPass(t, h)
	stored.Error = detail
	if got, ok := srLoad(t, h, "h1"); !ok || !srSame(got, stored) {
		t.Errorf("record of h1 = %+v (found %v), want the stored fields with error %q", got, ok, detail)
	}
	if _, ok := srLoad(t, h, "h2"); ok {
		t.Error("a host that never had a row got one from a failed read")
	}
	srFail(h, "h1", errors.New("connection refused"))
	srPass(t, h)
	if got, _ := srLoad(t, h, "h1"); got.Error != "the agent did not answer" || !got.CheckedAt.Equal(old) {
		t.Errorf("after a plain failure error %q, checked at %v; want %q and %v", got.Error, got.CheckedAt, "the agent did not answer", old)
	}
	notAfter := srNotAfter(h)
	srServe(h, "h1", ServerStatus{Installed: true, Mode: "ip", HostName: srIP, DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", UnitActive: true, CertName: srPin, CertNotAfter: &notAfter})
	srPass(t, h)
	if got, _ := srLoad(t, h, "h1"); got.Error != "" || !got.CheckedAt.Equal(h.clock.Now()) || got.DerperVersion != "v1.104.1" {
		t.Errorf("after a good read %+v, want no error, the pass time and the fresh version", got)
	}
}

// TestSrPassReadsAsTheServiceActor: the status reads carry the service's own admin principal.
func TestSrPassReadsAsTheServiceActor(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.addHost("h1", "alpha", false)
	var principals []core.Principal
	var refs []core.ActionRef
	agent := &coretest.FakeAgent{ReadFunc: func(_ context.Context, p core.Principal, a core.ActionRef, _ json.RawMessage) (json.RawMessage, error) {
		principals = append(principals, p)
		refs = append(refs, a)
		return json.Marshal(ServerStatus{})
	}}
	deps := srDeps(h)
	deps.Hosts = coretest.MapHosts{"h1": agent}
	if err := h.dp().checkServers(context.Background(), deps, h.clock.Now()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := core.Principal{Actor: "system:derp.servers", Scope: core.ScopeAdmin}
	if len(principals) != 1 || principals[0] != want || refs[0] != (core.ActionRef{Name: "derp.server.status", Version: 1}) {
		t.Errorf("reads as %+v of %+v, want one derp.server.status/v1 as %+v", principals, refs, want)
	}
}

// TestSrACancelledPassWritesNothing: a context that ends during a read ends the pass with its error before any
// row changes, and the service treats that as a normal stop.
func TestSrACancelledPassWritesNothing(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.addHost("h1", "alpha", false)
	stored := serverRecord{HostID: "h1", Mode: "ip", HostName: srIP, DerperVersion: "v1.100.0", UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: h.clock.Now().Add(-time.Minute)}
	srPut(t, h, stored)
	cancelling := func(cancel context.CancelFunc) ext.Deps {
		agent := &coretest.FakeAgent{ReadFunc: func(ctx context.Context, _ core.Principal, _ core.ActionRef, _ json.RawMessage) (json.RawMessage, error) {
			cancel()
			return nil, ctx.Err()
		}}
		deps := srDeps(h)
		deps.Hosts = coretest.MapHosts{"h1": agent}
		return deps
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	if err := h.dp().checkServers(ctx, cancelling(cancel), h.clock.Now()); !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
		t.Errorf("checkServers = %v, want context.Canceled", err)
	}
	if got, ok := srLoad(t, h, "h1"); !ok || !srSame(got, stored) {
		t.Errorf("record of h1 = %+v (found %v), want it unchanged", got, ok)
	}

	ctx, cancel = context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	_, services, err := (module{d: h.dp()}).Controller(cancelling(cancel))
	if err != nil || len(services) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("Controller = %+v, %v; want the one service", services, err)
	}
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { done <- services[0].Run(ctx) }()
	select {
	case err := <-done:
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("Run = %v after its context ended during the first pass, want nil", err)
		}
	case <-time.After(10 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("Run did not return after its context ended")
	}
	if n := h.clock.Pending(); n != 0 {
		t.Errorf("%d timers armed after the service ended, want none", n)
	}
}

// TestSrListReadsTheRecords: the read joins the rows with the host names, orders them by host id, leaves out a row
// without a host and names the controller's derper version.
func TestSrListReadsTheRecords(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.addHost("h1", "alpha", false)
	h.addHost("h2", "bravo", false)
	now := h.clock.Now()
	notAfter := now.Add(20 * 24 * time.Hour)
	srPut(t, h, serverRecord{HostID: "h2", Mode: "letsencrypt", HostName: "derp.example.com", DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", CheckedAt: now})
	srPut(t, h, serverRecord{HostID: "h9", Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.98", DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: now})
	srPut(t, h, serverRecord{HostID: "h1", Mode: "ip", HostName: srIP, RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "eu-derp", DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", CertName: srPin,
		CertNotAfter: notAfter, UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: now})
	got, _ := srList(t, h)
	if got.AvailableVersion != artifact.DerperVersion || len(got.Servers) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("servers %+v, want two and available version %s", got, artifact.DerperVersion)
	}
	one, two := got.Servers[0], got.Servers[1]
	if one.HostID != "h1" || one.Host != "alpha" || one.Mode != "ip" || one.HostName != srIP || one.RegionID != 900 || one.RegionCode != "eu-derp" ||
		one.DerperVersion != "v1.104.1" || one.CertName != srPin || one.CertNotAfter == nil || !one.CertNotAfter.Equal(notAfter) || !one.UnitActive ||
		one.Health != "degraded" || !reflect.DeepEqual(one.Reasons, []string{"the certificate expires on " + notAfter.UTC().Format("2006-01-02")}) ||
		!one.CheckedAt.Equal(now) || one.Error != "" {
		t.Errorf("first server %+v, want h1 (alpha) degraded by its certificate", one)
	}
	if two.HostID != "h2" || two.Host != "bravo" || two.CertNotAfter != nil || two.Health != "broken" || !reflect.DeepEqual(two.Reasons, []string{"derper is not running"}) {
		t.Errorf("second server %+v, want h2 (bravo) broken by its stopped unit and no certificate expiry", two)
	}
}

// TestSrListHealthIsComputedAtRead: a row the service stopped refreshing turns degraded as the clock moves, without
// a new pass.
func TestSrListHealthIsComputedAtRead(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.addHost("h1", "alpha", false)
	srPut(t, h, serverRecord{HostID: "h1", Mode: "letsencrypt", HostName: "derp.example.com", DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: h.clock.Now()})
	if got, _ := srList(t, h); len(got.Servers) != 1 || got.Servers[0].Health != "ok" {
		t.Fatalf("servers %+v, want one ok server", got.Servers)
	}
	h.clock.Advance(11 * time.Minute)
	got, _ := srList(t, h)
	if len(got.Servers) != 1 || got.Servers[0].Health != "degraded" ||
		!reflect.DeepEqual(got.Servers[0].Reasons, []string{"the last check was at 2026-11-02T12:00:00Z"}) {
		t.Errorf("servers %+v, want one degraded server whose last check was at 12:00:00", got.Servers)
	}
}

func TestSrListWithoutRecords(t *testing.T) {
	_, raw := srList(t, newDPHost(t))
	if !strings.Contains(raw, `"servers":[]`) {
		t.Errorf("value %s, want an empty servers list and not null", raw)
	}
}

// TestSrServiceChecksEveryMinute: a pass runs at start and the next one a service period later, not before, and
// the service ends without error when its context does.
func TestSrServiceChecksEveryMinute(t *testing.T) {
	h := srWorld(t)
	_, services, err := (module{d: h.dp()}).Controller(srDeps(h))
	if err != nil || len(services) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("Controller = %+v, %v; want the one service", services, err)
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	t.Cleanup(cancel)
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { done <- services[0].Run(ctx) }()

	srWaitUntil(t, "the first pass and the armed timer", func() bool { return h.clock.Pending() == 1 })
	if _, ok := srLoad(t, h, "h1"); !ok {
		t.Fatal("the pass at start stored no record for h1")
	}
	if err := deleteServerRecord(context.Background(), h.ctlDB, "h1"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h.clock.Advance(servicePeriod - time.Second)
	if _, ok := srLoad(t, h, "h1"); ok {
		t.Error("a pass ran before the service period ended")
	}
	if n := h.clock.Pending(); n != 1 {
		t.Errorf("%d timers armed before the period ended, want the one", n)
	}
	h.clock.Advance(time.Second)
	srWaitUntil(t, "the second pass", func() bool { _, ok := srLoad(t, h, "h1"); return ok })
	srWaitUntil(t, "the timer armed again after the second pass", func() bool { return h.clock.Pending() == 1 })

	cancel()
	select {
	case err := <-done:
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("Run returned %v after its context ended, want nil", err)
		}
	case <-time.After(10 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("Run did not return after its context ended")
	}
}

// TestSrServiceEndsOnAPassError: a pass that fails for a reason other than the context ending ends the service with
// that error, so the supervisor restarts it, and leaves no timer armed.
func TestSrServiceEndsOnAPassError(t *testing.T) {
	h := srWorld(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	err := h.ctlDB.Write(ctx, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		_, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DROP TABLE derp_servers`)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, services, err := (module{d: h.dp()}).Controller(srDeps(h))
	if err != nil || len(services) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("Controller = %+v, %v; want the one service", services, err)
	}
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { done <- services[0].Run(ctx) }()
	select {
	case err := <-done:
		if err == nil {
			t.Error("Run returned nil after a failed pass, want the error")
		}
	case <-time.After(10 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("Run did not return after a failed pass")
	}
	if n := h.clock.Pending(); n != 0 {
		t.Errorf("%d timers armed after the service failed, want none", n)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the test to verify it fails**

Run: `mkdir -p /root/.cache/w5-tmp/26 && TMPDIR=/root/.cache/w5-tmp/26 GOTOOLCHAIN=local timeout 300 go test -count=1 -run 'TestSr' ./internal/modules/derp/`
Expected: FAIL, a build failure naming `d.serverList undefined (type dp has no field or method serverList)`, `h.dp().checkServers undefined`, `undefined: serverHealth` and `module{…}.Controller undefined (type module has no field or method Controller)`. Quote it in the report.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

All of it goes in `internal/modules/derp/servers.go`. Its identifiers start with `server` (`serverList`, `serverListReader`, `serverHealth`) or `servers`, or are `runServers` and `checkServers`, so none can meet Task 17's `serverStatus…`, Task 25's `probe…`, Task 27's or Task 28's names. A doc comment on every exported identifier (`Controller`); comments elsewhere only where the why is not obvious.

1. `serverList()` returns `core.DefineRead[ServerListInput, Servers]` with `core.ActionSpec{Name: ID + ".server.list", Summary: "List the DERP servers the controller found, with their health.", Version: 1, Side: core.SideController}` and the handler `serverListReader{}`. No `LockKeys`, no `Available`, not `Internal`. Constructing the declaration reads nothing.
2. `serverHealth(r, now)` is pure and returns `reasons` as a non-nil slice. Conditions, in this order, each with its sentence: `!r.UnitActive`: `derper is not running`; the certificate, only for a non-zero `r.CertNotAfter`: when `now.After(r.CertNotAfter)`, `the certificate expired on <YYYY-MM-DD, UTC>`, else when `r.CertNotAfter.Sub(now) <= expiryWarning`, `the certificate expires on <YYYY-MM-DD, UTC>`; `now.Sub(r.CheckedAt) > staleAfter`: `the last check was at <r.CheckedAt in UTC, RFC 3339>`; a non-empty `r.Error`: `the host could not be read: <r.Error>`. Health is `broken` when the unit is inactive or the certificate has expired, else `degraded` when any other reason exists, else `ok`.
3. `serverListReader.Read(ctx, env, _ ServerListInput)` loads `loadServerRecords(ctx, env.DB.Read())` and the `core_hosts` id and name pairs (`SELECT id, name FROM core_hosts` on `env.DB.Read()`), and returns `Servers{Servers: [...], AvailableVersion: artifact.DerperVersion}`: one `ServerView` per record that has a `core_hosts` row (a record without one is left out), in host id order, `Host` the host's name, `CertNotAfter` nil when zero and otherwise the time in UTC, `Health` and `Reasons` from `serverHealth(record, env.Clock.Now())`, every other field copied from the record, `CheckedAt` as stored. The `Servers` slice and every `Reasons` slice are never nil, so they encode as `[]`.
4. `module.Controller(deps)` returns no route, one `ext.Service{Name: "derp.servers", Run: func(ctx context.Context) error { return m.d.runServers(ctx, deps) }}` and a nil error; it reads `deps` only inside `Run`, since the server calls it with whatever deps it has.
5. `runServers(ctx, deps)` has the shape of `runHostnames`: a pass at start (`d.checkServers(ctx, deps, deps.Clock.Now())`), then one every `servicePeriod`, the timer armed (`deps.Clock.AfterFunc` posting to a one-slot channel with a non-blocking send) only after the pass that preceded it, stopped on return, and a nil return when `ctx` ends. A pass error ends the service with that error so the supervisor restarts it, except when `ctx.Err() != nil`, which is a normal stop. A host that cannot be read is data, not an error.
6. `checkServers(ctx, deps, now)` runs one pass. Its `core.Env` is `{Principal: core.Principal{Actor: "system:derp.servers", Scope: core.ScopeAdmin}, DB: deps.DB, Sealer: deps.Sealer, Clock: deps.Clock, Log: deps.Log, Agents: deps.Hosts}`. Steps, in this order, with no write before step 4:
   1. Load the `core_hosts` ids (ordered by id) from `deps.DB.Read()` and the stored records (`loadServerRecords`) into a map by host id. A database error ends the pass with that error.
   2. Per host, `readStatus(ctx, env, id, nil)`, one after the other. When it fails and `ctx.Err() != nil`, return `ctx.Err()`. When it fails otherwise, remember the host as unreadable, with the text `the agent did not answer`, or the `errcode.From(err).Detail` when that error's `Code` is not `errcode.Internal` and its `Detail` is not empty. Otherwise remember the status.
   3. The region lookup, only when at least one status says `Installed` (a pass that found no server makes no provider call): when `d.d.connected(ctx, deps.DB)` reports true, call `d.d.policy(ctx, env)` once and `parseDERPMap` its `Text`; the region of a server is then `regionOf(HostName)` (its `RegionID` and `RegionCode`, or 0 and `""` when no custom region holds the host name). When it reports false, every region is 0 and `""`. An error from the connected check, the policy read or the parse is not a pass error: the region of each server then stays as its stored row has it (0 and `""` when it has none).
   4. The writes. An unreadable host with a stored row: `upsertServerRecord` of that row unchanged except `Error` set, `CheckedAt` untouched; with no stored row: nothing. A host whose status is not installed: `deleteServerRecord` (a missing row is not an error). An installed host: `upsertServerRecord` with `Mode`, `HostName`, `DerperVersion`, `CertName`, `CertNotAfter` (the zero time for nil, otherwise UTC), `UnitActive`, the region of step 3, `CheckedAt: now` and `Error: ""`. Last, `deleteServerRecord` for every stored record whose host id is not among the ids of step 1. A database error ends the pass with that error.

```go
package derp

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"net/netip"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/tailnet"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/tailnet/policy"
)

const (
	reIP   = "203.0.113.10"
	reAck  = "nodes will relay only through eu-derp"
	reUS   = `{"RegionID": 901, "RegionCode": "us-derp", "RegionName": "US", "Nodes": [{"Name": "901a", "RegionID": 901, "HostName": "198.51.100.5"}]}`
	reBase = `{
	// operators keep this comment
	"acls": [{"action": "accept", "src": ["*"], "dst": ["*:*"]}],
	"tagOwners": {"tag:derp": ["autogroup:admin"]}
}`
	reGrantsBase = `{
	"acls": [{"action": "accept", "src": ["*"], "dst": ["*:*"]}],
	"grants": [{"src": ["tag:web"], "dst": ["tag:web"], "ip": ["*"]}]
}`
)

var (
	rePin    = "sha256-raw:" + strings.Repeat("ab", 32)
	reNewPin = "sha256-raw:" + strings.Repeat("cd", 32)
)

func reAgent(h *dpHost, id string) *dpAgent {
	a := h.agent(id)
	if a.Reads == nil {
		a.Reads = map[string]func(json.RawMessage) (any, error){}
	}
	return a
}

// reStatus is derp.server.status of the ip-mode server on h1; its tags are deliberately not sorted.
func reStatus() ServerStatus {
	return ServerStatus{
		Installed: true, Mode: "ip", HostName: reIP, VerifyClients: true, DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", UnitActive: true,
		CertName: rePin, OnTailnet: true, Tags: []string{"tag:web", "tag:derp"}, Addresses: []string{"100.64.0.10"},
	}
}

// reUseServer makes host h1 report the server s and every controller probe reach it at addr.
func reUseServer(h *dpHost, s ServerStatus, addr netip.Addr) {
	reAgent(h, "h1").Reads["derp.server.status/v1"] = func(json.RawMessage) (any, error) { return s, nil }
	h.tls = map[string]dpProbe{s.HostName: {d: 10 * time.Millisecond}, addr.String(): {d: 10 * time.Millisecond}}
	h.stun = map[netip.AddrPort]dpProbe{netip.AddrPortFrom(addr, 3478): {d: 5 * time.Millisecond}}
	h.resolved = map[string][]netip.Addr{s.HostName: {addr}}
}

// reHost is a controller with the DERP host h1 (alpha) running reStatus, a probe host h2 (bravo) whose probes
// pass, a connected tailnet without devices and the policy reBase. No deploy change exists yet.
func reHost(t *testing.T) *dpHost {
	t.Helper()
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.addHost("h1", "alpha", false)
	h.addHost("h2", "bravo", false)
	reUseServer(h, reStatus(), netip.MustParseAddr(reIP))
	reAgent(h, "h2").Reads["derp.node.probe/v1"] = func(json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
		return NodeProbe{TLS: ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: 20}, STUN: ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: 30}}, nil
	}
	h.tailnet = true
	h.policy = tailnet.Policy{Text: reBase, ETag: "e1"}
	return h
}

// reDeploy adds a derp.server.deploy change of hostID in state, created age ago.
func reDeploy(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, hostID, state string, age time.Duration) {
	t.Helper()
	id := h.addChange("derp.server.deploy", state, DeployInput{HostID: hostID, Mode: "ip", HostName: reIP})
	at := h.clock.Now().Add(-age).UTC().Format("2006-01-02T15:04:05.000000000Z")
	err := h.ctlDB.Write(context.Background(), func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		_, err := tx.ExecContext(context.Background(), `UPDATE core_changes SET created_at = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`, at, at, id)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// reReady is reHost with a completed deploy of h1.
func reReady(t *testing.T) *dpHost {
	t.Helper()
	h := reHost(t)
	reDeploy(t, h, "h1", core.StateCompleted, time.Hour)
	return h
}

func reSetInput() RegionEditsInput {
	return RegionEditsInput{Op: "set", HostID: "h1", RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "eu-derp", RegionName: "Europe DERP"}
}

func reOmitInput(ack string) RegionEditsInput {
	in := reSetInput()
	in.OmitDefaultRegions, in.Acknowledge = true, ack
	return in
}

func reReveal(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, in RegionEditsInput) (string, RegionEdits, error) {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := json.Marshal(in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	identity, value, err := h.dp().regionEdits().Handler.Reveal(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), raw)
	if err != nil {
		return identity, RegionEdits{}, err
	}
	var out RegionEdits
	if err := json.Unmarshal(value, &out); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("value %s: %v", value, err)
	}
	return identity, out, nil
}

func reRun(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, in RegionEditsInput) (RegionEdits, error) {
	t.Helper()
	_, out, err := reReveal(t, h, in)
	return out, err
}

func reApply(t *testing.T, text string, edits []policy.Edit) string {
	t.Helper()
	out, err := policy.Apply([]byte(text), edits)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("applying %+v: %v", edits, err)
	}
	return string(out)
}

func reJSON(t *testing.T, raw []byte) any {
	t.Helper()
	var v any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &v); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("%s: %v", raw, err)
	}
	return v
}

// reSetUp runs the set op on the host's policy, applies its edits and leaves the result as the host's policy.
func reSetUp(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, in RegionEditsInput) {
	t.Helper()
	out, err := reRun(t, h, in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h.policy.Text = reApply(t, h.policy.Text, out.Edits)
}

// reEdit is an expected edit; a nil Value means the edit carries none.
type reEdit struct {
	Op, Path string
	Value    any
}

func reCheck(t *testing.T, label string, got []policy.Edit, want []reEdit) {
	t.Helper()
	if got == nil {
		t.Errorf("%s: the edits are null, want a list", label)
	}
	if len(got) != len(want) {
		raw, _ := json.Marshal(got)
		t.Errorf("%s: edits %s, want %d of them", label, raw, len(want))
		return
	}
	for i, w := range want {
		g := got[i]
		if g.Op != w.Op || g.Path != w.Path {
			t.Errorf("%s: edit %d is %s %s, want %s %s", label, i, g.Op, g.Path, w.Op, w.Path)
			continue
		}
		if w.Value == nil {
			if len(g.Value) != 0 {
				t.Errorf("%s: edit %d carries the value %s, want none", label, i, g.Value)
			}
			continue
		}
		if v := reJSON(t, g.Value); !reflect.DeepEqual(v, w.Value) {
			t.Errorf("%s: edit %d value %v, want %v", label, i, v, w.Value)
		}
	}
}

func reGrants(t *testing.T, text string) []any {
	t.Helper()
	canon, err := policy.Canonical([]byte(text))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var doc struct {
		Grants []any `json:"grants"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(canon, &doc); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return doc.Grants
}

func reRegionOf(reg map[string]any) map[string]any { return map[string]any{"900": reg} }

func reRegionObject() map[string]any {
	return map[string]any{
		"RegionID": 900.0, "RegionCode": "eu-derp", "RegionName": "Europe DERP",
		"Nodes": []any{map[string]any{
			"Name": "900a", "RegionID": 900.0, "HostName": reIP, "IPv4": reIP, "CertName": rePin, "DERPPort": 443.0, "STUNPort": 3478.0,
		}},
	}
}

func TestReDeclaration(t *testing.T) {
	def := newDPHost(t).dp().regionEdits()
	s := def.Spec
	if s.Name != "derp.region.edits" || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassRead || s.Side != core.SideController ||
		!s.Internal || s.Reveal || s.MinScope != "" || len(s.LockKeys) != 0 || s.Available != nil || s.Summary == "" {
		t.Errorf("spec %+v, want the internal controller read derp.region.edits v1 with no lock or availability check", s)
	}
	if def.InputType != reflect.TypeFor[RegionEditsInput]() || def.OutputType != reflect.TypeFor[RegionEdits]() {
		t.Errorf("types %v and %v, want RegionEditsInput and RegionEdits", def.InputType, def.OutputType)
	}
	if def.Handler.Reveal == nil || def.Handler.Read != nil || def.Handler.Plan != nil || def.Handler.Apply != nil {
		t.Errorf("handler %+v, want the reveal handler only", def.Handler)
	}
}

// TestReRegionObject: the node of the region object is the server's, with its address and pin in ip mode only.
func TestReRegionObject(t *testing.T) {
	node := func(extra map[string]any) map[string]any {
		n := map[string]any{"Name": "900a", "RegionID": 900.0, "DERPPort": 443.0, "STUNPort": 3478.0}
		for k, v := range extra {
			n[k] = v
		}
		return n
	}
	tests := []struct {
		name   string
		status ServerStatus
		node   map[string]any
	}{
		{"letsencrypt", ServerStatus{Mode: "letsencrypt", HostName: "derp.example.com"}, node(map[string]any{"HostName": "derp.example.com"})},
		{"ip with an IPv4 address", ServerStatus{Mode: "ip", HostName: reIP, CertName: rePin},
			node(map[string]any{"HostName": reIP, "IPv4": reIP, "CertName": rePin})},
		{"ip with an IPv6 address", ServerStatus{Mode: "ip", HostName: "2001:db8::10", CertName: rePin},
			node(map[string]any{"HostName": "2001:db8::10", "IPv6": "2001:db8::10", "CertName": rePin})},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		raw, err := json.Marshal(editRegion(tt.status, reSetInput()))
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		want := map[string]any{"RegionID": 900.0, "RegionCode": "eu-derp", "RegionName": "Europe DERP", "Nodes": []any{tt.node}}
		if got := reJSON(t, raw); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
			t.Errorf("%s: region object %v, want %v", tt.name, got, want)
		}
	}
}

// TestReSetEdits: the edits that create the region, per policy shape; applying them makes the region appear with
// the grant to the server's first sorted tag, and a second run on the result has nothing left to edit.
func TestReSetEdits(t *testing.T) {
	grant := reJSON(t, visibilityGrant("tag:derp"))
	if m, _ := grant.(map[string]any); !reflect.DeepEqual(m["dst"], []any{"tag:derp"}) {
		t.Fatalf("grant %v, want it addressed to the tag it was built for", grant)
	}
	region := reRegionObject()
	withUS := `{"derpMap": {"Regions": {"901": ` + reUS + `}}, "grants": [{"src": ["tag:web"], "dst": ["tag:web"], "ip": ["*"]}]}`
	omittedUS := `{"derpMap": {"OmitDefaultRegions": true, "Regions": {"901": ` + reUS + `}}}`
	tests := []struct {
		name   string
		policy string
		omit   bool
		want   []reEdit
	}{
		{"no derpMap and no grants", reBase, false, []reEdit{
			{"set", "/derpMap", map[string]any{"Regions": reRegionOf(region)}},
			{"set", "/grants", []any{grant}},
		}},
		{"no derpMap, default regions omitted", reBase, true, []reEdit{
			{"set", "/derpMap", map[string]any{"OmitDefaultRegions": true, "Regions": reRegionOf(region)}},
			{"set", "/grants", []any{grant}},
		}},
		{"a derpMap with another region and a grants list", withUS, false, []reEdit{
			{"set", "/derpMap/Regions/900", region},
			{"addItem", "/grants", grant},
		}},
		{"a derpMap without regions", `{"derpMap": {"OmitDefaultRegions": false}}`, false, []reEdit{
			{"set", "/derpMap/Regions", reRegionOf(region)},
			{"set", "/grants", []any{grant}},
		}},
		{"omitting default regions in an existing map", withUS, true, []reEdit{
			{"set", "/derpMap/Regions/900", region},
			{"set", "/derpMap/OmitDefaultRegions", true},
			{"addItem", "/grants", grant},
		}},
		{"default regions already omitted", omittedUS, true, []reEdit{
			{"set", "/derpMap/Regions/900", region},
			{"set", "/grants", []any{grant}},
		}},
	}
	for i, tt := range tests {
		h := reReady(t)
		h.policy.Text = tt.policy
		in := reSetInput()
		if tt.omit {
			in = reOmitInput(reAck)
		}
		identity, out, err := reReveal(t, h, in)
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: %v", tt.name, err)
			continue
		}
		if i == 0 && identity != "DERP region set edits for region 900" {
			t.Errorf("identity %q, want %q", identity, "DERP region set edits for region 900")
		}
		reCheck(t, tt.name, out.Edits, tt.want)
		if out.Warnings == nil || len(out.Warnings) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("%s: warnings %#v, want an empty list", tt.name, out.Warnings)
		}
		applied := reApply(t, tt.policy, out.Edits)
		m, err := parseDERPMap(applied)
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: the edited policy does not parse: %v", tt.name, err)
			continue
		}
		if r, ok := m.regionOf(reIP); !ok || r.RegionID != 900 || r.RegionCode != "eu-derp" || m.OmitDefaultRegions != tt.omit {
			t.Errorf("%s: region %+v (found %v), omitDefaultRegions %v; want region 900 eu-derp and omitDefaultRegions %v", tt.name, r, ok, m.OmitDefaultRegions, tt.omit)
		}
		h.policy.Text = applied
		again, err := reRun(t, h, in)
		if err != nil || again.Edits == nil || len(again.Edits) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("%s: a second run gives %+v, %v; want no edits", tt.name, again.Edits, err)
		}
	}
}

func TestReSetKeepsTheCommentsOfThePolicy(t *testing.T) {
	h := reReady(t)
	out, err := reRun(t, h, reSetInput())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if applied := reApply(t, reBase, out.Edits); !strings.Contains(applied, "// operators keep this comment") {
		t.Errorf("the edited policy lost the operator's comment:\n%s", applied)
	}
}

func TestReSetNeedsAConfirmedDeploy(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		deploys func(t *testing.T, h *dpHost)
		ok      bool
	}{
		{"no deploy", func(*testing.T, *dpHost) {}, false},
		{"only another host's deploy", func(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) { reDeploy(t, h, "h2", core.StateCompleted, time.Hour) }, false},
		{"the deploy failed", func(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) { reDeploy(t, h, "h1", core.StateFailed, time.Hour) }, false},
		{"the newest deploy awaits confirmation", func(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) {
			reDeploy(t, h, "h1", core.StateCompleted, 2*time.Hour)
			reDeploy(t, h, "h1", core.StateAwaitingConfirmation, time.Hour)
		}, false},
		{"the newest deploy is completed", func(t *testing.T, h *dpHost) {
			reDeploy(t, h, "h1", core.StateAwaitingConfirmation, 2*time.Hour)
			reDeploy(t, h, "h1", core.StateCompleted, time.Hour)
		}, true},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		h := reHost(t)
		tt.deploys(t, h)
		_, err := reRun(t, h, reSetInput())
		if tt.ok {
			if err != nil {
				t.Errorf("%s: %v, want the edits", tt.name, err)
			}
			continue
		}
		if coded := errcode.From(err); err == nil || coded.Code != errcode.DERPNotReady || !strings.Contains(coded.Detail, "deploy") || !core.IsDefinite(err) {
			t.Errorf("%s: err = %#v, want a definite derp.not_ready naming the deploy", tt.name, err)
		}
	}
}

func TestReSetRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	foreign := `{"derpMap": {"Regions": {"900": {"RegionID": 900, "RegionCode": "x", "RegionName": "X",
		"Nodes": [{"Name": "900a", "RegionID": 900, "HostName": "203.0.113.99"}]}}}}`
	noTag := reStatus()
	noTag.Tags = nil
	tests := []struct {
		name   string
		setup  func(h *dpHost)
		in     RegionEditsInput
		code   errcode.Code
		detail string
		field  string
	}{
		{"the probe does not reach the server", func(h *dpHost) { h.tls = map[string]dpProbe{} }, reSetInput(), errcode.DERPNotReady, "controller: tls:", ""},
		{"the region belongs to another server", func(h *dpHost) { h.policy.Text = foreign }, reSetInput(), errcode.DERPRegionTaken, "203.0.113.99", ""},
		{"default regions omitted without an acknowledgement", nil, reOmitInput(""), errcode.InvalidInput, "", "/acknowledge"},
		{"default regions omitted with another acknowledgement", nil, reOmitInput("nodes will relay only through other"), errcode.InvalidInput, "", "/acknowledge"},
		{"the server's node has no tag", func(h *dpHost) { reUseServer(h, noTag, netip.MustParseAddr(reIP)) }, reSetInput(), errcode.DERPNotReady,
			"the DERP host's node has no tag; the visibility grant needs one", ""},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		h := reReady(t)
		if tt.setup != nil {
			tt.setup(h)
		}
		_, err := reRun(t, h, tt.in)
		coded := errcode.From(err)
		if err == nil || coded.Code != tt.code || !core.IsDefinite(err) {
			t.Errorf("%s: err = %#v, want a definite %s refusal", tt.name, err, tt.code)
			continue
		}
		if tt.detail != "" && !strings.Contains(coded.Detail, tt.detail) {
			t.Errorf("%s: detail %q, want it to contain %q", tt.name, coded.Detail, tt.detail)
		}
		if tt.field != "" && (len(coded.Fields) != 1 || coded.Fields[0].Path != tt.field) {
			t.Errorf("%s: fields %+v, want one at %s", tt.name, coded.Fields, tt.field)
		}
	}
}

// TestReSetOmitNeedsNoAcknowledgementWithAnotherReadyRegion: another custom region whose server passes the probe
// makes the acknowledgement unnecessary; when that server stops answering it is required again.
func TestReSetOmitNeedsNoAcknowledgementWithAnotherReadyRegion(t *testing.T) {
	h := reReady(t)
	h.addHost("h3", "charlie", false)
	second := ServerStatus{Installed: true, Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.20", DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", UnitActive: true, CertName: rePin, Tags: []string{"tag:derp"}}
	reAgent(h, "h3").Reads["derp.server.status/v1"] = func(json.RawMessage) (any, error) { return second, nil }
	h.tls["203.0.113.20"] = dpProbe{d: 10 * time.Millisecond}
	h.stun[netip.MustParseAddrPort("203.0.113.20:3478")] = dpProbe{d: 5 * time.Millisecond}
	err := upsertServerRecord(context.Background(), h.ctlDB, serverRecord{
		HostID: "h3", Mode: "ip", HostName: "203.0.113.20", RegionID: 901, RegionCode: "us-derp", DerperVersion: "v1.104.1",
		CertName: rePin, UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: h.clock.Now(),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h.policy.Text = `{"derpMap": {"Regions": {"901": {"RegionID": 901, "RegionCode": "us-derp", "RegionName": "US",
		"Nodes": [{"Name": "901a", "RegionID": 901, "HostName": "203.0.113.20"}]}}}}`
	out, err := reRun(t, h, reOmitInput(""))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("with a ready second region: %v", err)
	}
	if len(out.Edits) < 2 || out.Edits[1].Op != "set" || out.Edits[1].Path != "/derpMap/OmitDefaultRegions" {
		t.Errorf("edits %+v, want the region set and then omitDefaultRegions set", out.Edits)
	}
	delete(h.tls, "203.0.113.20")
	_, err = reRun(t, h, reOmitInput(""))
	if coded := errcode.From(err); err == nil || coded.Code != errcode.InvalidInput || len(coded.Fields) != 1 || coded.Fields[0].Path != "/acknowledge" {
		t.Errorf("with the second server unreachable: err = %#v, want invalid input at /acknowledge", err)
	}
}

// TestReSetWarnsWhenOnlyTheControllerProbed: with no host other than the DERP host the controller's probe alone
// admits the region and its warning is passed on.
func TestReSetWarnsWhenOnlyTheControllerProbed(t *testing.T) {
	h := reReady(t)
	err := h.ctlDB.Write(context.Background(), func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
		_, err := tx.ExecContext(context.Background(), `DELETE FROM core_hosts WHERE id = 'h2'`)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	out, err := reRun(t, h, reSetInput())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := []string{"no agent other than the DERP host probed 203.0.113.10; only the controller's probe backs the region"}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(out.Warnings, want) || len(out.Edits) != 2 {
		t.Errorf("warnings %q and %d edits, want %q and the two set edits", out.Warnings, len(out.Edits), want)
	}
}

func TestReInputValidation(t *testing.T) {
	longName := strings.Repeat("n", 65)
	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		in    RegionEditsInput
		field string
	}{
		{"an unknown op", RegionEditsInput{Op: "bogus", RegionID: 900}, "/op"},
		{"a region id below the range", RegionEditsInput{Op: "remove", RegionID: 899}, "/regionId"},
		{"a region id above the range", RegionEditsInput{Op: "remove", RegionID: 1000}, "/regionId"},
		{"set without a host", RegionEditsInput{Op: "set", RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "eu-derp", RegionName: "x"}, "/hostId"},
		{"set with a bad code", RegionEditsInput{Op: "set", HostID: "h1", RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "EU Derp", RegionName: "x"}, "/regionCode"},
		{"set without a name", RegionEditsInput{Op: "set", HostID: "h1", RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "eu-derp"}, "/regionName"},
		{"set with a long name", RegionEditsInput{Op: "set", HostID: "h1", RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "eu-derp", RegionName: longName}, "/regionName"},
		{"rotate without a host", RegionEditsInput{Op: "rotate", RegionID: 900, CertName: reNewPin}, "/hostId"},
		{"rotate with a bad pin", RegionEditsInput{Op: "rotate", HostID: "h1", RegionID: 900, CertName: "sha256-raw:xyz"}, "/certName"},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		_, err := reRun(t, reHost(t), tt.in)
		coded := errcode.From(err)
		if err == nil || coded.Code != errcode.InvalidInput || len(coded.Fields) != 1 || coded.Fields[0].Path != tt.field || !core.IsDefinite(err) {
			t.Errorf("%s: err = %#v, want a definite invalid input at %s", tt.name, err, tt.field)
		}
	}
}

// TestReRemove: removing a region deletes it; the last one also clears omitDefaultRegions and takes the visibility
// grant (and only that grant) with it; another custom region keeps both.
func TestReRemove(t *testing.T) {
	grant := reJSON(t, visibilityGrant("tag:derp"))
	del := reEdit{"delete", "/derpMap/Regions/900", nil}
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		policy  string
		set     RegionEditsInput
		want    []reEdit
		regions int
	}{
		{"the last region takes its grant with it", reGrantsBase, reSetInput(), []reEdit{del, {"removeItem", "/grants", grant}}, 0},
		{"the last region clears omitDefaultRegions", reGrantsBase, reOmitInput(reAck),
			[]reEdit{del, {"set", "/derpMap/OmitDefaultRegions", false}, {"removeItem", "/grants", grant}}, 0},
		{"another custom region keeps the grant", `{"derpMap": {"Regions": {"901": ` + reUS + `}}}`, reSetInput(), []reEdit{del}, 1},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		h := reReady(t)
		h.policy.Text = tt.policy
		reSetUp(t, h, tt.set)
		out, err := reRun(t, h, RegionEditsInput{Op: "remove", RegionID: 900})
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: %v", tt.name, err)
			continue
		}
		reCheck(t, tt.name, out.Edits, tt.want)
		if out.Warnings == nil || len(out.Warnings) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("%s: warnings %#v, want an empty list", tt.name, out.Warnings)
		}
		applied := reApply(t, h.policy.Text, out.Edits)
		m, err := parseDERPMap(applied)
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: the edited policy does not parse: %v", tt.name, err)
			continue
		}
		if len(m.customRegions()) != tt.regions || m.OmitDefaultRegions {
			t.Errorf("%s: custom regions %v, omitDefaultRegions %v; want %d regions and omitDefaultRegions off", tt.name, m.customRegions(), m.OmitDefaultRegions, tt.regions)
		}
		if grants := reGrants(t, applied); len(grants) != 1 {
			t.Errorf("%s: %d grants left, want 1", tt.name, len(grants))
		}
	}
}

// TestReRemoveOfAnAbsentRegion: nothing to remove is an empty list, and the read needs no server, deploy or probe.
func TestReRemoveOfAnAbsentRegion(t *testing.T) {
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.tailnet = true
	h.policy = tailnet.Policy{Text: reBase}
	out, err := reRun(t, h, RegionEditsInput{Op: "remove", RegionID: 900})
	if err != nil || out.Edits == nil || len(out.Edits) != 0 || out.Warnings == nil || len(out.Warnings) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("remove of an absent region = %+v, %v; want empty edits and warnings, not null", out, err)
	}
}

func reRotateRegion() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf(`{"RegionID": 900, "RegionCode": "eu-derp", "RegionName": "Europe DERP", "Avoid": true, "Nodes": [
		{"Name": "900a", "RegionID": 900, "HostName": %q, "IPv4": %q, "CertName": %q, "CanPort80": true, "DERPPort": 443, "STUNPort": 3478},
		{"Name": "900b", "RegionID": 900, "HostName": "203.0.113.50", "CertName": "sha256-raw:%s"}]}`, reIP, reIP, rePin, strings.Repeat("01", 32))
}

func reRotateHost(t *testing.T, serverHostName string) *dpHost {
	t.Helper()
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.tailnet = true
	h.policy = tailnet.Policy{Text: `{"derpMap": {"Regions": {"900": ` + reRotateRegion() + `}}}`}
	err := upsertServerRecord(context.Background(), h.ctlDB, serverRecord{
		HostID: "h1", Mode: "ip", HostName: serverHostName, RegionID: 900, RegionCode: "eu-derp", DerperVersion: "v1.104.1",
		CertName: rePin, UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: h.clock.Now(),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return h
}

func reRotateInput() RegionEditsInput {
	return RegionEditsInput{Op: "rotate", HostID: "h1", RegionID: 900, CertName: reNewPin}
}

// TestReRotate: the new pin goes into the server's node only, on the region as the policy holds it, with every
// field the module does not know kept; the result converges.
func TestReRotate(t *testing.T) {
	h := reRotateHost(t, reIP)
	identity, out, err := reReveal(t, h, reRotateInput())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if identity != "DERP region rotate edits for region 900" {
		t.Errorf("identity %q, want %q", identity, "DERP region rotate edits for region 900")
	}
	want := reJSON(t, []byte(reRotateRegion()))
	want.(map[string]any)["Nodes"].([]any)[0].(map[string]any)["CertName"] = reNewPin
	reCheck(t, "rotate", out.Edits, []reEdit{{"set", "/derpMap/Regions/900", want}})
	if out.Warnings == nil || len(out.Warnings) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("warnings %#v, want an empty list", out.Warnings)
	}
	h.policy.Text = reApply(t, h.policy.Text, out.Edits)
	m, err := parseDERPMap(h.policy.Text)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r, ok := m.regionOf(reIP); !ok || r.Nodes[0].CertName != reNewPin || r.Nodes[1].CertName == reNewPin {
		t.Errorf("region after the edit %+v (found %v), want the new pin on the first node only", r, ok)
	}
	again, err := reRun(t, h, reRotateInput())
	if err != nil || again.Edits == nil || len(again.Edits) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("a second run gives %+v, %v; want no edits", again.Edits, err)
	}
}

func TestReRotateWithNothingToChange(t *testing.T) {
	absent := reRotateInput()
	absent.RegionID = 901
	tests := []struct {
		name    string
		host    string
		in      RegionEditsInput
		warning string
	}{
		{"the region is absent", reIP, absent, ""},
		{"the region has no node of the server", "203.0.113.77", reRotateInput(), "203.0.113.77"},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		out, err := reRun(t, reRotateHost(t, tt.host), tt.in)
		if err != nil || out.Edits == nil || len(out.Edits) != 0 || out.Warnings == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: %+v, %v; want empty edits", tt.name, out, err)
			continue
		}
		if tt.warning == "" && len(out.Warnings) != 0 || tt.warning != "" && (len(out.Warnings) != 1 || !strings.Contains(out.Warnings[0], tt.warning)) {
			t.Errorf("%s: warnings %q, want one naming %q (none when empty)", tt.name, out.Warnings, tt.warning)
		}
	}
}

func TestReRotateRefusesAnUnknownServer(t *testing.T) {
	h := reRotateHost(t, reIP)
	in := reRotateInput()
	in.HostID = "h9"
	_, err := reRun(t, h, in)
	if coded := errcode.From(err); err == nil || coded.Code != errcode.DERPNotReady || !core.IsDefinite(err) {
		t.Errorf("err = %#v, want a definite derp.not_ready for a host with no server row", err)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

All of it goes in `internal/modules/derp/region_edits.go`.

1. `regionEdits()` returns `core.DefineInternalRead[RegionEditsInput, RegionEdits]` with `core.ActionSpec{Name: ID + ".region.edits", Summary: "Compute the tailnet policy edits that set, remove or re-pin a DERP region; nothing is written.", Version: 1, Side: core.SideController}` and `d.regionEditsRead`. The `Revealed.Identity` is `DERP region <op> edits for region <id>`. Constructing it touches nothing.
2. `visibilityGrant(tag)` returns the JSON text of the grant Contracts "DERP region edits" names (`visibilityGrant`) with `tag` as the one `dst` entry. Nothing else in the file repeats the grant's content: the other code calls this function.
3. `editRegion(s, in)` builds the `set` op's region object: `RegionID`, `RegionCode`, `RegionName` from `in` and one node `{"Name": "<id>a", "RegionID": id, "HostName": s.HostName, "DERPPort": server.HTTPSPort, "STUNPort": server.STUNPort}`; in ip mode the node also has `"IPv4"` (an address that `netip.ParseAddr` reads and `Is4`) or `"IPv6"` set to `s.HostName` and `"CertName"` set to `s.CertName`.
4. `regionEditsRead` validates first, before any other read, with a definite `request.invalid_input` carrying one field error: `op` not `set`, `remove` or `rotate` (`/op`); `RegionID` outside 900–999 (`/regionId`); for `set` and `rotate` an empty `HostID` (`/hostId`); for `set` a `RegionCode` not matching `^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,31}$` (`/regionCode`) and a `RegionName` empty or longer than 64 bytes (`/regionName`); for `rotate` a `CertName` not matching `^sha256-raw:[0-9a-f]{64}$` (`/certName`). The result's `Edits` and `Warnings` are never nil. Every refusal below is wrapped with `core.Definite`. Reading the policy is `d.d.policy(ctx, env)`, parsing the region map is `parseDERPMap`; for the rest of the policy (the presence of `derpMap.Regions`, the `grants` items, a region object as the policy holds it) decode `policy.Canonical` of the text with `encoding/json`. A policy that cannot be parsed is returned as an error wrapped with `derp:`. Compare JSON values by decoding both sides to `any` and `reflect.DeepEqual`.
5. Op `set`, in this order:
   1. The newest `core_changes` row with `action = 'derp.server.deploy'` whose `input_json` decodes to a `hostId` equal to `in.HostID` (read the rows `ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC` from `env.DB.Read()` and take the first match in Go; do not rely on SQLite JSON functions) must have `state = core.StateCompleted`; none, or another state, is `derp.not_ready` with the detail `the deploy of the DERP server on <host id> is not confirmed (<state, or "no deploy found">)`.
   2. `probe, err := d.probeServer(ctx, env, ServerProbeInput{HostID: in.HostID})` (its own errors pass through unchanged); `ready, reason, warnings := probeReady(probe)`; not ready is `derp.not_ready` with the detail `the DERP server on <host id> is not reachable yet: <reason>`. The output's `Warnings` are `warnings`.
   3. Read and parse the policy. If `Regions[in.RegionID]` has a node whose `HostName` differs, ignoring case, from `probe.Server.HostName`, refuse `derp.region_taken` with the detail `region <id> already belongs to <that node's HostName>`.
   4. When `in.OmitDefaultRegions`: for each other custom region of the map in ascending order, find the first `loadServerRecords` row whose `RegionID` is that region and call `probeServer` for its host; the region counts when that call has no error and `probeReady` is ready (any error, a missing row or a not-ready probe means it does not count; a cancelled `ctx` is returned). If none counts and `in.Acknowledge` is not exactly `nodes will relay only through <in.RegionCode>`, refuse `request.invalid_input` with one field error at `/acknowledge` and the detail `omitting the default regions needs the acknowledgement "nodes will relay only through <in.RegionCode>"`.
   5. `probe.Server.Tags` empty: `derp.not_ready` with exactly `the DERP host's node has no tag; the visibility grant needs one`. The grant's tag is the lexically smallest tag.
   6. The edits, in this order. The region: with no `derpMap` member in the policy, `set /derpMap` to `{"Regions": {"<id>": region}}` plus `"OmitDefaultRegions": true` when asked (and no separate omit edit); with a `derpMap` that has no `Regions` member, `set /derpMap/Regions` to `{"<id>": region}`; otherwise `set /derpMap/Regions/<id>` to `region`, left out when the policy's region for that id already equals `region`. The omit flag, only when the `derpMap` member existed: `set /derpMap/OmitDefaultRegions` to `true` when it is asked and the map's flag is not already true. The grant: `set /grants` to `[grant]` when the policy has no `grants` member, else `addItem /grants` with the grant, left out when an equal item is present. When the policy already holds the whole result, `Edits` is an empty list, not null.
6. Op `remove` (no server, deploy or probe is consulted): read and parse the policy; with `Regions[in.RegionID]` absent the result is empty. Otherwise `delete /derpMap/Regions/<id>`; when no other custom region remains (`customRegions()` without that id is empty), then `set /derpMap/OmitDefaultRegions` to `false` when the map has it set, and one `removeItem /grants` with `visibilityGrant(tag)` for each item of `grants` that equals `visibilityGrant(t)` for the single string `t` in its own `dst` (so only grants this module builds are removed), in the order they appear.
7. Op `rotate`: `loadServerRecord(ctx, env.DB.Read(), in.HostID)`; no row is `derp.not_ready` with `no DERP server is known on <host id>`. Read and parse the policy; with `Regions[in.RegionID]` absent the result is empty. Take the region as the policy holds it (decoded to `map[string]any` with every unknown field kept) and find the nodes whose `HostName` equals the record's `HostName` ignoring case; with none, the result is empty with the warning `region <id> holds no node for <host name>`. Set `CertName` of those nodes to `in.CertName`; when none changed, the result is empty; else one edit `set /derpMap/Regions/<id>` with the whole region.
8. Declare no exported name, nothing Contracts gives and no private name that does not start with `edit` apart from `visibilityGrant`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `internal/modules/derp/fallback_check_test.go`. The fixture is a controller with the DERP host `h1` (region 900, link status from its fake agent) and, per row, other custom regions whose hosts are installed, probed and recorded in `derp_servers`. A region is "ready" when the controller's TLS and STUN probes of its address succeed (`h.tls` and `h.stun` carry an entry) and every fake agent answers `derp.node.probe` with OK results; a region that is not ready has no controller probe entry, so `probeServer` reports it not ready.

```go
package derp

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"net/netip"
	"strconv"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/errcode"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/core/schema"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/tailnet"
	"github.com/darkraise/darkpanel/internal/modules/tailscale"
)

const (
	fbHost = "h1"           // the DERP host whose upgrade or rotation is checked; it serves region 900
	fbIP   = "203.0.113.10" // its -hostname
)

// fbRegion is one custom region besides 900 in the fixture policy, and the host that serves it.
type fbRegion struct {
	id        int
	host      string // the core_hosts id
	ip        string // the server's -hostname, an ip-mode address
	row       bool   // the host has a derp_servers row for the region
	ready     bool   // the controller reaches the server over TLS and STUN
	statusErr bool   // the host's derp.server.status read fails
}

// fbPolicy is a policy file whose derpMap holds regions, each with one node at its address.
func fbPolicy(t *testing.T, omit bool, regions []fbRegion) string {
	t.Helper()
	m := map[string]any{}
	for _, r := range regions {
		id := strconv.Itoa(r.id)
		m[id] = map[string]any{
			"RegionID": r.id, "RegionCode": "r" + id, "RegionName": "Region " + id,
			"Nodes": []any{map[string]any{"Name": id + "a", "RegionID": r.id, "HostName": r.ip, "DERPPort": 443, "STUNPort": 3478}},
		}
	}
	b, err := json.Marshal(map[string]any{"derpMap": map[string]any{"OmitDefaultRegions": omit, "Regions": m}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return string(b)
}

// fbAgent makes host id answer derp.server.status with st (or an error) and every derp.node.probe with an OK
// TLS and STUN result, and tailscale.status as a running node.
func fbAgent(h *dpHost, id string, st ServerStatus, statusErr bool) {
	a := h.agent(id)
	if a.Reads == nil {
		a.Reads = map[string]func(json.RawMessage) (any, error){}
	}
	if a.Resources == nil {
		a.Resources = map[string]any{}
	}
	a.Reads["derp.server.status/v1"] = func(json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
		if statusErr {
			return nil, errors.New("the agent does not answer")
		}
		return st, nil
	}
	a.Reads["derp.node.probe/v1"] = func(json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
		return NodeProbe{TLS: ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: 12}, STUN: ProbeResult{OK: true, LatencyMs: 9}}, nil
	}
	a.Resources["tailscale.status"] = tailscale.Status{BackendState: "Running"}
}

func fbStatus(ip string, link bool) ServerStatus {
	return ServerStatus{
		Installed: true, Mode: "ip", HostName: ip, DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", UnitActive: true,
		LinkViaTailnet: link, OnTailnet: true, Tags: []string{"tag:derp"}, Addresses: []string{"100.64.0.10"},
	}
}

// fbSetup is a controller with the DERP host fbHost (region 900) and the other regions' hosts. noMap leaves the
// derpMap out of the policy; omit sets its OmitDefaultRegions.
func fbSetup(t *testing.T, link, noMap, omit bool, others []fbRegion) *dpHost {
	t.Helper()
	h := newDPHost(t)
	h.tailnet = true
	if h.tls == nil {
		h.tls = map[string]dpProbe{}
	}
	if h.stun == nil {
		h.stun = map[netip.AddrPort]dpProbe{}
	}
	regions := append([]fbRegion{{id: 900, host: fbHost, ip: fbIP, row: true}}, others...)
	h.policy = tailnet.Policy{Text: "{}", ETag: "e1"}
	if !noMap {
		h.policy.Text = fbPolicy(t, omit, regions)
	}
	h.addHost(fbHost, "derp-a", false)
	fbAgent(h, fbHost, fbStatus(fbIP, link), false)
	for _, r := range others {
		h.addHost(r.host, "derp-"+r.host, false)
		fbAgent(h, r.host, fbStatus(r.ip, false), r.statusErr)
		if r.ready {
			h.tls[r.ip] = dpProbe{d: 20 * time.Millisecond}
			h.stun[netip.MustParseAddrPort(r.ip+":3478")] = dpProbe{d: 15 * time.Millisecond}
		}
	}
	for _, r := range regions {
		if !r.row {
			continue
		}
		rec := serverRecord{
			HostID: r.host, Mode: "ip", HostName: r.ip, RegionID: r.id, RegionCode: "r" + strconv.Itoa(r.id),
			DerperVersion: "v1.104.1", UnitActive: true, CheckedAt: h.clock.Now(),
		}
		if err := upsertServerRecord(context.Background(), h.ctlDB, rec); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	return h
}

// fbRun reads derp.fallback.check through its handler.
func fbRun(t *testing.T, h *dpHost, in FallbackCheckInput) (FallbackCheck, error) {
	t.Helper()
	raw, err := json.Marshal(in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, out, err := h.dp().fallbackCheck().Handler.Reveal(context.Background(), h.ctlEnv(""), raw)
	if err != nil {
		return FallbackCheck{}, err
	}
	var got FallbackCheck
	if err := json.Unmarshal(out, &got); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("output %s: %v", out, err)
	}
	return got, nil
}

func fbCode(err error) errcode.Code {
	var coded *errcode.Error
	if errors.As(err, &coded) {
		return coded.Code
	}
	return ""
}

// TestFbPaths: the check answers the first path that holds, in the order link, default-regions, then the first
// other custom region (by id) whose host passes the probe, and refuses derp.no_fallback when none does.
func TestFbPaths(t *testing.T) {
	r901 := fbRegion{id: 901, host: "h2", ip: "203.0.113.20", row: true, ready: true}
	r902 := fbRegion{id: 902, host: "h3", ip: "203.0.113.30", row: true, ready: true}
	r903 := fbRegion{id: 903, host: "h4", ip: "203.0.113.40", row: true, ready: true}
	down := func(r fbRegion) fbRegion { r.ready = false; return r }
	noRow := func(r fbRegion) fbRegion { r.row = false; return r }
	failing := func(r fbRegion) fbRegion { r.statusErr = true; return r }
	outside := func(r fbRegion) fbRegion { r.id = 7; return r }
	tests := []struct {
		name         string
		link         bool
		noMap, omit  bool
		policyUnread bool // the policy must not be read at all
		others       []fbRegion
		want         string // the path; "" for the derp.no_fallback refusal
	}{
		{name: "the link does not run over the tailnet", link: false, omit: true, policyUnread: true, want: "link"},
		{name: "the policy has no derpMap", link: true, noMap: true, want: "default-regions"},
		{name: "the derpMap keeps the default regions", link: true, omit: false, want: "default-regions"},
		{name: "another custom region answers", link: true, omit: true, others: []fbRegion{r901}, want: "region:901"},
		{name: "the other region's server does not answer", link: true, omit: true, others: []fbRegion{down(r901)}},
		{name: "no other region", link: true, omit: true},
		{name: "the first answering region by id", link: true, omit: true, others: []fbRegion{r903, r902, down(r901)}, want: "region:902"},
		{name: "a region without a server row", link: true, omit: true, others: []fbRegion{noRow(r901)}},
		{name: "a region outside 900 to 999 is no candidate", link: true, omit: true, others: []fbRegion{outside(r901)}},
		{name: "a region whose host cannot be read is skipped", link: true, omit: true, others: []fbRegion{failing(r901), r902}, want: "region:902"},
	}
	for _, tc := range tests {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			h := fbSetup(t, tc.link, tc.noMap, tc.omit, tc.others)
			if tc.policyUnread {
				h.policyErr = errors.New("the policy must not be read")
			}
			got, err := fbRun(t, h, FallbackCheckInput{HostID: fbHost, RegionID: 900})
			if tc.want != "" {
				if err != nil || got.Path != tc.want {
					t.Fatalf("answer %+v, %v; want path %q", got, err, tc.want)
				}
				return
			}
			var coded *errcode.Error
			wantDetail := "the DERP host's link depends on region 900 and no other region is reachable; add a second region or clear omitDefaultRegions"
			if !errors.As(err, &coded) || coded.Code != errcode.DERPNoFallback || coded.Detail != wantDetail || !core.IsDefinite(err) {
				t.Fatalf("answer %+v, %v; want a definite %s refusal saying %q", got, err, errcode.DERPNoFallback, wantDetail)
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestFbReadErrors: a policy that cannot be read and a host that does not answer are errors of their own, not a
// refusal that claims no region is reachable.
func TestFbReadErrors(t *testing.T) {
	h := fbSetup(t, true, false, true, nil)
	h.policyErr = errcode.New(errcode.CloudNotConnected, "connect the tailnet account")
	if _, err := fbRun(t, h, FallbackCheckInput{HostID: fbHost, RegionID: 900}); fbCode(err) != errcode.CloudNotConnected {
		t.Errorf("unreadable policy: %v, want %s", err, errcode.CloudNotConnected)
	}
	h = fbSetup(t, true, false, true, nil)
	fbAgent(h, fbHost, fbStatus(fbIP, true), true)
	if _, err := fbRun(t, h, FallbackCheckInput{HostID: fbHost, RegionID: 900}); err == nil || fbCode(err) == errcode.DERPNoFallback {
		t.Errorf("unreadable host: %v, want an error other than %s", err, errcode.DERPNoFallback)
	}
}

// TestFbDeclaration: derp.fallback.check is an internal controller read without locks, with a schema.
func TestFbDeclaration(t *testing.T) {
	def := newDPHost(t).dp().fallbackCheck()
	s := def.Spec
	if s.Name != "derp.fallback.check" || s.Version != 1 || s.Class != core.ClassRead || s.Side != core.SideController ||
		!s.Internal || s.Reveal || len(s.LockKeys) != 0 || def.Handler.Reveal == nil || def.Handler.Read != nil {
		t.Errorf("spec %+v, handlers reveal %v read %v; want an internal controller read v1 without locks, served by Reveal only", s, def.Handler.Reveal != nil, def.Handler.Read != nil)
	}
	if _, err := schema.ActionSnapshot(def); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("schema: %v", err)
	}
}
```

Expected: FAIL to build with `h.dp().fallbackCheck undefined (type dp has no field or method fallbackCheck)`. Quote the output.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

In `internal/modules/derp/fallback_check.go`:

1. `func (d dp) fallbackCheck() core.ActionDef` returns `core.DefineInternalRead[FallbackCheckInput, FallbackCheck]` with `core.ActionSpec{Name: ID + ".fallback.check", Summary: <one sentence saying it names a management path that does not use the region, for the controller's own use>, Version: 1, Side: core.SideController}` and the handler `d.fallbackRead`. No `LockKeys`, no `Spec.Reveal` flag (`DefineInternalRead` itself installs the `Reveal` handler the test calls), no `MinScope`; constructing the declaration touches no socket, database or provider.
2. `func (d dp) fallbackRead(ctx context.Context, env core.Env, in FallbackCheckInput) (core.Revealed[FallbackCheck], error)` returns `Identity` `"DERP fallback check for host " + in.HostID` (no secret appears in it) and the first path that holds, evaluated in this order and stopping at the first:
   - `link`: `readStatus(ctx, env, in.HostID, nil)`; when `LinkViaTailnet` is false the answer is `link` and nothing else is read, the policy included. A read error is returned unchanged (the host's link is unknown, which is not a missing fallback).
   - `default-regions`: `d.d.policy(ctx, env)` (an error, such as `cloud.not_connected`, is returned unchanged) and `parseDERPMap(policy.Text)` (an error is returned wrapped as `derp: read the policy's derpMap: %w`); when the map is not `Present` or its `OmitDefaultRegions` is false the answer is `default-regions`.
   - `region:<id>`: `loadServerRecords(ctx, env.DB.Read())` once; for each id of `customRegions()` in ascending order except `in.RegionID`, take the first record (the list is ordered by host id) whose `RegionID` is that id and whose `HostID` is not `in.HostID`; no such record skips the id. Call `d.probeServer(ctx, env, ServerProbeInput{HostID: <that record's host>})` with no `Agents` (so the default agent set applies). An error skips the id, except that a done context returns `ctx.Err()`. When `probeReady` of the result reports ready the answer is `region:<id>`; its warnings and reason are ignored.
3. Nothing passes: return `core.Definite(errcode.New(errcode.DERPNoFallback, fmt.Sprintf("the DERP host's link depends on region %d and no other region is reachable; add a second region or clear omitDefaultRegions", in.RegionID)))`. The read writes nothing: no database write, no agent action other than reads.

If `TestFbPaths` fails on a "region:" row because `probeServer` reads something the fixture does not provide, fix `fbSetup` or `fbAgent` (test code), never the assertion or the production path order.

The tailscale module's `register_test.go` and the cloudflare module's `register_test.go` are the models of the tests; read them, never import or edit them.

**Depends on:** Task 15, Task 16, Task 17, Task 18, Task 19, Task 20, Task 21, Task 22, Task 23, Task 24, Task 25, Task 26, Task 27, Task 28

**Evaluation:** files 1 - spec 2 - coupling 2 - risk 1 = 6

The test prefix is `reg` (Contracts "Test prefixes"). The module declares no resource: the controller's list of DERP servers is the read action `derp.server.list`, because the dispatcher sends every resource read to an agent. The module declares no `Preflight`, no `ExternalUnits` and no `Settings`: availability is per action (`Available: d.available`), and the module drives only its own unit, which it owns.
